Just then, the moon that had been hidden behind clouds suddenly revealed itself.

As the surroundings grew bright.

The outline of a warrior hiding in the shadow of a wall some distance from her was exposed. He saw her as well.

The warrior tried to draw his sword and unleash sword qi, but Chai Ran was faster.

Shiiiiiik!

Puuk!

Chai Ran’s dagger flew straight and swift as light, lodging in his throat.

At the same time, sword qi flew at her from behind.

Swaaaak!

The sword qi filled the alley completely, and with Geom Mugeuk on her back, she kicked off the ground and leaped upward.

As she appeared in midair with the moon at her back, two warriors who had been lying in wait on the rooftops on either side rushed toward her as if they had been expecting it.

The warrior flying in from the left was faster.

Swaaaak.

Chai Ran stretched out her body and laid herself flat in the air. The sword grazed past her face and missed. Reflected on the blade, her expression was utterly calm.

Crack.

Grabbing her opponent’s wrist and twisting it, her form rotated together with his.

Puuuk!

The sword of the warrior rushing in from the right pierced the chest of the warrior on the left. Using the left warrior as a shield, Chai Ran blocked the attack and at the same time thrust out her right palm.

Puaang!

Force qi poured out like rain and struck the right warrior’s chest.

Pupupupu!

Holes were punched through his chest, and the warrior was sent flying far away. Her special palm technique, falling like night rain.

Heavenly Maiden Night Rain Palm (天女夜雨掌).

Her hidden unique martial art had been unleashed.

Ordinarily, she used only hidden weapons and never revealed her unique martial art, but today she was laying bare everything she had.

In an instant, she dropped back down and slipped into the alley.

Clouds covered the moon again, and the surroundings darkened.

After retrieving her dagger from the corpse, she quickly moved on again.

A low groan came from Geom Mugeuk’s mouth. The more violently she moved, the harder it would be on him.

But moving safely would be even more dangerous. However perilous, she had to increase her speed and break through.

When she turned into the next alley.

Four warriors lying in ambush attacked her at once.

Puaang!

She unleashed Heavenly Maiden Night Rain Palm at the warrior on the left, hurled the dagger in her right hand at the warrior on the right, and knocked aside the sword flying at her from the front with the back of her hand, twisting her body to avoid the attack from behind.

Crack!

The warrior struck by Heavenly Maiden Night Rain Palm flew backward, and the dagger lodged precisely in the other’s throat. She had also deflected the attack from the front, but the problem was the warrior behind her.

Shiiiiiik.

To keep Geom Mugeuk from being injured, she would have to sacrifice one arm.

Puuuk!

The sound of flesh tearing rang out.

But it was not a sound from Chai Ran’s body.

Someone rushed in, driving the man aside as if snatching him away, and stabbed his flank repeatedly with a dagger.

Puk! Puuuk!

The one who subdued him like that was none other than Ilgyo.

The warrior who had attacked from the front was being attacked by two others.

Clang! Clang!

He blocked Ogyo’s attack from the left, but.

Puk!

He could not block the dagger Sipchilgyo thrust into his back.

Just as he gritted his teeth, turned, and tried to swing his sword.

Puk! Puk!

This time, Ogyo stabbed him in the back repeatedly.

The warrior could no longer hold out and collapsed where he stood.

His martial arts had not been weak. If they let their guard down, they could have been the ones to fall.

The women exchanged only glances.

As Chai Ran ran on, sending a look of thanks, they returned a steady gaze telling her to leave the rear to them.

Ilgyo instructed Ogyo and Sipchilgyo to take the road on the right, then entered the alley on the left herself.

This was not a battle where moving in a herd gave an advantage. It was a battle of hunting one another in the dark.

Ogyo and Sipchilgyo moved as a pair. Their movements were cautious. They did not know where another hidden ambush might be. Chai Ran could avoid such attacks, but for them it would not be easy.

A scream erupted from the direction Ilgyo had gone. Fortunately, it was not the scream of a woman.

At the moment their attention was drawn by that sound.

A sword suddenly flew at Ogyo in a surprise attack.

Swaaaak!

The instant she struck aside the sword that had suddenly flown from the darkness.

Puuuk!

Another sword, its wielder dispelling his concealment as he appeared, stabbed Ogyo in the stomach.

Clang! Clang!

Sipchilgyo threw herself in and blocked the attacks that continued toward Ogyo.

The two warriors who revealed themselves were swift and powerful.

Even within the same organization, there were always those with greater skill. The ones they had encountered now were far stronger and faster than those they had fought before.

Clang! Clang clang!

Sipchilgyo protected Ogyo with all her might. If not for her single-minded determination to protect Ogyo, she would not have been able to stop the combined assault of the two.

Thanks to that, Ogyo earned time to stanch the wound in her abdomen and join the fight again. But Ogyo, rich in real combat experience, sensed it instinctively. At this rate, both of them would die.

Ogyo hurled herself fiercely at one of the warriors. It was a move meant to take him down with her in mutual destruction.

“You crazy—!”

It was an attack threatening enough to draw such a desperate outburst, an assault that disregarded her own life.

In that desperate attempt, Sipchilgyo found an opening.

At that very moment, the two women crossed past the opponents they had been fighting.

Shiiik!

Slice!

Sipchilgyo succeeded in cutting down the warrior Ogyo had tried to kill, but Ogyo, who had made the reckless move, was stabbed in the shoulder by the other warrior’s attack.

In the end, Ogyo sank to the ground where she stood. The wound in her abdomen was grave, but the wound in her shoulder was serious as well.

Sipchilgyo had no time to examine Ogyo’s injuries. The opponent continued to press her.

If she were to make a plan to kill him, Sipchilgyo could certainly do it. But this fight was different.

Assassins were people who killed others in secret, not those skilled at battles fought openly like this.

As Sipchilgyo was being pushed back, a strained word came from behind her.

“…Go.”

It was telling her to abandon her and run.

But Sipchilgyo fought to the end. Her arm was cut, and her thigh was cut as well, but she did not give up.

Kaaang.

The dagger she held flew from her hand and fell to the ground.

The warrior did not hesitate and thrust his sword toward her.

Shiiiiiik.

A hidden weapon appeared in Sipchilgyo’s hand. If the sword pierced her chest, she intended to seize the blade with one hand and throw the hidden weapon with the other.

Would that even work? But even so, doing that might give Ogyo the faintest chance of survival. Just as Ogyo had aimed for mutual destruction, she too intended to sacrifice herself.

Puuuk!

The sound of flesh tearing rang out.

It was not a sound from Sipchilgyo’s body. Nor was it the sound made by the hidden weapon in Sipchilgyo’s hand.

A dagger was lodged in the side of the warrior’s neck.

Papapak!

When the dagger was pulled out, blood sprayed forth, and he collapsed to the side.

Behind him stood a Faceless Guest. Sipchilgyo recognized him at once. He was the Crescent Moon Faceless Guest.

Of all people, he had saved her yet again. If she happened to meet him in this fight, she had wanted to be the one to save him.

The Faceless Guest picked up her dagger from the ground and handed it to her. It was the dagger she had wanted to give him, and now he was giving it to her.

But this was no time to be caught up in personal feelings.

She hurriedly examined Ogyo’s wounds.

She pressed her acupoints to stop the bleeding and applied Golden Wound Medicine. Ogyo’s face was pale, having already lost a great deal of blood.

Then the Faceless Guest handed her a small pill. It was an emergency medicine used by the Faceless Guests in urgent situations.

When Sipchilgyo placed the pill in Ogyo’s mouth, a slight flush returned to her pale face.

The Faceless Guest pointed in one direction with his finger. It was a path where they had already cleared out the enemy.

“Take your comrade and get out that way.”

It was the first time he had ever spoken to her.

Sipchilgyo bowed her head respectfully.

“Thank you.”

Regrettably, their conversation ended there. Sipchilgyo carried Ogyo on her back and ran in the direction he had indicated.

Before turning out of the alley, she looked back, but the Faceless Guest had already vanished into the darkness.

* * *

Amid the corpses, Chai Ran was gasping for breath.

“You brought quite a lot of spectators.”

Hwa Domyeong had said he would merely watch this affair, but in truth, he had staked everything he had on it.

She returned and looked at Geom Mugeuk, who sat leaning against the wall.

The bastards had tried to take him away from where she had seated him, and while forcing herself to stop them, she had been cut in two places. Fortunately, they were not severe wounds, but she had lost a great deal of blood, and her condition was not good.

‘Just a little farther.’

Now she had escaped the maze-like alleys, and if she only crossed that forest, Sinhwabang would not be far.

“Young Cult Leader, hold on just a little longer! I’ll make sure to get you there!”

Just as she was leaving the alley, Chai Ran stopped in her tracks.

Her expression darkened.

In the clearing before the forest, more than twenty warriors stood in formation with swords in hand.

They wore the same clothes as the ones she had fought earlier, but their gazes were sharper, their auras keener. They were likely the most skilled within the organization.

Even breaking through them in her current condition would not be easy.

And Hwa Domyeong stood among them.

That was not all. Behind Hwa Domyeong stood three warriors, two men and one woman, wearing bamboo hats. They revealed no aura whatsoever, but their very presence grated on her nerves. She could tell by an assassin’s instinct.

‘They’re dangerous.’

Hwa Domyeong looked at her and spoke calmly.

“He must be heavy. Put him down now.”

Chai Ran untied the cloth she had bound and carefully seated Geom Mugeuk in place.

She looked at Geom Mugeuk.

It seemed as though he would open his eyes at any moment and smile brightly. Yet Geom Mugeuk still had both eyes closed, saying nothing.

“I’m sorry, but it looks like the commission failed.”

A faint smile formed on her lips as she prepared herself for death.

“Abandoning a commission, failing a commission… I really am a hopeless assassin.”

Still, Geom Mugeuk said nothing.

After gazing at him for a moment, she slowly rose and turned around. A dagger was in her hand.

“May I choose who goes with me?”

Without waiting for an answer, Cha Iran aimed the dagger in her hand at Hwa Domyeong.

“You.”

Swaaaak.

Hwa Domyeong spread his fan and waved it gently. If it had been just the two of them, he would have hidden his face behind that fan. But before his subordinates, he became a different person altogether.

“Right. Only at times like this are you a spectator?”

As if he would not permit even a single word of mockery, Hwa Domyeong gave a cold order.

“Kill her and bring the Young Cult Leader.”

Just as some twenty martial artists were about to step forward.

Someone flew in from behind.

The one who rushed over and blocked the way in front of Cha Iran was Ilgyo. A few more assassins arrived behind her. The fighting was still continuing in the alley, and Ilgyo had arrived first with only a handful of assassins.

Cha Iran could tell. With just them, they could not turn the tide. Hwa Domyeong was one thing, but those three mysterious figures were even more ominous.

That was why she gave Ilgyo an order.

“Take the girls who are fighting and leave this place.”

“I cannot.”

Ilgyo had absolutely no intention of retreating. For the first time, she disobeyed Cha Iran’s command.

The same order fell from Hwa Domyeong’s lips.

“Kill them all.”

At that moment, the moon, which had been hidden behind the clouds again, revealed itself, brightening the surroundings.

Compared to the enemies closing in, their side’s forces were far too weak.

It was then.

Ilgyo said to Cha Iran,

“I will obey your order.”

At her sudden change in attitude, Cha Iran was puzzled, and Hwa Domyeong sneered.

“Yes, those who can live should live.”

As if to say that was not the reason, Ilgyo raised her head and looked upward.

Then everyone’s gaze followed hers.

The place Ilgyo was looking at was a great tree. Someone was up in that tree.

When on earth had they arrived?

On the high branches of the great tree were the Faceless Guests.

Some were standing, others crouching. Others were sitting astride the branches. Wearing masks, they silently looked down upon the scene below, their appearance so bizarre it was frightening.

Whish, whish, whish, whish, whish, whish, whish!

Those in the tree leapt down all at once.

Tap, tap, tap, tap, tap, tap, tap, tap!

Even from that height, the Faceless Guests landed lightly.

Like a shield, they formed a single line between Cha Iran and the assassins.

Hwa Domyeong stared at them with cold eyes.

Earlier, he had disregarded the assassins, but he could not disregard the Faceless Guests. Not because he feared them, but because—

‘If they have come…?’

Then a low voice came from behind Cha Iran.

“Young Cult Leader.”

When Cha Iran and the assassins turned around in surprise, Geugak Soma was already sitting before Geom Mugeuk, who lay behind them.

Bathed in cold moonlight, Geugak Soma’s white mask shone blue. From within that chill flowed a burning gaze.

“I have come, so please open your eyes!”

Chapter 712: Please pierce my heart!
------------------------------------
The creature was intelligent.

The Bihyeolsa, which had been attacking his heart nonstop, changed its strategy.

It began tearing at Geom Mugeuk’s blood vessels. At first, it bit into vessels of little importance, testing Geom Mugeuk’s reaction. When Geom Mugeuk paid it no mind, it began gnawing at increasingly vital vessels.

It was a strategy that exploited the fact that if he dispersed his internal energy to protect his blood vessels, the strength guarding his heart would ultimately weaken.

Of course, that did not mean it attacked hard enough to kill Geom Mugeuk. It knew that if Geom Mugeuk died, it would die as well. The creature was truly close to a spiritual being with intelligence.

It moved so swiftly he could not catch it. In the end, he had no choice but to pretend to yield his heart and trap it.

He gave up half of his heart, then mobilized all his internal power to prevent it from entering the remaining half.

Once the Bihyeolsa entered his heart, it did not leave again. At first, it merely coiled up and remained still.

He thought that was fortunate.

It was not.

Through the heart, the creature absorbed power, becoming even stronger than when it had roamed his blood vessels. Conversely, Geom Mugeuk grew that much weaker.

Having replenished its strength, the Bihyeolsa began attacking the remaining half of his heart. It pushed in with a force incomparable to before.

Geom Mugeuk poured all his internal energy into stopping the Bihyeolsa.

He endured, and endured again.

At first, he had done so to keep his blood vessels from being harmed. Now, he did it to keep his heart from being damaged. The toll on his mind was immense.

Enduring with every nerve and every ounce of strength was truly agonizing.

He wanted to scream, but he did not. Surely Cha Iran was also doing her utmost.

If she learned of his condition, her urgency would make her commit a mistake. And so Geom Mugeuk endured, and endured again.

When that pain reached its peak, Geom Mugeuk saw an illusion.

He was blocking an enormous wall that came pressing toward him, all alone.

Little by little, he was pushed back. It felt as though the muscles and bones throughout his body were melting away. He wanted to let the strength drain from his arms and give up.

But Geom Mugeuk did not give up. He was not the kind of person who surrendered simply because things were hard.

How long had he endured like that?

Thud.

At the presence beside him, Geom Mugeuk turned his head. In that instant, he was startled.

Someone was pushing the wall alongside him, and it was the handsome evil spirit among the east, west, south, and north of the Guhwa Demonic Art.

‘How are you here?’

Thud.

Then an evil spirit appeared on the opposite side as well. The evil spirit that appeared this time was the frightening-looking one. He glanced once at Geom Mugeuk with that terrifying face, then began pushing the wall.

Thud.

This time, the one who gave off a mysterious air appeared beside the handsome evil spirit, and lastly, the clever-looking one joined beside the frightening fellow.

When the four of them pushed the wall together, it no longer forced him backward.

Geom Mugeuk was overjoyed.

And at the final moment, something blocked the light, casting a gigantic shadow around Geom Mugeuk.

When Geom Mugeuk lifted his head and looked up, something enormous began pushing the wall.

The Heavenly Demon Soul?

Yes, it was the Heavenly Demon Soul. It was dark, so he could not see clearly, but it was unmistakably the Heavenly Demon Soul.

With the evil spirits and the Heavenly Demon Soul together, the wall began moving forward again.

‘Ah! Since even the Heavenly Demon Soul has come, I can let go now.’

The strength gradually drained from Geom Mugeuk’s arms.

But this illusion was a heart demon created by Geom Mugeuk’s unconscious because he was in too much pain. In reality, the wall was being pushed backward.

Heart demons often shook one’s mind by using the person they trusted most.

Yes, surely it was all right to rest now?

It was just as the strength was about to leave his hands completely.

From within the darkness, he heard someone calling him.

……Young Cult Leader.

This voice? A familiar voice, heard as though whispering from afar.

The first voice calling him sounded distant, but the next words rang out loudly, as though sword energy imbued with light had pierced through the darkness and flown toward him.

“I am here, so open your eyes!”

The instant he clearly realized it was the voice of Geugak Soma.

The things around him began to vanish.

The evil spirits disappeared one by one.

Lastly, the Heavenly Demon Soul vanished. As the shadow cast by the Heavenly Demon Soul disappeared, the surroundings brightened. It was the moment the heart demon shattered.

“Young Cult Leader!”

At Geugak Soma’s call, Geom Mugeuk’s eyes flew open.

Geugak Soma was truly looking at him from right before his eyes. Only then did Geom Mugeuk understand. Everything he had seen in the illusion had been a heart demon.

“Are you all right?”

The two eyes within the mask were filled with worry for Geom Mugeuk. He had sensed that Geom Mugeuk’s condition was not good.

Geom Mugeuk wrung out the last of his strength.

“Stab my heart!”

“!”

At those words, Geugak Soma’s eyes widened.

He knew the moment of prophecy had come upon them.

Geugak Soma, too, had thought many things about the prophecy.

Would he merely pretend to kill the Young Cult Leader? Or would he kill a fake disguised as the Young Cult Leader?

But he had never imagined that he would have to stab the real Geom Mugeuk in the heart.

Geom Mugeuk’s hand grasped Geugak Soma’s wrist. Then he pulled that hand toward his heart.

‘Trust yourself and stab.’

With that gesture as his last, Geom Mugeuk closed his eyes. By the time he had fallen into the heart demon, the Bihyeolsa had already invaded even the remaining half of his heart. The more of the heart it occupied, the stronger it became.

The moment Hwa Domyeong heard Geom Mugeuk tell him to stab his heart, he shouted.

“No! Stop him!”

He had mistaken it for Geom Mugeuk trying to take his own life. He thought Geom Mugeuk intended to die rather than be completely dominated by the Bihyeolsa.

If Geom Mugeuk died, then killing the leader of the Evil Alliance would be a failure, but more importantly, he would be unable to retrieve the energies within Geom Mugeuk’s body.

More than twenty of Hwa Domyeong’s subordinates all launched themselves forward at once.

The Faceless Guests also drew their daggers in unison and faced them.

Whoosh.

Dodging the sword of the martial artist who charged in first, a Faceless Guest drove his dagger into the man’s chest.

Clang!

The martial artist who blocked the attack was no ordinary expert. He was certainly more skilled than those they had faced in the alley.

But the ten strongest Faceless Guests of the Valley of Villains were a type of enemy they had never encountered before.

Just as the man thrust his sword again.

Shuk! Shuuuk!

The Faceless Guest’s finger winds flew toward his face. Since he had never imagined his opponent could fire finger wind, he barely avoided the first, but the second took off his ear.

Pak!

The instant the enraged martial artist’s movements grew larger.

Puk!

The Faceless Guest’s dagger lodged in his excited heart.

Immediately pulling out the dagger, the Faceless Guest shot to the side.

Just as one enemy was about to ambush the back of an assassin woman in combat.

Shiiiik.

The Faceless Guest from before snatched the opponent and drove his dagger into the man’s throat.

Puuuk.

The martial artist who tried to attack the Faceless Guest who had saved the assassin fell to Ilgyo’s hand.

The Faceless Guests and the assassins moved in perfect harmony. They helped one another and fought together like comrades.

Having confirmed that the opponents were stronger than expected, Hwa Domyeong issued an order to the three bamboo-hatted figures standing behind him.

“Kill them all!”

At that, two of the three bamboo-hatted figures—one man and one woman—launched themselves forward first.

The woman used a sword, and the man used a saber; their skills were anything but ordinary.

Shiiiik!

Shwaaaak!

Sword energy and saber energy, fast and powerful, flew in a surprise attack toward the crescent-moon Faceless Guest and the spider-patterned Faceless Guest beside him.

It was too late to dodge.

At that very moment.

Puaang!

Another surge of force swept out.

Kwaaang!

It collided with the incoming sword energy and saber energy.

The person who descended before the crescent-moon Faceless Guest and the spider Faceless Guest was Cha Iran. She had thrust out both palms and released the Heavenly Maiden Night Rain Palm, saving the two of them.

“Your opponent is me.”

The two Faceless Guests glanced at Cha Iran once, then launched themselves back into motion. It was not a situation where they could exchange thanks.

Cha Iran’s gaze turned toward Hwa Domyeong, who stood beyond the bamboo-hatted martial artists. His gaze was not on her. He was looking past her, at Geom Mugeuk and Geugak Soma.

She could feel his urgency. Perhaps the reason he had so often hidden behind his fan in front of her until now was to keep that impatience from being discovered.

Hwa Domyeong personally launched himself forward and landed before Cha Iran. Then the last bamboo-hatted figure behind him flew in as well. Judging from his movement, he was surely the martial artist tasked with escorting Hwa Domyeong safely away.

Hwa Domyeong had no intention of conversing with her. He also knew she was not the kind of person who would move aside simply because he told her to.

Shwaaaak.

A line of powerful energy surged from his fan and flew out.

Just as Cha Iran sprang up to avoid the force.

The remaining man and woman in bamboo hats flew in again, attacking together.

She struck away the incoming sword with her dagger and unleashed palm techniques in succession.

Cha Iran blocked with all her might, determined not to let even a single person pass behind her.

But facing all three of them at once, including Hwa Domyeong, was beyond her ability.

In the end, amid the continuous attacks, she failed to avoid a streak of saber energy from the bamboo-hatted man.

Peok!

The saber energy struck her body. She instantly raised her protective energy, but the shock was tremendous.

Even so, she did not move aside. In the midst of it, she hurled a palm strike at the bamboo-hatted man who had unleashed the saber technique.

It was then.

Pat!

Before she knew it, the bamboo-hatted woman had flown over and was suspended in the air before her. It was a remarkable movement art, as though she had moved through space in an instant.

Shiiiik!

The sword was already flying toward Cha Iran’s face. If her body had been in normal condition, she could have avoided it, but right now she was in a terrible state.

She would not die, but she could not avoid a grave injury to her face.

At that very moment.

Shiiik!

A dagger flew swiftly toward the bamboo-hatted woman.

Clang!

The sword knocked the dagger aside in midair and continued on to cut Cha Iran.

But Cha Iran had already escaped the woman’s attack. The one who had thrown the dagger was the spider-patterned Faceless Guest she had saved earlier.

Shwaaaak! Shwaak!

Without giving her a moment to breathe, waves of force poured toward Cha Iran.

Unfortunately, the assassins were fighting behind her, so Cha Iran did not dodge and instead unleashed her palm techniques head-on.

Boom! Kwaaang! Kwaang!

From the left and right came the bamboo-hatted figures, and from the front came Hwa Domyeong. Thus the attacks of all three focused on Cha Iran. Under the barrage, her figure, now suffering internal injuries, staggered.

Someone threw themselves forward and saved her. This time, it was the crescent-moon martial artist.

The crescent-moon Faceless Guest took the internally injured Cha Iran and retreated.

The assassin women rushed over and protected her.

The Faceless Guests formed a circle around Geom Mugeuk and Geugak Soma. They did not fear death, and their eyes said they would not yield even a single step.

“Sweep them all away!”

At Hwa Domyeong’s order, the two bamboo-hatted figures stepped forward first.

Puuuk!

The sound of a blade tearing flesh rang out. It came from the place encircled by the Faceless Guests.

When the Faceless Guests turned around, Hwa Domyeong could see the scene through the gaps between them.

Geugak Soma’s dagger was embedded in Geom Mugeuk’s heart.

In that instant, Hwa Domyeong’s eyes widened.

‘He really stabbed the Young Cult Leader?’

He knew that the Demon Venerable closest to Geom Mugeuk was Geugak Soma. He had thought that other Demon Venerables might be one thing, but Geugak Soma would never be able to kill Geom Mugeuk.

The Faceless Guests also stared at the sight with shocked faces before turning their eyes forward again.

The assassin women looked back and forth between the two of them in horror.

Geugak Soma had truly stabbed Geom Mugeuk in the heart.

While Cha Iran, the Faceless Guests, and the assassins were fiercely fighting the enemies.

Geugak Soma had his palm pressed against Geom Mugeuk’s heart and was focusing his mind.

For Geugak Soma, all sound around him had vanished, and he could feel only the beating of Geom Mugeuk’s heart. In this moment, Geugak Soma was both divine physician and demonic physician.

An alien energy could be felt within the heart.

He could feel the Bihyeolsa taking control of the heart. He knew instinctively. To kill the Bihyeolsa, he had to stab that head—no, that skull—precisely.

But Geugak Soma hesitated. If he stabbed incorrectly, Geom Mugeuk would die.

The problem was that the Bihyeolsa was moving in directions he could not predict, advancing toward the other side of the heart.

There would be only one chance.

The moment the creature seized the final portion of Geom Mugeuk’s heart.

The instant it occupied that last empty space.

Geugak Soma waited for that instant.

He knew a fierce battle was taking place outside, but Geugak Soma focused only on the Bihyeolsa.

And finally.

The moment the Bihyeolsa completely occupied Geom Mugeuk’s heart.

Geugak Soma’s dagger silently cut through the air. The character for laughter, 笑, engraved upon the dagger, cleaved the air.

With the dagger Geom Mugeuk had given him, with the martial art Geom Mugeuk had taught him, he stabbed Geom Mugeuk’s heart.

Puk!

He did not merely pretend to stab. He drove it in deeply enough to touch the heart.

In that instant, silence flowed there.

Everyone stared at the sight.

When Geugak Soma pulled out the dagger, Geom Mugeuk lowered his head, blood flowing from his chest.

Geom Mugeuk did not move at all. He truly looked dead.

The one who shouted belatedly was Hwa Domyeong.

“No!”

He thought Geom Mugeuk had died.

But the one that had died was the Bihyeolsa.

Geugak Soma’s dagger had succeeded in precisely stabbing the Bihyeolsa’s vital point.

Paaaaak.

The Bihyeolsa, its skull pierced, seemed to convulse once.

Paaaaaaa.

Then the Bihyeolsa’s body slowly began to scatter.

The energies of tremendous elixirs, rare herbs, and various divine objects filled the heart. That mysterious energy joined together the wound in the heart torn by the dagger.

Geom Mugeuk’s body began refining that energy.

It was the first time. Until now, no elixir had ever been refined starting from the heart.

The energy squeezed out by his heart flowed through his blood vessels and spread throughout his entire body.

An immense energy he had never felt before.

Geom Mugeuk began transforming the Bihyeolsa’s energy, which filled all his blood vessels, into internal energy.

Geugak Soma silently looked at Geom Mugeuk.

Geom Mugeuk’s aura was so faint that only he, being so close, could barely sense it.

But Geugak Soma could tell.

Geom Mugeuk’s expression had become peaceful. There was even a faint smile at the corners of his mouth, as though he were having a pleasant dream.

‘It worked.’

He had stabbed correctly. Never in his life had he ever been this tense. Even if it had been his own heart, he would not have been this nervous.

The prophecy had come true.

That unbelievable prophecy, that he would stab Geom Mugeuk, had come true exactly as foretold. The fact that the location had changed from a stage was probably because of that assassin woman.

When she changed her mind, the stage for selecting the greatest beauty under heaven had vanished.

At that moment, Hwa Domyeong’s furious voice rang out.

“You lunatic! Even so, how could you kill the Young Cult Leader?”

Geugak Soma slowly turned his head and looked at Hwa Domyeong.

The two eyes within the mask smiled coldly as he spoke.

“Then now you must understand.”

Blood dripped from the dagger in Geugak Soma’s hand.

“That there is no one I cannot kill.”

Chapter 713: Do We Stand a Chance Now?
--------------------------------------
Geukak Soma, who had been sitting before Geom Mugik, rose to his feet.

As Geukak Soma walked forward, the Faceless Guests standing in a circle closed ranks, sealing the entrance. Though it appeared as if they were determined to protect the Little Cult Lord's corpse, their true intention was to prevent Geom Mugik from being exposed to the outside.

At first, the Faceless Guests had been surprised as well.

Because when they turned around, they had seen Geukak Soma plunging a dagger into Geom Mugik's heart.

But now they understood.

That Geom Mugik, sitting with his head deeply bowed, was alive. That they had to protect him until the Little Cult Lord regained consciousness.

Geukak Soma's eyes met Chairan's, who stood among the assassins.

If she hadn't brought Geom Mugik all the way here, they might have missed their chance.

Chairan could feel it. That Geukak Soma's gaze upon her was gentle. Because she knew what the gratitude in those eyes meant, she was finally able to feel relieved.

'So the Little Cult Lord is alive.'

Right. If he had seemed like someone who would kill her, he wouldn't have asked her to bring him to Geukak Soma in the first place.

Chairan's gaze turned toward the Faceless Guests. They stood packed so tightly that Geom Mugik couldn't be seen.

'The commission has been completed safely.'

She had succeeded in a commission to save someone for the first time.

Furthermore, thanks to the Faceless Guests' exploits, all the assassins, including Ilgyo, had made it out safely.

Just then, sounds of fighting came from an alley far in the distance. That battle still wasn't over.

Geukak Soma spoke politely to Chairan. It was courtesy owed to the person who had saved Geom Mugik.

"Please leave this place to us now."

It was a signal for her to go rescue her subordinates.

Chairan didn't hesitate. If she remained here in her current state, injured internally, she would only become a burden to Geukak Soma.

That Hwado Myeong clearly had something he was putting his faith in. Because even with Geukak Soma right before his eyes, he wasn't thinking of fleeing.

She cupped her hands respectfully to Geukak Soma and bade farewell.

"Then I shall take my leave."

After paying her respects, she moved to leave first.

"Let's go."

The female assassins cupped their hands and greeted the Faceless Guests. Thanks to their efforts, none of the assassins who had come here had died.

Sibchilgyo, who had safely entrusted Ogyo to his comrades and joined the fight late, bowed politely to the Crescent Moon Faceless Guest. He owed him his life twice over now.

'You have to repay your debt, so don't die.'

Whether that man read the look in his eyes containing this sentiment, he couldn't know.

And so Chairan took her subordinates and leaped toward the alleyway.

Until the very moment she left, she was witnessing a new side of Hwado Myeong.

He didn't give her so much as a glance as she left.

He had acted like a friend, then tried to kill her.

And now he wouldn't even look at her.

Was it because he was lost in the shock and fury of mission failure? His usual relaxed, sly demeanor was nowhere to be found.

The composure he had shown earlier was, quite literally, the ease of a man who was at ease. Yes, adversity always reveals what kind of person someone truly is.

'Can the urgent desires hidden behind your fan truly overcome the loyalty behind that smiling mask?'

It was with this thought that she could leave that place without worry.

At this moment, Hwado Myeong was furious. He truly couldn't contain his boiling rage. The fact that the Little Cult Lord had died made Chairan not even worth his notice.

"What will your Cult Leader say when you return? Ah, truly living up to your name! Is that what he'll say?"

Geukak Soma considered this rather fortunate. If they misunderstood that Geom Mugik was dead, they would be relatively less concerned about his safety.

He wanted to rush in and slaughter them all immediately, but Geukak Soma wasn't hasty. Time was on their side. The enemy clearly had something they believed in, so they wouldn't run.

"It is already done."

Just then, words flowed out that refuted Geukak Soma's cold statement.

"That is a lie."

It was the man among the three wearing bamboo hats who had not stepped forward. His voice was very young.

Everyone's gazes focused on him.

"The Little Cult Lord is alive."

Even though the man was far from Geom Mugik, he had sensed that faint lifeforce. Moreover, the Faceless Guests were surrounding him, so it should have been even harder to read that energy.

Hwado Myeong's eyes opened wide in surprise.

"Is it true?"

The man was certain.

"It is certain. Right now, I sense no anger or sorrow from Geukak Soma. The Little Cult Lord is alive."

He had read not only the presence of someone far away but also the opponent's emotions. One could tell he was a very special individual.

"His heart was pierced; how did he survive?"

In response to Hwado Myeong's question, a surprising answer came.

"I cannot sense the aura of the Biheolsa from the Little Cult Lord's body."

Hwado Myeong's surprised eyes grew even wider.

"You don't mean we can't sense it because he's dead?"

"The Little Cult Lord is definitely alive."

Hwado Myeong's expression brightened. If he was alive, it meant the energy could still be recovered.

The man's gaze turned to Geukak Soma. Staring at the two eyes within the mask, he spoke.

"Geukak Soma plunged the dagger in to kill the Biheolsa that resided in the heart."

It was difficult to believe, but Hwado Myeong trusted the man's words.

This man was the disciple of the very person who had created the Biheolsa. In fact, he had helped his master craft the Biheolsa together.

Because this scheme had been orchestrated using the Biheolsa, he had come along to provide assistance.

Even more shocking words flowed from his mouth.

"Right now, the Little Cult Lord must be dissolving the Biheolsa's energy."

Hwado Myeong's expression instantly hardened.

"Didn't you say the Biheolsa's energy is so immense that a human cannot dissolve it?"

"That is correct."

Though he said so, his tone lacked the certainty it had held until now.

However, one thing was certain.

"If the Little Cult Lord awakens, we have no chance of victory. We must finish this before he wakes."

Just as Hwado Myeong was about to issue the command to kill Geukak Soma and all the Faceless Guests, Geukak Soma asked with a cold laugh.

"Do you even have a chance of victory now?"

The moment the words left his mouth, Geukak Soma gave the first order to his subordinates.

"Kill them all!"

The moment the command fell, ten Faceless Guests leaped forward at once.

From the other side, over ten surviving martial artists rushed out.

It was a fight with Geukak Soma watching. The morale of the Faceless Guests pierced the heavens.

Though they had fought alongside the assassins, these were Faceless Guests who had faced over twenty enemies and killed ten of them without a single casualty on their side.

The Faceless Guests' fight was truly a battle of villains.

They focused solely on winning. They rolled across the ground to unleash kicking winds, and when at a disadvantage, they threw dirt.

They didn't hesitate to stab their enemies in the back, and if a comrade stabbed their opponent in the back and killed them, they felt no revulsion whatsoever. They had no pride or self-esteem insisting that they had to be the one to kill their enemy.

Yet they did not fear death.

In the midst of it, two bamboo-hatted martial artists tried to leap into the fight.

But Geukak Soma stopped them with a single line. Not "if you move, I move."

"If you move, that man dies."

It was a warning that he would kill Hwado Myeong.

The threat worked perfectly. They couldn't move.

Having felled all the enemies, the Faceless Guests leaped back nimbly.

Thud-thud-thud-thud-thud-thud!

They surrounded Geom Mugik once more. Even as this battle unfolded, Geom Mugik remained still with his eyes closed.

The Faceless Guests protected him, encircling him in a ring once again. Blood continued to splatter onto their masks.

Once all the martial artists lined up in front were dealt with, Geukak Soma strode toward Hwado Myeong.

Filled with fear at the sight of the white mask approaching without a hint of hesitation, Hwado Myeong opened his fan.

Swish.

From behind the fan, he gave his command.

"Hurry. Didn't you hear the Little Cult Lord is alive?"

The two bamboo-hatted martial artists standing side by side strode forward. Black energy began to surge around their bodies.

The reason Hwado Myeong wasn't afraid of Geukak Soma was because of these two martial artists. They were experts who had mastered the Dark Divine Art of the Dark Palace.

Dark Divine Art.

A martial art passed down to the warriors of the Dark Palace, it boasted truly immense power.

However, because that powerful force inevitably came with restrictions and side effects, it couldn't be used indiscriminately. It was a martial art that could only be used at absolutely necessary moments. The moment one succumbed to the temptation of that strength, only ruin awaited.

Sssk.

As if tattoos were being engraved, long black lines were drawn across the cheeks of the two men beneath their bamboo hats.

The Dark Divine Art that explosively increased movement speed.

Dark Ghost Stride Technique.

That wasn't all. This time, a circle was drawn.

The Dark Divine Art that hardened skin and muscles like steel and even amplified the power of protective astral qi.

Dark Iron Body Art.

As the round circle was superimposed over the long drawn line, the pattern looked like wind brushing past the full moon.

With two Dark Divine Arts stacked atop each other, they became incomparably faster and stronger than before.

Shwick!

The two people's charging movements were fast. Their speed was on a completely different level from when they had fought Chairan earlier.

Especially the woman's movements, which had already been fast, were truly like lightning.

In a flash, the two bamboo-hatted martial artists were attacking Geukak Soma from left and right. Like people who had trained in combined techniques for a long time, they were in perfect harmony.

Shwaaack! Shwick!

From the left, a saber; from the right, a sword flew in.

It was a truly perfect attack with no openings to escape.

A smile formed on the watching Hwado Myeong's lips.

'How will he block this perfect combined technique?'

How? Geukak Soma blocked it while standing right there.

Kang! Kaang!

After deflecting the saber flying from the left, he parried the sword on the right.

Joy flashed across the two martial artists' faces. In this combined attack, if one didn't escape on the first move, they absolutely couldn't escape. They should have escaped even while accepting injuries.

Sword and saber coordinated as attacks of tremendous speed poured down on Geukak Soma. He absolutely wouldn't be able to withstand this speed and power.

Clang-clang-clang-clang-clang-clang!

Dozens of sword lines and saber lines, and even the lines created by the dagger blocking them.

In a flash, the surroundings were engulfed in blade light and saber light.

When they launched their first attacks, they hadn't known Geukak Soma would endure this well.

The woman couldn't believe it. Blocking the combined attack of two people wielding the Dark Ghost Stride Technique?

Geukak Soma was currently fighting two ghosts. No—they were the ones fighting a ghost.

'This won't do!'

Their decision was swift. The Dark Divine Art was not a martial art suited for drawn-out battles.

Sssss.

Another line appeared on the woman's face. The Dark Divine Art was being strengthened. She overlaid another layer of Dark Ghost Stride Technique, resolved to accept internal injuries in exchange for increased speed.

A change occurred on the man's face as well. He didn't strengthen the Dark Ghost Stride Technique, but the Dark Iron Body Art. By two more levels, at that.

Three circles overlapped on his face. Though they knew they would suffer severe internal injuries after the Dark Art dispelled if they pushed this hard, they had to cast the die.

The woman realized what his intention was.

Just as the sword, saber, and dagger of the three were creating a fantastic painting—

There was someone who threw a brushstroke into that painting.

The bamboo-hatted man threw his saber at Geukak Soma while simultaneously hurling his body forward. It was an unexpected ambush, a truly unpredictable move meant to fluster the opponent by throwing his weapon.

He would be cut, but if he could just grab Geukak Soma in that instant?

Then it would be over. Because she would sever Geukak Soma's neck with one blade.

He was confident. With the third stage of Dark Iron Body Art, he absolutely wouldn't die. In this moment, he was invincible.

The dagger slashed toward him as he charged.

Flash.

The single line created by the dagger.

The man without his saber naturally couldn't block this single move.

Kaaaang.

A metallic sound erupted from his body.

The man intuitively sensed he had succeeded.

'I did it!'

If he hadn't died to this single move—

'You die!'

The man grabbed Geukak Soma's body tightly with both arms.

In his eyes, he saw the woman flying in to cut Geukak Soma's neck.

Both the man and woman were smiling.

In that fleeting instant—

Geukak Soma's arm moved forward.

The man was shocked. He had been holding on so tight it felt like he would burst; how had he pulled his arm free?

The next instant, an eardrum-tearing explosion burst out.

Kwaaang!

The man standing behind saw.

The astral qi flying from Geukak Soma's palm.

The Demonic Ruinous Light Blast Palm exploding into the woman's face.

Boooom!

Hit by the Demonic Ruinous Light Blast Palm at such close range, the woman's face was obliterated without a trace.

The woman's body, now headless, spewed blood as it collapsed backward.

The man couldn't understand at all.

'Why can't I put strength into my arms?'

Even in this very moment, he couldn't put strength into his arms.

Only then did the bamboo-hatted man feel something hot flowing through his chest.

Drip.

His steel-like skin was split wide open.

Blood was gushing out from between that split.

'Was I cut?'

He was greatly shocked by the fact that the third-stage Dark Iron Body Art had been cut through in a single move.

But how could that move, which had looked so ordinary, be a mere ordinary attack?

It was the Secret Art of the Fragrant Smile, executed by Geukak Soma with a ten-thousand-year cold iron dagger.

One Extreme of the Fragrant Smile.

Then pain flooded in.

Because it wasn't an ordinary wound but one created by the shattering of the Dark Art, it was incomparable to the pain of being cut by a mere blade. Truly unbearable, excruciating pain flooded in.

"Uwaaaaah!"

A desperate scream burst from the bamboo-hatted man's mouth. The pain was so great that he wished someone would just kill him quickly.

Then someone covered his mouth. Before he knew it, Geukak Soma was behind his back.

"Shh!"

Geukak Soma, covering the man's mouth, slashed his throat with the dagger without hesitation.

Saaaak.

It was not to lessen his pain.

Standing behind the man as blood spurted from his neck, Geukak Soma spoke in a low voice.

"The Little Cult Lord is about to wake."

Chapter 714: It Was Dark, So I Couldn't See Clearly.
----------------------------------------------------
No sound could be heard.

No matter how loudly they fought outside, Geom Mugeuk did not hear it.

Right now, Geom Mugeuk was immersed in a world of his own.

As he dissolved Bihyeolsa’s energy into his internal energy, only thoughts related to internal energy and martial arts filled his mind.

Ordinarily, a state of selflessness was a fortuitous encounter that came during martial training or when one gained some martial enlightenment.

And yet now, he had fallen into that state while dissolving Bihyeolsa’s energy into his internal energy.

Perhaps it was because the process of dissolving it had been so dramatic, and because that energy was so immense.

More internal energy than any spiritual medicine he had ever taken began to accumulate in his dantian. No matter how much he dissolved, there was no end to it.

As a martial artist, could there be any greater joy than this?

Geom Mugeuk strove not to let even a single handful of the gift fate had given him slip away. It was, moreover, a fortuitous encounter made possible because Geugak Soma had stabbed his own heart.

And internal energy was not the only thing he had gained from this.

First, in the process of subduing Bihyeolsa, he had learned many new blood pathways. He could move qi in ways he had never imagined before, and thanks to that, he could execute his techniques more efficiently.

Techniques that were a little faster and a little stronger.

At Geom Mugeuk’s level, that “little” was more than enough to decide victory and defeat several times over.

And one more thing.

The process of enduring until the very end while protecting his heart had been an enormous experience for Geom Mugeuk.

It had been so terrible that it helped him; after all, had even Geom Mugeuk, who was more accustomed to enduring pain than anyone else, not fallen into an inner demon?

By experiencing his own limits, Geom Mugeuk matured further and grew stronger.

Geom Mugeuk could feel it instinctively.

He had taken one great step closer to the mastery of the Guhwa Demonic Art, which had seemed so vague until now.

He even thought that perhaps seeing the Heavenly Demon Soul within his inner demon had not been a coincidence.

‘Ah, so this was the feeling. The reason Father entered closed-door seclusion.’

He had the premonition that if he reached out his hand, he could achieve mastery. He needed one final bout of training.

Wooooong.

And so Geom Mugeuk remained deeply immersed in his own world, absorbing every last trace of Bihyeolsa’s remaining energy into his internal energy.

* * *

Shaaak.

Geugak Soma shook the blood from his dagger.

The eyes of the Faceless Guests, who had watched him cut down the bamboo-hat martial artists, were filled with respect.

Hwa Domyeong’s expression, on the other hand, hardened coldly.

It had been a joint attack by two people who had mastered the Dark Divine Art. He had never imagined they would lose, and even if they did, he had thought they would die only after inflicting a fatal wound on Geugak Soma. At the very least, he had assumed they would make him exhaust all his internal energy.

But not a single drop of blood had splattered onto Geugak Soma’s pure white mask.

Now Geugak Soma’s smiling mask was the smile of a death god.

At the chill of death that had come striding close, Hwa Domyeong’s mind grew calm. To face death, he too would have to be just as terrifying.

He fluttered his fan lightly and said,

“I am not someone you should lay a hand on lightly.”

A flicker of derision passed through Geugak Soma’s eyes.

“Then is our Young Cult Leader someone you can lay a hand on lightly?”

Hwa Domyeong could not refute those words.

Geugak Soma slowly walked toward Hwa Domyeong.

“If you touch me, my family will surely kill you.”

No threat could make that white, smiling death retreat.

“Then I’ll have to make sure I kill you. Before the Young Cult Leader wakes.”

It was Geugak Soma’s resolve not to make Geom Mugeuk a target of revenge.

Hwa Domyeong could feel it. Geugak Soma truly intended to kill him. Now he had no choice.

Hwa Domyeong took a small medicine bottle from his robes.

After opening the stopper, he lifted it and poured it over his face.

Shaaah.

The liquid streaming down began to erase the pure white makeup.

That makeup had been the seal controlling him.

When he had been injured earlier, he had resolved to settle this matter even if it meant releasing the seal, but he had not thought he would truly have to undo it.

There was no helping it. Geugak Soma’s martial prowess was beyond imagination.

As the makeup was erased, his original face was revealed.

Countless faint lines were drawn across his face. Not only the lines and circles that had appeared on the bamboo-hat martial artists’ faces when they used the Dark Divine Art earlier, but many other lines as well.

It looked as though his face had been stitched together piece by piece.

Lines drawn vertically, lines drawn diagonally, lines forming all manner of patterns. Some patterns could be recognized, like whirlwinds, while others could not be identified at all.

If anything, the fact that the lines were faint made them even stranger. They looked like wrinkles, and also like scars.

They were brands created by greed.

In the process of learning the Dark Divine Art, Hwa Domyeong had been too greedy in his desire to grow stronger, and had suffered this side effect.

He had gone about wearing heavy makeup to hide these traces.

Saaaak.

Now those faint lines began to darken.

The line crossing his cheek darkened, the circle overlapping it darkened, and even the diagonal line beside it darkened.

The bamboo-hat man who was watching sent a voice transmission.

—You must not overlap them like that.

Hwa Domyeong replied in a low voice transmission.

—And if I don’t? Shall we die to that Geugak Soma?

In the meantime, every line on his face had darkened.

Shaaak.

When he spread his fan, it too had changed before anyone knew it.

Black Fan (黑扇).

The color of the fan had turned pitch-black. In the place where a beauty had once been drawn, a fearsome evil spirit was depicted.

The change was not merely external. Hwa Domyeong’s aura had changed completely. In his intensely shining eyes, the power of darkness was surging.

“Now then, hand over my Young Cult Leader!”

Shaaak!

The moment he spread the fan—

Papapapapak!

Ganggi swept through the place where Geugak Soma had been standing.

Geugak Soma threw himself aside to evade, and Hwa Domyeong appeared in front of him. He appeared so suddenly it was as if a ghost had manifested.

If the Dark Ghost-Walk Technique the bamboo-hat woman had used earlier had been at the second stage, his was now at the third.

Swaeek.

Thud!

Struck by the fan, Geugak Soma slid backward.

Hwa Domyeong clung to him like a shadow.

Swaek! Swaeek!

With the folded Black Fan, Hwa Domyeong swiftly stabbed toward Geugak Soma’s vital points. The incoming Black Fan was as sharp and powerful as a treasured sword.

The faster Hwa Domyeong thrust his fan, the redder the three lines used to perform the Dark Ghost-Walk Technique became.

A line turning red was a danger signal that appeared when one used power beyond what one was capable of unleashing.

But Hwa Domyeong increased the speed of his attacks even more. The third-stage Dark Ghost-Walk Technique was surpassing Geugak Soma’s speed.

Thud!

Geugak Soma allowed another attack to land.

It was powerful enough that, if not for his protective ganggi, his shoulder bone would have broken.

Geugak Soma staggered and retreated, and just as Hwa Domyeong rushed in, thinking it was an opportunity—

Shuk!

A finger wind shot out in a surprise attack, flying toward Hwa Domyeong’s face.

Thud!

Hwa Domyeong twisted the fan he had been thrusting out and barely managed to block it. Had he been even slightly late, his forehead would have been pierced.

Hwa Domyeong’s furious counterattack flew out.

Whoooooong!

Wind raged.

It was no ordinary wind. It was Dark Turbulence (暗黑亂流), wind mixed with invisible ganggi.

Geugak Soma raised his protective ganggi and threw himself aside.

Srrrk.

The trees behind him were not smashed apart, but silently sliced through and toppled. The wind was a soundless assassin.

Srrrk, srrrk!

Wind continued to blow toward wherever Geugak Soma fled, and trees and rocks were sliced away with a soft srrrk.

This time, the pattern that signified Dark Turbulence turned red.

The more he overexerted himself, the greater the shock to his body, but Hwa Domyeong did not stop attacking. Truly, if his opponent had not been Geugak Soma, he would never have pushed himself this far.

Whiririririri!

Hwa Domyeong spun rapidly in place. As the lines of the Dark Ghost-Walk Technique grew even redder, the whirlwind pattern drawn on his face began to darken further.

Whiriri! Whiriririri!

From the main whirlwind, more than ten whirlwinds branched out like limbs.

They were not ordinary whirlwinds. They were powerful whirlwinds of ganggi that would shatter flesh and bone with the slightest graze.

One of the Dark Divine Art’s techniques, Dark Revolving Wheel (暗黑回輪), had been unleashed.

The whirlwinds pursued Geugak Soma as though they were living creatures.

Geugak Soma fled swiftly to avoid them and fired the Hyeorang Finger at the main whirlwind.

Shuk!

Unable to pierce the force of the rotation, the Hyeorang Finger was deflected and flew away.

The Mageuk Rampaging Palm he launched next was also deflected. Since it could be bounced off in any direction, he could not attack recklessly.

Whiriririririri!

The whirlwind was gradually growing larger.

At this rate, it would even damage the place where Geom Mugeuk sat. That was precisely what Hwa Domyeong was aiming for. A flustered Geugak Soma would inevitably reveal an opening.

At the very moment the smaller whirlwinds pressed Geugak Soma from every direction—

Geugak Soma’s form soared into the air.

The small whirlwinds stretched toward Geom Mugeuk all at once.

Geugak Soma’s target was the central whirlwind.

And among it, the very top!

Sure enough, there was no whirlwind there.

Toward Hwa Domyeong, who was looking up at him with a startled face from the center of the whirlwind far below—

Kwa-kwa-kwa-kwa-kwang!

He unleashed the Mageuk Rampaging Palm into it.

Kwaaaang!

With an enormous explosion, the whirlwind burst apart.

When the dust settled, Hwa Domyeong’s figure came into view.

Blegh.

As he spewed blood, his face was covered entirely in red lines. He had overexerted himself again while using the Dark Revolving Wheel. His face contorted in pain.

He was pushing himself too far.

But he could not help but do so. Even after pushing himself to the point of death like this, Geugak Soma had not died and was charging at him like that.

Hwa Domyeong mustered the last of his strength.

Shik-shik-shik-shik-shik!

Fan and dagger flew swiftly to pierce his opponent.

Red lines increased across Hwa Domyeong’s face once more.

The fan opened and closed, stabbed and struck aside. It moved like a flash of lightning.

Though he was clearly faster, he could not catch Geugak Soma. Nor was he inferior in power.

Yet he could not catch Geugak Soma.

He did not know that there was something he could not keep up with.

It was Geugak Soma’s combat experience.

While he had been ruining his body as he immersed himself in the Dark Divine Art, Geugak Soma had been fighting battles on the edge of life and death alongside Geom Mugeuk.

Geugak Soma had already read Hwa Domyeong’s attack patterns and habits.

Puaak!

Hwa Domyeong’s arm was cut by Geugak Soma’s dagger, and blood sprayed out.

At that moment, Hwa Domyeong saw it.

The white mask cutting through the blood scattering in the air, coming in to drive a dagger into his heart. The eyes within that mask were smiling.

Just when he thought it was over—

Hwaaaak!

A curtain of darkness suddenly appeared and swallowed Geugak Soma.

The one who had unfolded the curtain was none other than the last bamboo-hat man. A black line had been thickly painted across his forehead, and a red circle was drawn over it.

Dark Barrier (暗黑結界).

His hidden trump card had been unleashed.

“I cannot hold it for long.”

He too vanished as if melting into the darkness.

Hwa Domyeong was the same; he could not endure for long either.

Now Hwa Domyeong’s face was completely covered in red lines.

He walked toward where Geom Mugeuk was. He intended to kill all the Faceless Guests, suppress Geom Mugeuk’s internal energy, and flee just like this. It was his final chance to turn the tables.

The Faceless Guests looked at one another and exchanged glances. They knew well what kind of power Hwa Domyeong had displayed earlier.

Five of the Faceless Guests split left and right and charged toward him.

The remaining five Faceless Guests lined up in a row before Geom Mugeuk. From the back to the front, they injected their internal energy forward. They concentrated their internal energy into the person at the very front.

Hwa Domyeong fanned his fan fiercely.

“Annoying bastards! All of you, get lost!”

The ganggi released from the fan flew toward the Faceless Guests with tremendous force.

Swaeeeeek!

The Faceless Guest standing at the very front thrust out a palm and blocked the ganggi.

Kwaaang!

It was an attack that shook the earth, but because the internal energy of five people had gathered, he managed to block it.

Gulp.

When the first Faceless Guest suffered internal injuries and coughed up blood, the Faceless Guest behind him took his place, sending the injured one to the very back.

In the meantime, the five Faceless Guests who had charged forward—Crescent Moon and Spider, Cloud and Wolf, and Flame—attacked him from all sides.

Had he not overexerted himself in the fight with Geugak Soma earlier, Hwa Domyeong, using the Dark Divine Art, would have won without difficulty.

But his physical condition was not normal, and on top of that, his mind was urgent.

Thud!

Flame, struck by the attack, rolled across the ground. At the next attack, Crescent Moon flew over, wrapped him in his arms, and rolled with him.

Hwa Domyeong tried to make certain of killing at least one of them, but the other Faceless Guests did not stand by and watch.

Shuk! Shuk! Shuk! Shuk! Shuk! Shuk! Shuk!

The Faceless Guests unleashed finger winds all at once.

Unable to block all the finger winds pouring down on him, they embedded themselves in his protective ganggi.

As the fight failed to go his way, Hwa Domyeong grew even more agitated. His face flushed red as if it would burst. He had nearly reached his limit.

Hwa Domyeong executed his final move.

He simply pushed forward while taking the Faceless Guests’ attacks.

He was driving forward while circulating the very Dark Iron Body Art that the bamboo-hat man had displayed, pushing it to its extreme. The three red circles had turned as crimson as blood.

Kang! Kaaang! Kaaaang! Kang!

Neither finger winds nor daggers could pierce him.

Hwa Domyeong rammed aside the Faceless Guests blocking his path with his body and kept pushing forward.

‘Got it!’

At the very moment Hwa Domyeong launched himself to snatch Geom Mugeuk—

Puuuuk!

With the sound of flesh tearing, silence descended upon that place.

Spewing a gush of blood from his mouth, Hwa Domyeong looked down at his own chest.

A dagger was embedded in it.

There was a black circle in midair, and only an arm had emerged from it to stab him.

Sssssss.

The black circle from which the arm protruded gradually grew larger, and a person revealed himself.

It was the Extreme Evil Little Demon. In his other hand, the one not holding the dagger, he held the bamboo-hat man’s head.

The Extreme Evil Little Demon had broken through the Dark Barrier and come outside. As he emerged, the surrounding darkness vanished.

With his face covered in red lines, Hwa Domyeong spoke with difficulty. He still wanted to live. If he survived, he believed that one day he would be able to take this revenge.

“If you spare me……”

Before he could state his condition, the Extreme Evil Little Demon cut him off.

“It was dark, so I didn’t get a good look.”

After saying those incomprehensible words,

the Extreme Evil Little Demon swiftly pulled the dagger from his chest and stabbed it properly into his heart.

Puuk!

Hwa Domyeong died instantly and collapsed where he stood.

The Extreme Evil Little Demon threw the head he had been holding beside Hwa Domyeong’s fallen corpse. It was the moment every enemy in that place met their end.

The Extreme Evil Little Demon looked back at his subordinates. Though he said nothing, his gaze spoke for him.

You all did well.

Then he sat down in front of Geom Mugeuk. Since he had been there when Geom Mugeuk closed his eyes, he intended to be before him when he opened them as well. Because he knew better than anyone how hard Geom Mugeuk had worked to come this far.

“Do not worry about anything. Sleep well.”

The Faceless Guests scattered around the area.

Some treated their own wounds, while others tended to their comrades’ injuries. One Faceless Guest leaned against a tree and fell asleep, while another stood on a branch of that tree and gazed at the distant forest. Yet another crouched on the ground and looked up at the moon.

In that place filled with corpses, where blood flowed like a river, they all rested quietly as they waited for Geom Mugeuk to awaken.

Chapter 715: Worrying About Us as Soon as You Open Your Eyes?
-------------------------------------------------------------
Blood was splattered across the alleyway where moonlight flowed.

Behind the martial artist collapsing with his hands clutching his neck stood Chairan.

She closed her eyes and felt the energy around her. She surveyed the area with the unique senses of an assassin, but no enemies could be detected around her.

Shortly after, Ilgyo came to her and reported.

“It has all been taken care of.”

Behind her, the figures of the Gaingyo assassins came into view. Because Chairan, Ilgyo, and the remaining assassins had returned to the alley without delay, they had been able to minimize the damage. But in this battle, many Gaingyo assassins had died or been injured.

And the fight was not yet over.

“We are going back.”

Chairan sent those with severe injuries to receive treatment, and taking all the remaining assassins still capable of fighting, she returned to the place where she had been fighting Hwadomyeong.

She had not known it would become such a fate with the Demon Cult.

Everyone dies together, or everyone lives together. Today’s battle was that kind of battle.

She had come here to save her subordinates, trusting in Geukak Soma.

But the opponent was Hwadomyeong. This time he revealed a new side and disappointingly laid bare his true nature, but the fact that he was a strong person remained unchanged. The bamboo-hatted martial artists accompanying him were also no ordinary beings.

Above all, Hwadomyeong had not even tried to flee upon seeing Geukak Soma. It definitely meant he had something he believed in.

“Hurry.”

She displayed her lightness skill and ran ahead.

It was for Geom Mugeuk, and in the end, it was also for herself.

Because she had already become a traitor to the organization, if something were to happen to Geom Mugeuk in this situation, the choice she had made would become utterly meaningless.

While Chairan harbored such worries, Sipchilgyo, who was following behind, was thinking of someone else.

She desperately hoped only that the Crescent Moon Masked Guest was alive.

She kept having bad premonitions. She recalled the vision of herself discovering a mask painted with a crescent moon amidst tangled corpses of unknown people.

As that place drew nearer, her heart pounded harder at the thickening scent of blood.

And so, at the very moment Chairan and the assassins left the alley and arrived at the place where they had fought earlier—

They stopped in their tracks. The situation unfolding within the area was something they had never imagined.

First, Geom Mugeuk was still sitting with his eyes closed.

At that sight, Chairan felt relieved.

*The Young Cult Leader is alive.*

And Geukak Soma was alive as well. He was sitting in front of Geom Mugeuk with his eyes closed. He appeared to be circulating his energy, recovering his internal energy.

As for the Masked Guests surrounding the two of them—

They were disposing of the corpses. A large pit was already full of bodies, and even as they arrived, the Masked Guests were carrying corpses.

They were doing this so that when the Young Cult Leader opened his eyes, he would not be shown the sight of corpses strewn about.

Sipchilgyo searched for the Crescent Moon Masked Guest first. She had always been able to find him immediately, but perhaps because her heart was anxious, she could not see him.

So she counted the number of Masked Guests first.

One, two, three… eight, nine, ten!

Fortunately, all the Masked Guests were alive.

Then, she made eye contact with the Masked Guest carrying corpses nearest to her. Only then did she realize that he was the Crescent Moon Masked Guest. Although his clothes were drenched in blood, fortunately he did not appear to be badly injured.

Sipchilgyo slightly bowed her head to the Crescent Moon Masked Guest, who was staring at her fixedly.

The Crescent Moon Masked Guest slightly bowed his head in return, accepting the greeting.

A Masked Guest carrying corpses with another nearby looked at the two of them. The two pretended not to know each other and did not exchange glances any longer.

Meanwhile, Chairan was inwardly shocked.

*So they killed them all!*

The first person she looked for was Hwadomyeong.

At first, she could not find Hwadomyeong’s corpse. Judging by the three bamboo hats fallen on the ground, it seemed the bamboo-hatted martial artists had all died.

But Hwadomyeong’s corpse was not visible.

*Did he escape?*

Then, one object caught her eyes.

It was a fan.

A familiar fan sticking out prominently from within the corpse pit.

The person who had died clutching that fan was a man with countless lines drawn on his face. Because his makeup had been erased, she did not recognize him at first, but upon looking closely, he was Hwadomyeong. The lines that had crossed his face in red had all turned a grayish color.

What was more surprising than his death was that the Masked Guests had so unceremoniously tossed him into the corpse pit together with his subordinates.

The Masked Guests.

Their leader, Geukak Soma.

Chairan thought she must not let her guard down.

Just because the man called Geom Mugeuk showed a smile and an appearance unlike a demonic cultist, she must not mistakenly think that these people too might be good people.

They were demonic cultists of the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult. Moreover, they were demonic cultists of the Valley of Evil.

Watching the Masked Guests begin to cover the corpse pit, Chairan looked up at the night sky.

Now there was no turning back. Since Hwadomyeong, their blood kin, had died, the organization behind them would surely try to kill her.

Before she knew it, the moon had tilted, and countless stars had become the new masters of the night sky.

* * *

The energy of Bihyeolsa was completely converted to internal energy and stored in the dantian.

Geom Mugeuk was confident.

Now, if one considered only internal energy, he was the best in the martial world.

Of course, one never knows what will happen in the world… No. At this level, he was indeed the best.

The five energies that had rampaged through his body had also quietly settled into his body.

Now, only one energy remained.

Where on earth was the last pearl hidden?

And what would happen when he gathered all six energies?

With that final thought, Geom Mugeuk slowly opened his eyes.

Geukak Soma was looking at him. When he had closed his eyes, Geukak Soma had been there; when he awoke, Geukak Soma was still there.

“Have you awakened?”

Geukak Soma’s eyes were smiling brightly.

Geom Mugeuk, who had been silently gazing into those eyes, spoke while stretching languidly instead of offering thanks.

“I slept well, thanks to you.”

Geom Mugeuk rose from his seat, and Geukak Soma rose as well.

His body was more refreshed than ever. Of course, it was not because he had slept until dawn. The pure internal energy filling his dantian was as vast and deep as the sea.

The stability and confidence granted by this magnificent internal energy, which seemed as though it would not run dry no matter how much he used, was beyond words.

If he unleashed the Sixth Technique of the Nine Transformations Demon Art, Heavenly Demon World Destruction, with this internal energy?

Since the Heavenly Demon World Destruction required most of one’s internal energy, if it were unleashed with this enormous internal energy? Everything visible had already been annihilated back then; now, an unimaginable result would come about.

If, like his father, he could freely control the Heavenly Demon World Destruction?

If he created a sword of light with this internal energy?

Geom Mugeuk’s heart pounded.

As soon as Geom Mugeuk awoke, the Masked Guests formed a line and respectfully cupped their fists in salute. Joy could be felt in their eyes.

Geom Mugeuk surveyed their condition. His own body felt this light, yet the Masked Guests’ martial robes were soaked in blood. Many had been injured as well.

He could guess how fierce a battle they had fought.

“It must have been hard protecting me?”

At those words, the Masked Guests smiled brightly. For them, those words from the Young Cult Leader were enough. Because if the Young Cult Leader merely acknowledged them, they could lay down their lives as many times as necessary.

Opposite the Masked Guests were the assassins. Chairan was among the lined-up assassins.

Geom Mugeuk walked toward her.

“Wasn’t I heavy?”

“I was scolded for the weight.”

She had done what she had set out to do.

“You kept that promise—that Geukak Soma had to be present when I opened my eyes.”

“I’m the type who always keeps promises.”

“Then let us make one more promise.”

“You mean to make that person leave?”

She had thought he would say that they should go rescue Akgunhak together.

Geom Mugeuk shook his head. His promise was something she had never imagined.

“Until I have eliminated them all, rest in the protection of our cult.”

Put nicely, what Geom Mugeuk was saying now was this:

I will protect all of you.

Indeed, perhaps the only one who could speak such words to them was the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult.

“Are you worrying about us the moment you open your eyes?”

Geom Mugeuk replied with a deepening gaze.

“Because you are the one who let me open my eyes.”

Geom Mugeuk was thanking her in this manner.

Entering a full-fledged bond with the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult was something that required careful judgment. If the one making the offer had not been Geom Mugeuk, she would have refused tactfully.

But she had not involved her pride or the organization’s honor in this matter.

Thinking only of her subordinates’ lives, the answer was not difficult.

“Then I shall entrust my body to you for a while.”

Chairan cupped her fists in a polite salute. The assassin women behind her also bowed in unison. Among those women, one wore an inscrutable smile.

Geom Mugeuk also courteously returned their greeting.

“Once I have eliminated them all, you may freely leave whenever you wish. I promise in the name of the Young Cult Leader.”

Thus, the Gaingyo assassins decided to temporarily place themselves under the protection of the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult.

Geom Mugeuk asked Geukak Soma.

“What became of the one with makeup on his face?”

“He died.”

“Well done.”

Geukak Soma nodded and pointed out one thing.

“Judging by his speech, he seemed to be the blood kin of an important figure.”

Geom Mugeuk recalled what Chairan had said.

That it was best not to get involved with him.

He was surely of the Hwa Clan’s bloodline.

“Even so, could he be any more the blood kin of an important figure than me?”

At Geom Mugeuk’s joke, Geukak Soma laughed, and Chairan also smiled.

She felt it. Because Geom Mugeuk was there, the atmosphere changed. Faces brightened, and vitality overflowed. She felt that Geukak Soma, the Masked Guests, and even she and the assassins were all affected.

The sun rose brightly, and the world too grew bright.

Finally, the long night had passed.

* * *

Ian stood by the window, watching the sunrise.

*Geom Mugeuk will manage somehow; since Geukak Soma went with him, it should be fine.* No matter how much she thought positively, she could not help but worry.

Then, from behind, the Cheonhwaru Pavilion Lord spoke.

“Are you feeling sorry for no reason because of me?”

“Yes?”

When Ian turned around with a questioning expression, the Cheonhwaru Pavilion Lord revealed the reason.

“You wanted to go with the Young Cult Leader, didn’t you?”

Ian smiled and replied.

“Instead, Lord Soma must be fighting with a peaceful mind. Rather than having Lord Soma fight uneasily while I am there, it is better to have Lord Soma fighting comfortably in my absence.”

It was praise that held Lord Soma in high esteem, and humble words that understated herself. It was also an acknowledgment of the relationship between Lord Soma and the Cheonhwaru Pavilion Lord.

Looking at Ian with soft eyes, the Cheonhwaru Pavilion Lord asked.

“Are you worried that the prophecy will come true?”

Then Ian gave an unexpected answer.

“No, I actually hope the prophecy comes true.”

The Cheonhwaru Pavilion Lord looked at her with eyes asking for the reason, and Ian revealed her honest feelings.

“Because the Young Cult Leader already knows what kind of prophecy it is. He must have prepared well for it. I rather hope that no variables arise.”

From those words, the Cheonhwaru Pavilion Lord could tell.

“This martial artist believes more in the Young Cult Leader than in the prophecy.”

Ian did not deny it.

As Ian was looking out the window again, a delighted exclamation flowed from her mouth.

People were walking into the courtyard.

With Geom Mugeuk at the center, Geukak Soma and Chairan were at his left and right; behind Chairan were assassins, and behind Geukak Soma, Masked Guests were entering.

Geom Mugeuk saw Ian at the window and waved. At that sight, Ian was so overjoyed that tears welled up, but she forced herself to compose her emotions and waved back energetically.

Seeing them enter the guest quarters safely, the Cheonhwaru Pavilion Lord gave thanks to the heavens.

Because she had prophesied it, she had been indescribably afraid and trembling that it might truly come to pass.

Seeing her like that, Geukak Soma had smiled brightly for her. If there had truly been no one watching, she would have dashed into his embrace.

Ian saw that Geom Mugeuk had bandages wrapped around his chest.

“Were you injured?”

Surprised by Ian’s question, Geom Mugeuk clutched his chest.

“I got stabbed in the heart.”

At those words, Ian and even the Cheonhwaru Pavilion Lord started.

“It really was Lord Soma who stabbed me.”

The Cheonhwaru Pavilion Lord could tell that the prophecy had come true. She had thought this would be the one time the prophecy was wrong. But how had he survived?

“Are you alright?”

At Ian’s worry, Geom Mugeuk’s play-acting began.

“Alright? Lord Soma stabbed me so painfully. Ugh, don’t even mention it. It’s only because I have the strongest and greatest heart in the world that I survived.”

Ian whispered to Geukak Soma as if sharing a secret.

“You should have stabbed him harder. Or not his heart, but that mouth…”

Of course, it was a joke that everyone could hear.

“Not the mouth! Better to stab the heart!”

As Geom Mugeuk played along, everyone laughed.

“By the way, Ian, your championship prize money is gone.”

Since the Sinhwa Pavilion Lord had died, and even Hwadomyeong was dead, continuing the contest for the greatest beauty under heaven was impossible.

At that, Ian looked at Chairan and said.

“I suppose Lady Cha must be disappointed.”

“Is that sincere?”

To Chairan’s question, Ian smiled pleasantly and answered.

“About half.”

Chairan smiled along as well. Truly, if the final stage had unfolded, what would the result have been?

Geom Mugeuk said to Ian.

“Lady Cha will be with us for the time being. Ian, take responsibility.”

“Yes!”

Ian looked at Chairan. The emotion contained in that gaze was welcome and cheerful hospitality.

Chairan responded to that goodwill.

“Then I shall teach you in the meantime.”

Chairan looked down at her own bloodstained martial robe and said.

“That a martial robe is an outfit worn only occasionally on days like today.”

Choosing clothes, applying makeup. She meant to teach how a woman should adorn herself.

Ian smiled shyly and said.

“I do miss the clothes I wore on stage a little, too.”

As the two of them talked about everyday things, everyone finally felt that this battle had truly ended.

But for Geom Mugeuk, one matter still remained.

Since he had returned, there was someone he had to meet first. The person who had been the cause of all this.

“I shall go and see Sado Maengju for a moment.”

Chapter 716: Can You Handle Me?
-------------------------------
