For an instant, Geom Mugeuk was startled. The one even more startled was Seo Dae-ryong. With a stricken face, Seo Dae-ryong lowered his head deeply.

“Ah! I made the decision too impulsively. I was too hasty! What should I do?”

He had naturally thought the words were meant for him. But Geom Mugeuk said, his gaze deepening,

“No. Those words were meant for me.”

The moment he heard the Blood Heaven Blade Demon’s words, Geom Mugeuk understood.

Why he had told him to come to his residence.

His own heart had been too hurried. So much so that the Blood Heaven Blade Demon had worried over it.

Yes. If it had been from the seventh level to the eighth, or from the eighth to the ninth, he would not have been this desperate.

The Grand Completion of the Nine-Flame Demonic Art.

Because the realm he had longed for even in his dreams was right before his eyes, Geom Mugeuk’s heart had, without his realizing it, grown impatient.

When he felt something, he had to seize it! So he had been filled with the desire to enter closed-door training at once and achieve Grand Completion.

Ordinarily, he would have gone to see the Demon Venerables, and Jang Ho, and Jo Chun-bae as well. But now, with his mind stolen by Grand Completion, he had only wanted to run to the training grounds.

It was not as though he had reached the ninth level through closed-door training. It was not a result born of such impatience. He had achieved it among people.

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon had caught hold of that hurried heart and given him a lesson.

The greater the desire, the more an illusion appears real.

Geom Mugeuk smiled brightly and said in a loud voice,

“I’ll come again tomorrow.”

At that, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon appeared by the window. As if Geom Mugeuk’s guess had indeed been correct, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon said with a smile,

“You don’t need to come anymore.”

Seeing that warm smile, such a thought came to him naturally.

Could he truly have come this far if not for the Blood Heaven Blade Demon? Perhaps because he had been the first button among the Demon Venerables, Geom Mugeuk had been able to come safely this far.

Geom Mugeuk looked at Seo Dae-ryong, who was standing beside him blinking, and said,

“No, I will come. I must come and devise the grand operation for my right-hand man’s marriage.”

Seo Dae-ryong flinched and asked,

“Is this something that requires a grand operation?”

Geom Mugeuk nodded firmly, his eyes frighteningly serious despite the mischief hidden within them.

“How could I simply send you out into that fierce and endless battlefield unprepared?”

Chapter 721: Farther from Great Completion
------------------------------------------
When Geom Mugeuk and Seo Daeryong came out of Nam Dojong, their surroundings had grown dark.

Seo Daeryong, who had been walking lost in thought, looked as though he had something troubling him.

“Now that you’re actually getting married, all sorts of worries are cropping up, aren’t they?”

“My youngest sister-in-law once subtly mentioned something like that before.”

Seo Daeryong was now calling Danyeon—whom he had previously addressed as his elder brother’s wife—even more comfortably “youngest sister-in-law” than before.

“She said her sister was feeling burdened about holding the wedding ceremony.”

Geom Mugeuk could understand that feeling. She didn’t know anyone here, and her only family was her two younger siblings.

“She said she herself wasn’t ashamed of having no wedding guests, but she was worried it might somehow be a burden to me. Back then, I just laughed it off, saying I had neither friends nor family either.”

But even so, Seo Daeryong had I-an and Jang Ho, as well as investigators and enforcement martial artists. With just them, the groom’s side would have no shortage of guests.

“I will resolve the issue of guests for Miss Danyeon.”

“Would you really do that?”

When Geom Mugeuk nodded, Seo Daeryong’s face brightened brilliantly.

“When talk of marriage really gets going, you might get hurt over nothing. Be careful with your words, no matter what.”

“Yes!”

“Then, see you tomorrow.”

“I will see you tomorrow!”

Watching the back of Seo Daeryong running off excitedly, Geom Mugeuk smiled.

That’s right. Marriage is something you should do blissfully while it’s this good, without overthinking.

Geom Mugeuk moved his feet again. The place he headed toward was not his own residence, but Magachon.

* * *

The lights of Pungnyu Tavern had gone out.

Jo Chunbae was alone cleaning up the empty tavern, the waitstaff having already returned home.

Just then, an all too welcome voice came from outside.

“Innkeeper.”

The moment he heard that voice, Jo Chunbae shouted and ran outside.

“Young Cult Leader!”

But he had come out in too much of a hurry and tripped on the threshold.

“Oops!”

At the moment he was about to tumble forward and crash to the ground, he stopped in mid-air.

A gentle energy supported him.

He flew through the air as if riding a soft cloud and landed before Geom Mugeuk.

“I was just so overjoyed! Thank you, Young Cult Leader!”

“Our innkeeper must now be more careful of tripping than of a flying sword.”

The two looked at each other and smiled brightly.

Jo Chunbae was so delighted to see him that one wondered what he would have done if Geom Mugeuk had not come. When Geom Mugeuk had returned last time after leaving the cult, he had been too busy to see Jo Chunbae, so this was quite a reunion after a long while.

“Have you been well?”

“I’ve been well thanks to you. Our Young Cult Leader has become even more dashing and impressive.”

“If only the women of the world had eyes like our innkeeper.”

After exchanging such greetings, the two went inside. Though business had already ended, Jo Chunbae rolled up his sleeves.

“How many will be coming? I shall prepare the food in advance.”

“There is no one to meet. I just came because I wanted to have a drink with the innkeeper.”

In that moment, Jo Chunbae’s heart welled up with emotion. Who was he but a mere old tavern keeper? He was truly grateful to Geom Mugeuk for not forgetting him and seeking him out like this.

“I need no side dishes. Let us just have a drink.”

Geom Mugeuk went up to the upper floor. The seat he always sat in was spotless, without a speck of dust. Even if no guests were received there, one could tell how much Jo Chunbae cared for that spot.

Jo Chunbae brought out liquor and simple side dishes.

“Here, innkeeper. First, receive a drink from me.”

As always, Jo Chunbae received the drink with gratitude and respectfully poured the liquor.

“Now, let us drink.”

After toasting, the two emptied their cups. Jo Chunbae turned and downed his.

“Did the matter you went out for go well?”

At Jo Chunbae’s question, Geom Mugeuk looked back at the characters written on the wall and spoke.

“I went and rescued one of those who left words there.”

“Oh my!”

Jo Chunbae shook his head with a surprised face, saying it was something he could not even imagine.

“Was there nothing out of the ordinary at Magachon?”

“With so many people living crowded together, there are always matters.”

“Tell me some stories.”

Jo Chunbae told him this and that. A child was born at some merchant’s house, someone else had injured their leg, and someone else had moved away.

Even as a conspiracy to kill the Evil Sect Leader was underway, their lives continued. Even if another conspiracy unfolded tomorrow, even if the Great Demonic War broke out, Jo Chunbae would open the doors of Pungnyu Tavern in the morning.

Only then did Geom Mugeuk truly feel that he had returned home. When he set aside the ambition for Greater Achievement that had clouded his eyes, Jo Chunbae’s figure came into clear view.

“You know the person selling dolls up ahead, don’t you?”

“Of course I know.”

“A new Eldest Young Master doll has come out.”

“Ah! So it’s finally out.”

Jo Chunbae ran over to the counter and brought back a small box.

“I bought it in case you came by, to give to you, Young Cult Leader.”

Geom Mugeuk opened the box. The sullen face was unmistakably his elder brother.

Geom Mugeuk recalled the dolls displayed in his father’s room. Perhaps this doll was already standing on his father’s display shelf.

“Come to think of it, innkeeper, you didn’t buy my doll, only my brother’s.”

As if he had expected this.

“I already bought three of yours, Young Cult Leader. I have one at home, and I sent one to my hometown.”

After chatting about this and that, Jo Chunbae went down to the first floor, telling him to drink comfortably alone. He had declined to drink, saying he made mistakes when he drank on days like this, so Geom Mugeuk did not force him. He had only the first cup, exactly one cup.

Thus, as Geom Mugeuk drank alone, he looked down at the first floor.

Jo Chunbae sat at the counter bathed in moonlight, gazing out the window.

Suddenly, the image of Hyeolcheon Doma reading a book overlapped with his figure.

Geom Mugeuk understood.

That what Hyeolcheon Doma had spoken of was that very image of Jo Chunbae now.

No matter how glad he was, no matter how much he wanted to drink together.

Jo Chunbae did not sway to momentary emotion, but unchangingly protected his life according to his principles.

That very appearance now was Hyeolcheon Doma’s teaching itself. Innkeeper, you have already achieved Greater Achievement.

“Innkeeper.”

When Geom Mugeuk called, Jo Chunbae looked up at the upper floor and asked.

“Is there anything else you need?”

“Please prepare some good liquor.”

“You must be going to meet someone good.”

Geom Mugeuk smiled and conveyed words of inscrutable meaning.

“I’m trying to run even farther from Greater Achievement.”

* * *

At the training ground illuminated by torches, martial artists trained deep into the night.

Twelve martial artists.

They, who unfolded a single sword formation and struck out with their swords simultaneously, were none other than Geom Mugeuk’s guardian martial artists.

Without distinguishing between day shift and night shift, they were all training together.

Drawing a round circle, they thrust their swords simultaneously in all directions.

The sword formation they unfolded was a guardian sword formation that protected one person.

The life of a sword formation lies in harmony and connection. They covered each other’s weaknesses and moved as one body.

The momentum carried by their swords was no ordinary thing. They displayed a sharpness of spirit incomparable to when they had first come to Geom Mugeuk.

Thus, after one round of training.

Hah, hah, hah.

Only the sound of held-back breathing echoed there.

Just then, a voice suddenly rang out.

“Now you’re quite something.”

In that instant, the guardian martial artists turned around in surprise.

Geom Mugeuk was sitting in the center of the space they had been guarding with their sword formation.

“Young Cult Leader!”

At the shout of Jeokyeon, the guard captain, Geom Mugeuk spoke with a smile.

“Everyone remain seated as you are.”

The guards sat in their places, facing inward toward the circle. Everyone had surprised faces. Though there were twelve of them, no one had noticed until the Young Cult Leader had entered and sat in the center.

“Everyone finds it difficult, don’t they?”

“It is not difficult!”

At the hearty reply, Geom Mugeuk started with Jeokyeon.

“Guard Jeokyeon, I know you suffer the most.”

At those words, Jeokyeon was happy. He didn’t go through this hardship to be recognized, but he was truly grateful to be told he was understood.

“I also know well that it is not easy living as my guard.”

When they had been assigned the mission of being the Young Cult Leader’s guards, they must have imagined a different scene from now. They had imagined roaming the Central Plains with the Young Cult Leader and experiencing all sorts of things.

But except for the time when he left the cult once and the temporary leadership period guarding within the cult, they had spent most of their time doing martial arts training.

Jeokyeon spoke to Geom Mugeuk.

“I trained with the single-minded determination that I had to become stronger to protect the Young Cult Leader. However…”

With an expression of not knowing whether to laugh or cry, Jeokyeon spoke.

“You have returned even stronger.”

Geom Mugeuk looked at Jeokyeon and smiled.

“Am I someone who would easily accept just anyone’s protection?”

Geom Mugeuk opened the bundle he had brought beside him. What was inside flew out to everyone.

Srrk! Srrk! Srrrk!

What flew simultaneously were liquor cups.

He had prepared twelve cups, having decided to drink with them.

The cups did not fly all the way but floated before them.

The guards looked at the cups floating before them with surprised faces. The thought that arose in everyone’s mind:

Is this even possible?

Geom Mugeuk was now displaying divine prowess, moving twelve objects simultaneously through the air with void control.

Adding his own cup made thirteen, which soon became fourteen.

A liquor bottle slowly rose into the air and began pouring into the cup floating before Jeokyeon.

Clink.

The bottle, having finished pouring, moved toward another martial artist’s cup. It did not go to the martial artist right next to him. The cup the bottle sought was I-ho’s cup.

When they had gone outside before, they had told Geom Mugeuk that they were called by operation names from Il-ho to Sip-i-ho.

The bottle that filled I-ho’s cup then moved to Sam-ho’s cup, who sat a little further away, and then to Sa-ho’s cup.

Geom Mugeuk remembered exactly what he had heard then, from I-ho to the youngest, Sip-i-ho.

Because they had thought the Young Cult Leader didn’t know them well, the guards were inwardly moved.

“You remembered all of us.”

At Jeokyeon’s emotion, Geom Mugeuk made an apologetic expression. How indifferent must he have been to move them with something like this?

“Become stronger. That is how you protect me!”

Geom Mugeuk was not apologetic toward them. An apology like “I made you train just so I could travel comfortably” would only hurt their pride more.

He had made a great resolution today that he must never be impatient, but.

“Be impatient. Be more impatient than others. Who knows when our Young Cult Leader will become Cult Leader! Jump up from your sleep and swing your swords! Understand?”

“Yes!”

When Geom Mugeuk grabbed the cup floating before him, the twelve guards likewise grabbed their cups.

The Young Cult Leader they were to protect was someone who spoke like that while keeping thirteen cups suspended in the air. How could they not be motivated to protect such a person, and when such a person told them to become stronger?

“I permit it. Continue to become stronger.”

Having finished speaking, Geom Mugeuk drank, and the guards also emptied their cups simultaneously.

“And since we need to carry ourselves with dignity tomorrow, make sure to press your clothes properly!”

* * *

Cha Iran was looking out the window.

The first day after arriving at the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult had dawned. The lodgings Geom Mugeuk had prepared were truly excellent.

It wasn’t just her own room that was good.

The rooms provided for her subordinates were also clean and tidy. The beds and bedding in particular had been attentively prepared as new.

Knowing one reveals ten. There was a reason she had come here.

Just then, Il-gyo came and reported.

“We are prepared.”

“Let’s go.”

Geom Mugeuk had summoned them all. They wore martial attire instead of splendid clothes.

Now a new life was beginning.

When they arrived at the training ground located a short distance from their lodgings, there were people already waiting.

It was none other than the Ghost Shadow Squad led by I-an.

Ghost Shadow First Team led by Cheongmyeon and Ghost Shadow Second Team led by Seojin stood with an extraordinary spirit. They were truly showing what kind of organization a disciplined organization was.

I-an stood before them.

Cha Iran felt that I-an’s appearance standing before his subordinates was different from when she had seen him in Anhui.

‘So he wasn’t revealing his true spirit back then.’

I-an introduced Cheongmyeon and Seojin to Cha Iran.

Cheongmyeon was someone who could have become the next Extreme Evil Demonic Successor, so his spirit was no ordinary thing, and Seojin too had changed in spirit through bloody martial arts training and ghost arts training after returning from the Yellow Dragon Martial Hall.

Unlike Cha Iran, who looked at them calmly, the female assassins felt a competitive spirit they could not understand.

Now she had to live not as an assassin, but as a martial artist. It was time to turn the confidence that she could assassinate anyone into the confidence that she could fight and defeat anyone.

At that moment, Geom Mugeuk appeared there.

Behind Geom Mugeuk followed twelve guards, all wearing the formal uniform of the guard corps.

The emblem of the Heavenly Demon Hall Guard Corps was embroidered on their chests.

An evil spirit drawn within a shield.

And on the black masks they wore, the single character ma (魔) was embroidered in vigorous strokes.

Everyone gathered here was formidable, and the guard warriors were no less imposing.

Seeing them, Cha Iran and the assassin women were once again made keenly aware that Geom Mugeuk was the Young Cult Leader of the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult.

When Geom Mugeuk arrived, everyone present bowed respectfully at once.

“We greet the Young Cult Leader!”

Today, Geom Mugeuk also cupped his fist lightly and formally accepted their greeting. Given the occasion, he even gave a nod of acknowledgment to Dan Yeon, the youngest member of the Ghost Shadow Unit.

Then he asked Cha Iran,

“Did you sleep well?”

“Yes. Thanks to your consideration, I slept very soundly.”

The women standing behind her also bowed their heads in unison to express their gratitude. How could they not know? He had taken special care of them.

“I intend to give you the position of unit leader. However, I’ll need the Cult Leader’s permission, so you’ll have to wait a bit. In the meantime, choose a name as well.”

Then Cha Iran said something unexpected.

“If possible, we would like to enter the Ghost Shadow Unit.”

It was truly unexpected.

“Do you know what that means?”

“I do.”

It meant placing herself under Ian.

Cha Iran’s gaze turned toward Cheongmyeon and Seojin.

“Those two do not appear to be people who should remain mere squad leaders either.”

At that, Geom Mugeuk properly introduced the two of them.

“The First Squad Leader is someone who gave up becoming the next Geugak Soma, and the Second Squad Leader is a descendant of the Ghost Gate.”

As expected! Cha Iran nodded with that expression.

“For those of us who have lived as assassins to live as martial artists, it will take much effort and time. We will learn under Lady Lee—no, under Unit Leader Lee. And since we have decided to serve the Young Cult Leader, I do not believe rank is important.”

Geom Mugeuk thought her choice was truly wise. But he was not the one to make this decision.

“Ian. Will you accept them as the Third Squad?”

Ian looked at Cha Iran. There were reasons to take them in, and reasons to refuse them. Her deliberation did not last long. For Geom Mugeuk’s sake, and for Cheongmyeon and Seojin’s sake as well, she had to accept them. These burdensome squad leaders of hers!

“I will accept you as the Third Squad Leader of the Ghost Shadow Unit.”

Cha Iran showed Ian the same respectful courtesy.

“I greet the Unit Leader.”

“I look forward to working with you.”

With Cha Iran’s joining, the Ghost Shadow Unit was finally completed in earnest.

Inwardly moved, Geom Mugeuk looked at the three women.

Ian, Seojin, and Cha Iran.

The three women who had saved his life, both in his life before regression and in his present life, had gathered under the name of the Ghost Shadow Unit.

Geom Mugeuk said to Cha Iran,

“I have a first mission to personally assign to the Third Squad of the Ghost Shadow Unit.”

“Please tell me.”

Geom Mugeuk’s mission was truly something she had never imagined.

“I need you to become guests at a wedding ceremony.”

Cha Iran flinched in surprise, then asked in a cold voice,

“Is the person we must kill the groom? Or the bride?”

Chapter 722: I Will Choose a Day That Suits Me
----------------------------------------------
“It was a joke.”

With everyone’s eyes focused on her, Chairan said with a smile.

Their first mission surely would not be to attend a wedding as guests and kill someone.

And it made no sense for them to be sent there as actual wedding guests, either.

She assumed that “wedding guests” was some kind of code used by the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult.

“I was not joking.”

After staring at Geom Mugeuk for a moment, Chairan asked, unable to believe it.

“You really mean for us to go as wedding guests?”

“That is correct.”

“And then?”

“You simply congratulate them and return.”

At that, Ian asked with a surprised expression,

“Could it be that Pavilion Master Seo is getting married?”

When Geom Mugeuk nodded, Ian’s face brightened. He knew well that Seo Daeryong had been nurturing his love for Dan A. Still, he had not expected them to marry so soon.

Geom Mugeuk said to Chairan,

“When I called it a mission, I was joking. There is a young lady who came to our cult after living as a wandering martial artist. She is getting married this time, but she has no friends here, so I wanted you all to attend as her wedding guests.”

If she had not already experienced the kind of person Geom Mugeuk was, Chairan would never have taken those words at face value. She would have assumed there was some hidden meaning behind them.

But now she knew. The Young Cult Master was the kind of person who even looked after the guests at his subordinate’s wedding.

Chairan smiled and said,

“You have never attended a wedding as a guest of the bride, have you?”

Geom Mugeuk nodded. There had been several weddings in his previous life, but he had never paid attention to the ceremonies. He had either gone there to meet someone or simply considered his attendance meaningful in itself.

“The bride should be the star of her wedding.”

As she said this, she turned around.

“What do you think? Do you think our girls can make the bride the star of the day?”

Only then did Geom Mugeuk understand what Chairan meant.

“I was shortsighted.”

He truly had not thought that far. He had only thought that it would be enough for these women to attend as friends.

Geom Mugeuk walked over to Dan Yeon, who was standing with the second squad of the Ghost Shadow Unit.

“Youngest Miss.”

When Geom Mugeuk singled her out, everyone’s eyes turned toward her.

“Yes, Young Cult Master.”

They had once exchanged jokes freely and spoken casually, but with everyone watching now, she could not do that.

“Congratulations.”

“Thank you for taking care of my elder sister’s wedding, Young Cult Master.”

She understood. In the past, she had once mentioned the matter of wedding guests to Seo Daeryong, who would become her brother-in-law. He must have told Geom Mugeuk about it.

“Of course I should take care of it. The cult received great help thanks to Miss Dan.”

Geom Mugeuk made it clear that this matter did not stem from a personal relationship.

He was showing consideration because Dan A had rendered great service to the cult! It was a kindness meant for the Dan sisters as they made their way within the organization.

At that moment, Squad Leader Seo Jin stepped forward and asked Dan Yeon,

“Youngest, why didn’t you tell us?”

“It was decided yesterday.”

Seo Jin said to Geom Mugeuk,

“Are you worried that she might not have any wedding guests? She has us.”

Then came her astonishing declaration.

“If the youngest’s family, then they are our family.”

She had meant it sincerely. At least as far as she was concerned, she regarded the Ghost Shadow Unit—especially the martial artists of the second squad she led—as family. When she became a squad leader, she had devoted her life to this work.

Dan Yeon, on the other hand, was not there yet. Though everyone doted on her as the youngest, she had only recently joined the organization. For her, family still meant only her two elder sisters.

That was why Seo Jin’s words were both surprising and touching. Of course, she wondered if the woman had said it only because the Young Cult Master was present.

“Thank you, Squad Leader.”

Seo Jin looked at Geom Mugeuk and said,

“Don’t worry.”

Geom Mugeuk suddenly remembered Seo Jin from before his regression. During the darkest and most difficult period of his life, Seo Jin had always said those words.

Don’t worry. Everything will be all right.

Yes, thanks to you, everything did turn out all right. Thank you, my friend.

“With Squad Leader Seo here…”

Geom Mugeuk added with a smile,

“this was never something I needed to worry about in the first place.”

* * *

“I don’t know whether this was the right choice.”

A bouquet of flowers lay on the table in front of Dan A. Seo Daeryong had come a little while ago, given her the flowers, and formally proposed marriage. When he had blurted it out yesterday while they were eating, she had been so flustered that she had been unable to answer properly. Today, she had agreed.

“Congratulations, Sister.”

“What’s there to congratulate me for?”

Dan A glanced at her younger sisters. She felt needlessly sorry for marrying before them and leaving them behind. Something that should have been perfectly natural felt like a source of guilt to her.

She had never intended to marry in the first place, and even if she did, she had planned to marry only after both of her younger sisters had married. She had even told them so before.

But now that she had found a good man, she was suddenly saying she would marry first, and she could hardly bring herself to face her sisters.

“I’m sorry to you two.”

Then Dan Bi, the second sister with the blunt personality, said,

“We’re grateful that you’re leaving us.”

“What?”

“That you’re alone…”

The youngest, Dan Yeon, cut her off and jumped in.

“If you don’t get married, we’d be happy.”

The second sister had obviously been about to say that their elder sister being alone would leave them with a burden.

“Still, we have to see you get married before we can learn. How the wedding is held, how to spend the newlywed period, and how to give birth to and raise children. You have to experience it and teach us.”

Dan A smiled at those words. When had that little girl grown up enough to say something so thoughtful?

“There won’t be many guests on our side.”

At her practical concern, Dan Yeon reassured her.

“Some of my colleagues from the Ghost Shadow Unit will come.”

It looked as though more people would come than she had just said, but she did not guarantee anything. They might end up disappointed later for no reason.

Dan Bi, meanwhile, could not even confidently count on a single guest.

“The Yellow Springs Pavilion people are all guests of Pavilion Master Seo.”

Dan A forced a smile.

“It’s fine. Don’t worry about it.”

She said that, but she was secretly concerned. She truly did not care for her own sake. She was not the sort to worry about others’ opinions, nor did she wish to make a good impression on anyone.

She had never regretted or felt ashamed of the life she had lived as a wandering martial artist. Through that work, she had saved many people, and it was how she and her two younger sisters had survived until now.

The only thing that troubled her was Seo Daeryong.

She had no parents, and if she did not even have any wedding guests, it would certainly stand out. Why would he marry a woman like that? She did not want Seo Daeryong to hear people say such things.

“I’m worried I’ll be a burden to Pavilion Master Seo.”

“What are you talking about? You’re too good for him even if he’s the Young Cult Master, not merely the Pavilion Master. Only the Cult Master might be good enough for you.”

Dan A clapped a hand over her sister’s mouth at the outrageous statement. Then again, when they had first met at the inn, Dan A had chosen the Demon Cult Master as the best of the lot.

At that moment, Dan Bi took out an envelope she had prepared.

“Take this.”

Dan A accepted it and opened it to find a bank draft for a considerable sum.

“You’ll have a lot to spend money on, so use this. The youngest and I contributed an equal amount.”

“It’s fine! I have plenty of money!”

The money they had earned through their commissions had always been divided equally among the three.

But the two younger sisters knew that, no matter how evenly the money was divided and how they collected and spent their shared expenses, their elder sister had always spent far more.

They were old enough now to understand how large those unnoticed expenses could become once they accumulated.

“I have lots of money. So take it back. Save it well and use it when you two get married.”

Of course, the two younger sisters did not take it back.

“We still have a long way to go. And when we get married, you’ll help us too, won’t you?”

Dan Yeon picked up where Dan Bi left off.

“You have to show us that you’ve married well and are living happily before we’ll feel like getting married ourselves, don’t you?”

The younger sisters she had devoted everything to caring for were saying such things to her now.

“Sister, don’t feel intimidated. Use this money to prepare!”

* * *

“I told you that you don’t have to come anymore.”

When Geom Mugeuk returned to Hyeolcheon Doma’s quarters, Seo Daeryong had only just begun preparing for his training.

“I have to devise a strategy with Daeryong once his training is over.”

While Seo Daeryong trained, Geom Mugeuk pointlessly moved back and forth, sat down and stood up, and went into the room to take books off the shelf before putting them back.

Perhaps finding the sight distracting, Hyeolcheon Doma took a practice great saber from the corner and threw it at Geom Mugeuk.

“Don’t just fool around. Swing this, at least.”

“Why is this so heavy?”

Geom Mugeuk caught the great saber and complained that his arm was about to fall off, then said,

“But if I’m going to train, shouldn’t I be practicing swordsmanship?”

“You think you can train with that light sword that would break if you merely tapped it in our Namdo Sect? Not a chance.”

“My father uses a sword, too.”

“The Cult Master’s sword is heavy.”

Geom Mugeuk looked down at the great saber. He could guess why Hyeolcheon Doma had deliberately put a saber in his hand.

To make him distance himself. To make him distance himself from mastering the Guhwa Demon Art.

Geom Mugeuk swung the saber from the corner until Seo Daeryong’s training ended. He spent most of the time merely staring at the weapon, and only swung it a few times.

When the training was over, Hyeolcheon Doma asked Geom Mugeuk,

“Well?”

“It’s heavy. I was thinking about why it’s so heavy.”

The moment he heard Geom Mugeuk’s answer, a peculiar smile appeared at the corners of Hyeolcheon Doma’s mouth.

Seo Daeryong knew what that smile meant.

He remembered what Hyeolcheon Doma had said on the first day of his own training, when he had complained that the saber was heavy.

―Understanding that heaviness is the beginning and the end of the saber technique I intend to teach you.

Geom Mugeuk had understood the essence of the saber technique in an instant.

As Seo Daeryong looked at him with eyes that seemed to say, Of course!, Geom Mugeuk revealed something even more astonishing.

“You met Miss Dan before coming here, didn’t you?”

“How did you know?”

“You brought her flowers, too, didn’t you?”

Seo Daeryong’s eyes widened in shock.

“How did you know that as well?”

“I’m the Young Cult Master. Your Young Cult Master!”

At that moment, Hyeolcheon Doma picked up the flower petals scattered across the floor. They had fallen from Seo Daeryong’s clothes before training began. There had been more than one or two.

“Did you get into a fight with a flower?”

Only then did Seo Daeryong understand how Geom Mugeuk had figured it out.

Naturally, Geom Mugeuk immediately protested.

“No! How can you reveal the secret of my mystery and prestige?”

When Hyeolcheon Doma pretended not to hear and tried to go inside, Geom Mugeuk hurriedly grabbed him.

“Come now, Elder, sit here for a moment. You ought to know exactly what your disciple has been up to.”

The three of them sat side by side in the training yard.

“Before coming here to train today, I picked a bouquet of flowers. I formally asked Miss Dan to marry me.”

“And then?”

“She accepted my proposal.”

Seo Daeryong’s face was filled with emotion.

“Congratulations. Of the three of us, you’re the first to marry.”

At the words “the three of us,” Hyeolcheon Doma made an incredulous face, then gave a faint smile. Yes, there had once been a time when he had thought about marriage too. He had not known then that he would end up living alone until this age.

“I’m sorry for being the first to leave!”

“Don’t apologize. I should be the one apologizing.”

“What?”

“You said your right arm was going to open the gates of hell, and I didn’t stop you, did I?”

No matter how one looked at it, for Seo Daeryong this was the gate to happiness.

“As long as I’m with Miss Dan, even that hell doesn’t frighten me.”

“Don’t blame me later by saying, ‘Why didn’t you stop me back then?’ And don’t say, ‘Young Cult Master, you knew exactly how this would turn out! You know everything in the world!’ Don’t do that!”

“I absolutely won’t.”

Seo Daeryong asked cautiously,

“But even after I marry, I’m still your right arm, aren’t I? Nothing will change between us, right?”

Geom Mugeuk stared at him, then said,

“Something will change. You’ll have to step down as my right arm.”

“Oh, come on. You’re trying to joke again.”

“You know very well how dangerous being my right arm is.”

What Seo Daeryong knew was that the right arm was protected from danger better than anyone else. The right arm was supposed to protect the torso, but he was always the one being protected.

“You can’t have both happiness and being my right arm. Choose one.”

Seo Daeryong pulled a long face and stood up.

“I’ll go tell her. I’ll tell her that our love should be fulfilled in our next life.”

“What are you doing? Why aren’t you going?”

“I’m going. I really am.”

As Seo Daeryong shuffled away hesitantly, Geom Mugeuk said to Hyeolcheon Doma,

“His footwork is as heavy as the great saber. He’s still right where he started.”

“He went two steps.”

“Then he took one step backward.”

At that, Seo Daeryong, who had walked some distance away, came back.

“No! That’s too much! I can’t give up either of them!”

At the sight, the Blood Heaven Saber Demon shook his head and said with a look of disdain,

“What’s so great about being the right arm? Anyone would think it came with a monthly salary.”

Geom Mugeuk casually remarked,

“Come to think of it, there was someone who once agonized over whether to be the right wing or the left wing.”

The Blood Heaven Saber Demon pretended not to hear and gazed at the distant sky.

The position of right arm was more important to Seo Daeryong than any other position.

“I-I’ll work even harder. So even after I get married, please let me remain your right arm.”

He was not joking. The position held a special meaning for him.

As the master of the Yellow Springs Pavilion, he could not travel throughout the Central Plains alongside Geom Mugeuk. Even if he were not, he still lacked the ability to serve Geom Mugeuk properly.

He feared that if he lost even this position—where he insisted on calling himself Geom Mugeuk’s right arm despite being unable to fulfill the role—his relationship with Geom Mugeuk might be severed entirely.

The Blood Heaven Saber Demon merely gazed up at the sky in silence, his expression showing that he understood exactly what was in his disciple’s heart.

The playfulness vanished from Geom Mugeuk’s face. He spoke to Seo Daeryong with a calm gaze.

“When you have a wife and children, you’ll have to devote your strength to them. I won’t accept a right arm that can’t even look after his own family. Take care of your family first, then take care of me. Can you do that?”

Seo Daeryong stared at Geom Mugeuk in silence for a moment before letting out a sigh.

“Isn’t that a rather selfish right arm?”

“You only say that because you don’t know.”

“What?”

“An arm that bears responsibility for a family is far stronger than one that stands alone. You’re going to become truly strong now. The blade you wield will carry another kind of strength as well. From now on, that blade will never break.”

“!”

“If the torso refused to cut off such a right arm even after knowing that, wouldn’t the torso be the selfish one?”

Seo Daeryong gazed silently at Geom Mugeuk and spoke in a low voice.

“That’s good, then. A selfish torso and a selfish right arm. I’ll stay attached until the very end.”

Even if I’m cut off, I’ll be cut off after properly fulfilling my role as your right arm.

“Once a right arm, forever a right arm.”

Someday, he would seat his grandchildren on his knees and tell them stories about this man. He had far too many stories to tell.

The Blood Heaven Saber Demon, who had been listening to the two of them, suddenly spoke.

“I’ll officiate the wedding.”

Startled, Seo Daeryong sprang to his feet and stood before the Blood Heaven Saber Demon. He then offered a polite bow and expressed his gratitude.

“Thank you so much, Master!”

Given his master’s personality, he was not the sort of person who would officiate someone else’s wedding. Seo Daeryong knew very well how great a decision this must have been for him.

“Have you chosen a date?”

“Not yet. Please choose an auspicious day for us, Master.”

“I don’t know much about that sort of thing. The Chief Military Advisor or the Wind Heaven Cult Leader might know better.”

Just then, Geom Mugeuk extended a hand, and the book the Blood Heaven Saber Demon had been reading rose into the air from the desk by the window.

The title of the book was revealed to everyone.

Asking Heaven’s Will

There was a subtitle beneath it.

How to Choose an Auspicious Day

The Blood Heaven Saber Demon looked at Geom Mugeuk with an expression that said he was beyond speechless. He must have sneaked a look at it earlier, when he had been coming and going before training.

“It was a coincidence.”

As if that could possibly be true. Seo Daeryong was deeply moved.

“I really hope you’ll choose the date for us, Master. We’ll live happily together for that reason alone. Please, I’m begging you.”

The Blood Heaven Saber Demon rose from his seat and walked toward the building as he spoke.

“I’ll choose a day that’s convenient for me.”

Chapter 723: Don't Try to Shoulder It Alone
-------------------------------------------
Seo Daeryong’s wedding date was set.

Though he had said he would decide it as he pleased, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon pondered over it carefully, and, worried he might have chosen wrongly, even went to Cult Leader Pungcheon and the Chief Strategist to have it confirmed.

Since the date set in that way was so soon, Seo Daeryong and Dana hurriedly began preparing for the wedding.

In the meantime, Geom Mugeuk went to see the Fist Demon.

He could put off meeting the other Demon Supremes for a while, but the Fist Demon was his master before he was a Demon Supreme.

Considering their relationship, even if he had scolded him, saying, “You should have come to me first, not the Blood Heaven Blade Demon!” Geom Mugeuk would have had nothing to say. But the Fist Demon was not that kind of person. Even if Geom Mugeuk came to him last every time, he would never say a word about it.

A man like the high, solid cliff he had once sought to break down.

And now, a man who had even surpassed that cliff.

The Fist Demon sat cross-legged beneath that cliff, sunk in meditation.

Geom Mugeuk quietly sat cross-legged beside him.

Without realizing it, he almost began practicing his heart method, then stopped. Thinking about it, he had not done ordinary meditation for some time.

Whenever he sat like this, he practiced his heart method or the Heavenly Demon Body Protection Art. He had worked hard not to waste even a single moment.

Yes, for a while, let’s take it easy.

And so he simply kept his eyes closed. He did not even pay attention to his breathing. He just sat there blankly. Various thoughts came to him and disappeared.

He thought of his elder brother, and also of Cult Leader Pungcheon and Gowol. He thought of the other Demon Supremes too.

I should hurry and go see them. If I go too late, they’ll be disappointed.

Even as impatience rose in him, he calmed himself by thinking, This impatience is exactly what the Blood Heaven Blade Demon told me not to have!

How much time had passed like that?

“Mugeuk.”

At the Fist Demon’s call from beside him, Geom Mugeuk opened his eyes.

For some reason, his voice was different from usual, and Geom Mugeuk grew inwardly tense.

“Yes, Master.”

Geom Mugeuk looked at the Fist Demon seated beside him.

The Fist Demon, who had been gazing at the cliff, turned to look at him as well. What on earth was he about to say, to be looking at him with such deep eyes?

At last, unexpected words flowed from the Fist Demon’s mouth.

“Never take such a risk again.”

Only then did Geom Mugeuk understand. He had heard from Ian about how he had been stabbed in the heart.

The words, I had no choice, hovered on his lips, but Geom Mugeuk did not say them.

The Fist Demon had roamed the murim with his father since his youth. It was not as if he did not know such circumstances.

By nature, the Fist Demon was not someone who would say, Do not take risks. If anything, he was someone who would say, Never retreat.

“I understand, Master.”

Yes, it had not been something unavoidable.

He could have avoided the prophecy. When the Lord of Cheonhwa Pavilion told him the prophecy, he could have simply returned to the cult.

But because he was someone who did not avoid that prophecy and instead threw himself into fate, the prophecy had come true.

“Now, everything that happens to you is not yours alone.”

Geom Mugeuk understood the weighty meaning contained in the Fist Demon’s words.

The countless people connected to him.

The sorrow his death would create in equal number.

But the reason the Fist Demon was saying this now was likely because he had one single sorrow in mind.

His father’s sorrow.

Because he knew his father better than anyone.

And so, what the Fist Demon was saying now was the same as this:

If you die, the Great War between the Demonic and Orthodox Sects will break out.

Moreover, unlike before his regression, his father had mastered the Nine Flames Demonic Art to the twelfth star. The Demon Supremes had also become stronger than in the past.

The wailing caused by my death will soon become the drums of war.

Looking into the Fist Demon’s eyes, Geom Mugeuk answered.

“I will not forget the weight of my life.”

The Fist Demon nodded and rose from his seat.

Geom Mugeuk also stood, and with a joke, eased the stiff atmosphere.

“By the way, you almost became the father of the greatest beauty under heaven. What a shame.”

Of course, the Fist Demon was not disappointed at all.

“A shame? I am already the father of the greatest beauty under heaven.”

The Fist Demon cherished Ian more than his own daughter. Geom Mugeuk could not help wondering what would have happened if he had not taken Ian as his foster daughter, but he considered this to be the Fist Demon’s nature.

Once he gave his heart, he gave it to the end.

That was the kind of man the Fist Demon was.

“Master.”

“What is it?”

“Please teach me the Thunderbolt Asura Fist again.”

At the words “teach me again,” rather than “please teach me a move,” the Fist Demon looked puzzled.

“I have already taught you everything there is to teach.”

“In the meantime, you have changed, Master, and I have changed too.”

Both of their martial arts had grown stronger than when Geom Mugeuk had first learned fist arts.

“I am certain there will be something new to learn.”

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon’s teaching had meant to distance himself from his greed to attain full mastery of the Nine Flames Demonic Art; it did not mean he should distance himself from martial training.

In particular, the Thunderbolt Asura Fist was a martial art that had been pushed aside because of that greed. Because of the Nine Flames Demonic Art, and also because of the Time-Heaven Secret Art. It had been pushed back and pushed back like that. In truth, it was not a martial art that deserved such treatment.

The Fist Demon sensed that Geom Mugeuk stood at a certain turning point in his martial studies. And at such a time, he had come to him?

“Good. From now on, I will transmit the formula of the Thunderbolt Asura Fist to you.”

The Fist Demon transmitted the formula as if teaching it for the first time.

It was the same formula, but it felt different from when he had first learned it.

After receiving the entire formula, he discussed it with the Fist Demon. They spoke in very concrete detail.

Not only that, but they also spoke frankly about the supreme principles of martial learning each of them had come to realize in the meantime. This process did not end merely with the transmission of fist arts.

A deep sharing of martial learning.

Geom Mugeuk learned, and the Fist Demon learned. They taught while learning, and learned while teaching.

When the learning they shared through words came to an end, the Fist Demon immediately took off his upper garment.

Usually, when he took off his martial robe, he threw it aside carelessly, but today he neatly folded it and placed it beside him. Come to think of it, it was a new martial robe. It seemed the news Ian had brought had not been only that he had been stabbed in the heart.

Geom Mugeuk also took off his upper garment and stepped forward. The Fist Demon’s gaze flicked briefly toward the wound over Geom Mugeuk’s heart, but he said no more about it.

The two stood side by side and began unleashing the Thunderbolt Asura Fist.

In their last battle, the Fist Demon had shown it. No longer did the sound of thunder come from his fist.

Rumble! Boom!

Therefore, the fist that produced thunder was Geom Mugeuk’s.

The sound was louder and stronger than before. After this sound reached its peak, it would gradually lessen again, until, like the Fist Demon’s fist, it made no sound at all.

When they finished that bout of training, the Fist Demon smiled in satisfaction.

“Only now is that baby fist of yours starting to gain some strength.”

Through this training, Geom Mugeuk’s realm had clearly risen to the next stage.

“It’s at least a boy’s fist now, isn’t it?”

“You are still far from becoming an adult!”

“May I come to see you until it becomes an adult’s fist?”

The Fist Demon silently nodded.

The next day, and the day after that, Geom Mugeuk trained.

He stayed at the Fist Demon’s residence altogether and trained. He truly immersed himself in the Thunderbolt Asura Fist.

He had lived diligently all his life, but this was the first time he had trained with such complete absorption, without thinking of anything else.

Before he knew it, Geom Mugeuk had even forgotten the thought: Let’s distance myself from achieving full mastery of the Nine Flames Demonic Art!

* * *

Seo Daeryong and Dana sat facing each other.

Today was the day they would discuss a slightly sensitive yet important matter.

“This is the money I’ve saved.”

“This is the money I’ve saved until now.”

The two revealed the money they had put aside. They had to buy a house to live in, and to prepare for the wedding, they needed to know how much they had.

Dana’s money was far more than the money Seo Daeryong had saved.

“I’m sorry.”

The difference was so great that the words of apology came out on their own.

“Why be sorry? It hasn’t been long since you became Pavilion Master, has it? You can start saving from now on. There’s a certain joy in starting from a small house and gradually building up your household, isn’t there?”

Seo Daeryong silently looked at her money.

“What’s wrong?”

“When I think that this is money you earned while risking your life, I wonder whether I can use it.”

Dana smiled.

“That’s exactly why we should use it. We’re using it for the most valuable thing in my life.”

“I thought you’d scold me for only saving this much.”

“From now on, you will be scolded. You can’t spend recklessly like when you were a bachelor. Now, let’s go look at houses.”

The two left the main headquarters and began looking around the nearby villages.

Homes near the main headquarters and Maga Village were very expensive, and the price also differed depending on the size of the house.

With the money the two had combined, they would have to go quite a distance away.

But Seo Daeryong seemed reluctant to leave the vicinity.

He stopped in front of a house in a good location and asked her.

“What do you think of a house like this?”

“It’s quiet and clean. It’s not far from the main headquarters. There’s a large academy over there, and a martial hall behind it. And carriages don’t pass through this road. It seems like a really good place to raise children.”

“Let’s raise them well here.”

“Yes. Let’s save diligently and be sure to buy this house.”

Then Seo Daeryong said something unexpected.

“We don’t need to buy it. It’s already our house.”

“What?”

“I bought this house a few years ago.”

Dana’s eyes widened in surprise.

As if to prove it was true, Seo Daeryong took out a key, unlocked the door, and went inside.

“After becoming the Master of Hwangcheon Pavilion, I mostly lived in my residence in the inner court, so I only came here occasionally to clean.”

The house was more spacious and nicer than she had expected. For a house he could not visit often, it had been well cleaned too.

“When I bought this house, it wasn’t as expensive as it is now. After the academy and martial hall we saw earlier were built, this neighborhood became desirable.”

“Even so, it must have been expensive.”

At her reaction, still unable to believe it, Seo Daeryong squared his shoulders proudly.

“Even before becoming the Master of Hwangcheon Pavilion, I was a special investigator. I earned decent money in my own way. Of course, I did borrow money from a bank and add that to buy it.”

Seo Daeryong had saved money steadily.

Before meeting Geom Mugeuk, he had disliked socializing with people, so unlike some of his colleagues, he had been able to avoid squandering money on entertainment. Thanks to that, he had even been able to acquire a house like this.

“I paid back all the money I borrowed from the bank last year.”

“So you didn’t tell me this in advance, is that it?”

“I’m sorry! I wanted to bring you here and surprise you.”

“You know I have a temper, right?”

“Of course.”

Seo Daeryong took one step back.

“I almost said this earlier, you know. Did bandits rob you of all your money? Did you hand it all over to some courtesan?”

“I was prepared to take it even if your fist came flying.”

She was delighted. The reason she was delighted was not only because of the house.

“I’m so happy that we have a good house. But more than that, I’m happier to know that you weren’t someone who lived wastefully without a thought. I was worried about how I would educate this hopeless man from now on.”

“I’m not someone who lives just any which way!”

At last, Seo Daeryong made to her the boast he had always made only to Geom Mugeuk. Of all the boasts he had ever made, it was the one that felt the best.

“I may not look it, but I entered Hwangcheon Pavilion at the top of my class.”

* * *

At last, the day of the wedding dawned.

Dana was so nervous that she had barely slept.

She had fallen asleep for a short while, but even then, she had a nightmare. It was a dream of a desolate wedding with only a few guests present.

On top of that, rain poured down, and even the few guests who had come all left. It was the worst possible wedding.

Seo Daeryong, on the other hand, was grinning happily, as if everything delighted him.

“It’s finally today!”

There had been no major crises while preparing for the wedding. They said some people quarreled during the preparations and even called off their weddings, but Seo Daeryong followed Dana’s wishes well.

Looking at Seo Daeryong, Dana said,

“Promise me just one thing.”

“Say anything.”

Dana quietly gazed at Seo Daeryong, then took his hand and said,

“No matter what happens in life, don’t try to shoulder it all alone. A married couple always walks together.”

Seo Daeryong nodded.

“I promise.”

The two looked at each other and smiled brightly.

“Then I’ll see you later.”

“I’ll see you later.”

After Seo Daeryong left the residence, Dana looked up at the sky visible outside the window. At last, the moment had come for her to accept the commission called marriage in her life.

“I’ll make sure to complete it without failing.”

A little while later, there were people who came to see her. They were Ian and Cha Iran.

“Ah! Captain, you came in person.”

Seo Daeryong had formally introduced Dana to Ian and Jang Ho over a meal together.

That day, Ian had said that she would take care of the makeup and ceremonial robes on the day of the wedding, so Dana should not prepare them. And now she had come in person like this.

“I didn’t know you would come yourself. Thank you so much for coming.”

“Pavilion Master Seo is my friend. It’s only natural that I come. And today I’m only here as an assistant. I brought the real expert with me.”

When Ian looked at Cha Iran, she introduced herself.

“I’m Cha Iran, leader of the third squad of the Ghost Shadow Corps.”

“I am Dana. It’s an honor to meet you.”

She had heard about Cha Iran several times through her younger brother Danyeon. Seeing her in person, she was even more beautiful than Dana had heard.

“I’ll do your makeup.”

Dana was deeply moved. She had thought Ian would simply send someone, but she had not expected the captain and squad leader to come personally like this.

Cha Iran properly displayed her skill.

When the fairly long makeup session ended, everyone let out exclamations of admiration. Once Dana put on the splendid red ceremonial robes as well, she was truly beautiful.

“I think it would be all right even if the entire third squad came.”

Since Ian’s words were high praise for the makeup, Cha Iran smiled happily.

Danbi and Danyeon, who had been waiting outside, came in and were delighted to see their elder sister.

“You’re really pretty.”

“Our sister is this pretty, and she almost lived her whole life as a wandering warrior.”

At their praise, Dana lowered her head as if embarrassed.

Ian said to Dana,

“Are you ready?”

Dana nodded.

When she opened the door and went outside, an astonishing sight awaited her.

A flower palanquin had been prepared to take her to the place where the ceremony would be held.

It was a splendid and beautiful palanquin, but what surprised Dana were the people who would carry it.

The palanquin bearers were truly huge in build. And they were not merely large.

Possessing a terrifying aura that no one would dare approach lightly, they were none other than the Demon Soldiers.

“We will escort you to the place where the ceremony is being held.”

It was a flower palanquin that the Demon Army Lord, Jang Ho, had specially prepared for her.

To the surprised and moved Dana, Ian said with a smile,

“No one in the world has ever ridden in a flower palanquin carried by Demon Soldiers.”

From the very beginning, today’s wedding, which had gone beyond her expectations, finally began.

Chapter 724: Master Was No Master.
----------------------------------
The Blood Heaven Blade Demon looked at his reflection in the bronze mirror.

He had taken off the martial robes he always wore and was dressed in ceremonial attire. It was for officiating today’s wedding.

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon seemed to clear his throat for a moment.

“On this day, in this place, groom Seo Daeryong and bride Dana, brought together by a heaven-bestowed bond, shall pledge themselves to a hundred years of marriage.”

Then the Blood Heaven Blade Demon changed his tone this way and that.

“Shall pledge themselves. Shall pledge themselves, indeed.”

None of it quite rolled off his tongue, and displeasure rose over the Blood Heaven Blade Demon’s face.

If he had his way, he wanted to simply say this:

“Today is the day these two make a blood oath!”

But that was not something he could do. He practiced the officiant’s speech again.

“Groom Seo Daeryong entered our cult at an early age and walked the perilous path of the demonic way…”

When he could not remember the rest, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon took a paper from his robe and read it. After muttering the written contents to himself for a moment, he spoke again.

“After serving as a special investigator of the Yellow Springs Pavilion, he rose at a young age to the position of Pavilion Master of the Yellow Springs Pavilion. Bride Dana has traversed the Central Plains…”

He unfolded the paper again and memorized what came next. Just then, as his nervous voice trembled, someone spoke from behind him.

“No! What are we to do if the officiant is trembling more than the groom?”

A faint smile formed at the corners of the Blood Heaven Blade Demon’s mouth. The owner of the voice was Geom Mugeuk.

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon turned toward Geom Mugeuk and said,

“Who says I’m trembling?”

“Aren’t you failing to memorize it because you’re nervous?”

“You think I can’t memorize this little thing?”

Geom Mugeuk said with a smile,

“If you try to recite it by rote like this, you’ll definitely forget it. Your mind will go completely blank, and you won’t be able to think of anything.”

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon’s brow twitched, but he could not deny it. Geom Mugeuk was right. He had been memorizing it since last night, yet it still would not stick; in front of everyone watching, he would remember even less.

“I’d rather fight a life-and-death blood battle with the Murim Alliance Leader.”

If it were something like that, he would go out and fight without blinking an eye. Why was this trifling thing making him tremble so much?

Geom Mugeuk offered a solution.

“Just say what you want to say to Daeryong. It doesn’t matter if it’s short. No, these days, guests probably prefer it short.”

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon admitted honestly,

“When I was young, I could memorize an entire martial arts manual on the spot. Why won’t this stick?”

“It’s because it’s your disciple’s wedding, and you want to do well for him.”

“Why don’t you just say it’s because I’m old!”

After grumbling like that,

Whoosh.

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon burned the paper with the officiant’s speech using his fiery yang qi.

“I’ll just say, ‘Eat well and live well!’ One sentence and that’s it.”

“It suits you perfectly, elder. Everyone will like it.”

Then the Blood Heaven Blade Demon said something unexpected.

“Or you can give the officiant’s speech.”

“Me?”

Geom Mugeuk looked bewildered.

“Aren’t you the one who made that Daeryong fellow human and even got him married? You speak much better, too.”

“You’re the one who made Pavilion Master Seo human, elder. If you hadn’t taken him as your disciple, would the current Pavilion Master Seo exist?”

“That was because I fell for your scheming.”

“It also wasn’t me who got drunk and clung to your trouser leg, nor was I the one who let that Daeryong sleep in my bed.”

In that regard, he thought there was definitely a bond between the Blood Heaven Blade Demon and Seo Daeryong.

“You’ve worked hard, elder, making Pavilion Master Seo into a person and marrying him off.”

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon stared intently at Geom Mugeuk. Seeing how he had come to look after him, knowing he would be anxious on a day like this, he thought that Geom Mugeuk truly never changed.

“How did the fistfight with that fist-throwing master of yours turn out?”

Everyone knew that Geom Mugeuk had shut himself in the Fist Demon’s quarters to train.

Geom Mugeuk raised a tightly clenched fist.

“It went from a baby’s fist to a boy’s fist, and yesterday, I finally received acknowledgment. This fist is now an adult’s fist.”

The Thunderbolt Asura Fist had risen another level. When those whose martial arts were racing toward the ultimate essence trained while candidly sharing their attainments in martial learning, the effect was unbelievable.

It was not only Geom Mugeuk; the Fist Demon, too, had gained much from the training. At the very end, the Fist Demon had said this:

—I wonder if sound will come from my fist again.

The sound of thunder had once resounded, then ceased. If it sounded again now, what kind of sound would it be? No, what would the power of that fist be like?

“This is all thanks to you, elder.”

“Why would that be thanks to me? It’s thanks to your master teaching you well and you working hard.”

“Because you showed me the right direction. If not, I would have been running in the opposite direction. Effort made in the wrong direction would have returned as frustration in equal measure. If you don’t know which way to go, then rest instead! It’s all thanks to your teaching, elder.”

Seeing a satisfied expression appear on the Blood Heaven Blade Demon’s face, Geom Mugeuk smiled and added,

“Now, you must also tell Daeryong where he should go. Let’s go.”

* * *

At that time, Seo Daeryong, too, was standing before a bronze mirror.

He wore red ceremonial robes embroidered with clouds and cranes, silk shoes, a floral crown, and a jade belt. His own appearance looked utterly unfamiliar to him.

Seo Daeryong asked his reflection in the bronze mirror,

“Daeryong, is it all right for you to be this happy?”

Just then, a voice came from behind him.

“It is.”

Startled, he turned around to find Demon Army Lord Jang Ho standing at the door.

“Congratulations on your marriage.”

Seo Daeryong approached him with an overwhelmed expression.

“Thank you, my lord.”

They had once had a meal together so Seo Daeryong could introduce Dana, but afterward, they had been too busy to meet separately.

“How do you feel?”

At Jang Ho’s question, Seo Daeryong answered with a smile,

“You just heard me, didn’t you?”

Jang Ho laughed as well.

“Now, let us go.”

When they went outside, four Demon Army warriors were waiting.

“For today, we have decided to escort the bride and groom.”

“You did not have to go this far.”

It might seem like nothing, but this was deeply meaningful.

For the Demon Army to move like this meant that the Demon Army Lord acknowledged his friendship with Seo Daeryong. Considering Jang Ho’s strict nature in all things, this was truly a great show of goodwill toward Seo Daeryong.

“Thank you, my lord.”

“Not at all.”

If he had not met Geom Mugeuk and his life had not changed, this was someone with whom he would never have exchanged a single word in his entire life. Now, they had become this close.

They arrived at the venue where the wedding ceremony would be held.

The large training ground was decorated everywhere with red and golden cloth, as well as banners.

At its center stood a large folding screen, and in front of it, a table had been prepared for the wedding ceremony.

To its left and right were places arranged where the bride and groom would receive guests.

As he sat in the groom’s seat, Seo Daeryong was surprised. There were truly many seats prepared for the guests, and the first thought that came to him was whether all those seats could possibly be filled.

Soon after, the flower palanquin carrying the bride arrived.

When Dana stepped down from the palanquin, her heart sank. There was not a single person in the countless seats that had been prepared. Even though it was nearly time for the ceremony to begin.

With a trembling heart, she sat in the seat where the bride was to wait.

Next, Geom Mugeuk and the Blood Heaven Blade Demon arrived.

Seo Daeryong rose from his seat and greeted the two of them.

“Master! Young Cult Leader!”

Seo Daeryong was inwardly flustered because there were far too few guests.

Geom Mugeuk looked around.

“It’s almost time for the wedding to begin, but not many people have come.”

It was not simply that many had not come; aside from the Demon Army warriors who had come with them, there were truly only a few people.

“The Yellow Springs Pavilion investigators and enforcement warriors aren’t here yet?”

At Geom Mugeuk’s question, Seo Daeryong lowered his head deeply.

“It seems I have behaved poorly in daily life.”

Seo Daeryong looked as if he might cry at any moment. He himself was fine. After all, his life had been lonely anyway.

But for Dana’s sake, at least, the wedding could not be this desolate. Ah, he had not wanted to show this kind of sight. Just twenty more people—no, even ten more people, please come!

“It’s enough if the people who truly wish to congratulate you do so. They’re all here. Your master is here, Lord Jang is here, Ian is here. Second Young Lady and Youngest Young Lady are here. And most importantly, I’m here too!”

Seo Daeryong grabbed Geom Mugeuk by the sleeve and dragged him aside. Then he whispered desperately,

“Young Cult Leader, please call even your guards. I am too ashamed in front of Lady Dan.”

“I wonder if my guards will be enough?”

“Pardon?”

Geom Mugeuk raised his hand high.

Fweeeeng! Bang! Babang!

At that signal, fireworks burst from all directions. Brilliant and beautiful fireworks exploded in the blue sky.

Bang! Babang! Babang!

Only then did the guests begin entering the place. Geom Mugeuk had made them wait for a moment in order to tease Seo Daeryong.

“Young Cult Leader!”

Seeing those who entered the venue, tears welled in Seo Daeryong’s eyes.

“The bride isn’t crying, yet the groom is about to?”

“I was truly shocked!”

“Then did you think you could get married before me and not suffer at least this much?”

The first to enter were the Yellow Springs Pavilion investigators and enforcement warriors.

“Pavilion Master! Congratulations!”

Led by the most senior investigator, Gok Myeong, the investigators poured in and offered their congratulations. Behind them, the enforcement warriors entered.

“Even though you didn’t ask, my guards came too.”

Indeed, the Young Cult Leader’s guards had also come to congratulate him.

Seo Daeryong was so busy receiving the flood of congratulations from guests that he had no time to think.

Watching this, Dana felt greatly relieved. To be honest, until just a moment ago, she had been even more nervous and flustered than Seo Daeryong.

‘Thank goodness.’

Thinking of the situation just moments ago, when there had been no one at all, the fact that she had no guests of her own was no problem at all.

But that problem, too, was soon resolved.

A crowd of guests came rushing over to congratulate her. They were none other than Ghost Shadow Team Two, led by Seo Jin.

“Congratulations on your marriage, Lady Dan!”

When Seo Jin offered congratulations on behalf of them all, Danyeon stepped forward and introduced her to her older sister.

“She is the team leader of Ghost Shadow Team Two, the team I belong to.”

Dana bowed respectfully.

“My younger sister still lacks in many ways. Please guide her well from now on.”

“Yeon is already doing well. Please don’t worry.”

The other team members stepped forward and congratulated her.

“Congratulations on your marriage.”

“I am in the same team as Danyeon. Congratulations.”

“Congratulations.”

It was not just one or two people. Almost every team member had come to congratulate her, and Dana was deeply moved.

Dana felt relieved. It was the moment the worries that had kept her from sleeping all night disappeared. This much congratulations was enough. No, it was more than enough.

They were not the only guests today.

Various unit leaders and squad leaders of the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult came to congratulate Seo Daeryong. Given the nature of the Yellow Springs Pavilion, it did not have good relations with other organizations, yet many people had nevertheless come to congratulate him.

It was thanks to Seo Daeryong’s fair handling of affairs in ordinary times. There were many who disliked him, but there were also many who highly valued the way he did his work.

At that moment, the warriors parted all at once, forming a path.

There stood the Demon Exalt who had arrived first. The one who seemed as though he would come latest, yet had unexpectedly come first, was none other than the Vicious Little Demon.

Wearing a white mask, he led several Faceless Guests and walked over to Seo Daeryong.

The Vicious Little Demon congratulated Seo Daeryong.

“Congratulations.”

“Thank you very much. I did not think you would come personally, Little Demon.”

No matter that he held the position of Pavilion Master of the Yellow Springs Pavilion, he had not dared invite the Demon Exalts.

“Blade Demon invited me.”

In truth, even if the Blood Heaven Blade Demon had not invited him, the Vicious Little Demon would have attended. Seo Daeryong was Geom Mugeuk’s right-hand man, after all.

At that moment, Sipchilgyo, who stood behind Chai Ran, searched among the Faceless Guests behind the Vicious Little Demon to see whether the crescent-moon Faceless Guest was there. To her surprise, he was.

Just as she had, he too seemed to have made an effort to be part of this escort. Their gazes met in midair. Meeting not anywhere else but at a wedding ceremony made it a truly meaningful encounter.

He was not the only Demon Exalt in attendance.

The Drunken Demon and the One Flower Sword Exalt appeared there together.

Seo Daeryong was startled by their unexpected visit.

The Drunken Demon handed Seo Daeryong wine as a gift and congratulated him.

“It is a sweet fruit wine. If you later come to need strong liquor, come find me.”

The Drunken Demon called out loudly to Geom Mugeuk,

“I brought good wine for the celebration today!”

When Geom Mugeuk looked where he was looking, one of the Three Great Drunkards, Yeo Bin, was standing there with entire crates of wine stacked beside him.

Geom Mugeuk could feel it. In the time they had not seen each other, the Drunken Demon had become even freer.

Geom Mugeuk looked around at the Demon Exalts and said,

“Today, we drink all night! No one should think of going home today!”

The One Flower Sword Exalt had also brought a gift. However, the person who delivered it was not Seo Daeryong, but Danyeon.

“Congratulations on your marriage.”

“Thank you, Sword Exalt.”

After coming to the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult, Dana had once learned swordsmanship from the One Flower Sword Exalt. Though she had not formally become her disciple, in her heart, she regarded the One Flower Sword Exalt as her master.

She had desperately wanted to invite her, but had not dared invite a Demon Exalt; and yet the One Flower Sword Exalt had come like this.

The One Flower Sword Exalt gave her a potted flower as a gift. It was a flower she cherished even among the flowers she raised.

“It may seem as if flowers bloom on their own, but they take a great deal of care. They need sunlight, they need wind, and they must be repotted as well. Perhaps married life is the same, wouldn’t you say?”

Dana understood what meaning lay behind the gift.

“I will raise both well, Sword Exalt.”

Seo Daeryong, watching from the side, wanted to shout this:

The potted flower in my office—I still haven’t killed it and am raising it well!

To him, who had been thinking of such jokes, the One Flower Sword Exalt said,

“You are truly a fortunate man.”

At first, he thought she was speaking of Dana.

But now, those words were about someone else.

The One Flower Sword Exalt looked at someone. Seo Daeryong followed her gaze.

There stood the Blood Heaven Blade Demon.

He was smiling brightly as he received the guests.

“!”

The instant he saw that sight, a powerful emotion rang through Seo Daeryong’s heart.

The One Flower Sword Exalt said as if surprised,

“It is my first time seeing such a face on him as well.”

His master, who never smiled in ordinary times, was beaming as he welcomed the guests.

At Demon Exalts, at unit leaders and squad leaders. He even smiled at ordinary warriors.

He was thanking them for coming. He was telling them to sit over there. They were people he normally would not have spared a single glance, yet he was telling them to be sure to eat before leaving later.

In this moment, his master was not his master.

Chapter 725: Eat Well and Live Well
-----------------------------------
Seo Daeryong said nothing.

He merely stared silently at the Blood Heaven Blade Demon. Just as the sight of the Blood Heaven Blade Demon smiling so brightly was being etched deep into his heart—

“Pavilion Lord Seo.”

At the One Blossom Sword Venerable’s call, Seo Daeryong snapped back to his senses and looked at her.

“Yes, Sword Venerable.”

“You should receive the guests’ greetings.”

While he had been looking at the Blood Heaven Blade Demon, several martial artists had lined up and were waiting.

“Ah, yes.”

As the One Blossom Sword Venerable turned and walked away, Seo Daeryong spoke to her.

“Thank you, Sword Venerable.”

If she had not told him, he would have been too busy receiving the guests’ congratulations to notice that expression on his master’s face.

The One Blossom Sword Venerable nodded silently and walked on.

The place she headed toward was where Geom Mugeuk stood. Geom Mugeuk was a short distance away, standing side by side with the Drunken Demon as they talked.

“Since you’re not feeling hurt, hyung, I’m the one who feels hurt instead.”

Even though Geom Mugeuk had returned to the Cult and had not come to see him, the Drunken Demon now seemed not to mind at all. He should have grumbled and complained.

“You must have been busy.”

“I wasn’t busy. I had time to come see you, hyung.”

“Your heart must have been busy.”

Geom Mugeuk stared fixedly at the Drunken Demon.

“Did something happen while I was gone? Are you sick? Is it incurable?”

“Once it passes, all things are futile. Do you think I’ll die regretting that you didn’t come see me when I was dying?”

Just then, the One Blossom Sword Venerable approached the two of them and said,

“He has changed the way he shows he feels hurt. It is a change in tactics, so don’t be deceived.”

The Drunken Demon flinched, then laughed heartily as he looked up at the sky.

Leaving him behind, Geom Mugeuk gave the One Blossom Sword Venerable a proper greeting.

“I apologize for not coming to see you immediately after returning to the Cult.”

“I heard you were in the middle of fist arts training.”

The One Blossom Sword Venerable quietly observed Geom Mugeuk, then shook her head.

“When you are like this, I have no time to rest.”

It meant that every time she saw him, he showed signs of growth, and it also carried the meaning that she, too, was still striving.

“May I come to you soon as a sparring companion?”

The One Blossom Sword Venerable smiled and nodded.

“Come anytime.”

Just then, a new Demon Venerable arrived.

It was none other than the Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable. Since she had come as a guest, she had reduced her ghostly energy to a minimum, but even so, Geom Mugeuk could tell at a glance. She had grown even more than when he had seen her before. She, too, was growing by leaps and bounds every time he saw her.

The Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable offered her congratulations to Seo Daeryong. She had come not so much because of her friendship with Seo Daeryong, but because of her relationship with the Blood Heaven Blade Demon.

After giving her congratulations, she came to Geom Mugeuk first and greeted him. No matter what anyone said, the person she cared about most was Geom Mugeuk.

Because the person she most wanted to show her growth to was the Young Cult Leader.

“This waiting is a most interesting and pleasant wait.”

It meant he was waiting for the day she grew and became his Demon Venerable.

The Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable smiled happily. Those words were a reward for her efforts so far, and more than enough to become the driving force for her efforts to come.

The next Demon Venerable to arrive was the person with the most frightening face among all the guests who had come today.

At the appearance of the Fist Demon, everyone held their breath. His broad back cut through the guests as he walked.

The Fist Demon went not to Seo Daeryong, but first to the Blood Heaven Blade Demon and offered his congratulations.

“Congratulations.”

“Thank you for coming.”

Geom Mugeuk watched the two of them converse.

They were the two people closest to his father. Because of that, there was a bond that only they, with his father between them, possessed. And there was also a bond with him between them.

When the two looked at him and said something to each other, Geom Mugeuk smiled and said,

“You aren’t speaking ill of me, are you?”

The two did not answer and continued their conversation.

“That’s too much!”

Watching from beside them, Cha Iran could sense from the way they gave no reply and simply continued their own conversation just how deep their relationship with the Young Cult Leader was.

If she had not experienced the person called Geom Mugeuk, she would have had to make all sorts of guesses about that behavior just now.

Then Geom Mugeuk saw someone and ran over in delight.

“Owner.”

Jo Chunbae, dressed neatly, stood in a far corner. Naturally, he had also been invited to this wedding.

“Young Cult Leader.”

“I’m glad you came. Now, let’s go.”

Geom Mugeuk took his hand and brought him to Seo Daeryong.

“Actually, our owner here is acquainted with all the Demon Venerables, isn’t he? Shall we go around and greet them one by one?”

“Oh dear, please don’t say such things even as a joke.”

Cha Iran silently watched as Geom Mugeuk took Jo Chunbae over and introduced him to Seo Daeryong. The Young Cult Leader greeted him more warmly than he had the Demon Venerables. This, too, if she had not experienced Geom Mugeuk beforehand, would have made her ponder the tavern owner’s true identity.

“There is no need to doubt.”

The person who came beside her and spoke was Ian.

“I’m not doubting.”

She no longer thought that Geom Mugeuk was hiding something. He was simply new and astonishing every time she saw him.

“I just thought… it must be truly difficult to become the Young Cult Leader’s shadow.”

At that, Ian gave an unreadable smile.

“No. Because it isn’t difficult, it will be difficult for us.”

Cha Iran felt she roughly understood what Ian meant.

Just then, Geom Mugeuk brought Jo Chunbae over to where the two women were.

Ian welcomed Jo Chunbae warmly.

“Please come here and stay with us.”

“No, no. I can stay over there in the back.”

“Stay with me.”

Not far from them, the bride, Dana, was seated.

Dana was stunned by these incredible guests.

The great lords of the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult had come, the group leaders had come, and the Demon Venerables had come as guests. In her entire life, she had never seen nor heard of such a grand wedding ceremony.

To think she had worried that such a grand wedding ceremony might be desolate. What on earth had she been thinking?

And then one thought occurred to her anew.

‘He truly was an extraordinary person.’

She was so grateful that such an extraordinary person had liked her.

Geom Mugeuk looked around. He had found Jo Chunbae, but there were still people he had to find.

“By the way, why don’t I see them? I’ll be back in a moment.”

Geom Mugeuk set out to find someone in the place bustling with countless guests.
