The person he was looking for was squatting beneath a tree in the corner of the training ground. It was none other than the Poison King. He had come here without even offering a greeting.

Geom Mugeuk sat down beside him and asked,

“What is this fellow called?”

“Hongduui (Red-Headed Ant).”

Indeed, the heads of the ants crawling over the ground were red.

“If you look down on them because they’re small, you’ll suffer for it.”

They seemed to be highly venomous ants.

“When did you arrive?”

The answer came from behind him instead of from the Poison King.

“We arrived earlier.”

When he looked back, the Demon Buddha was standing there. And a short distance away was his older brother, Geom Muyang.

“What is this? Some suspicious meeting?”

In truth, there was nothing suspicious about it. His brother was close to the Demon Buddha, and the Demon Buddha was close to the Poison King.

“I know what to call this gathering. The Flame of Rebellion!”

At Geom Mugeuk’s banter, Geom Muyang replied bluntly,

“That flame went out long ago. Someone put it out very thoroughly.”

At Geom Muyang’s words, the Demon Buddha smiled faintly. Geom Muyang’s heart had now eased enough that he could make such a joke comfortably.

“If you have all come, you should offer your congratulations first.”

“We will do it later.”

Of course, it seemed they had all been held here because the Poison King had become absorbed in the ants.

“Since this is a rare outing for you, I thought you would enter flashing with golden radiance.”

Today, the radiance from the Demon Buddha’s body was fainter than usual. Since he had come as a guest, it seemed he had adjusted its intensity.

“At my wedding, you must enter shining brightly enough to blind the guests.”

“You intend to marry as well?”

When the Demon Buddha asked in surprise, Geom Mugeuk answered as if it were only natural.

“Of course. I must.”

Because he answered far too readily, the Demon Buddha instead did not believe him.

“Even if you do, there will be so many shining people at your wedding that my radiance will be nowhere near enough.”

It truly seemed like it would be a wedding where no one could imagine who might come.

At that very moment, the surroundings suddenly began to grow quiet. Everyone’s gaze turned toward one place. Postures that had been relaxed all straightened.

Even the Poison King, who seemed as if he would remain squatting even if a bloody battle broke out beside him, rose from his seat.

Geom Ujin was walking toward them.

When he appeared, no one even breathed loudly. Those who had thought, Surely even the Cult Leader wouldn’t come, were even more astonished.

Among them, the most shocked were Seo Daeryong and Dana. They had truly never dreamed that the Cult Leader would attend their wedding.

Step, step, step, step!

Everyone standing along the path Geom Ujin passed cupped their fists and paid their respects. He revealed no aura whatsoever, but his very presence overwhelmed everyone there.

Surprisingly, the place Geom Ujin walked toward slowly was the bride’s side. With gentle eyes, he said to Dana,

“Congratulations on your marriage.”

It was already astonishing that the Demon Cult Leader had come, but he had come to congratulate her first.

Dana’s mind went blank, not knowing what to do.

“How could the Cult Leader come here?”

At her astonishment, Geom Ujin said calmly,

“Do we not have a connection from the Central Plains?”

He was referring to the incident when the dishes sent by the women at the next table had gone to Geom Ujin.

Of course, that was not why Geom Ujin had come. Naturally, he had attended because of the Blood Heaven Blade Demon, but he had first shown care for her, who had thrown herself into the Cult with only her two younger siblings.

“Thank you so very much, Cult Leader.”

Without realizing it, she tried to prostrate herself in her wedding robes, but the gentle energy Geom Ujin sent out stopped her.

Danbi and Danyeon, too, watched the Cult Leader’s back in deep emotion as he turned and walked away.

Danyeon, who had sent food to Geom Ujin that day, truly wanted to shout like this:

‘The most handsome and wonderful person in this world is the Cult Leader!’

After taking care of the bride in that way, Geom Ujin approached Seo Daeryong.

“Pavilion Lord Seo, congratulations on your marriage.”

“Thank you, Cult Leader.”

Seo Daeryong was deeply moved. He was truly grateful that the Cult Leader had taken care of her before him.

When Geom Ujin sat in the central seat, Geom Mugeuk and Geom Muyang sat to either side of him, and the Demon Venerables lined up to the left and right. Naturally, the Poison King and the Demon Buddha, who had been in the corner of the training ground, sat with them as well.

The wedding proceeded according to ceremony, and at last it was time for the Blood Heaven Blade Demon’s officiating address.

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon had never been nervous before anyone, yet now it could be felt that he was nervous.

After clearing his throat for a moment, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon slowly opened his mouth.

“Today, in the presence of the august Cult Leader, my disciple, the Hwangcheon Pavilion Lord Seo Daeryong, and the beautiful bride Dana are to join in a lifelong bond as a match bestowed by the heavens.”

A smile formed on Geom Mugeuk’s lips.

You brats, eat well and live well.

That was what the Blood Heaven Blade Demon had said he would do. He must truly have wanted to do it that way. Yet he was delivering the officiating address while painstakingly remembering what he had memorized.

“The groom, Seo Daeryong, entered our Cult at an early age and walked the arduous path of the demonic way…”

Because this was not a place where he could simply act according to his own fiery temperament. This was not a place to reveal his own personality, but a precious place meant for Seo Daeryong and Dana.

Everyone listened to the Blood Heaven Blade Demon’s officiating address. In the middle, he stammered, and his words broke off.

“…Life is by nature a lonely road, and meeting a companion who will walk that road with you can be called one of life’s greatest blessings.”

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon’s words did not continue. He had forgotten the next line.

Flustered, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon closed his eyes and recalled the words, then continued. Considering his pride, it was unimaginable, but he silently carried on.

“…Do not look only at each other’s strength, but soothe each other’s wounds. Rest with your backs leaned against one another. In every moment, be together. A married couple is…”

Once again he forgot the next words, and the Blood Heaven Blade Demon continued the address after recalling them. He could have ended it briefly out of shame.

But the Blood Heaven Blade Demon spoke every word he had prepared, one character at a time.

Geom Ujin was watching the Blood Heaven Blade Demon with a deep gaze. The Fist Demon, the One Blossom Sword Venerable, the Drunken Demon, and the other Demon Venerables were the same. Today, for the first time in decades, they were seeing a new side of the Blood Heaven Blade Demon.

“…And when, after wandering the jianghu, you stop in some field, look up at the stars and share your final moments together.”

That was the end of the officiating address.

After finishing the address, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon looked at Seo Daeryong. Seo Daeryong, his eyes reddened, was doing his utmost to hold back his tears.

Now the Blood Heaven Blade Demon spoke from the heart. It was not the wedding address he had prepared in advance, but the words he wanted to say to his disciple and his bride in this moment.

“I am a man who has never married in all my life. So I do not know how one should live to have a good marriage.”

Seo Daeryong looked at his master with trembling eyes.

“I have not even lived as a good person. My life was filled with nothing but misfortune.”

Seo Daeryong nearly cried out, That is not true, Master.

“So, in truth, I do not know what I should say to you two.”

For a moment, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon looked past the two of them at Geom Woojin.

After exchanging glances with the Cult Leader, he slowly turned his head again to look at the Drunken Demon, then at the One Flower Sword Venerable.

“The older I get, the more I find myself regretting the days gone by.”

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon’s gaze, which had briefly drifted outside, returned to Seo Daeryong and Dana.

“You are both clever children, so do not live a life of regret like this master of yours.”

Seo Daeryong bowed his head deeply. His shoulders were trembling faintly. The tears he had been holding back flowed down. At Seo Daeryong’s tears, tears also spilled from Dana’s eyes. She knew well what Seo Daeryong thought of his master.

When the two of them began to cry, the flustered Blood Heaven Blade Demon looked at Geom Mugeuk.

Since weddings were not supposed to be this solemn, Geom Mugeuk stepped in with a smile as the rescue party.

“How ever did our elder hold back what he truly wanted to say?”

Imitating the Blood Heaven Blade Demon, Geom Mugeuk declared,

“O demons of the Divine Cult, born and raised in darkness! Betrayal means death, so forge an eternal blood alliance! Cast aside the sword and take up the blade!”

The Demon Venerables laughed, and one corner of Geom Woojin’s mouth also curled upward. At the relief that it was finally over, the tension eased from the Blood Heaven Blade Demon’s face.

Since both of them had lost their parents, the rite of bowing to their parents had been omitted.

Seo Daeryong and Dana tried to bow to the Blood Heaven Blade Demon. At that, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon waved his hands in refusal and walked far away.

Seo Daeryong and Dana bowed as they watched his retreating back.

With that bow, the two’s wedding ceremony came to an end.

When the two who had bowed rose from their places, a gust of wind blew in.

Whoooosh.

Then one petal fell from the flower Dana was holding.

The petal fluttered through the people and came to rest in the One Flower Sword Venerable’s hand. A smile formed on the lips of the woman who loved flowers.

Geom Mugeuk walked over to Seo Daeryong. He was smiling brightly. Seeing that expression as he approached, Seo Daeryong thought, Had he ever seen the Young Cult Leader’s face so filled with joy?

Bang! Baaang! Bang!

Beneath the exploding firecrackers, Geom Mugeuk said the words the Blood Heaven Blade Demon had not been able to say.

“My right arm, eat well and live well!”

Chapter 726: If You Want It More Desperately Than Anyone
--------------------------------------------------------
“I knew you would be here.”

At Geomguk’s words from behind, Hyeolcheondoma turned his head with a surprised face.

“How did you know I was here?”

This place was the very peak of Daecheon Mountain. Hyeolcheondoma stood at the summit, looking up at the stars in the night sky. Long ago, Seodaeryong had told him that his master would climb this mountain when he wanted to clear his head.

“Am I not someone who knows everything about you?”

Hyeolcheondoma said to Geomguk.

“You must have come up because you were frustrated too.”

Geomguk smiled without denying it.

“The celebration banquet?”

“It ended well.”

His father and Hyeolcheondoma had not attended from the beginning, and most of the Majon had also left after briefly keeping their seats. It was because only if they left would Seodaeryong be able to comfortably enjoy himself with his comrades.

After quietly watching Seodaeryong and Dana enjoying themselves in that boisterous place, he slipped out. Perhaps they would end up seeing the Hwangcheongakju reciting poetry while drunk.

“The officiant was truly wonderful.”

“I didn’t know you would forget your lines so many times.”

“It’s because I’m old.”

Hyeolcheondoma laughed while looking up at the stars.

“I’ve even sent off my right-hand man to get married. What will you do now?”

Until now, he had only spoken of distancing himself from the Great Accomplishment of the Nine Transformations Demon Art. But now the answer was different.

“Now I will run toward the Great Accomplishment of the Nine Transformations Demon Art.”

When he felt the Great Accomplishment of the Nine Transformations Demon Art was right before his eyes and tried to enter closed-door training, Hyeolcheondoma had stopped him.

If you desire something too desperately, it often slips further away.

He had gained great enlightenment then. The process of building everything he had achieved until now had been a result obtained among people, yet he had abandoned all that and been led by the desire to achieve the Great Accomplishment.

But the Great Accomplishment he spoke of now was different from then.

Because those were words spoken after having seen Daeryong through his wedding.

A changed desire dwelt in Geomguk’s eyes. Now Hyeolcheondoma did not stop him.

“If you’ve decided to desire it, desire it more desperately than anyone else.”

* * *

That morning, Geomguk packed his things.

A change of clothes, spices needed for cooking, leather to spread on the floor when sleeping, dried foods that could be kept for a long time, and a few extra things just in case.

Having prepared a full bundle like that, he climbed Daecheon Mountain again.

He found a suitable place among the various spots on the mountain. After searching, he found a place with a wide clearing that no one visited because the terrain was rugged. There was even a cave in the cliff rising at the back, and a stream flowed nearby.

Behind was a cliff, in front was wide-open space. It was perfect for training.

Geomguk put down his baggage in the cave.

He planned not to use the Spatial Heaven Secret Technique of the Space-Time Shifting Art in this training. Because the Great Accomplishment was approaching as if within arm’s reach, he resolved to train while feeling the energy of Daecheon Mountain with his body.

Long ago, his father had told him: if you feel stifled or hit a wall while training, climb this mountain. The energy of Daecheon Mountain would help.

He began training the next morning.

First, he felt the primal energy of Daecheon Mountain, sat in a meditative posture, and meditated.

And after eating a solid meal, he formally trained in the Nine Transformations Demon Art.

He unfolded the first through fifth stances in the clearing, and only for the sixth stance, Heavenly Demon World Annihilation, did he open the Space-Time Shifting Art to perform it.

Because if he performed Heavenly Demon World Annihilation here after even absorbing the energy of the Bihyeolsa, the surroundings would all be annihilated.

Only after performing even Heavenly Demon World Annihilation like that and expending all his internal energy did he come out of the Space-Time Shifting Art.

Geomguk, having completely exhausted his internal energy with Heavenly Demon World Annihilation, sat down and began to replenish it.

“One time!”

He planned to repeat this process continuously. Because he unfolded the stances with all his heart and effort, it was by no means an easy task.

Five times a day was the goal.

How many times would he have to remain here?

A hundred times? A thousand? Or perhaps ten thousand?

* * *

Eat, train, stroll, sleep.

That routine was repeated every day.

The Great Accomplishment that had seemed as if it could be grasped right before his eyes was not so near.

Something that seemed to be right in front but was actually far away—perhaps he had mistaken it for being close because it was too enormous?

Fortunately, Hyeolcheondoma’s teachings exerted their power when he entered into full-scale training. The two teachings he had passed on.

Don’t be too desperate.

Yet be more desperate than anyone.

Geomguk put both of these contradictory pieces of advice into practice.

On days when impatience arose wondering if this training method was wrong, he recalled the first teaching.

When his mind tempted him to go down and find another method, he recalled the second teaching.

People thought he was good at forming relationships among others, but his true strength was accomplishing something alone. He was someone who could devote his entire life alone to a single goal.

And today, when he had performed the two hundred and twenty-fifth Nine Transformations Demon Art, an unexpected visitor appeared.

The man who climbed up there as if taking a stroll with his hands behind his back was Geom Woojin.

“Father!”

Geom Woojin made a look of pity.

“Your appearance is like that of a beggar.”

Come to think of it, he hadn’t shaved his beard recently, and his attire was a sight to behold as he devoted himself solely to training.

“Will you meet them in that state?”

“Who?”

From his father’s gaze silently fixed on him, Geomguk could tell who that opponent was.

He meant that in the moment the Heavenly Demon Soul manifested, would he meet it in such a state?

It was something he had longed for so much; he couldn’t possibly do that.

“Please sit here, Father.”

Geomguk quickly unrolled the tiger skin he had rolled up in his pack. What was already spread on the floor was ordinary leather.

“Did you know I was coming?”

“I brought it just in case.”

He wouldn’t have brought it to another location. But this was the place his father had recommended. He had thought that perhaps his father might come by at least once.

Geom Woojin sat on the tiger skin.

“Have you eaten?”

“I have not.”

It had taken a very long time to hear those words from him. His father had come to share a meal with his son.

“Please wait a moment. I’ll quickly catch a wild boar or something.”

As Geomguk tried to go hunting, Geom Woojin stopped him.

“Let’s eat what you eat.”

“They are all cold.”

“It’s fine.”

Geomguk brought the meat and food he had left over.

He raised his Yang energy to warm the food and served it to his father. Of course, he had prepared clean tableware in his pack in preparation for his father’s arrival.

The two ate in silence. They only ate without speaking, but now it wasn’t awkward at all.

After the meal, Geomguk steeped tea. He also had his father’s favorite tea in his pack.

“I didn’t bring alcohol because I thought I might want to go down if I drank.”

“The tea tastes good, so it’s fine.”

The two sat facing each other and drank tea.

Geomguk conveyed his honest feelings.

“It feels like I’m almost done, like I’ll achieve it if I just finish this training. Is the Great Accomplishment originally like a mirage in the desert?”

Geom Woojin answered after taking a sip of tea.

“Originally, you wouldn’t have even been able to enter that desert.”

It meant that Geomguk’s progress was that fast.

Achieving the Great Accomplishment and becoming stronger was important, but in fact, his greater desire was to see his Heavenly Demon Soul.

What would it look like?

Did the Heavenly Demon Soul truly exist?

How would it commune with him?

There was so much he was curious about, but he didn’t ask his father. Because he wanted to experience everything directly.

But he had to ask this one thing.

“After achieving the Great Accomplishment, how do you call forth the Heavenly Demon Soul?”

“You will become able to call it forth with your will.”

“How much internal energy is needed?”

“No internal energy is needed for the Heavenly Demon Soul to descend. However, the Heavenly Demon Soul needs your internal energy to use martial arts.”

In the end, it meant the Heavenly Demon Soul was deeply connected to the one who performed martial arts.

The descent of the Heavenly Demon Soul.

His heart trembled just hearing those words.

After drinking the remaining tea, Geom Woojin rose from his seat.

“When you come down from the mountain, let’s play a game of baduk.”

His father didn’t say a single word of “do this” or “do that”; he truly just ate and left.

But one change came with this visit.

* * *

The day after his father’s visit, as always, he took a stroll after finishing his training.

Because it was training with all his might, his mental energy consumption was enormous, and when training ended, he was completely exhausted. But he never neglected taking a stroll.

He contemplated while walking. He thought about things from before his regression, about enemies he had killed. He also recalled martial arts formulas and pondered new training methods.

Then a giant tree that had collapsed as one side of the valley crumbled came into Geomguk’s eyes.

“What a pity.”

A giant tree that must have been a thousand years old lay fallen and broken.

Geomguk, who had been about to pass by, returned to the tree.

Swoosh! Swoosh!

Geomguk cut the tree with the Black Demon Sword.

And he brought wood cut into a fairly large box size back to his dwelling.

Geomguk began making something from it.

Swoosh, swoosh.

As it was carved away by the sword, what gradually revealed its form was a thick baduk board with legs.

The reason Geomguk didn’t just pass by the giant tree was that this broken wood was a spindle tree, the finest material for a baduk board. It was even a thousand-year-old spindle tree.

“When you come down, let’s play a game of baduk.”

He wanted to personally make a baduk board and give it to his father as a gift. There could be no better gift for a father who loved baduk.

Geomguk made the baduk board with all his heart.

Training continued, and he devoted his spare time to making the baduk board.

After ten days like that, the baduk board was finally completed.

It was so splendid that one could believe it was bought from a master craftsman.

Now one step remained; the most important task remained.

After briefly looking down at the baduk board and composing his mind, Geomguk began drawing lines on the board with the Black Demon Sword.

Slash! Slash! Slash!

Lines drawn without deliberation.

Nineteen horizontal lines were drawn, and soon vertical lines were drawn again.

Though a single mistake would ruin the whole thing, Geomguk did not lose focus.

Between the lines, they were drawn with not an inch of error, perfectly accurate.

Geomguk was satisfied. It was made even better than he had imagined.

Now that he had made the board, he felt like making the baduk stones himself too.

He cut more spindle tree wood and made a baduk container. Rather, making the container was more difficult than the board. It required more professional skill than it looked, and only after a few failures did he make a satisfactory baduk container.

And finally, he made the baduk stones.

While strolling, he found white rocks that shone like crystal, and a few days later, he found black stones that emitted a pitch-black light.

Geomguk began to emit sword qi from the Black Demon Sword to make the baduk stones.

Even if it was a bit more uncomfortable and difficult, he used the Black Demon Sword rather than a dagger. He controlled minute sword qi to carve the baduk stones. Finally, he used Gang qi to smooth the baduk stones.

He had never imagined he would use martial arts to make baduk stones.

Making the first stone took a long time, but gradually that time decreased.

He had almost never tried such minute control with sword qi before.

Swoosh! Swoosh!

The more he made, the more accustomed he became to the minute control of sword qi and Gang qi.

Train, make stones, train again, make stones again.

One or two, or at most three or four—he carefully made stones every day like that.

No matter how tired he was, he did not rest from training and making stones.

Days repeated like that, and Geomguk overcame the tedium.

Finally, today he made the last stone—the three hundred and twenty-first in total.

“It’s finally done!”

Geomguk placed the last stone he had made into the baduk container with trembling hands. Baduk stones of identical size sparkled inside the container.

After touching them with an overwhelmed heart, Geomguk carefully placed them in a corner of the cave and covered them with tiger hide. There was something he had to do now, more than enjoying this emotion.

Geomguk came out of the cave and drew his sword.

It was already night when today’s training had ended, yet he was trying to perform the Nine Transformations Demon Art again.

At the moment he completed the last baduk stone, the thought came to him that he had to perform the Nine Transformations Demon Art. He couldn’t know why such a thought came. He just felt that way.

The moment he unleashed the first form, Annihilation.

Slash! Slash! Slash! Slash!

The four evil spirits that appeared from the east, west, south, and north cleaved a single space.

Before they could even vanish, he unleashed the Second Form, Great Annihilation Style.

Now Geom Mugeuk was executing these five forms in succession.

Sssssssss!

The terrifying evil spirits split apart before his eyes. Their number was twenty-six!

Kwakakakakakakakakakakakakakak!

They charged forward, tearing space to shreds.

Even before seeing the result, Geom Mugeuk’s sword was already unleashing the next form.

Whoooosh!

With the sound of a gust of wind, a wall of gang qi arose before Geom Mugeuk.

Before the transparent radiance of the Third Form, Great Demon Wall, had fully faded, the Fourth Form, Dark Flash, was unleashed.

Huk.

A deep darkness seemed to settle over everything.

Shiiiiing.

A single streak of light crossed through the dark.

When the surroundings brightened again, sword qi was pouring down from the sky like lightning. It was the Fifth Form, Soul-Severing Demon Strike.

Shukshukshukshukshukshukshukshukshukshuk!

Pakpakpakpakpakpakpakpakpakpak!

Fragments of stone flew up.

The traces of sword qi left in the ground were spaced at even intervals. And the depths of the holes were identical as well.

Even though he had unleashed the First Form through the Fifth Form in succession, Geom Mugeuk felt no strain on his body whatsoever. The Ninefold Demonic Art was unfolding with true freedom.

He could not unleash the Sixth Form, Heavenly Demon Annihilates the World, here.

He could not destroy Daecheonsan.

Geom Mugeuk opened the Space-Time Transposition Art and unleashed Heavenly Demon Annihilates the World there.

Flash!

The instant light burst from the Black Demon Sword and covered the world in white,

the world within the Space-Time Transposition Art was extinguished.

It was Heavenly Demon Annihilates the World, which he had repeated hundreds of times after climbing Daecheonsan.

Normally, after completely annihilating the world within the Space-Time Transposition Art, he would release the technique himself and come out.

But today’s Heavenly Demon Annihilates the World was different. After flying endlessly and annihilating everything,

Kwijijijijik!

Cracks spread through the world inside the Space-Time Transposition Art in every direction, and it began to shatter.

‘!’

Cracks were forming in the Space-Time Transposition world that Geom Mugeuk was looking at. Even though he had not released it himself.

‘The Space-Time Transposition Art is breaking!’

Heavenly Demon Annihilates the World was smashing through and destroying the Space-Time Transposition Art. Since this had never happened even once before, Geom Mugeuk was flustered.

Kwaaaaaang!

As the Space-Time Transposition Art shattered, Geom Mugeuk was flung out into the outside world.

Peok.

With a tremendous impact, Geom Mugeuk slammed into the cliff. With no internal energy, he was helpless.

Kwakakakakakwang!

Even after destroying the Space-Time Transposition Art, the remaining shockwave of Heavenly Demon Annihilates the World smashed the surroundings apart.

Kwaang! Kwareureureung!

In the aftermath, the cliff where the cave had been behind him collapsed.

Wareureureu!

Geom Mugeuk’s eyes flew wide open. He rolled his body and dodged, but there was no avoiding another enormous boulder. It had all happened in the blink of an eye.

‘No!’

Kuuung!

Darkness came with a thunderous roar.

‘Am I dead?’

Judging from the way his entire body felt as if it would burst from the impact of hitting the wall earlier, he was not dead. How on earth had he survived?

At that very moment!

The darkness surrounding him began to move, then slowly started to open its eyes.

Chapter 727: I Am Your Master and Friend
----------------------------------------
The sight of darkness opening its eyes was as if a new world were unfolding.

Those two eyes looked down at Geom Mugeuk. The pupils were as deep and dark as an abyss, and they resembled Geom Mugeuk’s own aura.

Geom Mugeuk had seen these eyes before. In dreams, and in visions, they had appeared before him many times, and the only thing he had ever seen clearly was those eyes. Yes, these were the eyes.

Realizing what the being before him was, Geom Mugeuk shuddered from the tips of his toes to the crown of his head.

‘It’s the Heavenly Demon Soul!’

It was the moment his own Heavenly Demon Soul descended into this world for the first time. And it had done so while saving his life.

The Heavenly Demon Soul slowly raised itself up. The two eyes that had been so close receded.

Thud!

The huge boulder on the Heavenly Demon Soul’s back fell to the ground.

Geom Mugeuk understood. At the decisive moment of crisis, the Heavenly Demon Soul had descended and blocked the boulder with its body.

‘The Heavenly Demon Soul saved me.’

What was even more astonishing was this.

‘It descended even though I didn’t summon it!’

In the moment the boulder had been falling toward him, Geom Mugeuk had thought of those who remained. Those who would grieve his death.

Had it read that feeling and saved its master? Or had it acted to save its own fate, doomed to vanish before it could even emerge into the world the moment it was born?

Beneath the moonlight, the Heavenly Demon Soul revealed its majestic form.

It displayed a truly overwhelming presence.

An absolute being that ruled over all devils and evil spirits in the world.

The pinnacle of the Nine Hells Demonic Art, and the ultimate essence of all demonic arts.

The face of the Heavenly Demon Soul revealed beneath the moonlight was truly terrifying. This was how it looked when it had only just appeared in the world? If it were to gain martial arts and grow from here?

‘We’ll have to make a new statue for the cult, Father.’

Near the Heavenly Demon Hall stood a gigantic evil spirit statue modeled after the Heavenly Demon Soul. But seeing it in reality, the statue could not compare in terror. Especially that cold pressure emanating from its gaze—no statue could ever express that.

But was it because it was his own Heavenly Demon Soul?

Geom Mugeuk found himself thinking that this frightening face was handsome.

It looked terrifying and yet handsome, and above all, when he looked into those arrogant eyes, he could feel that this was no mere killing weapon that only did as it was ordered.

It was far more magnificent than the image he had imagined dozens, hundreds of times.

‘I like you!’

What should his first words be?

But then, just as the Heavenly Demon Soul’s gaze seemed to turn toward Geom Mugeuk, it began to disappear soundlessly.

‘No!’

Because it had manifested on its own when Geom Mugeuk’s internal energy was completely exhausted, it could not remain long and returned.

His father had said that summoning the Heavenly Demon Soul required only will, not internal energy. But to make the Heavenly Demon Soul exist in the world and display martial arts, internal energy was necessary.

His regret lasted only a moment. Exhilaration filled Geom Mugeuk’s face.

“……I’ve finally reached great completion.”

A life flashing before one’s eyes was not something seen only at the moment of death. Truly, countless events he had experienced on the way here passed before him.

His life had been for the purpose of stopping Hwa Mugi and saving everyone. And this was the moment he achieved the most important thing for fulfilling that purpose.

Geom Mugeuk laughed loudly. He laughed like a madman. His laughter echoed through the mountains.

He was truly happy. He had achieved many accomplishments in martial learning, but had he ever been this happy?

Even if he obtained some peerless martial arts manual, even if he obtained the most precious spiritual medicine in the world, could he be happier than this? No, he could not.

He felt as if he wanted to dance. No, what was stopping him? He could just dance the dance he had once danced with Bisain. He would dance alone beneath the moonlit night.

Geom Mugeuk, who had been sitting with his back against the cliff, was just about to rise.

“Ugh!”

Geom Mugeuk grimaced and sat back down. His condition was not normal. Earlier, when the Space-Time Transposition Art had been broken, he had been flung away and slammed into the cliff, and his whole body ached as if he had been beaten all over. It was because he had crashed into it with his bare body, without internal energy.

Just moments ago, when he had been looking at the Heavenly Demon Soul, it had not hurt at all. But now that the tension had loosened, the pain came flooding in.

Geom Mugeuk first examined his body. Fortunately, though the bruising was severe, no bones were broken and he had suffered no internal injuries.

Even as he grimaced at the surging pain, he giggled like a madman. It was fine even if it hurt. So what if it hurt? He had achieved the great completion of the Nine Hells Demonic Art he had dreamed of.

Now he understood why the Space-Time Transposition Art had been broken during his final training.

As the Nine Hells Demonic Art reached great completion, Heavenly Demon Extinguishes the World had torn apart the Space-Time Transposition Art. It meant that the Space-Time Transposition Art could be destroyed with Heavenly Demon Extinguishes the World at great completion.

Originally, the breaking of the Space-Time Transposition Art was a bad outcome, but this was something to rejoice over. It meant the Nine Hells Demonic Art was that extraordinary.

“I did it!”

Geom Mugeuk’s shout echoed through the mountains.

Geom Mugeuk leaned against the cliff for a moment and looked up at the night sky. How could accomplishing something so great have been possible through his efforts alone?

“Thank you.”

After offering his gratitude to the heavens like that, Geom Mugeuk first recovered his internal energy.

He closed his eyes as he was and circulated his energy. He guided his true qi through one heavenly circuit, then a second, then a third.

When his dantian was filled with internal energy, he was able to manage the pain in his body as well.

After recovering his internal energy like that, Geom Mugeuk’s gaze turned toward the cave. A huge boulder was blocking the entrance. It was fine if it hurt, and he would not be embarrassed even if he danced. But there was one thing—one thing that absolutely must not have happened.

‘Please, at least let the go board be safe!’

If the inside of the cave had collapsed, the go board and stones would not be unharmed either. Ah, he truly did not even want to think about it.

Geom Mugeuk went to the cave entrance and pushed aside the boulder blocking it. That huge boulder was moved away lightly, as if a ball were rolling.

Fortunately, the cave had not collapsed.

Geom Mugeuk hurried inside. Since the go board and go stones had been covered with tiger hide, not even the fragments that had poured down had left a scratch.

Only then did Geom Mugeuk let out a sigh of relief.

Geom Mugeuk stroked the go board with his hand. The reason this was precious was not merely because it was a gift he would give his father.

He could feel it. The process of making this go board and these go stones had undoubtedly helped him reach great completion.

If so, then the person he should truly thank was his father. If not for his father, he would never even have thought of making a go board.

‘Surely you didn’t ask me to play go because you foresaw this result, did you?’

It was truly an absurd guess, but because it was his father and not someone else, the thought came to him.

In any case, though he wanted to pack his things and descend the mountain immediately, there was something he had to do in the morning.

Geom Mugeuk slept his final sleep on Daecheon Mountain inside the cave.

He was in such a good mood that sleep would not come.

He was afraid that if he woke up, all of this might have been a dream.

* * *

The next morning, Geom Mugeuk came out of the cave.

Fortunately, it had not been a dream. The area in front of the cave was full of collapsed boulders and stones.

Geom Mugeuk stretched in delight.

He could feel that his martial realm had risen another level. Whether he performed the Flying Heaven Sword Art or the Thunderbolt Asura Fist, he would be able to perform them better.

This was the first time he had ever felt so clearly that he had grown stronger.

Geom Mugeuk washed his body clean beneath the waterfall and changed into the new martial robe he had tucked deep inside his leather pouch. This was an outfit he had brought to wear when he achieved great completion. He placed the go bowls inside the leather pouch, wrapped the go board in tiger hide, and fastened it firmly on top of the pouch.

After finishing his preparations to descend the mountain, Geom Mugeuk drew his sword.

First Form of the Nine Hells Demonic Art: Man-Annihilation Form.

With a person-sized boulder at the center, four evil spirits appeared in the east, west, south, and north.

If the thing he had most wanted to see after achieving the great completion of the Nine Hells Demonic Art was the Heavenly Demon Soul, then the next thing he had wanted to see was these fellows. Just how had they changed?

The swords of the four evil spirits flashed.

Slice! Slice! Slice! Slice!

Their attacks were far faster and stronger than before.

The power at the ninth stage and the power at great completion were completely different. It could not be compared to the difference between the eighth stage and the ninth. If someone were trapped in the middle and hacked apart by those evil spirits, no one would be able to withstand it.

Power was not the only thing that had changed.

After shattering the boulder, the four evil spirits looked at him. Their appearances had also changed. As if to say this was their true form, they showed him their faces.

They had become more frightening, more handsome, more intelligent-looking, and more mysterious. Distinctive patterns had appeared on their faces and bodies.

He had seen his father’s evil spirits before. Their appearances and the impressions they gave off were clearly different from his own evil spirits now, so he thought their forms and auras must be related to the person performing the martial art.

“Good to meet you, Donggwi, Seogwi, Namgwi, Bukgwi.”

Geom Mugeuk called their names. Of course, the evil spirits did not answer just because he had achieved great completion.

But he felt it. The way they looked at him was different from before. What was contained in their gazes was undoubtedly some kind of emotion.

The four evil spirits of east, west, south, and north merely looked at him with deepened gazes, but Geom Mugeuk interpreted those gazes as he pleased.

—Were those names really the best you could do?

—Father gave you those names, so there’s nothing I can do about it.

Of course, there was no way they had actually asked such a thing, so it was a question and answer created by Geom Mugeuk’s own mind.

There was another change after reaching great completion.

He could make the four evil spirits of the Man-Annihilation Form remain continuously.

However, the internal energy required to maintain the evil spirits doubled, tripled, quadrupled as time passed. It increased to an unbearable degree.

From this, he could guess something. The longer the Heavenly Demon Soul remained manifested, the more enormous the internal energy it consumed would become. Well, it was only natural that such a terrifying being could not remain descended forever.

Regretfully, Geom Mugeuk bid farewell to the four evil spirits.

“I’ll see you again next time.”

The four evil spirits vanished, and this time he performed the Great Annihilation Form.

Sssssss.

An evil spirit with a terrifying face appeared.

As he had seen earlier, it had become even more frightening than before, and the demonic energy flowing from it had also become far more powerful.

And the number of evil spirits that had once split into twenty-six now split into forty-four.

Kwa-kwa-kwa-kwa-kwa-kwa-kwa-kwa-kwa-kwa-kwa-kwak!

When the Great Annihilation Form was unleashed, they swept away everything before them and advanced.

The clearing, which had been a mess of fallen boulders and debris, was cleaned out. The Great Annihilation Form swept through without leaving anything behind. Naturally, its power was stronger, and he could control them with greater precision.

The place became a clean clearing, just as it had been when Geom Mugeuk first settled there.

After tidying up the area like that, he shouldered his leather pouch and walked the path he had strolled along every day.

Since coming up to Daecheon Mountain, two seasons had passed, and the third had arrived. During that time, after finishing his training, he had not missed a single day of walking.

He felt that these walks, as much as the arduous training each day and the process of making the go stones, had undoubtedly helped him achieve great completion in the Nine Hells Demonic Art. Good thoughts always came when one was moving.

And one more thing.

While walking, Geom Mugeuk had tried to loosen the tightly wound life he lived. During these walks alone, he had tried to make himself relax in a life where he had lived sparing even a single quarter-hour.

In truth, that had been harder for Geom Mugeuk. Blood-soaked effort was possible through his will, but having ease in life was something that required wisdom.

After finishing that final walk, Geom Mugeuk climbed to the summit of Daecheon Mountain.

The wind that blew in fluttered the hem of Geom Mugeuk’s robe.

Just then, a tiger’s roar sounded in the distance. He thought it might perhaps be the tiger he had spared when he had come here with his father and elder brother.

Geom Mugeuk fully savored the energy of Daecheon Mountain, brimming with mystery. His father’s words had been correct. This Daecheon Mountain had helped him achieve great completion.

After standing there a while and gazing at the scenery from the summit, Geom Mugeuk turned around.

And he summoned the Heavenly Demon Soul again.

Yesterday, he had met it while injured and without internal energy, so he intended to greet it properly.

He worried that perhaps he might not be able to summon it now, since he had only just achieved great completion. Yesterday, it had descended on its own in a situation where his life hung in the balance.

But his concern was needless. With a whoosh, the Heavenly Demon Soul was suddenly standing before him.

The Heavenly Demon Soul he had seen beneath the moonlight and the Heavenly Demon Soul he saw beneath the bright sunlight felt different.

The fact that such a pure, pitch-black being existed in this bright world seemed wondrous.

Srrrk.

Geom Mugeuk’s body rose into the air and ascended until he was before the Heavenly Demon Soul’s face.

In front of the Heavenly Demon Soul’s face, he met its eyes. Because it was something he had created, he could look at it from this close, but it was emitting such intense demonic energy and light from its eyes that no master would dare meet its gaze.

Geom Mugeuk introduced himself to the Heavenly Demon Soul.

“I am Geom Mugeuk, Young Cult Leader of the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult.”

The Heavenly Demon Soul showed no reaction.

“I am your master, and your friend.”

At times, it looked as if it had thoughts; at others, it looked like a being that merely obeyed orders without thinking at all.

But Geom Mugeuk believed it. The Heavenly Demon Soul was undoubtedly a being with reason.

When it had appeared in dreams and visions before, and when it had saved him yesterday as well, there had surely been some kind of emotion contained in those eyes of the Heavenly Demon Soul.

“Thank you for saving me yesterday.”

Perhaps the reason it had not yet revealed any emotion was that it had not accepted him as its true master. From now on, he would have to show this Heavenly Demon Soul what kind of person he was.

Geom Mugeuk wanted to become its true master and share an emotional connection with this Heavenly Demon Soul. He wanted to converse with the Heavenly Demon Soul.

And so, on the beach within the Space-Time Transposition Art, he wanted to lie beside it and speak with the Heavenly Demon Soul. Of course, for that, he would need a larger island and an enormous, sturdy chair.

Geom Mugeuk stopped looking at the Heavenly Demon Soul and turned around.

Together, they looked down from the summit over Daecheon Mountain.

They stood like that in silence until all his internal energy was exhausted.

Just before the Heavenly Demon Soul vanished, Geom Mugeuk told it this one thing.

From his life before regression to his life after regression.

“I have wanted to see you for a very long time.”

Chapter 728: I Must Not Lose to Anyone Anymore
----------------------------------------------
Geom Mugeuk entered the Heavenly Demon Hall.

After coming down from Daecheon Mountain, he had come straight to the Heavenly Demon Hall without even unpacking.

Since his regression, this was the longest he had ever been away from the main sect, but Geom Mugeuk had not worried. Because it was no longer the cult of the past. His father, and the Demon Venerables as well, had all changed.

Without that faith, he would never have been able to undertake training with no promised end deep within Daecheon Mountain, cut off from the world.

And indeed, nothing had changed.

The thunderous greetings of the martial artists guarding the main gate of the Divine Cult were the same as ever, and the figures of warriors coming and going through the outer court were no different from usual. The taut vigilance of the inner court remained unchanged.

The doors slowly opened, and Geom Mugeuk stepped inside.

Beyond the red carpet, far ahead, he could see the grand chair.

His father sat upon it, looking the same as always.

Geom Mugeuk used the Divine Eye Art to study his father’s expression closely.

The emotions that appeared on his father’s face were joy and gladness. Was it because he had been away for so long? Or was there some other reason? He was truly happy that his son had returned.

It was an expression he would never be able to see up close, so Geom Mugeuk stood there for a moment and savored it.

“Father!”

After calling loudly to his father from the entrance, Geom Mugeuk walked along the Path of Blood.

That distinctive scent of the Heavenly Demon Hall, the soft feel of the carpet beneath his feet, the statues of evil spirits positioned here and there—he had missed them all.

‘I’ve returned!’

As Geom Mugeuk walked the Path of Blood, he felt an incomparably comfortable sense of relief. It was the feeling of coming home after a long journey.

No, it felt like returning home in glory. Since this was his first time entering the Heavenly Demon Hall after attaining Great Completion in the Nine Hwa Demonic Art, this moment was exceptionally precious.

He went beneath the grand chair and stood below his father. Instead of greeting him, Geom Mugeuk expressed his gratitude.

“Thank you, Father!”

For always guarding that seat in the same unchanging manner.

At his father’s gaze, which seemed to ask why he was thanking him, Geom Mugeuk smiled and said,

“For not invading the Murim Alliance while I was gone.”

A sneer formed on his father’s lips.

Only after seeing that smile did Geom Mugeuk offer a proper greeting.

“I have safely descended the mountain, Father.”

Because the time he had spent alone and silent in the mountains had been far too long, Geom Mugeuk could not help but joke.

“Isn’t the cult running too well even without me? When I came in, everyone had this expression: Ah, that’s right. Our Young Cult Leader was away, wasn’t he?”

Geom Ujin, who had been quietly looking down at his son, rose from his seat.

He slowly descended the steps and stood before Geom Mugeuk. He recognized his son’s achievement.

“You attained Great Completion.”

“Ah! As expected, Father can tell at a glance. I was trying to conceal my aura as much as possible and surprise you.”

Unexpectedly, Geom Ujin shook his head.

“I did not say it because I saw through it.”

“You didn’t know?”

An astonishing statement flowed from Geom Ujin’s mouth.

“Who could see through the Nine Hwa Demonic Art after it has reached Great Completion?”

“!”

Geom Mugeuk asked in surprise,

“Did you not attain Twelfth-Star Great Completion, Father?”

“Even so, it is still the same Great Completion, is it not?”

From those words, he understood. That was how special the Great Completion of the Nine Hwa Demonic Art was. The moment one attained Great Completion, one entered an entirely different realm.

Only now did it truly feel real. That he had genuinely attained Great Completion in the Nine Hwa Demonic Art.

“Then how did you know?”

After a brief pause, Geom Ujin answered.

“Because you would not have come down before attaining Great Completion.”

Geom Mugeuk silently gazed at his father.

There was an indescribable joy that came when someone recognized one’s true worth.

Just like now.

“I nearly never came down at all. I almost ended up doing my older brother a favor.”

Yes, his father had seen him accurately. If he had not attained Great Completion, he had intended not to come down.

“What took me seven years, you accomplished in a little over one.”

Seven years was the time it had taken Geom Ujin to go from the ninth stage to Great Completion. Even though his son was outstanding, he had thought it would take five years. This time too, his son had surpassed his expectations.

“Among all past cult leaders, you have accomplished the feat of attaining Great Completion in the Nine Hwa Demonic Art the fastest.”

And he had done it not even as cult leader, but while still in the position of Young Cult Leader.

At that moment, Geom Mugeuk thought to himself,

‘Perhaps it may have taken the longest among all past cult leaders.’

Geom Mugeuk smiled brightly and said,

“It is thanks to you, Father.”

“Thanks to me?”

When his father looked at him with a gaze asking why, Geom Mugeuk set down the leather pack he had been carrying.

“No matter how much I think about it, I believe this played a great role in my attaining Great Completion.”

Geom Mugeuk set down the go board wrapped in tiger hide that he had fixed atop the leather pack. From the pack, he also took out the bowls containing the go stones.

“It is a go board I made to give to you, Father. The stones are here as well.”

When Geom Ujin extended his hand, the heavy go board whooshed up into the air.

What Geom Ujin examined closely were the lines drawn across the board. Lines drawn without even a hair’s breadth of error.

“Did you draw them in one stroke?”

“Yes.”

Geom Ujin nodded with a satisfied expression. As if, just by looking at the drawn lines, he could tell from what realm and with what state of mind they had been made.

This time, the go bowls opened on their own, and the stones inside all rose into the air at once.

Clatter-rattle.

Every go stone moved separately. Geom Ujin was displaying the divine might of moving each of them through void manipulation.

“Ah, this gift is ruined. Father, you don’t even need a go board!”

He meant that his father could simply use the air itself as a go board through void manipulation.

As if to say that was not so, Geom Ujin said,

“Go is about the feel of it in the hand.”

Then he examined the go stones floating in the air. They were all the same size and thickness. They looked as though one could believe they had truly been made by a master artisan of go stones.

“Did you make them yourself?”

“I carved every single one with the Black Demon Sword. From now on, please call me the filial son of Daecheon Mountain who carves go stones!”

One corner of Geom Ujin’s lips rose, but the finished product was indeed worthy of pride.

“You made them well.”

They were go stones into which so much care had gone that even if one deliberately wanted to find a flaw, it would be difficult.

Clatter-rattle.

The go stones floating in the air all entered the bowls.

“But why do you say it is thanks to me?”

“Coincidentally, I attained Great Completion in the Nine Hwa Demonic Art on the day I completed the final go stone. I think the practice of finely controlling sword qi and astral qi must have helped. I had never trained in such a way before.”

Geom Ujin silently nodded.

Geom Mugeuk stared intently at his father and said,

“If you had not told me to play a game of go with you when I came down, I would never have made the go board and stones. Then perhaps I would still be training on Daecheon Mountain. Did you perhaps break that torreya tree knowing this would happen?”

“How is that thanks to me? It is thanks to your decision and effort to carve the go stones.”

From his father’s expression and words, he could not tell whether that was truly the case or not.

“Since you have attained Great Completion in the Nine Hwa Demonic Art, there is one thing you must uphold.”

With such great power came immense responsibility. Added to that were the pride of his father and all the Heavenly Demons of the past.

“From now on, you must not lose to anyone.”

Geom Mugeuk promised his father.

“I will never lose.”

Geom Ujin turned around.

The go board with the bowls placed upon it floated through the air, following his father.

“Come with me.”

Geom Mugeuk walked after his father.

Naturally, he thought they were going to his quarters to play go. But his father brought him to the training ground reserved exclusively for the cult leader.

Geom Ujin set the go board down in the middle of the training ground.

“We are playing here?”

When Geom Ujin nodded, Geom Mugeuk looked around. Looking at the various weapons in the training ground, Geom Mugeuk said,

“You mean to crush my spirit with the atmosphere, don’t you? I am no longer that easily handled son of the past. Now I am a son who has a strategist skilled at go.”

Of course, that was not why his father had brought him here. He surely had something in mind.

The two sat facing each other across the go board.

Holding black, Geom Mugeuk placed the first stone.

Tak.

A clear sound spread out. The feeling of placing the go stone was good, and the sound was truly pleasant as well.

“You are right. Go is about the feel in the hand!”

Next, his father made a move.

Tak.

A pure, clean resonance rang out. The moment he heard that pleasing sound, he felt as though all the hardship he had endured while carving the stones had been rewarded.

With crisp sounds, move after move was exchanged. Since it had been so long since he had played go with his father, he felt very good, but today Geom Mugeuk’s mind was elsewhere.

Around the time the opening formation ended, Geom Mugeuk casually asked,

“There is something I wish to ask you about the Heavenly Demon Soul.”

Geom Ujin looked down at the go board and nodded.

“Have you ever conversed with the Heavenly Demon Soul?”

Geom Ujin shook his head. Well, even if it were possible, given his father’s personality, he was not someone who would converse with the Heavenly Demon Soul.

This time, Geom Ujin abruptly asked,

“Do you wish to chatter even with the Heavenly Demon Soul?”

Geom Mugeuk smiled and admitted it.

“Are you not curious? It is something created from within my heart, so what might it be thinking?”

For a moment, Geom Ujin’s hand, which had been about to place a stone, stopped in midair.

“You are curious about that?”

“Are you not, Father?”

“Not at all.”

Several more moves passed.

As he set down a go stone, Geom Ujin told him one fact.

“It remains in the records that there was once a Heavenly Demon who conversed with the Heavenly Demon Soul. It was a very long time ago.”

As expected! The Heavenly Demon Soul was definitely a being with its own will.

When joy flickered across his son’s face, Geom Ujin warned him.

“The Heavenly Demon Soul is a dangerous existence. If it were to run wild, it could not be stopped. Do you still wish to chatter with it?”

After a brief pause, Geom Mugeuk answered.

“Yes. If it is such an existence, then I should become even closer to it.”

Geom Ujin silently nodded.

“Yes, I thought you would say that.”

Geom Mugeuk gazed at his father and asked,

“Would you like to see my Heavenly Demon Soul once?”

He had thought perhaps that was why his father had brought him to this training ground.

“It likely will not descend before me.”

Geom Mugeuk looked at his father in surprise.

“May I check?”

Geom Ujin nodded.

Geom Mugeuk called forth the Heavenly Demon Soul. Astonishingly, just as his father had said, the Heavenly Demon Soul did not descend.

“How did you know?”

“It simply felt that way.”

Geom Ujin’s gaze deepened as he spoke of the Heavenly Demon Soul.

“The Heavenly Demon Soul is an existence that refuses to lose to anyone.”

“Ah! Then you mean it does not appear because it cannot defeat you, Father.”

As if to say that was not it, Geom Ujin shook his head.

“The Heavenly Demon Soul does not fear humans.”

“Then it must fear your Heavenly Demon Soul, Father.”

He had naturally assumed that would be the case.

“That remains unknown.”

If it had been any other Heavenly Demon Soul, he would surely have said that was the reason. But this was the son he had watched until now, the son who had attained Great Completion in this brief time.

“Because it is your Heavenly Demon Soul.”

Meaning, he could not easily determine the reason.

After saying that, Geom Ujin placed a decisive move.

Tak.

“I won.”

“Ah! When did my large group get captured?”

“It was not captured by me, but by your Heavenly Demon Soul.”

Understanding that this meant he had not devoted all his attention to the game but had instead focused on the Heavenly Demon Soul, Geom Mugeuk said,

“I apologize.”

His father answered with an expression that said it was fine.

“It is all right. I was the same.”

The reason Geom Ujin had brought him to the training ground that only he could enter was not to play go, nor to see Geom Mugeuk’s Heavenly Demon Soul.

It was precisely for this reason.

“From now on, I will transmit the seventh form of the Nine Hwa Demonic Art.”

“!”

The Seventh Form of the Nine Hwa Demonic Art: Demon Soul Genesis.

From the seventh form to the ninth were martial arts used by the Heavenly Demon Soul. Only after mastering all three techniques could one be said to have perfectly learned the Nine Hwa Demonic Art.

“Can I learn the seventh form even though the Heavenly Demon Soul has not manifested?”

“You are the one who learns the seventh form.”

Geom Ujin told him a new fact regarding the martial arts of the Heavenly Demon Soul.

“The Heavenly Demon Soul is connected to you. The power of the martial arts it displays is determined by how well you understand the formula.”

“So the stronger I become, the stronger the Heavenly Demon Soul becomes as well.”

Geom Ujin nodded.

“Now, listen carefully to what I say from this point on.”

Geom Ujin began transmitting the formula to him.

“When the demon soul descends and devours chaos, a new world shall arrive. The beginning of true qi starts at the Shimen acupoint. The true qi that turns through the great meridian and emerges must be swift as lightning…”

Geom Mugeuk first memorized the formula his father recited, repeating it again and again. Of all the martial arts formulas he had learned until now, it was the most difficult.

Geom Mugeuk could understand the reason. The martial system used by the Heavenly Demon Soul differed greatly from that used by humans, making the formula far more difficult to comprehend.

Whenever there was a part he did not understand, he asked his father then and there.

His father explained it to him sincerely. In truth, even at this moment, Geom Mugeuk was gaining a fortuitous encounter.

Originally, the formula would have been transmitted by his father when he was at Tenth-Star Great Completion, but now his father, who had attained Twelfth-Star Great Completion, was teaching him.

Since the level of enlightenment differed, the teaching was also different. Some parts were so advanced that they were instead difficult to accept, but who was the one learning?

If Geom Mugeuk did not understand even a little, he immediately asked his father. He spoke what he thought, his father interpreted it again, and Geom Mugeuk asked yet another question from there.

Though his father had taught him several times before, the higher the martial learning of the two became, the more indescribably precious the realizations they gained were becoming.

It is said that a good disciple makes a good teacher, and ultimately, today’s lesson became the highest-level teaching in history regarding the transmission of the final three forms.

Thus, after several hours, the transmission of the seventh form came to an end.

Geom Mugeuk, who had closed his eyes and organized the technique, slowly opened them.

“Did you understand?”

“Yes, I think I have understood it to some extent.”

Together with his words of thanks, Geom Mugeuk bowed to his father. It was truly such a great teaching that it deserved a deep bow.

“The Heavenly Demon Soul will be pleased.”

Geom Mugeuk was curious. What difference would there be in the Heavenly Demon Soul before and after learning the seventh form? How would the Heavenly Demon Soul react to his learning of the seventh form? Would it be grateful? Or would it remain as brusque as ever?

“When you have fully mastered the seventh form, I will transmit the eighth to you.”

Geom Ujin, who had begun walking out of the training ground first, stopped for a moment. And then he said the words he had not said earlier.

“Congratulations on attaining Great Completion.”

It was his father’s congratulation, given without forgetting.

Chapter 729: The Young Cult Leader Woke Him Up
----------------------------------------------
“Careful, careful. There’s a stone there. Watch your step.”

Seo Daeryong, who had been walking beside Dana, was anxiously scanning the ground.

“I’m fine! Anyone watching would think I’m crossing through a formation.”

Dana’s stomach was round and protruding. Pregnant, she was two or three months away from giving birth.

“It must be hard. Let me carry you.”

Seo Daeryong was not one to care about other people’s gazes. As he truly crouched down in front of her to carry her, Dana massaged his shoulders and said,

“Walking and moving deliberately is healthy for both me and the baby. I’ll head back, so go on now.”

“Let’s go home together. I’ll see you off and then go.”

“I’m fine, so go quickly. Master is waiting.”

Dana had come out to see Seo Daeryong off to his training.

“Can you go back alone?”

“Why? Are you worried enemies are lying in ambush on the way home?”

Though Seo Daeryong was making a fuss over everything, he had never been as happy as he was these days. He had been joyful when they married, but hearing that she was pregnant had truly made him ecstatic. The moment he heard the news, he had briefly lost his mind.

I’m going to be a father?

There was truly no way to describe this feeling. It was a joy of an entirely different nature than being admitted to Huangcheon Pavilion as the top student or becoming Hyeolcheon Doma’s disciple. It was such an immense joy that often it didn’t even feel real.

Of course, along with the joy, his worries were as great as Mount Tai. The baby had to be born healthy. Dana had to be safe. The child had to grow up healthy. He was already bringing in all sorts of future worries upon himself.

“Wait a moment.”

Seo Daeryong carefully placed his hand on Dana’s belly as she tried to turn back.

“He’s quiet today.”

Dana looked at Seo Daeryong with a smile. Could she really be this happy?

“I’ll be going now.”

“Go carefully. Don’t fall.”

“I’m a martial artist too! It’s not like my internal energy disappears just because I’m pregnant!”

After seeing her off, Seo Daeryong arrived at Hyeolcheon Doma’s residence.

“I’m late, Master.”

His demeanor was different from when he had been with Dana. When it came to training, he worked harder than anyone.

He greeted Hyeolcheon Doma and was about to head out to the training yard.

“Take this with you when you go.”

“What is this?”

What Hyeolcheon Doma handed him was medicinal ingredients.

“Ma Ui sent someone.”

“Ma Ui?”

“He said it’s good for both the baby and the mother, and to make sure she takes it.”

“But why would Ma Ui send this to me?”

“How should I know?”

He wasn’t close enough with Ma Ui to receive such a thing.

Then, a welcome voice came from behind.

“Why do you think? A friend asked him for a favor.”

Surprised, Seo Daeryong turned around to see Geom Mugeuk standing there.

“Young Lord!”

Seo Daeryong ran to Geom Mugeuk.

Hyeolcheon Doma, standing by the window, wore an even more surprised expression than Seo Daeryong.

“That person is a friend of Ma Ui?”

“Ah!”

Seo Daeryong looked at his master with a surprised face. He was a disciple, yet he hadn’t even known that?

In other words, it was his master who had prepared the medicine.

Before Seo Daeryong could say thank you, Hyeolcheon Doma disappeared inside from the window.

Geom Mugeuk spoke on Hyeolcheon Doma’s behalf.

“Disciple, I specially asked Ma Ui to prepare it. Elder, you could take some credit for this!”

Seo Daeryong shouted with a face full of emotion.

“Thank you, Master!”

No answer came from inside.

Geom Mugeuk congratulated Seo Daeryong.

“You’re finally becoming a father.”

“The one who seemed least likely to marry, the ugliest one, the most pathetic one, I… I’m becoming a father.”

Just thinking about it choked Seo Daeryong up.

“This is all thanks to the Young Lord and Master.”

“I’ll give you a congratulatory gift later.”

“It’s not necessary. The Young Lord’s very existence is a gift.”

“Really? I was going to give you something very expensive and nice.”

“…….”

“…….”

“My child doesn’t yet know the Young Lord’s existence… so maybe a gift is needed after all.”

Geom Mugeuk laughed loudly, and Seo Daeryong laughed along.

Then, Hyeolcheon Doma’s voice came from inside.

“If you’re here, stop yapping and come in.”

As Geom Mugeuk went inside, he said to Seo Daeryong,

“You’ll have to pass on secret techniques later. On how to raise a child.”

“Just let the birth happen first!”

With that, Geom Mugeuk entered Hyeolcheon Doma’s room. The moment he entered, he took a deep breath and smelled the air.

“Ah, the smell of books! I’ve truly missed it!”

Hyeolcheon Doma asked with a still-surprised expression.

“Did you truly attain mastery?”

“No, it was difficult, so I just came down.”

Hyeolcheon Doma didn’t believe those words. Rather, his gaze toward Geom Mugeuk was full of certainty.

“You truly attained it.”

“Why do you think so?”

Hyeolcheon Doma’s thoughts were the same as his father’s.

“If you hadn’t attained mastery, you wouldn’t have come down.”

The playfulness vanished from Geom Mugeuk’s face. He respectfully cupped his hands and bowed at the waist.

“Thanks to you, Elder, showing me the right path, I have attained mastery and returned. Thank you truly.”

The gaze with which Hyeolcheon Doma looked at the bowing Geom Mugeuk also became exceedingly respectful.

Hyeolcheon Doma also cupped his hands and returned the courtesy.

“I offer my congratulations on your mastery of the Guhwamagong.”

This greeting was not for Geom Mugeuk but a show of respect for the Guhwamagong. One would show respect merely upon seeing the Guhwamagong unfold, let alone upon witnessing it reach grand completion.

After exchanging greetings, Hyeolcheon Doma looked at Geom Mugeuk in silence.

Geom Mugeuk had surprised him many times before, but he had never been as surprised as he was now.

Yes, he had believed Geom Mugeuk would naturally attain mastery, but he hadn’t known it would happen this quickly.

Even when he heard that mastery was looming before his eyes, he had thought it would take at least several years.

After all, even Geon Woojin, who was said to possess heaven-defying talent, had attained mastery much later in life.

“What do you plan to do now?”

In response to Hyeolcheon Doma’s question, Geom Mugeuk pulled a book from the bookshelf and sat on the bed, opening it.

“I’ve heard that the reason people who achieve great success fall is because they try to make drastic changes to their lives. One should change only a little, but they try to change too much at once.”

Geom Mugeuk looked up from the book at Hyeolcheon Doma.

“Nothing will have changed. Returning after departing the sect, I will still come to see you first, sit here and read books. I will learn martial arts from Master, spar with the Sword Venerable, and help the Poison King with his poison research.”

And with the power of this unchanging life, I will achieve the Grand Completion of the Twelve Stars.

* * *

Having returned to his residence, Geom Mugeuk unleashed the Space-Time Transference Art.

A vast plain unfolded before his eyes.

There, he summoned the Cheonma Spirit.

The Cheonma Spirit had not appeared before his father earlier, but now it manifested immediately.

‘Why did you not appear before my father?’

Looking into the Cheonma Spirit’s eyes, he could tell that at least it wasn’t because of fear. His father had guessed as much even without seeing the Cheonma Spirit.

Geom Mugeuk smiled broadly and explained the place to the Cheonma Spirit.

“This is a space I created using the Space-Time Transference Art. If we meet, it will mostly be here.”

There wouldn’t be many places in the outside world where the Cheonma Spirit could comfortably descend.

“So even if you don’t like it, don’t destroy it. It’s the space I value most.”

The Cheonma Spirit merely gazed at the world within the Space-Time Transference Art with heavy eyes.

“It’s fine. I’m well-trained at chatting away by myself in front of reticent people. If you were created from me, I don’t think you’d be reticent either. So it’s fine to show your true colors. I like talkative people—no, I like a talkative Cheonma Spirit.”

It would have been nice if it had at least smiled, but the Cheonma Spirit showed no reaction.

Then, Geom Mugeuk looked at the Cheonma Spirit’s waist.

“You! You’ve changed?”

Geom Mugeuk could tell the Cheonma Spirit’s appearance had changed. It was wearing a sword at its waist. And surprisingly, that sword resembled the Black Demon Sword in shape.

This was a change that occurred upon mastering the seventh form of the Guhwamagong.

And there was one more change.

Upon closer inspection, a faint pattern was engraved on the Cheonma Spirit’s chest, where there had been none before. Its body was as dark as pitch-black ink, so one had to look closely to confirm the faint pattern, and its exact shape was unclear.

He supposed he would only see the Cheonma Spirit’s final form after mastering all nine forms.

Whoosh.

Geom Mugeuk flew up to the Cheonma Spirit’s face. Thus, he met eyes with the Cheonma Spirit.

The gaze and demeanor of Geom Mugeuk floating in the air changed. No longer the bright and talkative self from moments ago, but the calm and silent self from before his regression. That gaze filled with deep loneliness conveyed his sincerity.

“The reason I called you today is that I wanted to congratulate you on learning your first martial art. Being congratulated felt quite good.”

Geom Mugeuk spoke calmly to the Cheonma Spirit.

“Congratulations.”

Then, Geom Mugeuk saw it. A flicker in the Cheonma Spirit’s dark, deep pupils. Of course it understood his words.

‘It definitely feels emotions too!’

* * *

An old man sat in the darkness.

His waist was ramrod straight, and his hands were gathered before his dantian. His two eyes gleaming in the darkness dominated the dark.

Srrrk.

Then, a black circle appeared on the floor before the old man, and a person clad in black garments rose up.

The person in black prostrated before the old man and reported,

“The Young Lord has returned to the sect.”

The old man’s two eyes blazed intensely in the darkness.

“He has finally returned!”

It was as if he had been waiting for Geom Mugeuk to descend from Daecheon Mountain.

“Tell them to proceed.”

“Yes.”

The moment the old man’s command fell.

“Please withdraw that command for a moment.”

The person who revealed herself there was none other than Gaye, the Saintess Palace Lord. She was the one who had previously informed the old man about the appearance of a bead filled with red energy in the world. In that place where everything was black, she wore pure white martial robes.

“What business do you have?”

“Please come with me for a moment.”

She was not one to seek him out unless it was urgent.

The old man rose from his seat. The person in black disappeared back into the floor.

As the old man moved, an astonishing thing occurred. The darkness surrounding him moved along with him as if it were a living creature.

This was not simply dark energy emanating from his body. True darkness was following him.

Ssssss.

The darkness shifted into various forms. It floated along covering the old man’s body like a cloud, and changed shape to make him look like an evil ghost.

Then, as if light were descending from the sky, a single thread of darkness fell from the ceiling and covered him from head to toe.

That darkness was alive as if it possessed life.

At the end of the corridor where the two arrived, above the door was a pattern of an astrolabe and six beads.

Entering, it was the place where the two had conversed before.

In the corner sat a large astrolabe, and the ceiling was covered with constellations—precisely that place.

“What is the matter?”

Gaye looked up at the ceiling with trembling eyes.

“Please look there.”

The old man looked up. At that moment, fierce eye light burst forth from the darkness.

Among the stars painted on the ceiling, fire had ignited in the largest star at the center.

There was only one thing this meant.

“He has awakened.”

“!”

A silence of shock and astonishment flowed. Something truly unimaginable had occurred.

“He awakened on his own?”

“That is correct.”

“That is impossible!”

At the angry old man’s momentum, the darkness surrounding him scattered in all directions as if fleeing. Thus, the old man’s full form was revealed.

The traces of years upon his face. Wrinkles layered like the rings of an ancient tree made it impossible to guess his age. His features were covered by wrinkles, unrecognizable.

All who saw him would feel this. How could someone so old sit so upright, how could he emit such intense eyes, and how could he produce a voice with such deep resonance?

He was the current Dark Palace Lord.

Dark Palace Lord Hwamugyeong (Hwa Mugyeong).

Hwamugyeong, who kept his composure in all matters, was flustered at this very moment.

“How is this possible?”

Then, a person’s name left Gaye’s mouth.

“The day the Young Lord descended the mountain, he awoke.”

“!”

Hwamugyeong’s eyes blazed intensely.

“What does that mean? Do you mean the Young Lord’s descent awoke him?”

Surprisingly, Gaye believed so.

“I believe the two are connected by a single fate.”

Therefore, she spoke with conviction.

“It was definitely the Young Lord who awoke him.”

When the plan using Biheolsa failed, Hwamugyeong had pondered thus:

Should he awaken him?

At that time, Gaye had dissuaded him, saying the martial arts world would become a sea of blood. Hwamugyeong knew that awakening him was truly the last resort. He also knew that there was a difference as vast as heaven and earth between being awakened by another and awakening oneself.

“Even if he awakens, he cannot break the seal of the place where he is imprisoned.”

It was precisely then.

Pop.

Fire ignited in one of the countless stars on the ceiling.

The two watched with startled eyes.

Pop.

Fire ignited in another nearby star.

Pop, pop!

This time, fire ignited in a star and immediately went out again. Then fire ignited in yet another star.

This star would brighten and go out, that star would brighten and go out. Just as this repeated several times,

Pop-pop-pop-pop-pop-pop.

A constellation was completed. One of the countless constellations completed itself and began to shine.

Both Hwamugyeong and Gaye knew well what this meant.

“He has begun to break the seal.”

Chapter 730: Shall We Do It Here?
---------------------------------
“How long will it take to undo the seal?”

At Hwa Mugyeong’s question, Gaye looked up at the stars on the ceiling. After one constellation had been completed, no more constellations had lit up.

“I cannot say. I have never heard of anyone breaking the seal of the Seventy-Two Stars Soul-Forbidding Formation by their own power.”

If the person trapped inside had been anyone other than him, she would have answered that it was absolutely impossible. But if it was the man who had awakened on his own within this seal?

“At the earliest, it may take a few months. At the latest, it may take years. You must prepare countermeasures at once.”

Fear could be heard in her voice. The fear that, once he awakened, they would all die had ruled over her for a very long time.

Hwa Mugyeong, on the other hand, was calm. Earlier, he had been shocked and disbelieving at the fact that he had awakened, but if it was certain that he had, then what needed to be done was already decided.

“We do not have much time.”

And this was what had to be done in that allotted time.

“We have one final chance. If we can recover the Six Paths Primordial Qi before he breaks the seal, we should be able to control him.”

Hwa Mugyeong immediately called someone.

“Hui!”

Then a black circle appeared on the floor, and the black-clad man from before revealed himself once more.

“Go and deliver the message. Tell them they must recover the energy at all costs.”

“I obey!”

The black-clad man vanished into the floor.

Gaye looked up at the ceiling again. In her mind, she saw every constellation on that ceiling blazing with light.

“I am afraid.”

At her honest words, Hwa Mugyeong slowly moved his feet and walked toward the secret coffer set in the corner.

He slowly raised his hand and stroked the coffer. Even the back of his withered hand was deeply lined with wrinkles.

Deep remorse filled the eyes with which he gazed at the coffer. He seemed to have many things he wanted to say, but as he had always done until now, that remorse did not come out through his mouth and instead seeped into the folds of his deep wrinkles.

“I like that honesty of yours. When you are startled, you show it. When you are afraid, you say you are afraid. You do not have the affectation of your predecessors.”

The darkness that had scattered in fright gathered once more around Hwa Mugyeong.

As if it knew its master’s mood, the darkness did not play about as it had before. It became an exceedingly deep, cold darkness and settled around him.

“Yes. We should do it while trembling in fear. This is the matter of taking possession of the murim, after all.”

* * *

Geom Mugeuk was looking up at the signboard.

Eunwol.

Whenever he saw this signboard, only one emotion came to him.

Apology.

Because he knew better than anyone how hard Gowol had worked for a building bearing this signboard to stand in the main headquarters of the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult.

That was why Cult Leader Pungcheon’s nagging about matters related to Gowol was more welcome than anything else. It always reminded him not to forget Gowol’s contributions.
