Geom Mugeuk entered the building. There were no warriors guarding the entrance.

Instead, mechanisms of the highest caliber had been installed on both walls of the corridor. When he had introduced this place to Gowol before, they had not yet been functioning, but now they were in operation.

After passing through the long corridor where those mechanisms had been installed, the operations room finally appeared.

“Welcome, Young Cult Leader.”

Gowol greeted Geom Mugeuk respectfully.

“You look better than when I last saw you.”

“Is there not someone who looks after my health even more diligently than I do?”

He meant that Cult Leader Pungcheon was taking good care of him.

“How has Cult Leader Pungcheon been lately?”

“He has been busy trying to lose belly fat these days. Every day, he pesters me to tell him at once if there is any information about martial arts for losing belly fat. He says even demonic arts would be fine.”

Knowing that he truly would have said that, Geom Mugeuk laughed.

“Now, please take a look around.”

The inside of the operations room was bustling with activity.

Information arriving from all over the Central Plains was coming in through dozens of holes in the walls, and on one side it was being sorted again.

It truly felt like a miniature Tongcheongak.

“There is somewhere I need to inspect.”

Geom Mugeuk opened the door inside Gowol’s office. It was a space where only a single bed had been placed so that Gowol could rest.

“I knew it! I knew this would happen.”

Books and documents were piled up beside the bed.

Flustered, Gowol made an excuse.

“They are things I look at when I cannot sleep.”

“At times like that, just keep your eyes closed.”

Geom Mugeuk brought out all the documents that had been there.

“From now on, this is an order.”

“Yes! I will be sure to obey.”

Whose order would he dare disobey?

After looking around Eunwol like that, Gowol showed him the materials he had prepared in advance.

“These are the major incidents that occurred in the murim while you were at Daecheonsan.”

Geom Mugeuk slowly read through the materials Gowol had organized.

The hidden forces had even used the energy of Jeokjeong in an attempt to capture him, but they had failed.

They were surely plotting some new conspiracy, so there had to be something among these incidents connected to them.

“Are there any incidents worth paying special attention to lately?”

“Yes. There is one matter that has drawn attention recently.”

“What is it?”

“Have you perhaps heard of the Sword Tomb?”

Geom Mugeuk nodded. In the murim of the present age, there was only one meaningful Sword Tomb.

“Isn’t it the place where the masters of the previous generation gathered to fight and, in the end, all died?”

“That is correct. It became famous because it was said that the reason those masters fought was over treasures and artifacts so valuable that risking one’s life for them was not considered a waste.”

In his life before his regression, Geom Mugeuk had also entered that place. Of course, it had not been because of the treasures of the Sword Tomb, but because he had been searching for someone who had gone inside.

“Recently, rumors have spread that a treasure map marked with the location of the Sword Tomb has been discovered, and the movements of the murim have become unusual. There has already been a succession of people dying while fighting over it.”

Geom Mugeuk’s eyes sharpened.

The timing of the Sword Tomb’s appearance was wrong. The Sword Tomb had emerged much later than now and brought a bloody storm upon the murim.

‘The Sword Tomb is opening now?’

Gowol read the gravity in Geom Mugeuk’s expression.

“Is something troubling you?”

The timing of such a major incident had been moved far ahead.

It might be related to the forces behind the scenes, and even if it was not, it was certainly an incident that deserved attention.

“Investigate this under the assumption that it is connected to them.”

“Yes, understood.”

Gowol did not question him. In handling information until now, he too had experienced it. He knew how many different methods those people used to weave their conspiracies.

“Now, distribute these to the strategists.”

Geom Mugeuk took a stack of envelopes from his robes. Inside were promissory notes worth a fairly large sum.

“Since ancient times, nothing has raised morale like this.”

After saying it loudly enough for everyone to hear, Geom Mugeuk raised the envelopes high, and the strategists who had been working broke into bright smiles.

“It is said that a person’s lifespan decreases in proportion to the time he spends sitting. Everyone, stand up often and sleep well.”

After bidding farewell to the strategists of Eunwol in that way, Geom Mugeuk left the place.

Gowol followed him out to the entrance of Eunwol.

“Thank you truly for your concern.”

Before leaving, Geom Mugeuk gave Gowol a separate reminder. Gowol was always too devoted to his work.

“Your health is the most important thing. If your health deteriorates, the tens of thousands of pieces of information gathered here will become worth less than this one piece of information known to everyone in the world.”

Geom Mugeuk emphasized that one thing.

“Health is the most important.”

* * *

After leaving Eunwol, Geom Mugeuk went to the residence of the Ilhwa Sword Venerable.

“Sword Venerable.”

The door of the thatched hut opened, and the Ilhwa Sword Venerable came out.

“Young Cult Leader, welcome.”

The Ilhwa Sword Venerable greeted Geom Mugeuk warmly.

“It has been a long time since I saw you, yet our Sword Venerable’s beauty remains unchanged.”

“It feels very good to hear your flattery after so long.”

Now that she no longer attached meaning to her appearance at all, she could speak of it more comfortably instead.

“Come often and say such things to me, even if they are empty words.”

“Of course. I will come every day and say them for you. And they are not empty words!”

The two looked at each other and laughed.

“You must check how diligently I trained before coming down.”

At that, the Ilhwa Sword Venerable made an unexpected suggestion.

“Shall we do it here?”

She meant that they should hold their sparring match here in the yard, not at the training ground.

Geom Mugeuk looked around.

Rows of flowerpots with blooming flowers stood there, and there were several jars filled with food. On the clothesline stretched long across the center of the yard, the Sword Venerable’s pure white clothes swayed in the wind.

In other words, she meant that they should spar without breaking anything. It meant they would not conduct the kind of sparring they had done until now, but a sparring match of an even higher level.

“Very well.”

Naturally, Geom Mugeuk readily accepted.

The two drew their swords at the same time.

From the way he held his sword alone, the Ilhwa Sword Venerable could tell what the results of his training at Daecheonsan had been.

With their swords drawn, the two slowly moved their feet.

Just then, as they moved with the clothes hanging on the line between them, the wind blew and the garments fluttered.

Swish!

Geom Mugeuk’s sword flew in through the fluttering clothes.

Clang!

The Ilhwa Sword Venerable swiftly swung the Ilhwa Sword and struck his blade away.

It had clearly been a sword that flew in without any inner force, yet it was heavy. Truly heavy.

‘The momentum carried by his sword has changed.’

Swish!

This time, the Ilhwa Sword Venerable’s sword flew in, brushing past the clothesline.

Clang!

Geom Mugeuk likewise could tell that the momentum in the Ilhwa Sword Venerable’s sword could not be compared to before.

Clang! Claang!

The Black Demon Sword and the Ilhwa Sword swiftly moved back and forth among the laundry.

Their swords collided with perilous force, but neither the clothes nor the clothesline were torn or severed. The white garments merely fluttered in the sword wind.

The two moved again, exchanging sword blows.

Though it was not a sparring match where they poured everything out and pressed forward as before, it was far more tense and dangerous.

Their swords came and went in front of the pile of firewood stacked in the corner.

A log that had not yet been split flew up into the air.

The two swords flew in, each aiming for the other with the log between them.

Thudududuk.

The log fell as firewood. This was not a game in which they attacked together and made firewood.

Making firewood was an obvious condition both understood without needing to say it, and upon that condition, truly perilous moves came and went. It was, in every sense, a feast of swordsmanship unfolding within controlled movements.

They kicked off from the flowers in the pots and flew up, but not a single petal fell, and when they landed on the roof, there was not even the sound of a footstep.

Without using any proprietary sword arts, they sparred only with basic swordsmanship, but this sparring match was far more dangerous than a fight in which sword qi and sword aura ran rampant.

Whoosh.

After completing a clash that circled the yard in this way, the two returned to their original positions.

The objects around them were no different from before the sparring match. Nothing was broken, and there was not even the faintest mark of a sword grazing anything.

“Thank you truly for your instruction.”

At Geom Mugeuk’s respectful greeting, the Ilhwa Sword Venerable also cupped her fist with courtesy.

“Congratulations on the great completion of the Nine Flowers Demonic Art.”

She too first showed respect not to Geom Mugeuk, but to the Nine Flowers Demonic Art.

“How did you know?”

Her realization that he had achieved great completion was different from Geom Ujin’s or the Blood Heaven Blade Demon’s.

“In your sword before, I could feel that you were hiding your ability. There was a sense that you were trying to protect me.”

Even as she said such words, the Ilhwa Sword Venerable was surprised by her own heart, which did not feel wounded in its pride.

“But just now, I felt none of that. It felt as if you were doing your utmost.”

“Then would that not mean my skill has instead decreased?”

“No. It means you have now reached a realm where you can fight with all your strength and still not harm me.”

This time, the Ilhwa Sword Venerable cupped her fist while holding her sword.

“It was a sparring match for which I am deeply grateful.”

She had an intuition that this sparring match had greatly affected her and would be of great help in advancing to the next realm.

And she was amazed. To think she would witness the Nine Flowers Demonic Art brought to great completion twice in one generation. When had it been that she had seen the Cult Leader smile after reaching great completion?

“I, too, learned much. Thank you, Sword Venerable.”

After finishing their sparring match, the two sat side by side on the small wooden platform in the yard and drank tea.

“By the way, I intend to leave the cult tomorrow.”

“Where are you going?”

At that, a name he had already heard once today flowed from her lips.

“They say the Sword Tomb has appeared in the murim.”

Geom Mugeuk turned to look at the Ilhwa Sword Venerable. She was looking at her own teacup.

“As someone who uses a sword, should I not go and take a look?”

She would not be going because she coveted the treasures or artifacts said to be there.

She did not seem like someone who would get involved in this kind of matter.

Geom Mugeuk asked casually.

“Would it be all right if I went with you?”

The Ilhwa Sword Venerable looked surprised.

“You?”

“I wish to go with you, Sword Venerable. Am I not also someone who uses a sword?”

The Ilhwa Sword Venerable nodded readily.

“I would like that. Rather, it is something I would want to ask of you. But you have only just come down from Daecheonsan. Will you be all right?”

“Did I not confirm it this time? To my frustration, the cult runs far too well even without me.”

At those words, the Ilhwa Sword Venerable laughed pleasantly.

“Then I will see you at dawn tomorrow.”

* * *

At dawn the next day, Geom Mugeuk and the Ilhwa Sword Venerable met at the main gate of the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult. Geom Mugeuk was carrying a leather pack stuffed full with luggage.

Who in the world would believe it? That the Young Cult Leader of the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult would leave the cult carrying so much baggage like this. He was someone who, if he went out with a single sword, could obtain anything in the world.

“Let us go.”

“Please wait a moment.”

Geom Mugeuk bowed his head deeply to the Ilhwa Sword Venerable.

“I will apologize in advance.”

“Why? Are you unable to go with me?”

The Ilhwa Sword Venerable thought Geom Mugeuk had to go somewhere else. That was why he had so much baggage.

“No. It is the opposite. This time, I caused the trouble first, and I am apologizing now.”

Just as she looked at him as if to ask what he meant, the trouble Geom Mugeuk had mentioned came walking toward them.

Walking over with a sullen face and grumbling, carrying a huge greatsaber on his back, was the Blood Heaven Blade Demon.

“Where in the world are you dragging me at this hour of dawn?”

Chapter 731: The Heavens Gave Everything to the Sword Venerable
---------------------------------------------------------------
“Why is that person here?”

When Hyeolcheon Doma looked at Ilhwa Geomjon in surprise, the reaction was mirrored right back at him.

“That sounds like a question I should be asking.”

Her words implied that she, too, had not expected to meet Hyeolcheon Doma here, so naturally, Hyeolcheon Doma’s gaze turned to Geom Mugeuk.

“I intend to depart with the Geomjon-nim, and I wish to bring the Elder along as well.”

Hyeolcheon Doma was taken aback. He had not expected to be called upon in this situation.

“Then you should have said something beforehand.”

“I was afraid you would not come. And would it not be dangerous if just the two of us went out?”

Hyeolcheon Doma asked back, as if dumbfounded.

“Who is in danger? The Gangho? This Murim?”

As he spoke, Hyeolcheon Doma stole a glance at Ilhwa Geomjon. Ilhwa Geomjon simply stood there without a word. With her reacting like that, how could anyone ask him to come along?

“No!”

Hyeolcheon Doma refused and turned away.

“Ah, then I suppose we shall have no choice but to go by ourselves.”

At Geom Mugeuk’s disappointed words, Hyeolcheon Doma’s back flinched.

How could Geom Mugeuk not know his heart? The heart that said, *I’ll pretend to give in and follow along, so hurry and stop me.*

If he truly hated this situation, Hyeolcheon Doma would have already flown off and disappeared by now. Because Geom Mugeuk knew better than anyone that Hyeolcheon Doma wanted to go together, he had stirred up this trouble without telling Ilhwa Geomjon beforehand.

Right as Geom Mugeuk was about to call out to him,

“Let’s go together.”

The one who spoke was, surprisingly, Ilhwa Geomjon.

Hyeolcheon Doma turned around with a surprised expression. Geom Mugeuk also looked at Ilhwa Geomjon in surprise. He had thought she would be angry at being called out without a word.

“Do you hate it?”

At Ilhwa Geomjon’s question, Hyeolcheon Doma flinched and answered.

“It’s not that I hate it.”

As if there was nothing more to hear, Ilhwa Geomjon turned around and walked toward the carriage.

Geom Mugeuk knew that the two of them had been getting along well recently. The fact that Hyeolcheon Doma’s flower garden was still kept in good condition was proof of that. That was precisely why he had asked them to go together.

Hyeolcheon Doma asked Geom Mugeuk,

“Where are we going?”

“Have you heard of the Geomchong?”

“It’s been noisy lately. Why there?”

As expected, it was clear Hyeolcheon Doma was not someone who merely spent his days reading. He was well aware of the affairs of the murim.

Then Ilhwa Geomjon, who had boarded the carriage first, called out to the two.

“Are you not coming?”

The two hurriedly boarded the carriage as well.

Geom Mugeuk loaded the luggage onto the carriage and took the driver’s seat. Whether it was awkward or not, he intended to drive the carriage himself so they could have as much time alone together as possible.

“Now, let us depart!”

It was the first time the three of them had set out together.

* * *

That evening, after resting the horses, Geom Mugeuk prepared the campsite.

He lit a campfire and made a place where the two Majon could rest.

There was nothing Geom Mugeuk’s massive leather pouch did not contain. What took up the most space was a fur pelt prepared so the two Majon could lie down and sleep comfortably. It was soft, plush, high-quality leather that Geom Mugeuk had specially obtained for this journey.

On the first day, they had dinner using food Geom Mugeuk had prepared in advance.

After the meal, Geom Mugeuk looked at the Myeolcheon Daedo and spoke.

“That saber of yours is too eye-catching.”

Geom Mugeuk took out a cloth from his leather pouch.

“How about wrapping it with this?”

He had prepared an incredibly worn cloth to make it look shabby.

“I cannot wrap my saber in such cloth!”

“That is why I shall wrap it.”

Hyeolcheon Doma could not insist until the very end. As Geom Mugeuk said, it was such an extraordinary blade that there might be someone who recognized the Myeolcheon Daedo. Since he could not cause trouble through such a matter, he eventually handed over the saber, pretending he had no choice.

Geom Mugeuk began wrapping the cloth around the Myeolcheon Daedo.

“Bear with it for a moment, even if it feels suffocating.”

At Geom Mugeuk’s words, Ilhwa Geomjon cut in.

“The saber endures well. It is only because someone with a short temper cannot endure.”

Geom Mugeuk said with a smile.

“The Elder does have a fiery temperament.”

“Your foul temper is exactly the same as it was back then. How is it you do not change at all?”

When Ilhwa Geomjon spoke ill of him, Hyeolcheon Doma replied gruffly.

“Who is one to talk? When it comes to temper…”

Hyeolcheon Doma had been about to say that she had quite the temper herself, but stopped. She had already drawn her sword with her eyes.

“In the end, you’ll be the one who cannot hold your temper and draws your sword first.”

“Me? That’s absurd!”

“I would even bet on it.”

“Let’s make that bet.”

The two stared at each other, then looked back at Geom Mugeuk.

“Yes, I shall serve as witness to this bet.”

Geom Mugeuk preferred to handle matters quietly, so it would be best if the two endured as much as possible.

“A bet must have something wagered. What will you stake?”

Since the two could not suddenly think of something, Geom Mugeuk decided for them.

“Granting each other a wish! How about that?”

One might have expected someone to ask how they could wager such a thing without knowing what the other wanted.

Yet the two Majon nodded without question.

After making the bet, Hyeolcheon Doma asked Geom Mugeuk,

“You think those fellows are involved in this Geomchong affair, don’t you?”

Ilhwa Geomjon, too, waited for Geom Mugeuk’s answer.

They had guessed that this was the reason Geom Mugeuk had asked them to come along. There must be a reason he was setting out like this so soon after descending from Daecheon Mountain.

Indeed, it was as expected.

“I believe it is worth investigating.”

Hyeolcheon Doma and Ilhwa Geomjon exchanged glances. The fact that the Little Cult Leader was moving like this meant there was definitely a reason. Therefore, this matter was no longer Ilhwa Geomjon’s personal affair alone.

“It is also true that I invited you, Elder, in hopes that you would protect us.”

“What strength does an old man have?”

“You are not old. Among the Majon, you are the most vigorous. I believe you are the youngest among the Majon. Your way of thinking is also the youngest.”

He was saying on Hyeolcheon Doma’s behalf what Hyeolcheon Doma had wanted to say to Ilhwa Geomjon. He intended to continue doing so throughout this journey. That was why he was going along. His goal was for Hyeolcheon Doma to return overflowing with confidence.

“There, it’s all done.”

Geom Mugeuk returned the wrapped saber. Wrapped in cloth, the Myeolcheon Daedo had become an ordinary great saber.

“Let us hope there is no need to unwrap that cloth.”

Receiving the saber, Hyeolcheon Doma lay down in his spot and spoke.

“With someone trailing such a bloody storm, how could things go as one wishes?”

* * *

The carriage continued running the next day as well.

The destination kept changing.

As battles for control over the Jangbyeongdo were continuing even now, its owner kept changing.

Geom Mugeuk did not hurry. He believed that if he simply followed the information coming in through Eunwol and Tongcheongak, he would eventually meet the final owner. If they were behind it, it would be even more certain. It would surely be a scheme meant to lure him out.

And today, Ilhwa Geomjon spoke unexpected words.

“I shall prepare dinner today.”

Geom Mugeuk asked with a surprised face.

“The Geomjon-nim will cook for us?”

“Why? Do you not trust me?”

“Not at all. Where could there be a greater honor? I shall boast of it to everyone I meet!”

Geom Mugeuk looked at Hyeolcheon Doma. It was a situation where he seemed about to say something, yet Hyeolcheon Doma wore a subtle expression.

Ilhwa Geomjon had even gone hunting. She had caught two rabbits herself after leaping off.

“I shall make rabbit porridge for you.”

“Oh, I look forward to it! I shall be trying this for the first time.”

While Ilhwa Geomjon prepared the rabbits, Geom Mugeuk washed and trimmed the vegetables and mushrooms she had asked him to gather.

Even the dagger meant for cooking was a black sword; Ilhwa Geomjon handled the ingredients with truly expert skill.

“Do you have a pot and seasonings?”

“Of course. Everything is prepared right here.”

Geom Mugeuk took out seasonings from his leather pouch and handed them to her.

Chak-chak-chak-chak!

The way she sprinkled seasonings was just like performing martial arts. It was so elegant that expectations reached their peak.

And so, the dinner prepared by Ilhwa Geomjon was completed.

“I shall eat well!”

The moment Geom Mugeuk took a single spoonful.

“!”

Geom Mugeuk’s eyes went wide.

It was not delicious. What on earth was this peculiar taste? Was it because his expectations had been too high? No. Objectively speaking, this was not delicious.

“How does it taste?”

At Ilhwa Geomjon’s expectant question, Geom Mugeuk forced a smile and said,

“It is delicious! Of all the dishes I have ever eaten, it is the most unique!”

He could not bring himself to say it was the most delicious.

Ilhwa Geomjon said with a smile,

“I made it for my demonic swordsmen subordinates once before. They all ate well, saying it was delicious.”

*That is because they are your subordinates! There are no insane subordinates who would say, “Geomjon-nim, this is not delicious!” That is something even the Little Cult Leader could not do!*

Ilhwa Geomjon also ate the porridge she had made. She ate with an expression that said it was not so bad.

*Ah! Heaven gave the Geomjon everything but stole away her sense of taste!*

Geom Mugeuk stole a glance at Hyeolcheon Doma.

Hyeolcheon Doma lifted his porridge bowl and drank it down.

“It tastes good.”

But Geom Mugeuk saw. The porridge in Hyeolcheon Doma’s bowl had not decreased at all.

*He intends to secretly eliminate that porridge using his internal energy!*

Now he understood. The truth behind that subtle expression Hyeolcheon Doma had made when she said she would cook.

*The Elder knew! He knew what kind of food would come out!*

Indeed, that was so.

Hyeolcheon Doma recalled the past.

“Your skill is truly exactly the same. It has not changed one bit.”

At those words, Ilhwa Geomjon made a surprised expression.

“You still remember that?”

She, too, still remembered the day she had cooked for Hyeolcheon Doma. That day from when they were so very young.

“How could I forget that taste?”

He had once tasted food she personally made long, long ago. Only once, but still.

“That dish was the first meal I ever cooked in my life.”

As if learning this fact for the first time, Hyeolcheon Doma’s eyes opened wide in surprise.

“That was your first time?”

Ilhwa Geomjon nodded.

“I had memorized a cookbook the night before.”

At those words, Hyeolcheon Doma’s pupils trembled slightly. The two locked eyes briefly before turning their gazes away again.

Geom Mugeuk subtly revealed his envy.

“It must be nice. Elder, you have tasted the Geomjon-nim’s first-ever cooking, have you not?”

Hyeolcheon Doma slurped down the porridge he held. Of course, only Geom Mugeuk, who was beside him, could see him furrowing his brows as though drinking the Poison King’s poison. Even so, Hyeolcheon Doma manfully finished it all.

Now Geom Mugeuk himself was the one who had to eliminate the remaining porridge with his internal energy.

Of course, he could not bring himself to do so, and Geom Mugeuk also emptied the remaining porridge.

Hyeolcheon Doma watched that sight with satisfaction.

The moment Geom Mugeuk finished eating, Ilhwa Geomjon spoke words like a thunderbolt from a clear sky.

“I shall cook again tomorrow. Tomorrow I shall catch ducks and make duck soup!”

Earthquakes struck simultaneously in the pupils of Geom Mugeuk and Hyeolcheon Doma.

Geom Mugeuk quickly stepped forward.

“No, from tomorrow on I shall serve you! Precious cooking must be eaten occasionally to retain its value.”

“Is that so?”

“Now that you have eaten, you must have a cup of tea.”

While Geom Mugeuk brewed tea, Hyeolcheon Doma washed the dishes. The two who had overcome the crisis smiled at each other in relief.

Shortly after, Ilhwa Geomjon was moved by the taste of the tea Geom Mugeuk had brewed.

“How did you know this is my favorite tea?”

“Ah, is it? I did not know. I had drunk this tea at the Elder’s residence long ago and remembered it because it was so delicious.”

At that, Ilhwa Geomjon looked at Hyeolcheon Doma.

“Do you remember this tea?”

At that, Hyeolcheon Doma nodded.

Geom Mugeuk asked Ilhwa Geomjon,

“Is there a story behind it?”

Ilhwa Geomjon merely wore a faint smile. He could tell from their expressions. That this was the tea Hyeolcheon Doma had given her as a gift long ago.

Though there had been a time when they had hated each other so, a few memories alone were packed tightly into both of their lives.

“The tea tastes good, and the mood is good as well. It was good to come out.”

After drinking their tea, they prepared to sleep.

Geom Mugeuk took out a thick book from his leather pouch and handed it to Hyeolcheon Doma.

“You did not prepare anything, not knowing you would come out this far, did you?”

“What is this?”

“I prepared it for you to read if you felt bored.”

It was Hyeolcheon Doma’s own favorite book. The book he read when his mind was troubled. Just like the cloth prepared in advance to wrap the saber, so too with the book. It seemed Geom Mugeuk had been determined to bring him along no matter what.

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon looked at Geom Mugeuk.

“You were certain I would go.”

“I was planning to persuade you somehow.”

With an expression that said there was no stopping him, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon placed the book atop the Heaven-Annihilating Great Saber and slowly began to read.

Geom Mugeuk leaned back and looked up at the night sky.

“Fine.”

The Ilhwa Sword Venerable was seated, circulating her qi and regulating her breath. Perhaps she was thinking pleasant thoughts, for her expression was gentle.

Toward dawn, Geom Mugeuk awoke from his sleep.

Beyond the extinguished campfire, he saw the backs of the Blood Heaven Blade Demon and the Ilhwa Sword Venerable sitting side by side, gazing up at the dawn sky.

The two of them said nothing, simply staring at the stars in silence. Though they did not speak, it felt as if countless conversations were passing between them.

Lying where he was, Geom Mugeuk also gazed up at the stars for a while. Then he closed his eyes again and fell into a deep sleep.

* * *

Geom Mugeuk and his party arrived near Tianzi Mountain in Hunan.

At the rumor that the person carrying the treasure map had been chased into hiding on Tianzi Mountain, heroes were gathering there.

The closer they drew to Tianzi Mountain, the more martial artists there were heading in that direction.

Some traveled alone, others in groups. The place was truly bustling with martial artists of every sort.

Geom Mugeuk left the carriage at a house below the mountain, took only their luggage, and headed up the mountain.

They did not hurry. Even if they rushed ahead now, they would only be swept up in the chaos.

In any case, Tongcheongak and Eunwol were relaying every development to them, so there was no need to rush.

As the three of them were slowly walking up the mountain path, someone shouted from behind.

“Move!”

“Don’t get in the way!”

When they turned around, two martial artists were rushing toward them with lightness skill, radiating a fierce aura. Their momentum as they sped forward was no ordinary thing. If anyone got in their way, they looked ready to simply ram through them.

They were speaking so rudely either because they were confident in their skills, because they were naturally foul-tempered, or perhaps because Geom Mugeuk, who was with the group, was carrying a great load of baggage and thus did not look like an expert.

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon shifted toward the path they would pass through, placing the Ilhwa Sword Venerable on the inside.

Seeing him worry that the Ilhwa Sword Venerable, of all people, might get hurt, Geom Mugeuk laughed. No, even if a hundred fellows like that came charging in, do you really think the Sword Venerable would be injured?

The Ilhwa Sword Venerable also looked at Geom Mugeuk with an incredulous expression, as if embarrassed for no reason, but a faint smile had formed at the corners of her lips.

As the two martial artists passed, one of them shouted at the Blood Heaven Blade Demon.

“Don’t block the road, old man! If I weren’t busy, you’d be dead!”

A vein bulged on the Blood Heaven Blade Demon’s forehead.

Beside him, the Ilhwa Sword Venerable whispered.

“You’re letting fellows like that off?”

At her provocation, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon’s expression relaxed. Soon after, words flowed from his mouth, carrying a will that said he would never lose the wager.

“He said he was busy, didn’t he?”

Chapter 732: How Dare a Mere Porter?
------------------------------------
“You’re a lucky fellow, surviving even after cursing at the elder.”

The Ilhwa Sword Venerable took up Geom Mugeuk’s words.

“The only one.”

Despite the two of them teasing him, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon remained steadfast. If anything, he even smiled benevolently as he said,

“When a person lives long enough, he may curse now and then.”

At those words, Geom Mugeuk burst into loud laughter. Perhaps the Blood Heaven Blade Demon found it funny himself after saying it, for he turned his head away. Most likely, he couldn’t keep his expression under control either.

“If only to see how this wager turns out, I too intend not to make a move.”

If all three of them endured from now on?

“Then the result of the wager will be decided soon.”

It was her confidence that she could endure better than the Blood Heaven Blade Demon.

It was then.

“Move! If you don’t want to die!”

Another group of martial artists roughly unleashed their qinggong, kicking up dust as they passed by.

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon waved his hand like a fan, brushing away the dust.

Geom Mugeuk asked worriedly,

“Are you all right?”

“I’m fine.”

“Normally, shouldn’t force be bursting out from that palm of yours? If someone who never holds back suddenly holds back too much, they’ll fall ill.”

“When martial artists use qinggong, dust can be raised. That’s all.”

At the sight of him looking like a high monk disciplining his mind, Geom Mugeuk feigned a sigh and said,

“Ah! If I’d known this kind of mental cultivation awaited us, I should have brought the Demon Buddha with us.”

“He’d be even worse at enduring it. Isn’t his Buddha the sort who kills first, then prays for rebirth in paradise?”

Geom Mugeuk and the Ilhwa Sword Venerable laughed together.

Though the situation around them was growing urgent, the three of them did not hurry.

After walking a little farther, they came upon a broad clearing beneath the mountain.

It was packed with martial artists, and from them radiated a kind of immense heat.

“I heard the bastard is hiding in the cave!”

“Move aside at once!”

“Move before we kill you!”

Beyond the shouting and protesting crowd, a cave could be seen.

Ten Thousand Holes Cave.

The Ten Thousand Holes Cave of Tianzi Mountain was famous for being enormously spacious inside, and for being as complicated as a maze.

So many people had entered only to die after failing to find the way out that it was also called the Cave of Death.

Those blocking the cave entrance were none other than the martial artists of the Flying Dragon Gang, famed in Hunan for their belligerence.

“Ten Thousand Holes Cave has been sealed off. Everyone, withdraw.”

The one who spoke with internal energy infused in his voice was Song Byeokchan. He was an expert who ranked within the top ten of the Flying Dragon Gang.

The assembled heroes argued and demanded answers.

“Why are you stopping us from going in? Aren’t you trying to monopolize the treasure map for the Flying Dragon Gang?”

At that, Song Byeokchan said,

“We are trying to prevent bloodshed from breaking out in our gang’s territory. This is to stop people from losing their lives in pointless fighting, so do not misunderstand.”

Of course, no one took those words at face value.

In truth, the Flying Dragon Gang had released its experts to search the interior of Ten Thousand Holes Cave. Since many outside experts had already entered before the blockade, and the inside of the cave was far too complicated, they had yet to find the person who had hidden inside with the treasure map.

The martial artist who had protested earlier incited the crowd.

“Let us force our way in. If all of us join forces…”

Swish! Thud!

Song Byeokchan’s sword lodged itself in the back of the man’s neck.

Struck by a surprise attack from behind, the man was helpless.

Song Byeokchan looked coldly down at the man as he collapsed, spraying blood, then spoke to the gathered crowd.

“Is there anyone else who still dares look down on our gang?”

Silence fell over the place. There were not many in Hunan who could disregard the power of the Flying Dragon Gang.

Watching from the back, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon shook his head.

“A cave? I absolutely detest cramped places.”

The Ilhwa Sword Venerable smiled.

“It seems I have the advantage in many ways.”

She meant that the Blood Heaven Blade Demon would become even more irritable if he had to stay somewhere stifling.

“Now, everyone, return.”

As Song Byeokchan stepped forward, the Flying Dragon Gang martial artists lined up behind him all drew their swords at once and advanced threateningly.

Even in this situation, the assembled heroes did not retreat.

The desire and greed that, if only they could obtain the treasure map, they would become peerless experts and gain tremendous wealth kept everyone rooted to the spot. Even at this moment, more people continued to gather there.

It was right then.

Swish, swish, swish, swish, swish, swish, swish!

Concealed weapons poured down on the Flying Dragon Gang martial artists from behind.

“Ambush!”

Song Byeokchan cried out urgently, but the Flying Dragon Gang martial artists were already falling one after another to the incoming hidden weapons. Astonishingly, the attackers were people who had emerged from inside the cave.

Clang, clang, clang, clang, clang!

Song Byeokchan hurriedly swung his sword to knock away the concealed weapons, but there were too many pouring in.

Thwack!

At that moment, another palm strike flew into his back.

His body flew through the air and tumbled across the ground. The man who had been shouting so boldly just moments ago had become a corpse before anyone knew it.

The person who had been hiding among the heroes and launched the surprise attack on him was a muscular man with a vile-looking face. Before the Flying Dragon Gang martial artists had sealed the entrance, he had already hidden his subordinates inside the cave.

“Why is this your territory? It’s ours.”

Someone who recognized him shouted,

“The Green Forest Heavenly Lord!”

He was a Green Forest bandit who had a mountain stronghold here on Tianzi Mountain and was famous even in Hunan. The name had not been given to him because he was as great as the Green Forest King, but because he was the master of Tianzi Mountain.

He was a vicious man with a base disposition, one who committed all manner of evil deeds if there was money to be made.

However, he had recently clashed with the Flying Dragon Gang, and after suffering consecutive defeats in several fights against them, his influence had greatly diminished. Having spent his days sunk in money and women, even his once-formidable martial arts were not what they used to be.

“Shameless bastards. If you get the treasure map, you can obtain a treasure of the ages, and you thought you’d swallow it all by yourselves?”

When he spat on Song Byeokchan’s corpse, cheers erupted from every direction.

“Now, let us go in!”

The assembled heroes surged toward Ten Thousand Holes Cave.

But once again, concealed weapons poured down on them.

Swish, swish, swish, swish, swish, swish!

Those at the front were struck by the hidden weapons and collapsed. In front of the Green Forest bandits who had thrown them, the Green Forest Heavenly Lord blocked the cave entrance.

“Who said you could enter as you pleased?”

At his ringing shout, the atmosphere sank in an instant.

The Green Forest Heavenly Lord spoke loudly to everyone.

“If you wish to enter this place, you must pay a toll.”

Everyone wore dumbfounded expressions. However, in this atmosphere, no one dared protest.

Someone among the heroes asked,

“How much is the toll?”

“One thousand taels per person. If you have no money, I will also accept weapons or valuables of equivalent worth.”

The heroes began murmuring. One thousand taels was far too great a sum merely to enter a cave.

“Is that not too expensive?”

At the heroes’ protests, the Green Forest Heavenly Lord tempted them.

“You would miss the chance to obtain a treasure of the ages just to save a mere thousand taels? Do as you please.”

The Green Forest Heavenly Lord himself had no intention whatsoever of entering the cave.

If he collected from just ten people here, that was ten thousand taels, and if he collected from a hundred, it would be an astonishing one hundred thousand taels. Since heroes were continuing to flock here, collecting tolls from a hundred people was not impossible.

The life of the Green Forest was to lie in ambush for days just to rob a few dozen or a few hundred taels. There was no way he could miss such an excellent opportunity.

Since it was certain his band would soon be ruined because of the Flying Dragon Gang, he intended to make one great haul this time and leave this place.

“Are we of the Green Forest not people who collect tolls by nature? This path happens to be the path to becoming an absolute expert, so the toll is merely expensive. There is no such thing as free in this world, so since you pay a high price, you will surely gain a great reward.”

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon, who had been listening, blurted out,

“That bandit bastard uses his head well.”

Unfortunately, he said it at the exact moment the noisy field briefly quieted, so those words reached the Green Forest Heavenly Lord’s ears.

The Green Forest Heavenly Lord whipped his head toward the source of the voice.

“Who was that!”

When the people in front stepped aside, Geom Mugeuk could be seen behind them. The voice had clearly come from around there.

“Was it you? The bastard who just spouted that nonsense?”

“It wasn’t me.”

Geom Mugeuk shifted aside and looked behind him as he said,

“It was this person.”

When Geom Mugeuk tattled on him, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon wore an expression of disbelief. The Ilhwa Sword Venerable smiled brightly, her expression saying, As expected, the Young Cult Master is on my side.

The Green Forest Heavenly Lord stared at the Blood Heaven Blade Demon and said coldly,

“Old man, do you want to die?”

While Geom Mugeuk and the Ilhwa Sword Venerable were grinning, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon calmly said,

“I meant that you were clever, so do not take it too much to heart.”

When the other party acted meekly, the Green Forest Heavenly Lord was all the more unwilling to let it slide.

He walked a few steps forward. He intended to properly beat him down as an example.

Because he had revealed no aura at all, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon had seemed like an insignificant old man.

Yet the closer he approached, the more an inexplicable unease arose in him. Why? Was it because of those eyes?

But he had already started forward. If he backed down now, those jackal-like bastards watching would all rush in to tear him apart.

The Ilhwa Sword Venerable watched with a pleased expression, certain she had won the wager. No matter that it was a wager, there was no way the Blood Heaven Blade Demon would simply stand by and let a bastard like that lay hands on him.

“Wait.”

After briefly stopping him, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon put a hand into his robe.

The Green Forest Heavenly Lord flinched and stopped, drawing up his internal energy as he prepared for a surprise attack.

What the Blood Heaven Blade Demon took out of his robe was a small pouch.

The Green Forest Heavenly Lord retreated another step.

‘Poison?’

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon took something out of the pouch and held it forward.

“Here are one thousand taels.”

Geom Mugeuk and the Ilhwa Sword Venerable were startled and looked at each other. They truly had never dreamed that the Blood Heaven Blade Demon would hand over one thousand taels.

“Take it.”

The Green Forest Heavenly Lord accepted the banknote in spite of himself. It was a genuine banknote.

“I am your first guest to pay the toll, so please overlook my earlier mistake.”

Since the man had paid, he could not hound or beat him. If he wanted others to pay, he had to let this one in smoothly.

“Very well. Go in.”

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon urged Geom Mugeuk and the Ilhwa Sword Venerable.

“What are you doing? Pay up.”

Geom Mugeuk whispered to the Ilhwa Sword Venerable,

“You have the treasure map, don’t you, Sword Venerable? He’s doing this so he can win the wager and make you hand it over, right?”

The gaze the Ilhwa Sword Venerable turned on the Blood Heaven Blade Demon deepened. What is it that you want?

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon casually asked the Green Forest Heavenly Lord,

“There is something I am curious about.”

“What is it?”

“How long do you intend to remain here?”

The Green Forest Heavenly Lord flinched and asked back,

“Why do you ask that?”

“Never mind. I was merely asking.”

And that only made the Green Forest Heavenly Lord feel unpleasant for no reason.

At that moment, the Ilhwa Sword Venerable said,

“I do not have one thousand taels right now.”

At that, the Green Forest Heavenly Lord looked the Ilhwa Sword Venerable up and down.

Thinking her the companion of someone who had obediently paid, he did not imagine for a moment that she was anything special. Seeing even the young fellow beside her burdened with packs, he was certain they were no absolute experts.

That mistaken certainty made him utter words he should never have said.

“If you have no money, I can accept something else.”

The Ilhwa Sword Venerable’s expression, which had been relaxed until now, hardened at once.

Her original temperament emerged immediately. Wager or not, the thought that she should first cut off his head flashed through her mind.

Then she saw it. The Blood Heaven Blade Demon’s hand moving toward the knot of the cloth binding the Heaven-Destroying Great Blade. He could endure what happened to himself, but he could not endure the Ilhwa Sword Venerable being insulted.

At that sight, a smile formed on the Ilhwa Sword Venerable’s face, and her anger naturally subsided. Before the Blood Heaven Blade Demon could untie the knot, she said,

“There are many eyes here right now, so let us meet again here later. I will give you something very good as a gift.”

“Do you take me for a fool? What guarantee is there that you will come out alive?”

At that, the Ilhwa Sword Venerable gazed meaningfully at the Green Forest Heavenly Lord and said,

“Do not worry. I have a reason I must come out.”

The Green Forest Heavenly Lord did not know just how earnestly the Blood Heaven Blade Demon and the Ilhwa Sword Venerable wished to meet him again.

It was right then. From behind came a woman’s sharp, ringing voice.

“Kill that lecherous bastard.”

When the Green Forest Heavenly Lord turned in surprise, a young woman stood there with martial artists in tow.

As soon as her command fell, the martial artists lined up behind her all launched themselves forward at once.

The Green Forest Heavenly Lord swung his fists and resisted, but he could not withstand the coordinated attack of the martial artists. Their skills were far stronger than those of the Flying Dragon Gang martial artists he had faced earlier.

He had been hacked to death, and the subordinates who had been throwing hidden weapons moments before fled without even looking back. They knew who the woman was.

The only daughter of the Flying Dragon Gang Leader.

Cold Moon Sword (冷月劍), Chuyeon (秋燕).

Among the warriors of the Flying Dragon Gang, she was famous for her mercilessness toward enemies.

Once all of the Green Forest Heavenly Lord’s men had fled, several of the heroes near the entrance rushed into the cave. Then, before anyone could stop them, the rest all surged forward and threw themselves inside.

Chuyeon did not stop them. She merely watched with a cold, contemptuous gaze.

At that moment, three people who had not entered her sight came into view.

Geom Mugeuk searched the Green Forest Heavenly Lord’s corpse and found the note that the Blood Heaven Demon Saber had given him. The note was stained with blood.

Geom Mugeuk took that note for himself and handed the Blood Heaven Demon Saber a fresh note worth one thousand nyang.

Then the Blood Heaven Demon Saber held out his hand, asking for the bloodstained note.

“Take the new one.”

“It’s fine.”

A thought suddenly occurred to Geom Mugeuk. Perhaps, to the Blood Heaven Demon Saber, even this note was part of his memories with the One Blossom Sword Venerable.

Just as Geom Mugeuk was handing the bloodstained note back to the Blood Heaven Demon Saber—

“You should be ashamed.”

The one who spoke coldly was Chuyeon.

Geom Mugeuk and the Blood Heaven Demon Saber looked at her.

“No matter how valuable a treasure map may be, you call yourselves martial artists, yet you paid money because you couldn’t withstand the threats of Green Forest.”

On top of that, she had found it displeasing to see them search a corpse to recover the money.

At her gaze, filled with contempt, Geom Mugeuk replied.

“You are mistaken, young lady.”

In an instant, Chuyeon’s face hardened coldly. A mere porter had dared to step forward and say she was wrong.

Geom Mugeuk looked at the Blood Heaven Demon Saber and said,

“First of all, the elder made that choice in order to save all of them.”

This time, the Blood Heaven Demon Saber flinched inwardly. To save them all? When did I ever?

“If they entered this place, they would eventually end up fighting one another and dying. But because our elder paid, an atmosphere was created where everyone had to pay in order to enter. None of them would have had the enormous sum of one thousand nyang, so they would not have been able to go in. In the end, the elder saved all their lives. This was all born from the elder’s compassion.”

At an interpretation better than the dream itself, the Blood Heaven Demon Saber gave a dry cough.

Just as Chuyeon was about to snap back and ask what sort of sophistry he was spouting, Geom Mugeuk struck at the heart of the matter.

“But after he went to such trouble to save them, they all just went in anyway. You, young lady, could have stopped them if you wished. Could it be that you thought, ‘Go on in and die, all of you’?”

For an instant, Chuyeon’s face twitched. Astonishingly enough, she really had thought that just moments ago. Having the truth hit home by someone who merely carried baggage infuriated her.

Just then, a shout burst out from inside the cave.

“There it is! The treasure map!”

Screams erupted from within, followed by the successive clash of weapons. Then those sounds receded into the distance again.

“You must go in at once.”

At her subordinate’s words, Chuyeon nodded.

Just before entering the cave, she turned a cold gaze back toward Geom Mugeuk. She did not go so far as to make a throat-slitting gesture with the edge of her hand, but her eyes were practically warning him, If I see you again, you’re dead.

After they went inside, the three of them entered the cave as well. The Blood Heaven Demon Saber, who had been teased the entire time, smiled with satisfaction and said,

“Looks like you’ll be the one to draw your sword first, won’t you?”

Chapter 733: I Have a Good Feeling About This Path
--------------------------------------------------
The inside of the cave was dark.

Of course, to Geom Mugeuk, who had mastered the Divine Eye Art, it looked brightly lit, and the two Demon Venerables were not so hindered that they could not move.

Those with torches would at least fare better, but those without them were likely wandering through the darkness, relying on instinct alone.

As Geom Mugeuk took a small torch from his leather satchel and lit it, the two Demon Venerables spoke.

“This cave is supposed to be a maze?”

“Surely the three of us won’t get lost?”

“Wouldn’t we be more likely to get lost because there are three of us?”

Meaning, precisely because they trusted one another, they might end up losing their way.

At the Blood Heaven Saber Demon’s words, the One Flower Sword Venerable smiled faintly. To others, the Blood Heaven Saber Demon seemed like the sort of person who would say, If we get lost, we can just smash everything apart.

But she knew very well that, unexpectedly, he had such delicate sides to him.

Of course, neither of them was the least bit worried about losing their way. It was because of Geom Mugeuk, who walked ahead with utter nonchalance.

“When else would I get the chance to lose my way with the two of you? And just because we lose the path, it doesn’t mean the road we must take disappears, does it?”

Thus the three of them walked inside. The interior of the cave was already filled with the heavy stench of blood, and the sounds of weapons clashing, curses, and screams mingled together and echoed. It was, in every sense, a hellscape of killing and being killed.

“We’ll take the path on the right first.”

Geom Mugeuk chose the right-hand path among the three forks.

“It’s convenient moving with the Young Cult Leader. It makes one believe he’ll take care of everything on his own, doesn’t it?”

At the One Flower Sword Venerable’s blind faith, the Blood Heaven Saber Demon glanced at Geom Mugeuk’s back for no reason and said brusquely,

“As if he has some grand plan. He’s just walking in without thinking.”

Geom Mugeuk answered with a laugh.

“As expected! Our elder sees right through me! Just where is the bastard with the treasure map hiding?”

“But how did that fellow end up being chased in here? Was he driven this way like prey?”

“Or he came in on purpose.”

“On purpose?”

Geom Mugeuk clearly seemed to have some premonition, but he did not tell them what it was. They did not bother asking. He was the sort who, even if told not to explain later, would explain until their ears hurt.

As they walked through the cave, the Blood Heaven Saber Demon flicked a gust of finger wind and destroyed a stone protruding from the ceiling. It had been sticking out at a height where it would have struck the One Flower Sword Venerable’s head as she walked.

“I saw it too.”

“I’m sure you did.”

“No matter how kind you are, I won’t go easy on the wager.”

“Who asked you to?”

Geom Mugeuk’s expression softened as he listened to their conversation. Yes, this was why he had left the cult with them—because he had wanted to hear them talk like that.

The cave was complicated. After walking a little, another fork would appear, and at times there were three or four paths.

Yet Geom Mugeuk strode along with ease, as if he knew exactly where he had to go.

Shiiik.

Just then, a sword suddenly shot out of the darkness, aiming for Geom Mugeuk.

After lightly twisting his body to avoid the attack, Geom Mugeuk simply continued walking forward. He did not even look at the one who had ambushed him.

The warrior who had launched the surprise attack was the one who became flustered instead. His opponent had passed by as if he did not exist, and even the two people following behind did not give him so much as a glance as they walked past.

Shiiik.

The sword he belatedly swung missed as well. The three of them had already disappeared into the darkness.

“What in the world is this…”

Puuk!

A sword burst out through the chest of the man who had been standing there in a daze.

Someone else had ambushed him from behind and killed him.

The attacker searched the fallen corpse. When the treasure map did not appear, he disappeared back into the darkness.

There were no rational choices or judgments here. Everyone was hiding themselves, ambushing people, killing them, and searching their bodies for the treasure map. It was truly a situation where the lucky one would take possession of it.

Clang clang clang clang clang!

This time, several people were fighting.

“Let’s just go.”

Surely one among them might have had the treasure map, no?

And yet, for whatever reason, Geom Mugeuk simply passed by.

Startled swords flew toward Geom Mugeuk as he crossed through the fighting, but not even a strand of his hair was grazed.

As if he were playing tag, Geom Mugeuk lightly slipped out of the place where the battle was taking place.

Would the Blood Heaven Saber Demon and the One Flower Sword Venerable have been any different?

“Why don’t you stop them?”

When the Blood Heaven Saber Demon asked, Geom Mugeuk looked back at the place behind them where the fighting was going on.

“Look at their eyes.”

All of them were caught up in greed-maddened frenzy.

“It would be easier to make a gambler quit gambling.”

Geom Mugeuk moved deeper into the cave.

“Do you even know where he’s hiding?”

“If we’re lucky, we’ll meet him.”

He was not searching by luck.

In truth, Geom Mugeuk had been to the Ten Thousand Blood Cave before.

He had roamed all throughout the Central Plains in search of materials for the grand art of regression; how could he possibly have passed by this famous cave of Mount Tianzi?

This mysterious maze cave had not easily let him leave. It had felt as though something was hidden somewhere he had not yet gone.

He had forgotten much of it now, but at the time, he knew this place so well that he could have drawn a map if told to.

‘Will those things still be here at this point in time?’

He had come up empty-handed when it came to finding materials for the grand art here, but he had found other things instead. Only, because the time period was different, he could not be certain they were still here now.

He also had an idea of where someone might hide, if they were to hide.

The Blood Heaven Saber Demon and the One Flower Sword Venerable, following behind him, exchanged glances.

It would not have been strange for him to get lost and wander at least once along the way, but Geom Mugeuk had not passed the same place even a single time.

After circling through the cave like that, they came upon a somewhat wider space.

Geom Mugeuk took two bamboo tubes from his leather satchel and handed one each to the Blood Heaven Saber Demon and the One Flower Sword Venerable.

“Please have some water.”

The two drank the cool water.

Geom Mugeuk asked the Blood Heaven Saber Demon with a worried look.

“You feel stifled, don’t you?”

“I’m fine.”

He was not fine. The Blood Heaven Saber Demon was sweating. As he had said before entering the cave, he truly hated cramped places.

“I’ll go check this tunnel. Please rest here for a moment.”

Geom Mugeuk entrusted the torch to the Blood Heaven Saber Demon.

“What about you?”

“I have good night vision.”

In truth, Geom Mugeuk found it more convenient to move without a torch.

Swaaaak.

Using Swift Steps, Geom Mugeuk vanished from before their eyes in an instant.

The Blood Heaven Saber Demon and the One Flower Sword Venerable sat side by side on a flat rock.

“I’ve been surprised by the Young Cult Leader so many times that something like this feels completely ordinary now.”

The Blood Heaven Saber Demon nodded and wiped away his sweat.

“Even when you were young, you hated cramped and narrow places. You’re still the same.”

“Do people change that easily?”

At those words, the One Flower Sword Venerable laughed.

“Why are you laughing?”

“It’s nothing.”

Others might have said such a thing, but the Blood Heaven Saber Demon should not have. After meeting Geom Mugeuk, he had truly changed a great deal.

“Do you remember? We went to visit the Cult Leader in his training cave, didn’t we?”

It was back when Geom Ujin had still been the Young Cult Leader. Though she no longer remembered what incident had caused it, Geom Ujin had been ordered to train in the training cave for a hundred days.

“We secretly went there with liquor.”

A smile formed at the corners of the Blood Heaven Saber Demon’s mouth. It was one of the few memories that made him smile when he recalled the past, so how could he have forgotten it?

“You boasted that I should just trust you, but you got lost right away.”

“Did I?”

In truth, he had not gotten lost that day. He had only pretended to lose his way because he wanted to walk with her a little longer.

Now that he thought about it, the cave that day had not felt this stifling. Even while sweating profusely, he had not once thought it suffocating.

“We were so young back then.”

He recalled the sight of the Cult Leader smiling at them when they came to him, shaking the bottles of liquor.

Just as they were briefly immersed in old memories—

Shik shik shik shik shik!

Hidden weapons flew out of the darkness toward the One Flower Sword Venerable.

Dadadadang!

What deflected the hidden weapons was the cloth-wrapped Heaven-Destroying Great Saber.

The Blood Heaven Saber Demon lowered the Heaven-Destroying Great Saber that had shielded the One Flower Sword Venerable.

When the ambush failed, the one who had thrown the hidden weapons had already disappeared. The two Demon Venerables had no intention of pursuing him. They were not interested at all in what was happening here.

“What is it? Did you think I couldn’t stop those?”

At the One Flower Sword Venerable’s question, the Blood Heaven Saber Demon looked at the torch standing on his side and said,

“It seemed dark over there.”

Then he added words he did not need to add.

“Your night vision gets worse when you grow old, doesn’t it?”

“It’s rude to tell an older woman she’s old.”

“What’s wrong with growing old? You don’t have to try to look young. Evening is more beautiful than morning, after all.”

“I told you I don’t like it, and still you keep going!”

When the One Flower Sword Venerable glared at him, the Blood Heaven Saber Demon quietly turned his head to the other side.

Once again, a group of warriors burst into that place. Entering with blood dripping from their swords, they were Chuyeon, daughter of the Flying Dragon Gang’s leader, and the warriors following her.

The ten warriors had been reduced to eight.

Chuyeon had thought she would be able to leisurely sweep through this cave. After all, the ones she had brought with her were the Flying Dragon Gang’s elites, the Flying Dragon Ten Heroes.

But during the search, there had been two ambushes, and two of the Flying Dragon Ten Heroes had lost their lives. Fighting while holding torches in a narrow, complicated cave—one that was pitch-dark, no less—was different from what she had imagined. Today was the first time she learned that even the Flying Dragon Ten Heroes were beings who could die like this.

That was why her nerves had become frayed.

“What were you two doing here?”

There was another reason these two stood out more than others.

“Where’s that porter?”

The insolent words he had spoken earlier had lingered in her mind the entire time. For a mere porter, he had been so brazen. She had resolved that if she met him again, she would teach him a proper lesson.

“Is he already dead? Why aren’t you answering?”

At her sharp question, the Blood Heaven Saber Demon, who had been looking elsewhere, slowly turned his head.

The moment Chuyeon’s eyes met the Blood Heaven Saber Demon’s, her heart sank. It was not that he had revealed his aura, nor had he shown killing intent. And yet goosebumps rose all over her body.

It was because of his gaze. The torchlight reflected in the Blood Heaven Saber Demon’s pupils and flickered there, making it look as though flames were rising from his eyes.

At the same time, she felt as if her entire body were blazing, and she reflexively drew up her inner force.

At Chuyeon’s reaction, the Flying Dragon Ten Heroes all pointed their swords at once.

The Blood Heaven Saber Demon’s hand moved toward the knot of the cloth wrapped around the Heaven-Destroying Great Saber. The moment he pulled it, he would lose the wager.

But if it was a situation where they had to fight anyway, he had no intention of making the One Flower Sword Venerable step forward just to win the bet.

It was then.

Grip.

The Blood Heaven Saber Demon’s gaze went to the knot. The One Flower Sword Venerable had stretched out her hand and was gripping it. It meant she was telling him not to untie it.

“Young lady.”

At the One Flower Sword Venerable’s calm call, Chuyeon’s gaze, which had been fixed only on the Blood Heaven Saber Demon, turned toward her.

The moment she saw the One Flower Sword Venerable’s calm eyes, Chuyeon finally came to her senses.

‘Both of them were true masters!’

If they could give off this feeling without even revealing their aura, then she and her people were no match for them from the beginning.

Chuyeon had the Flying Dragon Ten Heroes lower their swords. Her heart grew heavy.

‘Even masters like these have entered this place. Can I really obtain the map?’

The state of her mind now was vastly different from when she had entered.

Just then, Geom Mugeuk returned to the place.

“Young lady, you’re safe!”

When Geom Mugeuk greeted her with such delight, Chuyeon was instead flustered.

“I was worried because so many people have been injured and killed. I’m glad you’re safe.”

Now she understood why this porter had been so insolent. With masters like those at his side, what would he have to fear?

“Why were you worried about me?”

“We talked outside, didn’t we? That makes us connected by fate.”

To Chuyeon, it would sound as though he were mocking her, but he was half sincere. If this affair involved a force behind the scenes, then surely there were arrangements they had put in place.

‘Are you one of them, perhaps?’

She could be, or she could not be. She might be a connection he would see today and never again, or if he followed the thread tied to her, he might encounter them.

As always, Geom Mugeuk intended not to miss a single thing. He intended to accept all of it while pretending not to know, then turn their plans against them.

“There are corpses all over that way right now. Please be careful.”

She could not scowl and curse at someone who was so glad to see her and so worried for her. No, more than that, it was probably because of those two masters. Yes, someday the chance to teach him a lesson would come.

“You be careful yourself, porter.”

Chuyeon turned and walked away. At the end, she glanced back briefly at the two Demon Venerables, but neither of them even looked at her.

Once she and the Flying Dragon Gang warriors had left, Geom Mugeuk took something out of the leather satchel he had been carrying.

“I didn’t find the person with the treasure map in that tunnel, but I found this.”

What was carefully wrapped in paper was a deep blue-green moss.

“What is this?”

The Blood Heaven Saber Demon and the One Flower Sword Venerable were startled when they confirmed what it was.

“Stalactite Immortal Moss?”

Stalactite Immortal Moss was a spiritual medicine made of moss that grew on stalactites hanging from cave ceilings. It was a truly rare spiritual medicine that grew only when countless conditions aligned perfectly.

“This is my first time seeing it in person.”

It was the same not only for the One Flower Sword Venerable, but also for the Blood Heaven Saber Demon.

“It’s my first time too.”

The two Demon Venerables looked at Geom Mugeuk in astonishment. He had found Stalactite Immortal Moss in that dark chaos and brought it back? It was truly something difficult to believe even after experiencing it firsthand.

“Why are you both so surprised? When people go into caves, they all find at least this much spiritual medicine, don’t they? Once we get out later, the three of us can share it amicably. I’ll cook it so it tastes good. However, I’m the one doing the cooking!”

Stalactite Immortal Moss differed from ordinary spiritual medicines in its effects; it did not merely increase one’s inner force.

As its name suggested—a moss eaten by immortals—it was known to restore vitality and grant health and longevity when consumed, making it a spiritual medicine extremely beneficial to the body. That was why he intended to feed a great deal of it to the Blood Heaven Saber Demon in particular.

As they listened to Geom Mugeuk, the two Demon Venerables were thinking similar thoughts. If such great fortune followed the Young Cult Leader as it always had…

If it was the Young Cult Leader, he truly might be able to find the person with the treasure map in this place.

Geom Mugeuk shouldered his leather satchel again and took the lead down a path they had not yet tried.

“I have a good feeling about this path. Now, let’s go!”

Chapter 734: I Can't Stand Being the Only One Who Can't Eat
-----------------------------------------------------------
Ilhwa Sword Venerable gave up on memorizing the route.

For a while after entering the cave, she had at least tried to keep track of the path. But now she had given up completely.

She looked back at the Hyeolcheon Blade Demon. He was following Geom Mugeuk with a relaxed expression, as if he had given up long ago.

Ilhwa Sword Venerable asked Geom Mugeuk,

“How do you know the way so well?”

“I suppose I was born with an instinct for finding paths.”

“Even the Cult Leader wouldn’t be able to find his way this well. Do you have some kind of secret?”

“It’s instinct, instinct!”

In truth, it was the result of training and experience. As for this Ten Thousand Blood Cave, he simply knew it inside and out; and his sharp sense of direction was something honed from wandering countless places in his life before regression. If he did not remember properly, he would end up wandering the same place again. Back then, there was nothing in the world he hated more than being lost.

A moment later, the Hyeolcheon Blade Demon teased him.

“That instinct led us to a dead end!”

The path they had walked for quite some time had turned out to be a dead end.

“I was sure this way felt promising.”

As if unable to let go of his lingering hope, Geom Mugeuk went all the way to the end of the dead end and examined the wall.

“There’s something down here.”

At the very bottom of the left wall, Geom Mugeuk discovered a narrow passage. At a glance, the protruding stone hid the passage beneath it from sight. One had to lie flat on the floor to see it.

That was why ordinary people would never have discovered it. After wandering through a complicated maze and realizing a path was clearly a dead end, who would go all the way to the end, lie on the floor, and check both sides?

How had he found it back then? He had not found it from this side, but from the opposite side.

“As expected, Young Cult Leader has sharp eyes.”

When Ilhwa Sword Venerable praised him, the Hyeolcheon Blade Demon grew needlessly peevish.

“You just stumbled onto it. How could anyone know that was there?”

“Half of it was thanks to you, Elder.”

“Because of me?”

“Since you teased me, I thought I should find something, so I came all the way to the end.”

“What about the other half?”

Geom Mugeuk looked down at the hole and said,

“I felt a new current of air from this direction.”

At that answer, the Hyeolcheon Blade Demon and Ilhwa Sword Venerable were startled. They had not realized Geom Mugeuk had been paying attention to even such things.

“I’ll go in first.”

No matter how much he acted out of spite, the one most worried about Geom Mugeuk was the Hyeolcheon Blade Demon.

“Be careful.”

Geom Mugeuk crawled along the ground and entered the passage.

Soon, Geom Mugeuk’s voice came from within.

“Please come in this way.”

The Hyeolcheon Blade Demon and Ilhwa Sword Venerable also entered. Naturally, the Hyeolcheon Blade Demon did not look pleased.

“I have no idea how many decades it’s been since I last crawled like this.”

Admiration flashed in the eyes of the two Demon Venerables who entered. The place was outside the cave, an isolated space surrounded by cliffs.

Water flowed there, and flowers, grass, and trees grew as well. Truly, it seemed like the perfect place to build a house and live.

Because of the unique terrain, unless one jumped down from that endlessly high sheer cliff, it would be difficult to discover this space from the outside.

Before his regression, Geom Mugeuk had climbed down that cliff. Back then, to exaggerate only slightly, he had climbed every cliff there was.

After descending to this place, he had discovered that hole. He had only learned that the passage connected to the Ten Thousand Blood Cave after entering it.

‘So this place was this beautiful.’

When he had come here before his regression, he had only checked whether there was anything that would be useful to him. At the time, he had not known this space possessed such charm.

“I’ll go take a look up there.”

Geom Mugeuk launched himself toward the cliff.

Ilhwa Sword Venerable slowly looked around the place. The first things that drew her eyes were the flowers blooming in the corner. There were some flowers she had never seen before.

“It would be nice to build a house in a place like this someday and live quietly.”

“It wouldn’t be easy to invite guests.”

At the Hyeolcheon Blade Demon’s words, Ilhwa Sword Venerable smiled. As he said, it would become a house where only peerless experts capable of climbing up and down that cliff could come as guests, let alone live there.

A moment later, Geom Mugeuk descended back to the place, carrying something none of them had expected.

“I found these on a tree halfway up the cliff.”

What Geom Mugeuk had brought were three peaches.

“You must be hungry. Please have one each.”

The peaches gave off a brilliant glow and a profound energy, and their fragrance was anything but ordinary.

The Hyeolcheon Blade Demon, who had been wearing an expression of disbelief, widened his eyes.

“Surely these aren’t… Immortal Peach Spirit Fruits?”

At his trembling voice, Geom Mugeuk smiled and answered,

“Why wouldn’t they be?”

Astonishingly, these peaches were the elixir known as Immortal Peach Spirit Fruits. Like the Stalactite Immortal Embryo they had found earlier, they were rare treasures not easily seen in the world.

“They’re especially known to be excellent for women. Your skin is already good, Sword Venerable, but now you’ll have the skin of a baby. Here, please eat.”

If the Stalactite Immortal Embryo from before had been more beneficial to the Hyeolcheon Blade Demon, then this Immortal Peach Spirit Fruit was an elixir beneficial to Ilhwa Sword Venerable.

Geom Mugeuk handed Immortal Peach Spirit Fruits to the Hyeolcheon Blade Demon and Ilhwa Sword Venerable.

They had obtained the Stalactite Immortal Embryo, and now they had found Immortal Peach Spirit Fruits? And he was handing them out as if they were peaches bought at a market?

The Hyeolcheon Blade Demon did not eat his.

“Elixirs each have their own destined owner. This is a fortuitous encounter that came to you, so you should take them all.”

Ilhwa Sword Venerable nodded, as if she thought the same.

Geom Mugeuk looked at the two Demon Venerables. Yes, before his regression, he had eaten them all alone.

“My fortuitous encounters are the two of you.”

“!”

“You’ve seen what I’ve done up until now, so don’t you already know?”

The Hyeolcheon Blade Demon could not refute him. Yes, he knew better than anyone what kind of person Geom Mugeuk was, and what kind of effort he had made. He knew very well that Geom Mugeuk would never eat them alone.

“Even so, you eat them.”

Geom Mugeuk quietly looked at the Hyeolcheon Blade Demon, then said with a mischievous smile,

“Very well. I’ll eat two.”

“Why two? There are three!”

The Hyeolcheon Blade Demon looked at Ilhwa Sword Venerable. She was smelling the fragrant scent of the Immortal Peach Spirit Fruit.

“Being too reserved wouldn’t be proper etiquette either, would it?”

At her words that she would eat it, the Hyeolcheon Blade Demon flinched.

Geom Mugeuk smiled and held out his hand to the Hyeolcheon Blade Demon.

“I’ll only eat yours, Elder.”

When Geom Mugeuk tried to take the Immortal Peach Spirit Fruit, the Hyeolcheon Blade Demon smoothly pulled his hand back. He could tolerate it if none of them ate, but he could not stand being the only one who did not!

“Now that I think about it, I do feel a little hungry.”

Geom Mugeuk burst into loud laughter, and the two Demon Venerables also smiled. He was sharing such spirit fruits without the slightest hesitation; how could their gratitude possibly be expressed in words?

“Please eat quickly. I’ve heard Immortal Peach Spirit Fruits are effective only if consumed within half a gak after being picked from the tree.”

Geom Mugeuk took a large bite of the Immortal Peach Spirit Fruit in his hand.

If there truly was such a thing as the taste of heaven, then this must be it. Sweet juice unlike anything he had ever tasted flowed out and slid down his throat in an instant.

Geom Mugeuk no longer needed more internal energy, but consuming this Immortal Peach Spirit Fruit was still worthwhile.

The Immortal Peach Spirit Fruit did not merely have the effect of increasing internal energy.

Even if it could not grant eternal youth and immortality, it made the body resistant to illness and strengthened the five senses. In addition, it regrew lost hair and rejuvenated the skin.

“Ah, it’s delicious!”

At Geom Mugeuk’s exclamation, Ilhwa Sword Venerable also took a large bite of the Immortal Peach Spirit Fruit. She usually ate with elegance and dignity, but this time she ate while trying hard not to let even a single drop of juice spill.

Her expression said everything about the taste. It would not be an exaggeration to say it was the most delicious thing she had ever eaten in her life.

The Hyeolcheon Blade Demon then ate the Immortal Peach Spirit Fruit as well.

“It’s sweet. So sweet.”

Who could have known when he left the cult that he would end up eating the Immortal Peach Spirit Fruit he had only ever heard about?

Ever since leaving the cult with Geom Mugeuk, he had truly been experiencing one unexpected thing after another. And those things were far too intriguing and enjoyable.

The three sat down in their respective places and began circulating their qi.

Each of them began melting the miraculous energy of the Immortal Peach Spirit Fruit into their internal energy through their own unique mental cultivation methods. Was it because a pleasant fragrance rose from both inside and outside their bodies? It felt fresher and more pleasant than absorbing any other elixir.

After dissolving the immense energy contained within the Immortal Peach Spirit Fruit into his internal energy, the Hyeolcheon Blade Demon opened his eyes. Not only had his internal energy increased, his body also felt lighter.

Geom Mugeuk, who had finished circulating his qi first, was looking up at the small patch of sky visible between the surrounding cliffs.

Fortunately, he had obtained both fortuitous opportunities within the Ten Thousand Blood Cave. The fact that exactly three Immortal Peach Spirit Fruits had grown there might also have been fate.

After the Hyeolcheon Blade Demon, Ilhwa Sword Venerable also finished circulating her qi.

“Thank you, Young Cult Leader.”

For Ilhwa Sword Venerable, who was striving to become stronger, the effects of the Immortal Peach Spirit Fruit had been truly helpful. And as an added bonus, she had gained a healthy body and taut skin as well.

Geom Mugeuk, who had been looking up at the sky, turned to her with a bright expression.

“I should be the one thanking you instead. If you had not left the cult this time, how could I have gained such a fortuitous encounter? This is all thanks to you, Sword Venerable.”

Ilhwa Sword Venerable silently gazed at Geom Mugeuk. What could she say to a man who even credited this to her?

She said to the Hyeolcheon Blade Demon,

“You heard him, didn’t you? You should be grateful to me.”

The Hyeolcheon Blade Demon touched his own cheek and replied,

“I really do feel like my skin has improved.”

At that, Geom Mugeuk drew his sword and reflected the Hyeolcheon Blade Demon’s face on its blade.

“Is that all? Look here. Your wrinkles have smoothed out quite a bit too.”

“Is that so? It’s not just my imagination?”

“If you take the Stalactite Immortal Embryo later too, even the Poison King’s skin will have nothing on yours. What does age matter? As long as your heart is young and your body is healthy, that’s enough.”

Geom Mugeuk praised him to the skies in front of Ilhwa Sword Venerable.

Since there were few compliments more pleasing than being told one looked young, the Hyeolcheon Blade Demon could not hide his smile.

‘Please live young and live long.’

Geom Mugeuk walked back toward the hole they had entered through.

“Now, let’s go.”

Geom Mugeuk crawled out first.

“How was it? Wasn’t it worth crawling along the floor?”

Behind him, the Hyeolcheon Blade Demon grumbled for no reason.

“I still hate crawling!”

If it had been the usual him, he would have suggested breaking the wall and walking out, but for some reason, he obediently crawled out of the place.

After coming out, Geom Mugeuk once again took the lead and retraced the path they had come from.

“This way feels promising this time!”

The Hyeolcheon Blade Demon followed behind and said,

“That feeling got us Immortal Peach Spirit Fruits, so I suppose we should follow it.”

And so the two Demon Venerables once again walked after Geom Mugeuk.

Outside, the hellscape still continued to unfold. The screams ringing out from every direction made them feel that they had returned to reality.

How long had they walked? Ahead of them, two martial artists stood facing each other with their weapons drawn.

Their auras were anything but ordinary, and indeed, they were men worthy of such presence.
