One was the Azure Sky Great Axe, renowned throughout Hunan for his single axe, and the other was the Strange Blood Saber, an expert from Jiangxi who had come all the way here after hearing rumors of the treasure map. As fate would have it, experts of similar skill had met head-on.

The force emitted by the two filled their surroundings. Because they knew each other well, neither moved rashly.

“You are someone I should be fighting at the very end.”

The Azure Sky Great Axe esteemed both himself and his opponent that highly.

He wanted to avoid this fight. The Strange Blood Saber, on the other hand, intended to settle the match here.

“If we must fight once anyway, is there any need to put it off?”

It was because the place where he had encountered the Azure Sky Great Axe was narrow, and if a fight broke out, he thought he would have the advantage over his opponent, who used a large axe.

Just then, as tension hung in the air as if a blow might fly at any moment, someone’s voice was heard.

“If you aren’t going to fight…”

What followed was truly something they had never expected.

“I’ll just pass through for a moment.”

The two men were startled.

Pass through where? Here?

Here, where the Azure Sky Great Axe and the Strange Blood Saber had their blades aimed at each other to kill? And in this narrow passage, no less?

They thought, surely not, but someone began walking toward them.

Truly, the thought naturally occurred to both men: Is this bastard insane? But that insane bastard really did pass between them.

As he walked between the flashing blade and axe, he even greeted them.

“My, my. You’re both working hard.”

The two martial artists were truly dumbfounded. They were so dumbfounded that they could not even laugh.

There was not just one madman. A woman striking enough to seize the eye followed after him, and last of all, an old man with something large strapped to his back passed by, constantly patting his own cheek. These lunatics did not so much as glance at them.

While they passed one after another, the two men could not move. If they moved recklessly, they would give the other an opening to attack. And if those lunatics joined forces as well, an even greater disaster might unfold.

“What was that just now?”

“They must have gone mad from fear.”

Of course, despite their words, they could feel that those people were no ordinary figures.

Whether they were lunatics or true masters, for some reason, the battle of a lifetime had suddenly lost all its momentum.

As if by mutual agreement, the two slowly put away their weapons and disappeared in opposite directions.

Geom Mugeuk continued onward.

The two Demon Venerables following behind him held torches and looked around. Caves appeared in every direction. The path had been complicated until now, but this place was truly a maze. In one space alone, there were as many as ten openings. When they entered one of them, five or six more forks appeared. Every path was like that.

They could clearly understand why this place was called the Cave of Death.

On top of that, the paths in this area had been made to wind round and round, so unless one knew the exact route, one would be forced to circle the same paths endlessly.

“Even someone who knows the way would get lost here.”

At Ilhwa Sword Venerable’s words, the Blood Heaven Saber Demon nodded. Sweat continued to pour from him. Even spirit medicine could not dispel the stifling feeling of being in such a narrow place.

At that moment, Geom Mugeuk spoke to the two of them with a meaningful look in his eyes.

“So if someone were hiding, wouldn’t they hide somewhere around here?”

The two Demon Venerables looked at each other and exchanged glances.

They understood. Geom Mugeuk was trying to find the one who had the treasure map here.

Earlier, Geom Mugeuk had spoken to them as though the person with the treasure map might have deliberately entered Manhyeoldong. He had clearly had some sort of conjecture in mind.

When they arrived after following Geom Mugeuk, a new sight unfolded before them. The tightness in the Blood Heaven Saber Demon’s chest was swept clean away.

A magnificent spectacle wrought by the passage of ages spread out before their eyes.

The place, as large as a plaza, was filled with enormous stalactites and stalagmites.

Stalactites that had grown long like a beast’s fangs hung down from the ceiling, while stalagmites towered upward toward it.

Among them were some where stalactite and stalagmite had nearly met. They looked as though heaven and earth were about to touch.

Drip, drip, drip.

The sound of water droplets falling from the stalactites broke the silence and echoed through the cavern.

Geom Mugeuk, who had been quietly gazing at the place, began walking into that mystery.

“Somehow, this feels like a place where something good might happen, doesn’t it?”

Chapter 735: I Must Have Been Here in a Past Life
-------------------------------------------------
“So there was a place like this.”

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon and the Ilhwa Sword Venerable marveled at the mystery wrought by nature.

The complicated maze they had passed through now seemed only natural. To witness such a magnificent sight, should one not have to struggle at least that much to find it?

After gazing at the place for a while with the two Demon Venerables, Geom Mugeuk revealed the reason he had come here.

“If I had to hide somewhere in Manhyeoldong, I think I would hide here. Those who made it through that complicated maze and arrived here—how could they possibly imagine it? That someone was hiding in a place like this.”

They would be entranced by this majestic sight for a moment, then set out again to look for another path.

But the Blood Heaven Blade Demon looked around with a puzzled expression.

“Here? Where is there to hide?”

No matter how he looked, there did not seem to be anywhere a person could conceal themselves.

“Aren’t there many places to hide?”

“Many?”

As the Blood Heaven Blade Demon looked around again, a disbelieving expression crossed his face.

His gaze turned to the huge stalagmites rising from the ground.

‘No way. Inside those?’

As if to say he had seen correctly, Geom Mugeuk slowly walked among the stalagmites.

“If he was someone capable of snatching the treasure map and inciting a struggle over it, then he must have known what kind of place Manhyeoldong was. He wouldn’t have recklessly entered a place where, without knowing the way, one would wander the maze until death. He is likely someone who knows the inside of this place well, and knows this location we are in too.”

“Then he’s no ordinary fellow.”

Geom Mugeuk had grasped the opponent precisely.

“He is more than that. He is someone who intended to make those pursuing him kill one another in this pandemonium.”

Geom Mugeuk stopped before one stalagmite.

There was a sunken part in the stalagmite, and inside the curved hollow was a fairly large hole.

Even so, it looked far too narrow for a person to enter.

Geom Mugeuk sent a glance to the Blood Heaven Blade Demon and the Ilhwa Sword Venerable. The two Demon Venerables had already sensed the presence of a person within.

“I have wondered for a long time what the inside of these stalagmites looks like.”

At Geom Mugeuk’s words, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon replied.

“Why not cut it open?”

Geom Mugeuk drew the Black Demon Sword and tapped the stalagmite.

“Shall I cut it around here?”

“Cut it diagonally. That way we’ll be able to see better.”

“Like this?”

As he spoke, he tapped the stalagmite with his sword.

“Now then, I’m cutting!”

It was at that very moment. A panicked voice came from the hole.

“Wait! There’s someone here! There’s a person inside!”

A man stuck his head out of the hole. It seemed impossible for a person to come out of that tiny opening, yet he slipped out smoothly, like an octopus squeezing free.

“Please don’t kill me! Please!”

He was a man of slight build. His face was that of an adult, but his body was the size of a boy’s.

“How on earth were you inside such a narrow place?”

Geom Mugeuk peered into the hole. His two eyes, empowered by the Divine Eye Art, examined the dark interior of the stalagmite.

The man who had come outside looked at the three of them with eyes full of fear. Judging by the aura that could be felt from him, he was indeed a martial artist, but not someone with martial arts or internal energy worth boasting of anywhere.

“Please spare me.”

After watching their reactions, the man apparently decided that, with three opponents before him, he could not handle them, and threw himself flat on the ground.

“I came in to find the treasure map, lost my way, wandered around, and ended up here. I was too afraid to leave, so I hid here. I’m not a bad person!”

“You probably aren’t a good person either.”

“No, no, that’s not true.”

Trembling all over, he began revealing his personal history without even being asked.

He said his father, the head of a small martial family, had fallen into gambling late in life, causing the household to fall to pieces. In the end, they had been driven to the point where his young younger sister was about to be sold to a rich household, and that was when he heard the rumor that the person with the treasure map had appeared on Mount Cheonja and blindly came up here.

“If I can’t get the treasure map, my sister will be sold. Please just pretend you didn’t see me and pass by!”

Perhaps the thought of his sister was unbearable, for tears fell from his eyes in drops.

Geom Mugeuk quietly stared at the man, then spoke coldly.

“With your level of martial arts, how were you going to obtain the treasure map? Did you think that if you hid here and then went out, the people outside would all have died in mutual destruction, and the treasure map might be lying among them? You shouldn’t curse your father. You’re making an absurd gamble yourself.”

At those words, the man lowered his head deeply and could not answer.

“Let’s go. I’ll take you outside.”

Geom Mugeuk spoke to the two Demon Venerables.

“I think we’ve roughly looked through the cave as well, so shall we head out now?”

They were leaving without obtaining the treasure map?

The Demon Venerables knew Geom Mugeuk well. If he had never come in to begin with, that would be one thing, but he was not someone who would enter to find the treasure map and then give up so easily.

“Let’s do that.”

The two Demon Venerables exchanged glances. They thought they understood.

‘It’s that man.’

Geom Mugeuk must have discovered something they could not see. He had said he felt something good would happen, and indeed, he had found the person with the treasure map.

—We should immediately suppress that fellow’s internal energy and search his body!

That was what he would have done. The Ilhwa Sword Venerable, on the other hand, was not worried at all.

—The Young Cult Leader will surely handle it in his own way.

Before leaving, she looked around the place. There was lingering regret in her eyes.

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon stood beside her.

“Why?”

“When will I ever have another chance to come to a place like this? I want to take it all in before I go.”

“We can come again.”

“How?”

She meant that they could not come even if they wanted to because they did not know the way here. She was right. Without Geom Mugeuk, even getting out of Manhyeoldong would not be easy.

“The Central Plains are full of caves like this. Next time…”

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon did not continue, but the Ilhwa Sword Venerable knew what he had been about to say.

Geom Mugeuk watched their backs and waited for them. He did not intrude.

Just as when they had sat side by side watching the morning star, what those two needed now was time for only the two of them to share.

Then Geom Mugeuk looked at the man.

Terrified, the man quickly averted his gaze when his eyes met Geom Mugeuk’s.

“Are you truly going to spare me?”

“You used up all the gambling luck of your lifetime this time. Because you met me.”

The man looked relieved, yet perhaps because of worry for his sister, he could not hide the complicated feelings on his face.

A short while later, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon and the Ilhwa Sword Venerable walked over to the two of them.

“Now, let’s leave.”

And so they left the space with the stalactites and stalagmites.

The atmosphere inside the cave had grown even more heated. The more dead there were, the stronger the madness of those who survived became.

The sensation of killing a person and rifling through another’s clothes was more thrilling than any pleasure they had ever experienced. Perhaps this time, the treasure map would come out! That intense expectation drove them into slaughter and frenzy.

Chuyeon of the Flying Dragon Gang was no different.

Swiik! Puaaak!

Blood splattered across her face as she cut the throat of an opponent charging at her.

She searched the body of the fallen man herself. But this time too, there was no treasure map.

Wiping the blood from her face with her sleeve, Chuyeon scowled fiercely.

The situation was not unfolding as she had expected. When they had first entered, there had been ten of the Flying Dragon Ten Elites, but now only six remained.

It was a situation where she should have given up and left, but she did not give up.

Just then, people approached in her direction. Seeing the man at the front, she flinched in surprise.

“You?”

The one leading them was that porter. She had met them again.

Geom Mugeuk greeted her cheerfully.

“Seeing as we’ve met three times in one day, perhaps we are fated.”

Chuyeon glared coldly at Geom Mugeuk. Just seeing him put her in a foul mood. The fact that a mere porter dared treat her so casually displeased her. He was acting as if he were the treasured disciple of those experts.

But if he were truly their disciple, he would have called them Master.

He was calling the old man elder. He was surely a porter or a low-ranking martial artist. She could not even sense any trace of martial training from him.

‘If only it weren’t for those people.’

As he died by her hand, he would be leaving behind the final words that he should not have met her a third time. No, he would already have died the last time they met.

Her gaze turned to the Blood Heaven Blade Demon and the Ilhwa Sword Venerable. As before, they did not give her so much as a glance.

‘They are ignoring me completely.’

Chuyeon’s gaze shifted again to the man with them.

“Who is that man?”

“We happened to meet him and are traveling together.”

“Then why are you taking him with you?”

“Apparently his younger sister is about to be sold. If it were some other circumstance, I might not know, but with such desperate circumstances, shouldn’t we save him?”

Chuyeon looked at the man suspiciously.

“You believe that?”

“Are you suggesting that this man has the treasure map? With that feeble body of his?”

“Surely you haven’t yet searched his body?”

“I haven’t.”

At his confident answer, Chuyeon was dumbfounded.

“May we search him in your stead?”

She was not asking the porter before her. She was seeking permission from the two experts behind the porter.

“To think you distrust people so much. Very well, go ahead.”

When Geom Mugeuk gave his permission, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon inwardly flinched. He had thought Geom Mugeuk would use them as a shield if necessary to prevent anyone from laying a hand on the man’s body.

‘The treasure map will surely come out from that fellow’s body.’

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon thought that, in the end, he would have to untie the cloth wrapped around the Myeolcheon Great Saber here.

One of the martial artists of the Flying Dragon Ten Elites searched the man’s body. He searched more thoroughly than he had the others, but the treasure map did not appear.

Chuyeon had searched him just in case, but the ones truly surprised were the two Demon Venerables.

—He doesn’t have the treasure map?

—Then why is the Young Cult Leader simply leaving? And why is he taking that man with him?

—How are we supposed to know what goes on inside that rascal’s head?

Geom Mugeuk smiled as he bid farewell.

“Well then, let us still be alive when we meet for the fourth time!”

In truth, Chuyeon wanted to search the leather pack Geom Mugeuk was carrying. Truly, she wanted to search the bodies of those two experts.

It felt far too unsettling to simply let them go, so she mustered her courage. It was courage intoxicated by the blood shed by those she had killed today.

“May I see that baggage of yours?”

Geom Mugeuk shook his head with a relaxed expression.

“If it were most things, I would want to show you, but there are many things you must not see.”

Hearing him say that made her even more curious. In truth, she wanted to give the order: take that leather pack from him!

‘Could I possibly be mistaken?’

What if those two were not experts, and she had merely mistaken them as such?

It was right then.

“Let’s go.”

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon was the first to move.

He strode straight toward Chuyeon, who was blocking the path, and she stepped aside without realizing it. If he could make her retreat without any aura or killing intent at all? Yes, it could not be a mistake.

At that moment, a shout rang out from deep inside the cave.

“The treasure map!”

Chuyeon flew toward the sound with her subordinates.

The porter’s voice came from behind her.

“Miss, be careful!”

Chuyeon bit her lip. If she were ever to see him again, please let it be when those two experts were not with him! Just once. Let him fall into her hands just once.

Geom Mugeuk started walking again, and the man and the two Demon Venerables followed behind him.

How long had they walked like that?

—Look there.

At the Ilhwa Sword Venerable’s sound transmission, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon saw the subtle mark carved into the wall.

—It’s a mark I secretly left when we came in.

It was something she had left at first in an attempt to remember the way. She had given up after the path became too complicated.

—The Young Cult Leader is going back exactly the way we came in.

—That brat must have come here in a previous life.

At the Blood Heaven Blade Demon’s words, the Ilhwa Sword Venerable smiled faintly. How could the two of them know? That this joke of the Blood Heaven Blade Demon’s was, in truth, precisely correct.

In that way, Geom Mugeuk led them out of the cave.

As it happened, when they emerged, there was no one outside.

Breathing the outside air, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon drew in a deep breath, his expression saying he finally felt alive.

“Did you come out because of me?”

He wondered unnecessarily whether Geom Mugeuk had given everything up and come out because he had seemed so stifled.

“I could have endured it. If you’re that worried about me, leave me here and go back in.”

“How could I go anywhere leaving you behind, Elder?”

“You go around just fine leaving me behind.”

At that, Geom Mugeuk smiled and said,

“Still, it wasn’t a complete waste, was it?”

Geom Mugeuk looked at the man. Perhaps afraid the three might change their minds, the man hurriedly bid farewell.

“Thank you for saving me. Then I will be on my way.”

Seeing his dejected figure, Geom Mugeuk thought that if they parted ways like this, he might go back into the cave.

“Wait a moment.”

Geom Mugeuk approached him.

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon and the Ilhwa Sword Venerable considered it only natural. Yes, the Young Cult Leader would not simply send him away. If it was the Young Cult Leader, he would surely ensure that the man’s sister would not be sold.

Sure enough, as though to give him money, Geom Mugeuk reached into his robes.

“Do you know what this is?”

But what emerged was not money. What Geom Mugeuk took out was a sheet of paper.

On the paper was a drawing of a person’s face. Beneath it, dense writing filled the page. It was the information sent from Eunwol.

“It’s the portrait and personal details of the man who was chased into Manhyeoldong with the treasure map. Well? Isn’t our information network incredible?”

As the unexpected situation unfolded, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon and the Ilhwa Sword Venerable opened their eyes wide. Neither of them had known Geom Mugeuk had received that information. They had sensed that, all along the way, he kept receiving something from Tongcheongak and Eunwol.

“The name of the man who snatched the treasure map is Tae Su, the son of Tae Gon, the great thief called the greatest thief of this era.”

Tae Gon was a name known to both the Blood Heaven Blade Demon and the Ilhwa Sword Venerable. He was that famous a thief.

Geom Mugeuk read aloud to the man the information written beneath the portrait.

“From a young age, Tae Su received training as a great thief from his father and was taught various techniques. He is skilled in deciphering codes and analyzing terrain, and has considerable knowledge of dismantling mechanisms. He has an outstanding memory, so once he sees something, he never forgets it. And that is not all. Having mastered the Bone-Shrinking Art, he can easily enter narrow spaces as well. A truly remarkable fellow, isn’t he? And as for his appearance…”

Geom Mugeuk held the paper with the portrait beside the man’s face so they could be seen side by side.

When the two faces were compared, the two Demon Venerables understood why Geom Mugeuk had suddenly said they should take only this man out with them. Geom Mugeuk had known from the moment he first saw him.

“It’s you, isn’t it?”

Chapter 736: You Have to Know How to Steal Hearts Too
-----------------------------------------------------
“It wasn’t me!”

The man jumped up and vehemently denied it. But anyone could see that the person in the painting looked exactly like him.

Geom Mugeuk looked at the painting again.

“Do you know what I thought when I first saw you? I should give the person who painted this a special reward and a vacation too.”

“No, it’s a misunderstanding!”

He stomped his feet, looking unbearably wronged. When they still didn’t believe him, he thumped his chest in frustration.

“It isn’t me. Surely there are people in the world who look alike? I need to hurry back and save my brother first. Please, let me go!”

Tears welled up in the man’s eyes. Just looking at him, it truly seemed like a misunderstanding.

Seeing that, Geom Mugeuk pointed to a part of the text written beneath the painting.

“Ah, look here. I missed this part while reading your information earlier. It says you act well and can deceive others perfectly, right?”

That was exactly what was written there.

“Didn’t you see when you searched my body earlier? I don’t have the treasure map.”

“I know. You already burned it, and it’s in your head. Wasn’t that written in what I showed you earlier? That you have excellent memory. You probably have an even greater ability for memorizing terrain.”

“No, why on earth are you doing this!”

“Why? Because it’s you.”

Geom Mugeuk grinned and poked his side in rapid succession.

Thwack! Thwack!

The next moment!

Pfff.

His body began to grow larger, as if air were entering it. His frame, previously as small and frail as a boy’s, became that of an ordinary man.

Geom Mugeuk had poked him playfully, but it was no joke. His Bone Shrinking Technique was undone, and he returned to his original body.

“Nice to meet you, Taesu.”

He was Taesu, the son of the great thief Taegon. He was surprised that the other had seen through his identity, but he was even more surprised that his Bone Shrinking Technique had been undone in an instant.

The surprise was not his alone.

The old man and woman accompanying this young fellow had not been particularly surprised even when his true form was revealed as the Bone Shrinking Technique was undone.

“Give me some water.”

At Hyeolcheon Doma’s words, Geom Mugeuk set down his pack and took out a bamboo container from inside.

“You should drink lots of water for your skin to get even better. Now that your skin has improved, you need to take care of it.”

Swoosh.

At that very moment, Taesu launched his body. His martial arts were mediocre, but he prided himself on having faster qinggong than anyone.

Having escaped that place in an instant, he leaped again. The distance would already have exceeded twenty *jang*, and by the second leap, fifty *jang*; by the time he landed after the third leap, he should have already vanished from their sight.

‘Just who are those people?’

To think they knew his identity so precisely.

Just as he landed after the third leap.

“Ugh!”

Taesu let out a groan and opened his eyes wide.

They were standing right in front of him.

Geom Mugeuk was handing the bamboo container he had taken out of his pack to Hyeolcheon Doma. Hyeolcheon Doma opened the lid again and drank water.

‘How on earth?’

For a moment, he wondered if he was dreaming. Or perhaps seeing an illusion?

But now wasn’t the time to worry about that.

Swoosh!

He stomped the ground and launched his body again. He moved at the fastest speed he could muster.

When he landed, the three people were before his eyes once more.

“Give me some too.”

This time, Ilhwa Geomjon was extending a hand.

Geom Mugeuk took out the bamboo container for Ilhwa Geomjon as well. Their actions were continuing exactly as they had been before he ran.

Taesu stood in a daze. He felt as if they were standing in front of the original cave and only the surrounding scenery kept changing.

But that wasn’t the case. He had clearly fled using his qinggong, and this was a different location.

They hadn’t chased after him quickly. They had arrived first and were continuing what they had been doing.

‘Just how fast are they?’

And Geom Mugeuk was even carrying that large pack, wasn’t he? But that wasn’t the only question.

‘How did they know I would flee in this direction?’

Geom Mugeuk put the bamboo container back into the pack and shouldered it.

“You… what are you?”

Taesu’s voice trembled involuntarily, as if he had seen a ghost.

The youngest ghost smiled and walked up to him.

“People get sensitive for no reason when they’re hungry, right?”

Geom Mugeuk waved his hand lightly and suppressed Taesu’s internal energy.

“Stop running and let’s eat first.”

* * *

A campfire blazed in a forest far from the Manhyeol Cave.

While Geom Mugeuk prepared the food, Taesu sat with his internal energy suppressed, watching him.

‘He’s preparing for torture.’

They wouldn’t be eating while leaving him at such an important moment.

They’d probably mix a confession drug into the food. Or poison him to threaten him.

They could force-feed him, but then he might attempt suicide or die from emotional shock without talking, so they must be trying to make him eat naturally.

‘Just who are these people?’

He had never heard of three such experts existing in the martial world. It was such a unique combination that rumors couldn’t help but spread.

Geom Mugeuk began stir-frying vegetables and mushrooms. He added salt and a secret seasoning as well.

Having finished cooking skillfully and quickly, Geom Mugeuk divided the stir-fried mushrooms into four bowls.

He brought one of them to Taesu.

“Here, eat.”

Geom Mugeuk released one of his hands so he could move it.

“I have no appetite.”

“Eat now so you don’t say you’re hungry later.”

He set the bowl of food down in front of him and walked to the pack.

Geom Mugeuk took out something wrapped in paper from the pack. He unfolded the paper to reveal moss inside.

When a unique scent wafted out, Taesu was startled.

‘Could this scent be? Jongyu Seontae?’

Geom Mugeuk divided the Jongyu Seontae and placed it only atop his own bowl and those of the two Demon Venerables.

“It’s too tasteless to eat on its own. But if you eat it like this together, it should be fine.”

Taesu’s eyes grew even wider. Those words were also true. Jongyu Seontae was known to be too tasteless to eat plain. Could it be? Was it real?

‘It can’t be. No matter what, they wouldn’t put Jongyu Seontae in a mushroom dish, would they?’

But its appearance and scent were exactly that of Jongyu Seontae. How much could a thief have studied elixirs?

‘No, it can’t be.’

Jongyu Seontae was truly a legendary elixir. Yes, it couldn’t be real. For starters, it made no sense for three people to divide and eat something so precious.

‘Ah! They’re trying to bait me.’

He could tell it was bait. If they pretended it was Jongyu Seontae and all three ate, wouldn’t he want to eat it too?

‘It must be fake Jongyu Seontae laced with poison or a confession drug.’

They must have already taken the antidote.

Geom Mugeuk looked at him and asked, picking up the Jongyu Seontae on his own bowl.

“Should I give you some of this too?”

Taesu inwardly sneered.

‘As expected! They don’t deviate an inch from my prediction.’

Taesu shook his head.

“I’m fine.”

Geom Mugeuk said with a surprised expression.

“To think you’d refuse this, impressive. You’ll regret it later, pounding the ground in frustration.”

Taesu snorted. It couldn’t be real. If it were real, they wouldn’t share Jongyu Seontae.

Hyeolcheon Doma compared the Jongyu Seontae in his own bowl with that of the other two and spoke.

“Mine looks like it has the most?”

His bowl had the most Jongyu Seontae piled on it.

“You’re mistaken.”

Of course, Hyeolcheon Doma could tell that Geom Mugeuk had given him the most.

Ilhwa Geomjon, seated across from him, gestured with her eyes as if to say, “Just pretend you don’t know and eat.”

“Come, eat quickly.”

“I truly never thought I’d eat something so precious with mushrooms.”

“Isn’t food that’s delicious going into your mouth better for your body?”

The three began to eat the dish.

“It’s tastier than I thought.”

Ilhwa Geomjon was satisfied with the taste, and Hyeolcheon Doma nodded as if in agreement.

And so the three finished all the Jongyu Seontae.

After consuming it all, without anyone saying who should go first, they sat down and circulated their energy.

After finishing their breathing exercises, the eyes of the three grew even deeper. Jongyu Seontae was known to restore the consumer’s vitality and grant a long, disease-free life.

“You look younger than before you ate.”

“Really?”

Geom Mugeuk drew his sword and used it as a mirror again.

“I can’t really tell.”

But there was definitely a difference.

The two elixirs consumed today had been a great help to Geom Mugeuk as well, but they had been truly tremendous help to Hyeolcheon Doma and Ilhwa Geomjon.

Hyeolcheon Doma, quietly gazing at his reflection in the blade, suddenly spoke. When in his life had he ever said these words? No, had he ever?

“This is fun.”

Ilhwa Geomjon looked at Hyeolcheon Doma with a surprised expression. Because she knew he wasn’t someone who said such things in his life, she well understood how much meaning those words held.

What would Geom Mugeuk answer?

Geom Mugeuk’s answer was simple.

“I am having fun too.”

Ilhwa Geomjon didn’t speak either, but she was enjoying this situation. It was a joy she hadn’t felt in a very long time.

Taesu, watching the three, felt a strange emotion.

‘What the hell are these people!’

They were really acting as if they had eaten Jongyu Seontae. And without paying him any mind at all.

Taesu cautiously asked Geom Mugeuk.

“What are you going to do with me now?”

Then, an unhesitating reply flowed from Geom Mugeuk’s mouth.

“We have to go find the Geomchong together.”

Who asked you! Taesu was inwardly speechless, but he continued his act.

“It’s truly a misunderstanding. I was there by chance.”

Taesu denied it to the end.

“I was training. Training to find my way in that complex cave, and training in the Bone Shrinking Technique.”

Geom Mugeuk smiled and took out from his bosom the portrait he had shown earlier.

“You probably don’t know how many people’s efforts are contained in this single sheet of paper before it came to my hands? Information imbued with the labor of dozens who have devoted their lives to one task. People several times smarter than you or me.”

The two Demon Venerables listening thought inwardly. It was all true, but.

“Not smarter than you.”

That was why the two were inwardly curious how Geom Mugeuk would find out the location of the treasure map.

“Denying the situation like this is the worst of the worst strategies. Ah, so this guy only uses worthless methods. He only looks foolish to the opponent. You’re not that kind of guy, are you? It’s written here that you’re not.”

Taesu stared at Geom Mugeuk for a moment and finally changed his strategy. Seeing his opponent, he realized that blindly insisting wouldn’t work.

“You’re right. I have the location memorized. Not just the location, but how to enter through the real entrance rather than the fake ones, and how to avoid the internal traps. Only I in this world know.”

It was only half true.

Because in his life before the regression, Geom Mugeuk had entered the Geomchong.

But back then, it hadn’t been because of a treasure scramble, so he hadn’t known much about the interior of the Geomchong. He had gone there to meet one person.

Geom Mugeuk gazed at Taesu in silence.

Taesu put strength into his stomach and eyes, not avoiding that gaze.

He felt a strange emotion from the clear eyes looking at him.

‘Why is he looking at me with those eyes?’

Hyeolcheon Doma, watching the two, sent a telepathic message to Ilhwa Geomjon.

—If we just beat him up, he’d spill everything.

Ilhwa Geomjon added:

—Or cut off his fingers one by one.

Hyeolcheon Doma spoke with a strange smile:

—The Young Cult Leader doesn’t know you’re someone who says such things, does he?

—It’s something only the two of us do.

Hyeolcheon Doma gave a slightly pleased smile at her words.

Geom Mugeuk neither beat him nor cut off his fingers.

Srrrung.

Geom Mugeuk drew the Heukmageom and spoke.

“Yes, a place like the Geomchong might be better off remaining a secret of the martial world forever.”

Geom Mugeuk raised his sword with a flash.

Shiiiiik.

Without hesitation, Geom Mugeuk brought the sword down toward Taesu’s neck.

“Wait!”

At Taesu’s cry, the sword stopped just before it cut his neck.

Trickle.

Blood flowed from the shallowly cut wound.

‘This crazy bastard really tried to kill me.’

Taesu spoke to Geom Mugeuk with eyes mixed with fear and shock.

“If you kill me, you die too.”

“Why would I?”

“You know who my father is, don’t you?”

Taegon, the greatest thief of the era.

“If I die, my father will definitely take revenge. My father has never failed to find someone or break into a place. He will silently enter your residence while you sleep. No matter how deep you hide, it will be useless.”

The corners of the two Demon Venerables’ mouths rose. They wondered how his expression would change if he knew where that “deep place” was.

Geom Mugeuk met threat with threat.

“Then I’ll have to take care of your father first.”

Taesu flinched in surprise for a moment. But he soon showed composure.

“You’ll never find my father’s whereabouts.”

“Yes, it won’t be easy. But it’s not impossible. First, I’ll spread rumors. That the one who has the Geomchong’s treasure map is Taegon. Then countless people will rush to find your father.”

Geom Mugeuk held up the portrait he had shown earlier.

“I’ll use my information network to find out everything about your father. I’ll find out his face as well and spread it throughout the Central Plains.”

“!”

“If your father, angered by this incident, comes to find you, I can deal with him then.”

Geom Mugeuk made his threat very specific and detailed, and it worked on Taesu.

When Taesu couldn’t offer any rebuttal, Geom Mugeuk withdrew the sword aimed at his neck.

“I told you to eat, didn’t I? Your stomach is empty, so your head isn’t working, which is why you’re making such clumsy threats. At times like this, you shouldn’t threaten, you should negotiate. ‘What will you give me if I tell you the location?’”

Taesu, lost in thought for a moment, asked.

“What will you give me?”

“That depends on what’s there.”

“How can I trust that you’ll save me when we get there?”

Then, unexpected words flowed from Geom Mugeuk.

“I believe that one who dreams of becoming the greatest thief of the era must not think that stealing is only about taking objects.”

Taesu inwardly flinched. Those were words his father had often said to him. They had come out exactly the same, not a single word wrong.

“To become the greatest thief, you must know how to steal people’s hearts as well.”

“!”

Surprise crossed Taesu’s face.

‘A coincidence?’

But it wasn’t a coincidence that Geom Mugeuk had said the exact same thing.

Because in his life before the regression, they were words Geom Mugeuk had heard directly from his father, Taegon.

The person he had met at the Geomchong back then was none other than Taegon.

“So try to steal my heart while we make our way there.”

Chapter 737: Only One Person Is Capable
---------------------------------------
“Have you ever met my father?”

He was saying the exact same words that his father had once said! Could it truly be a coincidence? Or could it be that this man somehow knew his father?

Geom Mugeuk shook his head.

“I have not.”

At least not in this life.

“But I have heard that he is a man of firm principles.”

When the word *principles* came out, Taesu’s expression stiffened slightly.

“I heard he only steals from the unjust. And that he shares part of his stolen goods with the poor?”

Taesu nodded silently.

He had taught even his son, Taesu, not to break those principles.

But Taesu had been dissatisfied with that teaching.

If he wanted to do such good deeds, he should have lived as a knight-errant instead. What thief dares to speak of conscience? Since he was already being called a thief anyway, he should have stolen from everyone without discrimination.

There came a day when those feelings finally exploded.

“Are those principles of yours for the sake of doing good? Or are they to rationalize your thievery?”

His father had not answered. At that moment, Taesu had seen it. The anger, disappointment, and confusion revealed on his father’s face.

He had not seen his father since that day.

“Father is……”

He had begun to speak, intending to say, *a great man.* For even a short while ago, when Geom Mugeuk had pointed his sword, it was his father whom he had used as a shield.

But the words had caught in his throat and would not come out.

It was Geom Mugeuk who continued the hesitant words.

“Fathers are difficult. For us sons, I mean.”

Taesu’s stiffened expression loosened slightly without him realizing it. At that moment, he once again saw the strange gleam in Geom Mugeuk’s eyes that he had noticed before.

*He’s looking at me with that gaze again.*

It was the gaze of someone who had something he wanted to say.

“Well then, we’ve eaten, so shall we be on our way?”

Geom Mugeuk walked ahead, and the two demonic lords followed behind him.

When Geom Mugeuk turned back after walking some distance, Taesu was still standing in the same spot.

“What are you doing? Aren’t you coming?”

“You must release my demonic acupoints before I can move.”

“They were released a while ago.”

The demonic acupoints that had subdued him were already released. He simply couldn’t use his internal energy, but his body could move freely.

*When did that happen?*

He hadn’t even sensed the wind of a finger flick approaching. How extraordinary this feat was, Hyeolcheon Doma and Ilhwa Geomjon understood far more clearly than the victim himself.

Geom Mugeuk’s group, coming down from the mountain, found the carriage they had left in the care of villagers and set off.

Hyeolcheon Doma and Ilhwa Geomjon rode inside the carriage, while Geom Mugeuk and Taesu sat on the driver’s seat.

As Geom Mugeuk started the carriage on his own accord, Taesu asked with a puzzled expression.

“Do you know where we are going?”

He had expected to be asked. At the very least, Geom Mugeuk should have asked if this direction was roughly correct.

“Until you tell me, I’ll go wherever my heart desires. If by chance I’m going too far in the opposite direction, just subtly pull the reins.”

Looking at that face that seemed like it might poke his side again at any moment, Taesu felt that figuring out what kind of person this young man was would be no easy task.

“Come to think of it, I don’t even know your name.”

Geom Mugeuk’s gaze deepened slightly. After watching the open road the carriage was traveling on for a moment, he spoke his name.

“I am Geom Mugeuk.”

Hyeolcheon Doma and Ilhwa Geomjon, riding in the carriage, exchanged glances.

They knew that Geom Mugeuk usually used the name Geom Yeon when he left the sect. Revealing his real name meant he was willing to reveal his identity as well.

Of course, Taesu didn’t know that the name of the Young Cult Leader of the Heavenly Demon Cult was Geom Mugeuk. Even if he had known, he would have thought it a case of the same name. There was no way the Young Cult Leader of the Heavenly Demon Cult would carry luggage like that and personally cook mushroom dishes to give to him.

Taesu glanced at Geom Mugeuk.

“Can you tell me which sect you belong to?”

“I could tell you, but it’s better if you don’t know.”

“Why is that?”

“Because if you knew, stealing my heart would become much more difficult. Just move as your heart tells you.”

Taesu misunderstood the reason Geom Mugeuk wouldn’t reveal his sect in this way:

*So he’s orthodox.*

If he were like those demonic cult or unorthodox bastards, he would have revealed his identity without being asked, just to intimidate him.

*He must be from a prestigious clan.*

Then what he needed to figure out was who the leader of these three was.

At a glance it seemed to be the old man, but watching them eat, it wasn’t necessarily so. Although the young man carried luggage and cooked, it felt like he held the initiative.

“Then may I ask what your relationship is with those two?”

A family elder? A master? But Geom Mugeuk’s answer was something he had never imagined.

“They are both my wings.”

At those words, Hyeolcheon Doma and Ilhwa Geomjon laughed together. The very two who had once competed over whether they were the left wing or the right wing were riding in that carriage.

“With just those two, I could even devour the Central Plains.”

“The three of you?”

“You’re on our side now too. The four of us.”

Taesu turned his head toward the opposite side of the carriage.

*Steal the heart of this unpredictable person?*

The most difficult theft he had ever attempted was just beginning.

* * *

The carriage stopped beside a lakeside.

“Get down and look over there.”

At Geom Mugeuk’s words, Hyeolcheon Doma and Ilhwa Geomjon got down from the carriage.

The setting sun draped over a large lake beside the road. The light stretching toward the sky and the light seeping into the lake intertwined to create a magnificent spectacle.

Geom Mugeuk lightly hopped up and sat on the carriage roof.

“This is truly spectacular.”

Hyeolcheon Doma and Ilhwa Geomjon nodded together. It was the first time the two had watched such a beautiful sunset together.

Geom Mugeuk had stopped the carriage for the sake of these two.

*Take this moment in to your heart’s content!*

While the three were gazing at that spectacle, Taesu sat on the driver’s seat looking in the opposite direction of the lake.

He could understand the two experts in the carriage, but why would someone so young be so enthralled by a sunset?

It all felt like hypocrisy and pretense. He was probably trying to suck up to the two experts in the carriage.

*How should I escape?*

At this rate, he would die at their hands. He had to take them to the Geomchong first, then use the mechanisms there to escape or eliminate them.

It was then.

*Swoosh.*

His body was pulled by an unknown force and soared through the air, then dropped down beside Geom Mugeuk, who was sitting on the carriage roof. Geom Mugeuk had seated him by his side using Vacuum Draw.

“You were just thinking wicked thoughts, weren’t you? Studying how to kill us and shake us off? Planning to use the mechanisms at the Geomchong to kill us?”

“No!”

Taesu’s voice trembled at the sudden strike to the heart of the matter. It was impossible to prepare for such pinpoint accuracy.

“You can think wicked thoughts later, but this is a sight you won’t see later. Watch it unconditionally while you can. There is no later.”

Why is there no later? He was risking his life to survive for that *later.*

Taesu forcibly swallowed the words that had risen to his throat and looked at the sunset.

Just then, birds flying low over the lake grazed the water’s surface, and droplets splashed up, bursting into red hues. It was beautiful.

Suddenly, Taesu realized this was the first time he had seen such a sight. He had never stood by a lake at sunset before.

His life had been one of being chased or chasing, lived so busily.

“If I pretend to feel that the scenery is beautiful by your side, can I steal your heart?”

Geom Mugeuk smiled and nodded.

“Yes. Because then I might think, ‘At least this fellow is making an effort.’”

Taesu looked at Geom Mugeuk.

“Even if that is deceiving you?”

“Isn’t sitting still without making any effort a deception in itself?”

Taesu bit his lip slightly and steeled his resolve.

“Very well. If you wish, I can actively put on a show of hypocrisy.”

A smile formed on Geom Mugeuk’s lips.

“Good, then go ahead and try.”

Geom Mugeuk watched the sunset and waited.

But now that the stage was set, his mouth wouldn’t open.

“It’s not difficult, is it? Just smile and say, ‘It is truly beautiful.’ That alone would suffice. ‘Thank you for seating me here like this.’ That would be even better.”

But even though Geom Mugeuk even set an example, the words wouldn’t come easily.

When they first met, he had tried to deceive him with tears welling in his eyes. He had prided himself on never being outdone in acting. Was it because of pride? Or perhaps?

“I’ll do it later.”

“Alright, then.”

Geom Mugeuk did not press him at all.

Hyeolcheon Doma, standing side by side below the carriage and gazing at the lake, sent a telepathic message to Ilhwa Geomjon.

*That thief will have a hard time too. He’s been told to steal the most difficult thing to steal in the world.*

Hyeolcheon Doma knew. Geom Mugeuk seemed to give his heart to anyone freely, but that was absolutely not the case. He knew better than anyone how difficult it was to truly win that heart.

*Only one person has succeeded in stealing it.*

*Who?*

Ilhwa Geomjon looked at Hyeolcheon Doma.

*Me?*

Hyeolcheon Doma shook his head as if to say that was absurd.

*Not me. Perhaps if it were the Masked Warrior, maybe?*

*Is that so? I thought it was you, Brother.*

At the word *Brother,* Hyeolcheon Doma flinched. It was a form of address he hadn’t heard in a long time.

It was something he could only hear when Ilhwa Geomjon was in a good mood.

*You are in as good a mood as I am.*

It was right then.

*Thud-thud-thud-thud-thud.*

A group of martial artists rushed to the spot. Their numbers reached several dozen. In an instant, they surrounded and encircled the carriage. Each and every one of them possessed no ordinary aura.

A familiar face walked out from among them.

“Hey, porter.”

The woman who walked forward was none other than Chuyeon, the daughter of the Biryongbang Leader.

There were now only four of the Biryong Ten Geol standing behind her. They had lost six of the Biryong Ten Geol in that cave.

“We searched everywhere for your corpses, but they were nowhere to be found.”

They had not found the Jangbyeondo either.

Her gaze turned toward Taesu.

“You’re taking him with you.”

“We are traveling together to save my younger sister.”

Chuyeon snorted.

“Do you take me for a fool? You think powerful experts like yourselves would move for a nobody like him?”

She didn’t believe him. There were far too many suspicious points.

Her gaze turned toward Hyeolcheon Doma and Ilhwa Geomjon. They weren’t even looking this way; they were gazing at the lake.

The two were no different now than they had been in the cave. They still paid her no mind whatsoever.

*Let’s see how far they can look down on me.*

Then a white-haired man walked out from behind Chuyeon. He was Jongbaek (宗伯), the elder of the Biryongbang.

He was the Biryongbang expert whom the Leader—her father—trusted most. Not only did he possess powerful martial arts, but he also had extensive knowledge of the martial world.

His temperament was terrifyingly fiery, making him the person Chuyeon feared most.

“Are you certain it’s them?”

“Yes, they must surely possess the Jangbyeondo.”

Jongbaek imbued his voice with internal energy as he spoke.

“This one is called Jongbaek, serving as an elder of the Biryongbang. Might I ask where you esteemed guests have come from?”

He was asking politely, but Jongbaek had drawn a blade in his heart.

No matter who the opponent was, he intended to secure the Jangbyeondo. This time, the Biryongbang had staked the life and death of the sect on obtaining the Jangbyeondo. They had suffered too much damage in the Manhyeoldong already; they could not retreat like this.

Jongbaek’s gaze turned toward Hyeolcheon Doma and Ilhwa Geomjon, who still stood with their backs turned.

*As expected, they are no ordinary individuals.*

Outwardly, it was impossible to gauge their strength at all.

But that was fine. Their forces here would not lose no matter who they fought.

His gaze returned to Geom Mugeuk.

“Then you answer me.”

“We are not a great sect like the Biryongbang. How could we reveal ourselves in the midst of a struggle for the Jangbyeondo? I hope you will understand.”

Jongbaek assessed Geom Mugeuk. Then Chuyeon stepped forward and spoke politely.

“He is merely a porter. There is no need to concern yourself with him greatly.”

But Jongbaek could see that Chuyeon was misjudging her opponent. The young man before him showed no trace of having learned martial arts, yet he exuded a presence greater than anyone else present.

“Very well. Then is it true that you possess the Jangbyeondo?”

“You are mistaken, young lady.”

Chuyeon stepped forward and spoke.

"If you are so confident, then allow us to search your belongings."

"As for my body and luggage, I can show them to you as much as you wish. However, those two cannot be searched. There is only one person in this world capable of that."

When Geom Mugik refused, a palpable tension filled the air.

At that very moment, a calm yet elegant voice resonated through the area.

"Long time no see, Elder Jong."

Ilhwa Geomjon slowly turned to look their way.

Jong Baek finally saw her face.

It was clearly a face he had seen somewhere, yet he could not recall it. The voice, too, was one he had definitely heard before.

'Who is it?'

His survival instinct urged him on. He had to remember quickly.

Ilhwa Geomjon slowly walked toward Jong Baek.

"I have changed so much that it will not be easy to recognize me."

Then, the scabbard at Ilhwa Geomjon's waist caught Jong Baek's eye.

A red camellia flower, engraved as vividly as blood.

Jong Baek's gaze returned to Ilhwa Geomjon. Having recognized his opponent, he was startled in shock.

"You!"

Only then did he realize that his opponent was Ilhwa Geomjon. As she had said, she was completely different from before. Back then, she had worn heavy makeup and flashy clothes.

He had failed to recall her simply because he had never imagined meeting Ilhwa Geomjon in a place like this.

Before he could say a word, Ilhwa Geomjon turned to Geom Mugik and spoke calmly.

"We are on a journey escorting a precious person."

Jong Baek trembled as if struck by lightning. For the words "precious person" to come from the mouth of the Demon Lord—

He looked at Ilhwa Geomjon and spoke calmly.

"Our side has committed a grave mistake. This old man wishes to seek your forgiveness by cutting off one arm; please accept it."

At those words, Chuyeon was shocked to the core. That fearsome and mighty Elder Jong would cut off his own arm without even fighting? Not only her, but all the martial artists with her were thrown into confusion.

Without hesitation, Jong Baek drew his sword.

"I beg for your forgiveness."

Shiiik!

Without a moment to stop him, he swung his sword down at his own arm.

Chuyeon let out a scream.

*Clack!*

The next moment!

Jong Baek was more shocked than if his arm had been severed.

Geom Mugik had somehow come right up to him and seized his wrist.

He had failed to perceive his opponent's movements for even an instant, right up until the moment his wrist was captured.

In a flashing instant, he was caught. If his opponent had stabbed at his neck instead of grabbing his arm, he would have died on the spot.

'......I am nothing but a one-second opponent to him.'

It was truly unbelievable. His opponent was not the Demonic Cult Leader, but the Young Cult Leader.

To him frozen in shock, Geom Mugik spoke with a smile.

"Elder Jong, there is something you must do with this precious arm."

Chapter 738: Do You Know Whose Tomb This Is?
--------------------------------------------
Jongbaek had heard all sorts of rumors about the young cult leader of the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult.

Incredible, outstanding, and absurd.

If he had to describe in a single word how he felt now, facing the young cult leader in person, it would be this:

Overwhelming.

So much so that he could not even breathe.

“Haa.”

When the breath Jongbaek had been holding slipped from his mouth, Geom Mugeuk released the wrist he had been gripping.

He had snatched up an arm that had been chopping down with speed in an instant; it would have been only natural for a mark to remain on the wrist.

Yet there was no trace whatsoever on his wrist. As though no one had ever grabbed it in the first place.

This was precisely the difference!

What distinguished masters who had climbed and climbed until they had nearly reached the final realm was not a difference in who could strike and smash with greater force. It was not how high they could fly or how quickly they could move.

Jongbaek knew well that the true gap in skill was revealed in subtle, seemingly trivial differences like this.

Jongbaek’s gaze, which had been fixed on his own wrist, turned toward Geom Mugeuk.

‘This man is the young cult leader of the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult.’

Jongbaek could sense no malice in Geom Mugeuk’s gentle eyes as he looked at him. That was what made him even more frightening. After all, the other party was the young cult leader of the Demonic Cult.

Just what did he want? What if he demanded something even more important than his arm?

“What is it you desire?”

Meanwhile, Chuyeon and the other warriors watching from the side had no idea how astonishing Geom Mugeuk’s single move had been. All of them had been so fixated on Jongbaek cutting off his arm that they assumed Geom Mugeuk must have arrived in the midst of it.

Under normal circumstances, Chuyeon would have stepped forward and said something.

How dare you lay a hand on him!

But the atmosphere was strange and unreadable, and thanks to him intervening, Jongbaek’s arm had been spared.

She could not understand this situation. The Jongbaek she had known since childhood was an invincible master. There had never been a matter he could not resolve once he stepped forward, and whenever a crisis came to the Flying Dragon Gang, he was always the first to stand at the front.

‘But why? Just who is that porter?’

Why was he offering up his wrist and preparing to grant a request?

At last, Geom Mugeuk opened his mouth.

“You have two arms, so I have two requests.”

At those words, Jongbaek flinched inwardly.

‘I was only going to cut off one arm.’

Of course, it had been Geom Mugeuk’s joke. He simply had two things to ask.

But everyone was so tense and shocked that the only ones who gave even a faint smile at his words were the Blood Heaven Blade Demon and the One Blossom Sword Venerable.

“My first request is…”

Geom Mugeuk looked at Chuyeon. Jongbaek immediately understood what Geom Mugeuk meant to say. She had treated the young cult leader of the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult as a mere porter and dismissed him; even if he killed her, there would be nothing to say in her defense.

And since he had said they had met three times in the cave, her rudeness must not have happened only once.

“What are you doing? Hurry and apologize!”

At Jongbaek’s thunderous shout, Chuyeon dropped flat to the ground on the spot.

“I failed to recognize an honored person and have committed a sin worthy of death!”

Chuyeon had known Jongbaek since she was young. Whenever he saw her, he had doted on her and cherished her more than anyone. This was the first time she had ever seen him so angry.

Jongbaek spoke to Geom Mugeuk with utmost courtesy.

“She is a thoughtless child who made a mistake. Please, show mercy.”

He was worried that Geom Mugeuk might demand Chuyeon’s arm instead of his own.

If the young cult leader wanted to punish her, he intended to stop it even if he had to offer his own life in her place. If he could not protect the girl he had watched over since she was young, he would have no face to show the leader of the Flying Dragon Gang.

Geom Mugeuk walked over to Chuyeon and helped her to her feet.

“I never told you who I was, so it was only natural that you did not recognize me.”

When Geom Mugeuk treated her gently, she was left bewildered. She had expected to be shouted at for failing to recognize him.

“Do you remember? That we said we should meet alive even a fourth time?”

“Of course.”

Geom Mugeuk conveyed what he wished to say to the frightened woman.

“But what matters is not that fourth meeting. What matters is those four.”

Geom Mugeuk’s gaze turned toward the Ten Heroes of the Flying Dragon standing behind her. Of the ten, only four had survived.

He meant that meeting him a fourth time was not the important thing; the more serious and important matter was that only four of her subordinates remained.

It meant she should not have clung to her greed for the treasure map until all her subordinates were dying around her.

Even now, the treasure map probably weighed more heavily in her heart than the deaths of her subordinates.

“If you do not understand the difference, you will not live to meet me a fifth time.”

“Yes, I will keep it in mind.”

She answered well, but Chuyeon did not understand what Geom Mugeuk meant. What on earth was so important about four?

Geom Mugeuk looked at Jongbaek and said,

“Now do you know what you must do with that arm?”

He meant that rather than cutting it off, he should take a switch in that hand and properly teach this reckless, arrogant girl.

Understanding Geom Mugeuk’s meaning, Jongbaek let out a long sigh.

“I have used the busyness of my life as an excuse and failed to pay attention to what is most important in our gang.”

If the busy gang leader could not do it, then he should have paid attention himself.

The problem was that he had only indulged and adored her too much. Since she had always been respectful toward him, he had assumed she behaved the same way outside.

“I will teach her properly.”

Chuyeon’s life, with her head bowed deeply, would become very difficult from now on.

‘For the sake of your own life, endure it well and work hard, you immature young lady.’

Geom Mugeuk turned back toward Jongbaek. Jongbaek grew tense inwardly.

This would be the real demand. If anything, Jongbaek became even more nervous. He expected Geom Mugeuk to demand something greater in exchange for forgiving Chuyeon.

Yet the second request, too, was something he had never imagined.

“Persuade the leader of the Flying Dragon Gang and do not get involved in this matter any further. At the very least, you must not meet me again over the matter of the treasure map.”

Geom Mugeuk deliberately spoke firmly. For the sake of the Flying Dragon Gang as well, they could not become involved in this affair.

Jongbaek did not hesitate to answer.

“I will keep it in mind. This old man will take responsibility and obey your words.”

“Thank you, Elder Jong.”

Jongbaek asked cautiously,

“Is that all you wished to say?”

“Yes. After all, you only have two arms, don’t you?”

Even if he had not said it outright, it was only natural that the Flying Dragon Gang would withdraw. Having become entangled with the young cult leader of the Demonic Cult like this, how could they continue trying to obtain the treasure map?

Seeing Geom Mugeuk offer a courteous salute, Jongbaek was finally able to understand. Both requests had been made for the sake of the Flying Dragon Gang.

He was someone who had no need to do this.

‘Even if the young cult leader came alone, our gang would be annihilated.’

He was someone who could have beaten Chuyeon half to death and threatened Jongbaek that if he interfered again, he would die.

Jongbaek’s gaze turned to the One Blossom Sword Venerable standing beside him. He truly had changed a great deal since the last time they had met. And that change was not limited to his appearance.

‘He has grown stronger! Incomparably so.’

Even before, Jongbaek could not have opposed him; now, even daring to stand face-to-face with him felt burdensome.

Finally, Jongbaek’s gaze went to the Blood Heaven Blade Demon, who still had his back turned. The great blade on his back was wrapped in cloth.

He had not turned around, but now Jongbaek knew who he was.

‘The Blood Heaven Blade Demon!’

If he were the Blood Heaven Blade Demon of rumor, he would already have stepped forward and swept them all away.

‘The Heavenly Demon Divine Cult has changed!’

Jongbaek silently offered a cupped-fist salute to the Blood Heaven Blade Demon as well. It was not because he was frightened of the Demonic Cult and blindly lowering his head.

He was expressing sincere gratitude for the mercy Geom Mugeuk and the two Demon Venerables had shown.

“Thank you truly for showing mercy.”

“Thank you truly for readily accepting my wishes.”

Geom Mugeuk also returned a courteous cupped-fist salute, preserving his dignity in front of his subordinates.

Jongbaek left the place with Chuyeon and his subordinates.

Chuyeon glanced once at Geom Mugeuk, then lowered her head deeply and left.

There would come a time when, after she had grown older, she would look back on this day. He could only hope that she would live a life in which today remained a shameful memory.

In the meantime, the lake where the setting sun had been descending grew dark.

“Let’s spend the night here today.”

Geom Mugeuk drove the carriage to a field near the lakeside.

After unharnessing the horses and letting them rest, he lit a campfire.

The two Demon Venerables went together and caught something for dinner. Geom Mugeuk dressed it with practiced hands and served up an excellent meal.

Taesu had not eaten the food before, but this time he silently ate everything Geom Mugeuk gave him. He now knew they were not the kind of people who would put poison in the food or mix in a confession drug.

After the meal, the two Demon Venerables went out for a stroll along the lakeshore, leaving only the two of them there.

“That thing you refused to eat last time, saying you wouldn’t have it. It really was stalactite immortal moss.”

“I know.”

He had thought of that a little while ago while eating. If it was these people, then that, too, must have been genuine.

“If I had said I would eat it, would you really have given it to me?”

“I would have given you a little of mine.”

Instead of thinking, He never would have given it to me, Taesu found himself wondering, Would he really have given it to me?

Then Taesu immediately scolded himself.

Given what? Something that precious? Don’t get confused by that man!

“Why don’t you ask? Who are you people?”

At Geom Mugeuk’s question, Taesu looked into the blazing fire and answered,

“I’m not curious.”

In truth, he was curious. He had also been terribly shocked to see an elder of the Flying Dragon Gang so frightened.

At first, he had actually suspected that these people might be from the Flying Dragon Gang. Perhaps they were on the same side, and this was all an act arranged to frighten him.

But he soon thought that could not be true. If they had wanted to, they could simply have tortured him outright; there was no reason for them to make things so difficult.

“If I ask who you are, will you answer?”

“No.”

Taesu lifted one corner of his mouth as if he had known it.

“Do you know why I can’t tell you?”

“What is it?”

“The way you speak has changed.”

“!”

Now that he heard it, that was true.

Before meeting Elder Jong of the Flying Dragon Gang, he had spoken more casually and bluntly, but now he was addressing Geom Mugeuk with great politeness.

“I… did not notice.”

“You don’t have to notice that, but you do need to know this. If you learn that I have power and belong to a famous house, you will give up on stealing my heart.”

“Why do you think so?”

“Because you have the kind of personality that can’t say even one simple thing like, ‘The scenery beside you is so beautiful.’ You have an enormous amount of pride.”

Taesu could not refute that.

“Pride is important. But do not forget this: if your pride is too high, in the end you will be forced to submit from a low place.”

Those words stabbed into his chest because he knew that he was not the sort of person who could protect even that paltry pride to the very end.

Taesu stared silently at the flickering campfire, then asked Geom Mugeuk,

“With your ability, whether by threats or torture, you could find out everything you want from me. Why are you giving me these chances?”

Then an unexpected answer came.

“In the past, I owed a debt to a thief.”

This man? Just who had he owed, and what kind of debt? It did not seem like such a thing could happen.

“Who was it?”

Taesu knew the lineages of most thieves in the Central Plains by heart. If Geom Mugeuk only said the name, he would know who it was, but Geom Mugeuk did not tell him.

“Then answer this. I am not even the person involved, so why are you trying to repay me?”

Geom Mugeuk flopped down where he was and looked up at the stars in the night sky as he said,

“I don’t think I’ll ever meet a thief again in my life with such a deep connection. So just take it! Didn’t I tell you? You used up all the gambling luck of your lifetime when you met me. Then you ought to gain something from it too.”

Taesu silently looked at Geom Mugeuk, then at the campfire again, and then up at the night sky where tiny sparks rose into the air.

After remaining deep in thought like that for a long while, Taesu abruptly brought up the past.

“Forty years ago, a single treasure map appeared in the world.”

Still lying down, Geom Mugeuk turned his head and looked at him. Beyond the campfire, Taesu continued speaking.

“The murim was turned upside down by the rumor that it was a treasure map marking the location of a tomb filled with enough riches to let one live as a tycoon for generations. Only, whose tomb it was was never revealed.”

“Like now?”

“Yes, like now.”

As always, the history of the murim was repeating itself.

“That tomb is this very Sword Tomb. At the time, peerless masters entered it, and not a single one came out alive. Leaving aside all the treasures inside, what if one could obtain the weapons of those masters? What if there happened to be martial arts manuals as well? That alone would make it worth risking one’s life.”

Then, from Taesu’s mouth came a fact even Geom Mugeuk did not know.

“Do you know whose tomb that originally was?”

When Geom Mugeuk shook his head, Taesu mentioned one person. It was a fact unknown to the world.

“It is Zhu Jiashen, the One Sword of the Evil Path.”

Zhu Jiashen, the One Sword of the Evil Path.

He was a figure who, three hundred years ago, had been famous as the leader of the Evil Path Alliance and the greatest master of the unorthodox factions.

“The Sword Tomb is his tomb.”

Geom Mugeuk, who had been lying down, sat up.

“Why are you telling me this?”

“Why did you tell me you owed a debt to a thief?”

Silence flowed between them, and their gazes briefly intertwined in midair.

Taesu had revealed the secret because he had made up his mind.

“It does not matter who you are. From now until we reach the Sword Tomb, I will steal your heart and survive. Not only my life—I will also obtain the treasure I want from that place. I will stake everything on this contest with you.”

Then he would show his father. He would show him that he had survived by stealing the heart of someone truly extraordinary.

“The Sword Tomb is in Zhejiang Province.”

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon and the One Blossom Sword Venerable, who were returning from their stroll, also heard his words.

“I will tell you the exact location once we arrive.”

Knowing that Taesu’s words were not false, Geom Mugeuk said to the two Demon Venerables,

“Starting tomorrow, we head for Zhejiang Province.”

Once the destination was set, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon felt regretful inwardly.

‘Why is he in such a hurry!’

He could have spoken a little more slowly. They could have wandered a little longer wherever their feet carried them.

Then again, after being caught by whom, how could he hold out without talking?

Yes, and who would not understand the Blood Heaven Blade Demon’s regretful heart?

“We need to give this friend here a chance, so we’ll go slowly!”

Chapter 739: If Not Us or You
-----------------------------
The carriage passed along a narrow road.

Whenever the lush blades of grass by the roadside brushed against the carriage, they made a soft rustling sound.

Taesu glanced at Geom Mugeuk, who was driving the carriage beside him.

‘What is he going to do if this ends in a dead end?’

They wouldn’t be able to turn the carriage around, would they?

What if, at the very least, they ran into another carriage? Ah, on second thought, that probably wouldn’t happen. No one would drive a carriage into such a narrow forest path.

But Geom Mugeuk drove the carriage with confidence, as if he knew the road.

Inside the carriage, the Blood Heaven Saber Demon was watching the grass brush past the window.

‘Is he taking a shortcut?’

Otherwise, there was no reason he would have chosen such a narrow path.

‘He said we’d take it slow, so what’s the rush?’

The Blood Heaven Saber Demon’s gaze turned toward the Ilhwa Sword Venerable. She had her eyes closed, circulating her qi. She had always looked young to begin with, but after taking the Seondoyeonggwa and Jongyuseontae, it seemed as if her skin was glowing.

Just then, the Ilhwa Sword Venerable opened her eyes and said,

“I hear water.”

Sure enough, from far outside the carriage came the sound of water. Was he planning to cross a river with the carriage?

And just as the sound of water seemed to grow louder and louder—

The moment the carriage left the forest trail, a magnificent sight unfolded before them.

Kwaaaaaa!

The powerful roar of water echoed through the valley.

Stopping the carriage, Geom Mugeuk called out loudly,

“Please get down for a moment, both of you.”

The Blood Heaven Saber Demon and the Ilhwa Sword Venerable stepped out of the carriage.

Before their eyes spread a grand waterfall that drew admiration the instant they saw it.

White torrents of water poured down from a sheer cliff, crashing against the rocks and spraying upward. The mist met the sunlight, forming a rainbow that made them feel as if they had stepped into an immortal paradise.

The four of them silently gazed at the waterfall.

There was a tranquility born from its grandeur. Standing here, one felt as though they could remain for an entire day.

“How did you know of a place like this?”

At the Blood Heaven Saber Demon’s admiration, Geom Mugeuk seized the chance to boast as if he had been waiting for it.

“I studied up in advance so I could show it to you, Elder.”

As if that could be true. Until yesterday, he hadn’t even expected they would be heading toward Zhejiang.

Moreover, this waterfall was in an extremely secluded place. In other words, the fact that Geom Mugeuk had driven the carriage all the way into this corner meant he had already been here before.

“If we head back out that way later, there’s a restaurant by the road that makes incredible beef noodles. Let’s eat there before we go.”

Then he looked at the Blood Heaven Saber Demon and added,

“You like beef noodles, don’t you?”

The Ilhwa Sword Venerable slipped into the conversation about his tastes.

“You still have the same palate.”

“Did he like beef noodles even when he was young?”

“Oh, he liked them so much. He ate them every day.”

The Blood Heaven Saber Demon lightly waved his hand.

“You remember the strangest things. And it wasn’t to that extent.”

Even so, the fact that the Ilhwa Sword Venerable remembered those days put him in a subtly good mood.

“Shall we get going?”

The Blood Heaven Saber Demon was grateful to Geom Mugeuk. As if this waterfall was somewhere he had come because he himself wanted to see it. He had driven down that narrow path to show it to him and the Ilhwa Sword Venerable.

“My eyes have truly feasted.”

That was how the Blood Heaven Saber Demon conveyed his thanks.

The carriage set off again.

Geom Mugeuk did not turn the carriage back the way they had come, but found another path out. After running along the narrow road for quite some time, they emerged onto a main road.

“How do you know the roads so well?”

“I know because I’ve traveled a lot.”

Considering Geom Mugeuk’s young age, Taesu could not understand it.

‘Could his family perhaps run an escort agency? Is that why he’s been all over the Central Plains since he was young?’

He thought that might be the case. Just as Taesu was making a wild guess while recalling the escort agencies he knew—

Geom Mugeuk suddenly grabbed Taesu and pulled him to the side.

Shiiiiik.

Thud!

A dagger flew in and embedded itself in the spot where Taesu’s face had just been.

At the sudden ambush, Taesu’s eyes widened in shock.

“Take the reins!”

The moment Geom Mugeuk handed the reins to Taesu—

Swish, swish, swish, swish, swish, swish!

Hidden weapons flew in from every direction. Since they were aimed at the horses, Geom Mugeuk launched himself forward.

Clang, clang, clang, clang, clang!

Taesu was astonished once again by Geom Mugeuk’s lightning-fast movements. Having knocked away the hidden weapons flying in from the left, Geom Mugeuk was already deflecting those from the right.

The deflected hidden weapons flew back precisely toward those who had thrown them, and screams rang out from the forests on both sides.

His movements were so fast that, in Taesu’s eyes, it looked as if twins were knocking away hidden weapons from both sides at the same time.

No, triplets. Before he knew it, Geom Mugeuk was sitting beside him again, taking the reins back.

“Hold on tight!”

When Geom Mugeuk pulled the reins to the side, the carriage changed direction.

Pak, pak, pak, pak, pak!

The hidden weapons that would have struck the carriage missed and buried themselves in the ground. When the next attack came flying in, he avoided it by slowing their speed.

To think he could drive a carriage this well!

Taesu was certain.

‘He’s definitely from an escort agency!’

He had probably followed along since childhood and learned how to handle a carriage.

Just then, something entered Taesu’s sight.

“There! Look over there!”

Up ahead, a tree had fallen across the middle of the road. It had been felled to stop the carriage. And it was no mere log. A massive tree had been brought down, so there was no way to simply pass over it.

“Hold on tight!”

It was obviously a situation where they should stop the carriage, but Geom Mugeuk increased their speed even more.

He was going to run? Surely he didn’t mean to jump over that?

“Stop! No! This is a carriage, not a horse!”

Despite Taesu’s shout, Geom Mugeuk charged forward even faster like a madman.

“Here we go!”

“Uaaaaaaah!”

At the very moment Taesu screamed—

The horses collided with the tree. No, he thought they had collided.

In that instant, the horses leaped over the tree and soared into the air.

‘We’re getting over this? This isn’t just a horse, it’s a carriage!’

As if it were happening in a dream, everything seemed to move slowly for a moment.

Rattle.

The carriage that had leaped over the fallen tree began racing swiftly forward again.

When Taesu looked back, masked men emerged from the forests on both sides far behind them. They too were staring this way with startled expressions.

“How did the carriage jump over that?”

How else? Geom Mugeuk had used Object Seizing Through Empty Space to lift the horses and carriage into the air and send them over in one piece.

“This carriage has wings riding in it!”

Earlier, Taesu had asked Geom Mugeuk who the two experts riding in the carriage were. At that time, Geom Mugeuk had called the two of them his wings.

Then had they done it? Was such a thing possible, even for experts?

When the carriage had just flown into the air, the left wing inside the carriage had looked out the window and said,

“We really are flying.”

At that, the right wing smiled and said,

“Who else but the Young Cult Leader would even think of something like this?”

Lifting a moving carriage with Object Seizing Through Empty Space to avoid an obstacle? It was something they could not do.

Even if they had the ability to pull it off, they would not have thought to jump over it.

They would have stopped the carriage and killed all the pursuers instead.

“Thanks to him, I even get to ride a carriage through the sky.”

Seeing the Ilhwa Sword Venerable looking excited, the Blood Heaven Saber Demon smiled as well. Truly, there was not a single dull moment on this journey.

Unlike the two at peace inside, Taesu was racing through an adventure in which his life hung by a thread.

Looking back, Taesu shouted,

“There! They’re chasing us!”

Behind them, about twenty masked men were pursuing the carriage with lightness skill.

“Now, take the reins again.”

Geom Mugeuk rose from the driver’s seat, opened the door of the moving carriage, and went inside.

He smiled at the two Demon Venerables and said,

“Once you come out into the Central Plains, carriage chases are the sort of thing that happen all the time, aren’t they?”

Geom Mugeuk opened his leather pouch and began searching for something. Looking inside the pouch, the Blood Heaven Saber Demon frowned.

“Why do you have so much junk packed in there to clutter the mind?”

“It may look that way, but when you don’t have these things, it’s terribly inconvenient. Ah, here it is.”

What Geom Mugeuk found was a leather belt with about twenty daggers stuck into it.

“I said I wouldn’t draw my sword either, so I’ll make do with this.”

“It’s all the same.”

As the Blood Heaven Saber Demon said, how could daggers thrown by Geom Mugeuk be ordinary daggers?

Geom Mugeuk strapped the leather belt to himself and climbed onto the carriage roof.

In the meantime, the masked men had nearly caught up to the carriage.

Geom Mugeuk drew a dagger and threw it at the masked man charging closest to them.

Shik!

The dagger cut through the air, and the masked man flipped over and collapsed.

When the dagger had first flown toward him, he had thought he could avoid something of that level easily enough, but he had failed to dodge it.

One man fell, but the enemies continued to pursue them.

Shik! Shik!

This time, two daggers flew.

It was not an attack as fast as light like before. They flew at a speed that made one think they could avoid them, but this time too, they could not.
