Because the daggers, which had flown in normally, changed speed and direction at the very last moment. Because of that, the one who tried to knock one away with his sword could not block it, and the one who twisted his body to evade met the same fate.

Even after two more fell, they still kept running.

As if to show them clearly, Geom Mugeuk took out three daggers this time. He deliberately held them out in front for them to see. This time, three!

Then one of the masked men at the rear raised his hand and stopped the others. The pursuers all halted at once.

Geom Mugeuk could tell that he was their leader.

Not only had the man realized that three of his subordinates would surely die again this time as well, but from Geom Mugeuk’s dagger-throwing skill alone, he had instinctively understood that they could not handle him.

Geom Mugeuk’s gaze met the man’s.

The eyes above the mask were calm. The other party was someone who knew how to give up decisively when it was time to give up.

Watching him turn away with the masked men, Geom Mugeuk returned to the driver’s seat.

“Are you all right?”

“Yes. Thank you for saving my life.”

Taesu bowed his head and expressed his gratitude. If Geom Mugeuk had not pulled him aside when the first dagger flew in, he would have died.

“Where are we going now?”

Since they had pursuers, Taesu thought they would surely come up with some sort of countermeasure. Wouldn’t they have to abandon the carriage and move with lightness skill?

But the one holding the reins was none other than Geom Mugeuk.

“To eat beef noodles.”

* * *

Half a double-hour later, the four of them really were eating beef noodles.

“It tastes good.”

The Blood Heaven Saber Demon was satisfied with the flavor. It truly was worth going out of one’s way to eat. The dumplings they had ordered alongside it were also incredible.

Even the Ilhwa Sword Venerable, who did not usually enjoy beef noodles, finished a bowl cleanly.

“That was delicious.”

“I’m glad it suited your palate. Please look forward to the next one.”

It meant that the next famous restaurant would be one the Ilhwa Sword Venerable liked.

“Do you know what foods I like?”

“I know that when you come out to the Central Plains, you enjoy five-spice sauced pork. I know a place that makes it exceptionally well.”

Her gaze toward Geom Mugeuk was gentle. He had generously shared such precious spirit medicines, and now he was even trying to treat her to the food she liked.

“Thanks to you bringing me out, both my eyes and mouth are enjoying a feast.”

He bestowed things ordinary people could never even imitate, yet he was the one saying thank you.

Truly, Geom Mugeuk was a person who engraved what he received in stone and what he gave in water.

How could one not become wings for such a person?

‘I will gladly become your wings.’

Just then, the Blood Heaven Saber Demon cut in and said,

“Why does this person get five-spice sauced pork while I get beef noodles? I like plenty of expensive dishes too.”

At those words, Geom Mugeuk and the Ilhwa Sword Venerable laughed together.

Since Taesu did not know the relationship between them, he laughed awkwardly along with them.

Inwardly, he was anxious. He knew very well that these people were experts, but—

‘Is it really all right to be this relaxed?’

What would they do if those masked men brought other experts with them?

Fortunately, Geom Mugeuk mentioned the earlier attack.

“Judging from the movements of those who ambushed us earlier, they were well-trained.”

They were not hired warriors or wandering swordsmen, but people belonging to a single organization.

At that, the Blood Heaven Saber Demon suspected the Flying Dragon Gang.

“Did the Flying Dragon Gang leak our information? Or were they the ones behind it?”

It was a suspicion worth having. After all, they were the last people they had met, and despite Jongbaek’s attempts to dissuade him, the leader of the Flying Dragon Gang might have grown greedy.

Geom Mugeuk thought of Jongbaek. Considering the way he had acted when they left—

“It probably wasn’t the Flying Dragon Gang.”

He felt that Jongbaek would have kept his promise no matter what.

“If we assume it wasn’t Biryongbang, then there are two possibilities. Either they were after us.”

Geom Mugeuk’s gaze turned to Taesu.

“Or they were after Taesu.”

Naturally, there was a high chance they had been after Taesu, who had possessed the treasure map. If they had known who they were, they would not have launched an attack like the one earlier.

As the three of them focused their gazes on him, Taesu grew visibly tense.

“Is there anyone besides us who knows you had the treasure map?”

“No, there isn’t.”

Taesu shook his head firmly.

“Then it might not be because of the treasure map, but because of some other grudge that they’re trying to kill you.”

“As you know, my father and I have only ever stolen from villains. So only our names are known; very few people know our faces.”

That was why he had been so shocked when Geom Mugeuk had held out a paper with a likeness of him drawn on it.

“Not us, and not you?”

In truth, this was precisely what was bothering Geom Mugeuk right now.

How did they find us?

Though he had not shown it outwardly, Geom Mugeuk had paid close attention to any pursuit.

He had alternated between main roads and side roads, and from time to time had even used the Divine Eye Art to check whether anyone was following. Moreover, this time they had turned off onto a side path to see the waterfall. Even when he had checked then, there had definitely been no one tailing them.

As he quietly looked at Taesu, a hint of “could it be?” crossed Geom Mugeuk’s face.

“If it wasn’t us, and it wasn’t you, then there’s only one thing left.”

Geom Mugeuk immediately rummaged through his leather pouch and took out a small vial.

“Taesu, come here.”

Taesu came over with a tense expression.

“Hold out your hand!”

“What is this?”

“A medicine that tells us the answer.”

“Is it some kind of confession drug? It’s not like my hand has a mouth.”

Drip, drip.

Geom Mugeuk poured the liquid from the vial onto his palm.

Then his hand turned blue.

“Your hand is answering, isn’t it?”

“What is this?”

“Your hand has Soul-Chasing Incense on it.”

Taesu shook his head in confusion.

“I’ve never been hit with Soul-Chasing Incense.”

Geom Mugeuk had not checked because he thought Taesu himself had been marked in the first place. If it was neither them nor him.

“It must have been applied to the treasure map.”

Someone had put it there in preparation for the possibility that the treasure map might be lost.

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon and the Ilhwa Sword Venerable exchanged astonished looks. No matter how experienced they were as Demon Venerables, they had not thought to check for that in this moment.

“Can’t you remove the Soul-Chasing Incense?”

“I can.”

“Please remove it!”

When Geom Mugeuk drew his sword, Taesu yelped in alarm and pulled his hand behind his back. It meant there was no way unless they cut off his hand.

“This is Soul-Chasing Incense, far more effective than Tracking Incense. Until a certain amount of time passes and the scent disappears on its own, you can never shake it off. It’s so expensive that not just anyone can use it.”

On top of that, the ones pursuing them had not been ordinary martial artists either. If martial artists like that were chasing them while even using Soul-Chasing Incense, then—

“You. Who did you steal the treasure map from?”

Chapter 740: Those Three Characters Are All He Is
-------------------------------------------------
“I don’t know.”

At Taesu’s answer, Geom Mugeuk asked again.

“You stole the belongings of someone whose identity you don’t know? I thought you only stole from evildoers.”

Taesu lowered his head deeply.

“I broke my principles this time.”

When rumors spread that a treasure map had appeared in the martial world, his father had strictly warned him not to get involved.

But in the end, after saying hurtful things to his father, he had thrown himself into the struggle for the treasure map.

“When I went after the treasure map, it had already changed hands several times. People were killing and being killed—it was utter chaos. I practically picked it up in the middle of that mess, so I don’t know who originally owned it.”

Geom Mugeuk nodded.

“You’ll find out who they are sooner or later. They’ll chase you to the ends of the earth.”

For some reason, those words sent a chill through Taesu.

“If you torture me, find out the exact location of the Sword Tomb, and then kill me, you’ll be able to avoid their pursuit.”

Geom Mugeuk was delighted by the probing remark.

“That’s a good idea.”

Yes, this was better than promising never to kill him, only to stab him in the back later.

“So I absolutely won’t tell you the Sword Tomb’s precise location. You’ll have to protect me until we arrive.”

Geom Mugeuk spoke to the Blood Heaven Sword Demon.

“We’ll have to take one master with us.”

The Blood Heaven Sword Demon answered indifferently.

“One master or two, what difference does it make?”

He had actually been referring to Geom Mugeuk, but Taesu misunderstood him as referring to the Ilhwa Sword Venerable.

“I’m only looking forward to the five-spice meat at the place you’re taking us.”

At the Ilhwa Sword Venerable’s words, Taesu established a nonsensical hierarchy in his mind.

‘The woman is at the top, followed by the old man, and then this man.’

* * *

Even as they rode along in the carriage, Taesu’s misunderstanding continued.

“I think I know what sort of family you’re from now.”

“What family is that?”

“You’re from an escort agency, aren’t you?”

When Geom Mugeuk reacted with a startled gasp, Taesu became certain that his guess was correct.

“You’re good at driving a carriage, you can even make it leap over obstacles, and you know the roads extremely well.”

If they were merely experts from an ordinary sect, he would surely have heard rumors about the three of them.

But if they were experts from an escort agency? Escort agency and merchant guild experts were generally less well-known than experts from martial sects.

“What’s the name of your escort agency?”

Geom Mugeuk smiled and told him,

“Have you heard of the Heavenly Horse Escort Agency?”

“I’ve never heard of it.”

“It’s an escort agency that hasn’t made a name for itself yet. It’ll become the greatest escort agency in the world.”

“Why is it called the Heavenly Horse Escort Agency?”

He had named it for the sake of giving this very answer.

“Because we have a thousand horses.”

“A thousand horses!”

Taesu’s eyes widened in surprise. Even so, how could they possibly have a thousand horses? He assumed it was a boast, but he still reacted with astonishment.

“If we get the chance later, I’ll give you an opportunity to pay your respects to our agency master.”

“So he’s the Heavenly Horse Agency Master.”

“That’s right.”

The Blood Heaven Sword Demon and the Ilhwa Sword Venerable, who were riding in the carriage, laughed at the same time.

―You’ve suddenly become an escort guard.

The Blood Heaven Sword Demon answered the Ilhwa Sword Venerable through telepathy.

―Why does he enjoy fooling around so much?

―He’s young.

―Young, my foot. He’s an old man in a young body.

The Ilhwa Sword Venerable laughed as though she agreed. She recalled the past.

―Think back to when we were young. How much did we want to fool around whenever we gathered together?

The Blood Heaven Sword Demon nodded. Back then, the atmosphere had been completely different from now. The former leaders of the cult and the Demon Venerables had been even more terrifying than they were now.

And yet, those had been happy days. They had also been the days when they acted without giving any thought to the consequences. Watching people had been so fascinating and amusing.

But at some point, meeting anyone had become an utterly exhausting affair.

―Compared to our time, the youngsters these days have it easy.

―Don’t say things like that to your disciples. They’ll call you old-fashioned.

―Let them.

Normally, they would have simply laughed and let the matter pass, but the Ilhwa Sword Venerable added one more thing.

―Don’t be like that anymore. The Young Cult Master even gave you a chance.

She meant that Geom Mugeuk had given him the opportunity to live a more vibrant and healthier life.

―Live young. Younger than the young.

The Blood Heaven Sword Demon’s gaze deepened as he looked out the carriage window.

Thundering hooves filled the air.

How long had they been traveling?

From the driver’s seat, Taesu cautiously asked Geom Mugeuk,

“Now that they’ve seen your strength, they’ll bring even stronger experts, won’t they?”

“They probably will.”

“Aren’t you afraid? You don’t know whom they’ll call.”

Geom Mugeuk answered with a smile.

“We found you in that labyrinthine Myriad Blood Cave and drove off the men who attacked the carriage. You, who had carried the treasure map, said you would tell us its location. There’s something you’ve forgotten in the midst of all this.”

“What is it?”

“Throughout this entire affair…”

This was what Taesu had failed to realize.

“We still haven’t even drawn our swords.”

* * *

At the inn where Geom Mugeuk and his companions had departed, an old man was eating beef noodles.

Seated across from him was the leader of the masked men who had been pursuing Geom Mugeuk.

His name was Jihan.

Jihan waited until the old man had finished his meal. He was anxious, but the old man was someone worth waiting for.

“You should eat as well.”

“I’m fine.”

“If it’s because of the mask, you can take it off for a while. It’s not as if we don’t know each other’s faces.”

Jihan always wore a mask whenever he received a mission. He felt strangely at ease while wearing one. The mask made him feel as though he had become someone else.

When the old man finished his meal, Jihan finally took an envelope containing a bank draft from inside his robes and held it out.

“I need information about him.”

The old man did not even check the amount.

“I don’t know.”

For an instant, Jihan’s eyes gleamed sharply.

“So there is something you don’t know.”

The old man was called Old Song.

He was the most famous information broker in the region. His family had been information merchants for several generations, and there was nothing he did not know.

Especially since this struggle over the treasure map was taking place in his territory, he knew every piece of information connected to it. He was probably making a fortune from that information.

Jihan had been doing business with Old Song for quite some time. Old Song had always given him the answers he wanted. There had been only one reason he failed to obtain the answer he sought: when he did not have enough money to buy the information.

Jihan took out another envelope.

Two envelopes now lay on the table.

Old Song shook his head.

“I don’t know.”

Another envelope appeared.

As befitted an information merchant, Old Song knew the nature of this envelope as well.

“I know how your organization works. The first two envelopes are the organization’s money, but the third one is your personal money, isn’t it?”

Jihan did not deny it.

“Why are you trying so desperately to buy information about them?”

Jihan recalled a scene.

The sight of a young man standing atop the carriage and looking down at him.

“I have a bad feeling.”

Old Song stared at Jihan in silence for a moment. They had always met for business and parted for business, but today, his gaze held a personal emotion.

“How many years have we known each other?”

“About nine years.”

“Has it really been that long?”

Then he confessed a thought he had never expressed before.

“To be honest, I’ve always liked you. You were more courteous and refined than the countless predecessors who held your position before you.”

Old Song’s gaze deepened as he looked at Jihan.

“Withdraw from this matter.”

“!”

Jihan understood. Old Song knew something about the man they were up against.

“Please tell me even just his name.”

Old Song shook his head.

“I can’t. Those three characters are everything he is.”

It was the first time this had ever happened. He knew the information, yet refused to sell it.

Just who was that man?

“You know I can’t withdraw.”

“When your life is on the line, is there anything you can’t do? Leave your organization as you are. Hide yourself for about ten years, then come out and start a new life. If it were you, you could hide better than anyone, couldn’t you?”

Yes, he could hide where no one would ever find him.

Jihan’s role in the organization was to track people down. Using Soul-Chasing Incense and pursuing its scent was the duty of the organization he led.

Jihan, Chief of the Soul-Chasing Squad.

That was his position within the organization.

Jihan had never imagined that Old Song would say such things to him.

It clearly meant that their opponent was someone beyond his ability to handle. Old Song was trying to save his life.

When Jihan did not answer, Old Song rose from his seat first.

“This will probably be our last meeting. I’ll pay for the meal. Thank you for everything.”

As Old Song turned to leave, Jihan’s trembling question flew after him.

“Is there any choice I can make besides leaving?”

“If you can’t leave…”

Old Song offered him an enigmatic answer.

“Then charge toward death.”

* * *

A man stood atop a cliff.

He possessed a massive build and a temperament as fierce as blazing fire.

Cheon Daeung, Master of the Fire Dragon Corps.

Famous for his fiery temper, he was in overall command of the recovery of the treasure map.

Jihan arrived behind him.

“We failed to recover the treasure map.”

Without turning around, Cheon Daeung asked,

“I heard you gave up during the pursuit. Even though there were only three casualties.”

“That is correct.”

“Why did you flee like a coward?”

Jihan knew he should not answer this way, but he had no choice. There was no more accurate answer.

“I had a bad feeling.”

“You had a bad feeling?”

As Cheon Daeung repeated the words, his expression hardened further.

“When did my orders become less important than your feelings?”

Jihan could offer no excuse.

Cheon Daeung turned around, exuding a fierce aura.

“Answer me!”

His aura surged like a powerful gale, like the roar of a tiger.

“I’m sorry.”

Jihan lowered his head.

Cheon Daeung seemed ready to smash his head to pieces at any moment, but fortunately, he did not make a move.

“The Soul-Chasing Incense saved your life.”

Only the Soul-Chasing Squad could track the Soul-Chasing Incense.

Cheon Daeung shouted,

“Blood Wind Four Swords!”

At once, four martial artists appeared before him.

Cheon Daeung gave Jihan his order.

“Guide the Blood Wind Four Swords to them!”

As he turned back toward the cliff, Cheon Daeung added,

“We’ll speak again about that unpleasant feeling of yours after we find the treasure map.”

* * *

Taesu tossed and turned in his sleep.

Ever since he had been told that Soul-Chasing Incense had gotten onto his hand, he had been unable to stop worrying. Even when he closed his eyes to sleep, slumber would not come.

Meanwhile, the carefree trio slept soundly. What would they do if someone attacked them? Right, he would have to stand guard himself.

But what good would staying awake do? He could not notice anyone approaching until they were already upon him.

Swish.

Before he knew it, a cold blade was pointed at his throat.

Taesu’s heart sank.

‘When did they…?’

Taesu cautiously shifted his eyes and looked at the other three. Martial artists were standing over them as well, swords pointed at their throats. They were the Blood Wind Four Swords.

Even with swords aimed at them, the three continued sleeping soundly.

‘They’re even stronger than those three!’

Hadn’t he been worried about this? What if they brought stronger experts? The three had acted so high and mighty, completely carefree!

Even if it cost him his life, he had to shout. If even one of the three could evade the sword aimed at them and counterattack, he might at least avoid dying a meaningless death.

But with a sword at his throat, no shout would come out.

‘Please! Please wake up!’

The Blood Wind Four Sword who had his blade pointed at Geom Mugeuk’s throat spoke quietly.

“To think you fled without killing such pathetic fools.”

They normally never let down their guard when facing an enemy. But if these men had failed to awaken even after they had come close enough to touch their throats with their swords, they were opponents against whom it was safe to be careless.

Even now, with a little pressure from his hand, he could pierce this man and end his life.

Another of the Blood Wind Four Swords, standing beside the Ilhwa Sword Venerable, spoke.

“Aren’t they just a bunch of hounds chasing after the scent?”

“Did they take some kind of drug? They still haven’t woken up.”

They took turns speaking, but all three remained deeply asleep.

“What kind of weapon are they carrying, wrapped up so tightly?”

Just as one of them reached out to untie the knot around the upright Heaven-Destroying Great Saber—

“If you untie that, you’ll die.”

He was so startled that his heart nearly leaped out of his chest. He had not noticed anyone approaching until they spoke right beside his ear.

The next person to be startled was the one who had been holding a sword to Geom Mugeuk’s neck. Geom Mugeuk, who had been lying down, had vanished without a sound and was now standing beside the companion who had tried to untie the knot.

“Kill them all!”

The Blood Wind Four Swordsman holding Taesu issued the order.

Flash!

Taesu saw twins again. This time, they were quadruplets.

At the same moment, the necks of three Blood Wind Four Swordsmen twisted.

Crack.

Because they had all been twisted simultaneously, there was only a single sound. In Taesu’s eyes, three Geom Mugeuks had truly appeared at once, and all three had twisted their necks at the same time.

It was the power of the Wind God Four Steps, which had reached the realm of perfection as Geom Mugeuk’s martial arts had risen even further.

Why quadruplets?

Because another Geom Mugeuk had appeared beside the Blood Wind Four Swordsman who had a sword pointed at Taesu’s neck. As though he had carried out the order to kill them all himself, Geom Mugeuk said to him:

“Yes, I killed them as ordered.”

The Blood Wind Four Swordsman’s trembling eyes turned toward Geom Mugeuk standing beside him. He truly could never have imagined that there was a master capable of killing the three of them in the blink of an eye. And the number had not been three, but four.

“Come to think of it, you said to kill them all, didn’t you?”

Shaaak.

Crack.

Though he had watched from right beside him, Taesu could not see how Geom Mugeuk twisted the man’s neck. The last remaining Blood Wind Four Swordsman fell as a corpse.

“Waaaaah!”

Taesu belatedly let out a scream, then quickly clapped a hand over his mouth.

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon had irritably raised himself up.

“How long are you going to keep making noise? Go to sleep!”

Geom Mugeuk answered with a smile.

“Go back to sleep. We’ll clear away the corpses and go to sleep as well.”

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon lay back down.

Taesu quietly came over beside Geom Mugeuk and spoke in a low voice.

“Sometimes, there are treasures like that. Things so precious that they seem ordinary instead.”

Taesu’s gaze turned toward Geom Mugeuk.

He had dealt with the enemies so quickly that killing them looked like nothing at all. Once dawn came, they would learn just how formidable those enemies had been.

Just then, Geom Mugeuk gazed into the distant darkness, then waved toward it.

“The masked man who was following us watched from afar, then left.”

Taesu looked in that direction, but all he could see was darkness.

“If he was watching, shouldn’t you have concealed your abilities? Now that he’s seen this, won’t someone truly terrifying come after us next time?”

At Taesu’s worried question, Geom Mugeuk spoke with an expression that seemed to ask what he was supposed to do.

“Ah, but this is me concealing my abilities as much as possible.”

Chapter 741: I Think You Stole My Heart
---------------------------------------
Jihan was sprinting through the darkness.

His heart pounded as though it would burst. Since becoming a martial artist, had he ever been this startled?

'What did I just see?'

In an instant, the Hyeolpung Sageom had fallen to that man. Because he had watched from afar, he could not tell exactly how they had been killed.

'Poison? A hidden weapon?'

He did not know how they had been killed, but it had happened in an instant.

But what truly made Jihan's heart pound like this was not because they had been killed.

'He definitely saw me.'

He had looked straight at Jihan in the darkness. It was no mistake. He had even waved his hand at him.

He was this far away. Hidden in such thick darkness.

How far had he run?

Jihan stopped and gasped for breath. In such a startled state, he had executed his lightness skill without regulating his breath properly. His qi and blood tangled, and he nearly suffered an internal injury. Truly, this was the first time such a thing had ever happened.

"Haa, haa."

The image of the young man smiling at him from atop the carriage resurfaced.

The ominous premonition had been correct.

'An opponent we should never have touched.'

Now he understood clearly why Songno had not sold the information.

'A big shot. One we cannot handle.'

He could now be certain that telling him to wash his hands of it and walk away had truly been advice for his own good.

But if he left the organization without permission, the organization would mobilize every means to track him down.

One might think the pursuing would stop after a few years, but that pursuit continued forever. The more wicked and despicable the organization, the more it never forgave betrayal.

Since he knew the art of tracking better than anyone, he would not be caught, but he would have to live his entire life being pursued and on edge.

Leaving was not as easy as it sounded.

Even if he left, he could not do so this abruptly. He had to leave only after thorough preparation.

That was why, for now, it was time to return and file a report.

How would Hwaryong Danju Cheon Daeung take the news of the Hyeolpung Sageom's death?

—The Hyeolpung Sageom are dead. They are opponents we should not touch.

The moment he uttered these words, the man might fly into a rage as though bereft of his limbs and kill him. He was the type to act without regard for consequence.

At this moment when his own side felt more troublesome than the enemy, Jihan recalled what Songno had said.

—If you cannot leave... then charge toward death.

It did not mean to die. Songno had spoken those words while showing him a kindness.

Would that death be a merciful one?

Standing alone beneath the dawn starlight, Jihan's worries deepened.

* * *

Cheon Daeung was pouring wine behind the hanging screen.

It was truly a rare sight to see him pouring wine for someone so politely. It meant that the person drinking with him now was someone of great importance.

Only after pouring the wine did he repeat the report he had heard moments ago.

"The Hyeolpung Sageom are dead?"

Jihan felt even greater fear at his unexcited voice.

"Yes. They are all dead."

From behind the hanging screen, Cheon Daeung spoke politely to his companion.

"Please drink and wait a moment."

One side of the screen was drawn aside, and Cheon Daeung walked out.

The refined man became a different person the moment he stepped outside the screen.

Cheon Daeung threw a punch at Jihan without warning.

Swish!

Bam!

The palm force that grazed past Jihan's face smashed the door to pieces. The martial artists standing guard outside the door were struck by a disaster out of nowhere.

"Who did you say is dead?"

Swhack! Crash!

Cheon Daeung smashed everything in sight. The person inside the screen and the person now were completely different.

He was this sort of person by nature, but the reason he had behaved politely inside the screen was out of courtesy toward that person.

Rather, his rampage outside seemed to emphasize his courtesy toward the person inside the screen.

Bam! Crash!

The ground around where Jihan stood was pulverized by the palm force, and the floor tiles were overturned. Debris flew at Jihan's body, but he did not dodge.

This Cheon Daeung was the type of human Jihan hated most.

One who revealed his anger through violence.

One who believed he could express his emotions to his heart's content because he was strong.

The saving grace was that this rage was not flying directly at Jihan.

Only after throwing such a tantrum did Cheon Daeung's anger subside.

"Whose doing is it? Was it the old man said to be with them? Or did that mysterious woman kill them?"

Jihan had clearly seen at least one thing. That it was the doing of that young man. That is, unless the other two had displayed their martial arts while lying down.

Jihan answered honestly.

"I could not see properly."

He had expected a furious order to fall, but instead, Cheon Daeung considered it normal.

"Yes, naturally."

He considered himself far weaker than the Hyeolpung Sageom. In fact, not only him, but many in the organization disliked and despised him. They treated him like a hunting dog that sniffed out scents.

There was a clear reason they disliked Jihan.

Most of the targets he tracked were people who had defected from within the organization. In other words, Jihan was the person who would chase after them if they tried to escape the organization.

"You were hiding far away like a coward, weren't you?"

"The Hyeolpung Sageom told me to step back, saying I would get in the way of the fight. That I would needlessly reveal my presence to the enemy."

It was a lie. After relaying their position, he had withdrawn far away on his own.

Because it was a surprise attack in the dead of night, the Hyeolpung Sageom had been confident. They were ones who believed that even if they fought in broad daylight, as long as all four gathered, they would lose to no one.

"Still, you must have seen something, haven't you?"

"The battle was decided in an instant, so I could not see anything."

Cheon Daeung grabbed Jihan by the collar.

"If you can't even tattle properly like this, what can you do besides sniffing out scents? Shall I cut off this nose of yours?"

To avoid that scornful gaze, Jihan lowered his head deeply.

Cheon Daeung threw him. Jihan tumbled to the floor. He could have arrested his fall with his movement technique, but had he done so, Cheon Daeung might truly have used a killing move.

Cheon Daeung turned toward the rear screen and spoke politely to the person beyond it.

"This matter must be handled by you, Elder."

The shadow inside the screen drank and then set down the wine cup.

"I shall."

Then the screen was drawn aside, and the one called Elder revealed himself. Upon confirming who it was, Jihan understood why that foul-tempered Cheon Daeung was showing him such courtesy.

"Guide them."

* * *

The carriage departed early the next morning.

Because Geom Mugeuk acted as though nothing had happened the night before, Taesu wondered if he had dreamed it all.

"By any chance, do you know who those attackers were yesterday?"

Geom Mugeuk nodded.

"I know."

"You know? Who were they?"

"Why are you curious about that now?"

"I feel like I should know who I helped bury."

That was not the reason. He was curious just what level of expert had been killed. Yesterday, he had been too surprised and disoriented to ask.

"The Hyeolpung Sageom."

Taesu was startled out of his wits for a moment, but soon he shook his head.

"Come on, don't joke around."

Four people had been killed, so he had definitely just picked the name of some four-member group of experts at random.

If you were going to joke, you should have attached a plausible name, but the Hyeolpung Sageom?

The Hyeolpung Sageom were renowned experts of the Demonic Path.

Just the martial artists they had killed were innumerable. Many of those they had killed were famous experts whose names alone were known.

Was that all? Like the experts of the Demonic Path they were, they were ones who had committed innumerable evil deeds.

They were the sort that if someone so much as uttered the name Hyeolpung Sageom while eating at an inn, the surroundings would fall silent in an instant.

They died without even swinging their swords properly? That was preposterous.

"I'm not that naive. I've rolled around in the martial world long enough. So stop teasing me and tell me who they were."

Geom Mugeuk drove the carriage without a word. He had not insisted they were the Hyeolpung Sageom. Therefore, it felt all the more real.

Recalling Geom Mugeuk's lightning-fast movements from yesterday, Taesu asked again in a trembling voice.

"Was it really the Hyeolpung Sageom?"

When Geom Mugeuk nodded, Taesu turned deathly pale.

Even when the battle for the Jangbyeondo broke out earlier, those chasing him had merely been nameless martial artists blinded by greed.

He just had to evade them with his lightness skill, with his Bone Compression Technique. He had no need to know their names, nor was he curious.

But now, names had finally started to emerge.

The Hyeolpung Sageom.

Those terrifying Hyeolpung Sageom he had only ever heard of in tales had pointed their swords at him and died right before his eyes.

Perhaps this was only the beginning of hearing astonishing names.

Having composed himself, Taesu looked at Geom Mugeuk. Had this person really killed the Hyeolpung Sageom?

"Did you kill them knowing who they were?"

"I didn't know when I killed them."

"Then how do you know now?"

"Because a report flew in this morning."

"To whom?"

"The Cheonma Escort Agency has an information network all over the Central Plains. They have a thousand horses—just how fast do you think they exchange messages?"

Taesu looked at him with eyes wondering whether he should believe this or not.

Geom Mugeuk took a sheet of paper from his breast pocket and handed it to him.

Taesu let out a shriek as he read the contents.

"Argh!"

It was because of another name written on the paper.

—The Hyeolpung Sageom have been active as the limbs of Hwaryong Danju Cheon Daeung of the Sawangmun.

"They belonged to the Sawangmun?"

Sawangmun.

It was a sect famous in the Demonic Path for being savage and infamous.

It had not originally been this notorious, but from a certain point, it began accepting those who had committed crimes and fled from the martial world, and the sect's power had changed.

It was a sect where so many evildoers had gathered that there was a saying: if you cause trouble in the martial world, run to the Sawangmun.

"We must hide. Now is not the time for this!"

Taesu was truly terrified. Every rumor about the Sawangmun was enough to make one grimace.

The fact that they killed people was not what mattered. What terrified everyone was how they killed. They killed people so cruelly. They were ones who had abandoned their humanity, and they used that terror to grow their sect even stronger.

He could remain this calm after provoking the Sawangmun?

"Give me the reins. I'll find us a place to hide."

Because Taesu was always accustomed to running and hiding, he thought he had to take charge.

The two people in the carriage and this Geom Mugeuk as well—why were they so peaceful when the name Sawangmun had come up?

"I'll teach you the Bone Compression Technique, so go back to Manhyeol Cave and hide in that hole."

He was serious; it was no joke. If it had been another sect, maybe not, but the Sawangmun was truly a sect swarming with evildoers.

"I might be able to go in, but could we really make our elders enter such a narrow place?"

"The opponent is the Sawangmun. They are not people that a mere escort agency can handle! If we hide somewhere for a few years, they will forget about the Jangbyeondo."

Geom Mugeuk smiled at Taesu. He and the Demon Lords did not have a few years to spare, or even a few days. You brat, we're busy people.

"What about your scent?"

"Ah!"

Taesu had forgotten for a moment. What they were being pursued for. Unless he cut off his hand, there was no escaping the Sawangmun's pursuit.

Geom Mugeuk mentioned one place.

"Shall we ask the Sadomaeng for help?"

Taesu made an incredulous face.

"Are you saying we should call in a tiger to chase away a wolf?"

"We're fighting poison with poison."

"No matter how vicious the Sawangmun are, don't arms bend inward? If the Sawangmun fall, the power of the Demonic Path weakens, so the Sadomaeng won't attack them."

"If a war breaks out with the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult or the Righteous Faction, would the Sawangmun really fight on the front lines? Not at all! They are the sort who would try to gain their own profit from that chaos. The current Sadomaeng will want to eliminate them. Even among evil, there must be minimum standards."

Even if Geom Mugeuk was exactly right about that.

"Even if the Sadomaeng stops them, would they simply leave the Jangbyeondo alone?"

"And if they don't?"

"Obviously they would take it. To keep the secret, they'll kill us all."

"They won't."

At his nonchalant answer, Taesu finally let out a sigh.

Could he really survive with Sawangmun after the treasure map? Did this calm truly come from composure and skill?

“If masters we can’t handle appear, hand me over.”

Of course, he wasn’t being sincere. He was testing Geom Mugeuk.

“Naturally, that’s what we should do.”

It was only natural, yet he felt strangely disappointed.

“But that won’t happen.”

“What?”

“Because there won’t be anyone we can’t handle.”

At those words, Taesu suddenly recalled what his father had once told him.

—Do not covet the treasure map! It is something we cannot handle.

Even if it was something he himself could not handle.

At least in this moment, he wanted to believe in that bluff of Geom Mugeuk’s.

Please, may you be able to handle it!

How long had they run like that?

An old man stood blocking the road.

Exuding an extraordinary presence, he was none other than the old man who had been drinking earlier with Cheon Daeung.

He stood there in a white robe with his snow-white beard fluttering, looking just like an immortal. From the outside, he seemed like someone who had transcended the secular world—but in truth, he was someone who had transcended it in a different sense.

Knowing that their opponent was Sawangmun, it was not difficult to guess who this old man was, dressed in robes with a swastika emblazoned on the chest.

“Wan’e Shanren!”

Taesu trembled from head to toe. Wan’e Shanren was an absolute master of the evil faction, a man who had committed every manner of atrocity.

There were countless rumors about him. Some said he was called Wan’e Shanren because he had killed ten thousand masters; others said the name came from his ten thousand evil deeds. And some claimed both were true.

‘No matter how skilled these people are, they can’t face Wan’e Shanren!’

Wan’e Shanren did not ask who had killed the Blood Wind Four Swords.

“Who has the treasure map?”

At that, Geom Mugeuk looked at Taesu without the slightest hesitation.

No, I know it’s true, but do you have to look at me that blatantly?

Taesu finally felt that the moment he could not handle had arrived. Though it had come far sooner than expected.

He understood why Geom Mugeuk would abandon him. Who could possibly handle Wan’e Shanren? His bluff ended here.

If things had come to this anyway—

With a solemn look in his eyes, Taesu spoke to Wan’e Shanren. He did not know himself why he felt this way.

“I have memorized the treasure map. Take me and let these people go. If you kill them, you will never learn the location of the treasure. Please, spare them!”

At Taesu, who had dared to set conditions for him, Wan’e Shanren gave a murderous smile.

Meanwhile, beside Taesu, a beaming smile appeared—one bright enough to offset all that chill.

“I think you just stole my heart.”

Chapter 742: Just What Kind of Life Have You Lived?
---------------------------------------------------
“You can joke even in a situation like this?”

Taesu thought it was the last shred of Geom Mugeuk’s pride. He must be trying to cover the guilt of handing himself over with a joke.

And yet, Taesu did not hate this man. This man who boasted about having a thousand horses.

“Was your heart something that could be stolen with a single sentence?”

At Taesu’s question, Geom Mugeuk answered with a smile.

“It was a sentence saying he’d save my life.”

Taesu knew that if he was dragged away like this, he would die in the end. So he spoke his honest feelings.

“I don’t know why I want to save you. I just don’t know the reason, but I suppose I took a liking to you. And I have one request. If, by any chance, you meet my father later, please be sure to tell him I was sorry.”

What he regretted most was dying without seeing his father. He did not want the last words he had left his father to be words that hurt him.

It was right then.

“What a load of shit.”

The one who opened his mouth was the Ten Thousand Evils Hermit. A cold killing intent flowed from his eyes.

“You think I’ll spare them just because you tell me to?”

“If you kill them, I will never tell you the location of the Sword Tomb.”

A sneer formed at the corner of the Ten Thousand Evils Hermit’s mouth.

“Do you think torturing people was never among the things I’ve done? Everyone talks bravely at first, just like you. But there has not been a single person who didn’t open their mouth. You seem confident that you’ll be different.”

The Ten Thousand Evils Hermit proved why he was called Ten Thousand Evils.

“Before that, I’ll kill them all, one by one, right in front of your eyes.”

Taesu was flustered. This was not the situation he had wanted.

“All of you in the carriage, crawl out! Before I blow the whole thing away.”

The Ten Thousand Evils Hermit behaved as if there was no one beneath heaven worth fearing. That was how confident he was in his own abilities. No matter who came out of that carriage, he was sure he could kill them.

Click.

The carriage door opened, and Ilhwa Geomjon stepped out.

She wore neither splendid makeup nor beautiful clothes, but the moment he saw her, lust flickered in the Ten Thousand Evils Hermit’s eyes. Among all the evil deeds he had committed, how could there not have been acts of lust?

“It has truly been a long time since my lust stirred. I’ll kill you last.”

Shrrng.

The Ilhwa Sword was about to be drawn slightly.

But she barely managed to stop her sword. Yes, she could not lose the bet over a bastard like this.

Clack.

After sheathing the sword she had been about to draw, Ilhwa Geomjon smiled and said to Geom Mugeuk,

“It seems the two pills you gave me were effective. Seeing as they even stir the lust of such an ugly old man.”

Geom Mugeuk smiled brightly and said,

“Would the result have been any different if you hadn’t taken them?”

At Geom Mugeuk’s words, Ilhwa Geomjon laughed in good spirits.

Click.

The carriage door on the other side opened irritably.

From inside came the annoyed voice of Hyeolcheon Doma.

“I don’t even want to lay a hand on you.”

Geom Mugeuk and Ilhwa Geomjon could tell that Hyeolcheon Doma was very angry. Of course, they knew the reason as well.

Only then did Hyeolcheon Doma step out of the carriage.

“Just kill yourself.”

Hyeolcheon Doma’s cold gaze shot toward the Ten Thousand Evils Hermit.

There should have been some kind of reaction, whether curses burst out or killing intent poured forth.

But as the Ten Thousand Evils Hermit stared at Hyeolcheon Doma, his eyes gradually widened.

“You are!”

The Ten Thousand Evils Hermit recognized Hyeolcheon Doma. They had met in the past.

“Why are you here?”

His voice trembled violently.

Instead of answering, Hyeolcheon Doma asked coldly,

“You don’t want to? Then I’ll kill you myself.”

Hyeolcheon Doma strode toward him. The moment terrifying demonic energy surged toward the Ten Thousand Evils Hermit—

The Ten Thousand Evils Hermit struck the crown of his own head with his palm.

Crunch!

His skull shattered, and he collapsed where he stood. He had truly killed himself.

“Aaah!”

The one who screamed was Taesu. It was a scream that burst out before he knew it.

‘What in the world did I just see?’

The Ten Thousand Evils Hermit, that wicked man, had killed himself? Why?

The Ten Thousand Evils Hermit he had heard of was the kind of man who, if he could not win in a fight, would resort to every despicable method, and if even that failed, would unleash a self-destruction technique.

Then who in the world was that old man, for the Ten Thousand Evils Hermit to kill himself without even trying to fight?

“Oh my, please stay over there.”

Geom Mugeuk backed far away from Hyeolcheon Doma.

“Just what kind of life have you lived?”

Hyeolcheon Doma smacked his lips, and Ilhwa Geomjon said with a smile,

“He once had quite the fierce temper.”

He had been so terrifying that even the Ten Thousand Evils Hermit trembled before him.

“That temper’s all died down.”

Geom Mugeuk smiled and responded to Hyeolcheon Doma’s words.

“I like you a hundred times better now. So please don’t bring it back to life.”

Meanwhile, Taesu cautiously approached the Ten Thousand Evils Hermit’s corpse. He wanted to confirm that he was dead, but he was too afraid to shake the body. It felt as though the man might spring up at any moment.

“Why did he kill himself?”

To that dazed, absentminded question, Geom Mugeuk replied,

“Because he was scared?”

In the end, Taesu thought of another conspiracy theory.

“You’re all in on this and acting, aren’t you? You’re acting because you’re after my treasure map, right?”

And so he imagined that the moment he entered the Sword Tomb, all the people who had died until now would appear, stand in a line, and mock him.

“There’s one more actor over there.”

Geom Mugeuk looked to one side and waved.

Then a masked man who had been hiding far away revealed himself. The one who appeared, his eyes as startled as Taesu’s, was Jihan. Taesu thought he would immediately use his movement technique and flee.

But Jihan launched himself toward them.

Jihan landed in front of Geom Mugeuk. Just like that day, Geom Mugeuk was smiling at him today as well.

On his left stood Hyeolcheon Doma with his arms crossed, and on his right stood Ilhwa Geomjon with calm eyes.

These were people who had made the Ten Thousand Evils Hermit kill himself without even drawing their swords.

After bowing his head in greeting, Jihan revealed his identity.

“I am Jihan, captain of the Soul-Chasing Unit of the Sawangmun.”

As he did, he removed the mask he had been wearing. His appearance suited his calm eyes.

Geom Mugeuk asked him,

“If you’re the captain of the Soul-Chasing Unit, then are you the one who applied the Soul-Chasing Scent?”

“Yes. That is my work.”

“Why did you come to me instead of running away?”

Jihan looked at the Ten Thousand Evils Hermit’s corpse and let out a sigh.

“Cheon Daeung, the leader of the Fire Dragon Unit and the one responsible for obtaining this treasure map, will never believe that the Ten Thousand Evils Hermit killed himself without even fighting. Since he has always found me displeasing, he will frame me by saying I colluded with the enemy and killed him.”

One way or another, he would place the blame on him. Someone had to take responsibility for the deaths of the Blood Wind Four Swords and the Ten Thousand Evils Hermit.

He then revealed another reason.

“Because I treated someone with courtesy for over ten years, I was able to obtain one piece of information.”

He was referring to the information broker Elder Song.

“He absolutely did not sell your information. And he advised me to leave. If I could not leave, he told me to run headlong toward death.”

Jihan stared at Geom Mugeuk.

“I intend to stake my life and my future on that man’s advice.”

Jihan desperately hoped that the death before his eyes would be a merciful one. That it would be a death with enough breadth to embrace him.

Throughout his speech, he kept thinking that this was madness. He was trying to entrust himself to someone without even knowing who the other party was.

Geom Mugeuk quietly stared at Jihan. Those calm eyes did not suit the Sawangmun.

“Why did you enter the Sawangmun?”

“From birth, I was sensitive to smells. Even when all kinds of scents were mixed together, I could distinguish them one by one, and even if a scent grew distant, I could follow it. I liked the many scents of the world. Then I met my master and learned the Scent-Chasing Art, the study and pursuit of fragrances. My master belonged to the Sawangmun. I was young, so I did not know what kind of place the Sawangmun was, and back then, it was not as infamous as it is now.”

Geom Mugeuk sensed that he was not lying.

“Have you ever killed an innocent person?”

“My duties mostly involved chasing down and bringing back those who fled from our sect. In the process, there were times when I had no choice but to kill those who attacked me, prepared to die.”

“Other than that?”

“None.”

A person who could handle the Soul-Chasing Scent was certainly someone the Ghost Shadow Unit needed.

“What do you want?”

“Please take me in as your subordinate.”

Hyeolcheon Doma and Ilhwa Geomjon looked at each other. Taesu was one thing, and now this man named Jihan as well.

—Why does everyone like the Young Cult Leader so much?

At Hyeolcheon Doma’s sound transmission, Ilhwa Geomjon replied,

—Ask yourself.

Hyeolcheon Doma turned his head away with a huffing expression.

Geom Mugeuk did not immediately accept his request.

“Do you even know who I am?”

“You are the person whom someone who has regarded me favorably for ten years told me to throw myself toward. I trust that man’s eye. And I have nowhere to return to.”

In truth, there was one more reason.

After spending a long time studying and pursuing scents, he sometimes smelled something from people. Not a real smell, but a kind of imaginary scent.

If Cheon Daeung gave off a stench, then this person gave off a pleasant scent.

At last, Geom Mugeuk made his decision.

“Fine. I accept.”

At Geom Mugeuk’s ready answer, Jihan was actually startled. He had not expected to be accepted so easily. He had thought there would be all sorts of conditions. Bring this back, or prove your loyalty.

“From now on, you are one of my people.”

“!”

He had used the expression not subordinate, but my people.

“Do you trust me?”

Geom Mugeuk shook his head.

“I don’t know who you are, so I can’t trust you yet.”

“Then why take me in?”

“When you first chased me, why did you stop the pursuit?”

When three men died, he had given up the pursuit.

“I judged that if we went any farther, all my subordinates would die.”

“Because I trust that wise judgment. Because I trust the heart that cherishes your subordinates.”

Cheon Daeung had mocked him and scorned him as a coward for the very same fact that he had returned after only three men died.

Geom Mugeuk, looking at the exact same fact, was saying that his judgment had been excellent.

Jihan hesitated no longer. He respectfully cupped his fist and paid his respects.

“Jihan greets my new lord. I will serve you with loyalty.”

“Your ability is more important than any other. I look forward to working with you.”

As the resentment of all the years he had been treated like a hunting dog within the organization surged up, his eyes reddened. These were words he had wanted so badly to hear all this time.

Watching them, Taesu suddenly found himself envying Jihan. In that moment, Taesu realized that he had wanted to do work that someone would acknowledge.

Perhaps the reason he had always been angry and dissatisfied with everything was not simply because of his father’s rule to steal only the possessions of the wicked, but because his own work was not something that could be recognized with pride.

And yet now, before his eyes, someone was changing his own fate. He was showing that fate could be changed like this, if one tried to change it.

Jihan looked at Taesu and said,

“I cannot completely erase the Soul-Chasing Scent on that man’s hands, but I can make it faint. Then we should be able to gain enough time to shake off the pursuit.”

Even without him, the subordinates of the Soul-Chasing Unit would come after them.

“Do so.”

Jihan took several medicine bottles from his robe and poured them over both of Taesu’s hands in order.

“Now we won’t have to cut off your hands.”

At Geom Mugeuk’s joke, Taesu smiled broadly in delight. All this time, he had felt uneasy, as though his hands had become shackles on everyone.

Geom Mugeuk said to Jihan,

“Guide us.”

“To where, sir?”

The place Geom Mugeuk wanted to go was somewhere he had never expected.

“To where the Fire Dragon Unit Leader is.”

“!”

The reason was even more astonishing.

“He tormented and tried to kill one of my people, so I will kill him.”

Jihan could tell. Geom Mugeuk meant it sincerely. And also that he might truly be able to kill Cheon Daeung.

Taesu, on the other hand, jumped in shock as he listened.

“You want to strike the Sawangmun’s martial artists first? Absolutely not!”

“Didn’t you hear the information broker? If you want to live, throw yourself into death!”

Taesu tried to stop him because he was afraid, while Jihan tried to stop him for a different reason.

“You must not kill him.”

“Why?”

“If he fails to recover this treasure map, he will fall into great trouble anyway. On the other hand, if you kill him now, the Sawangmun will become fully involved in this matter. I believe the correct choice is to leave a fool in the enemy camp alive.”

Geom Mugeuk smiled pleasantly at Jihan's sound judgment.

“I shall follow your wishes. However, you must die.”

“!”

Jihan was startled, as was Taesu.

Of course, he did not truly intend to kill him.

“Do you still have any of the Chuhonhyang that was applied to the Jangbodo?”

“Yes, I do.”

Geom Mugeuk looked at the Manaksanin's corpse and said,

“Apply it to that man’s hands.”

Jihan did as he was told and applied the Chuhonhyang to the corpse’s hands.

“That bastard killed everyone here and seized the Jangbodo.”

Only then did they understand Geom Mugeuk’s plan. He intended to make it appear that the Manaksanin had intercepted the Jangbodo. Since he was a man who had committed all manner of evil deeds, no one would doubt it.

Geom Mugeuk let out a long whistle.

At that, a martial artist revealed himself. He was a martial artist who had traveled between Geom Mugeuk and Tongcheongak, delivering information. He was a master whose skills in qinggong and stealth techniques were enough to be called an expert.

“Take this corpse and head toward Seomseo.”

Seomseo was in the opposite direction from Jeolgang, where they were currently headed.

“There will be people chasing after you. Go as far as you can, leave the corpse behind, and get away. The Chuhonhyang has been applied to the corpse’s hands, so you must never touch them.”

“Yes, I understand.”

The man bound the corpse’s hands behind its back to secure them, then hoisted the corpse over his shoulder and left. Since a qinggong expert was departing first, there would be no chance of him being caught by pursuers later.

“If the corpse is found with Chuhonhyang on its hands? They’ll think he fled all the way to Seomseo, had the Jangbodo stolen by someone else, and then died.”

Jihan marveled. It was an excellent plan.

First, it would sow confusion that the location written on the Jangbodo was in Seomseo.

Next, since he would become a dead man, he would be able to avoid the organization’s pursuit.

In the end, Cheondaeung would have to take responsibility for having entrusted this matter to the Manaksanin.

Taesu asked cautiously,

“Who is that man who took the corpse? Is he a guard? Or a porter?”

At the word “guard,” Jihan looked at Taesu with a puzzled expression.

Taesu felt a useless sense of pride that he knew more about this Geom Mugeuk than Jihan. A needless competitive spirit was arising.

But Jihan could instinctively tell. That Geom Mugeuk and the two experts were by no means people from an escort agency.

“Now, let us be on our way too.”

With Geom Mugeuk between them, the two sat on either side.

“One is a master of fleeing, and the other is a master of pursuit. Isn’t this perfectly balanced?”

Taesu and Jihan looked at each other. Never in their dreams had they imagined that after constantly chasing and being chased, they would end up sitting side by side in the same carriage.

“Now, let those Sawangmun bastards suffer to their hearts’ content in the wrong place.”

The carriage once again began to race toward Jeolgang Province.

“Let’s go eat five-spice braised pork!”

Chapter 743: This Is Why We Queue
---------------------------------
The carriage raced toward Zhejiang.

“I’ll drive the carriage. Please go to the back and get some rest.”

Jihan felt uncomfortable seeing Jian Wuji continue to hold the reins.

“I’m fine.”

When Jian Wuji declined, Taesu acted like he knew all about it.

“If I may speak of the gentleman here, he hails from an escort agency. There’s nowhere in the Central Plains he hasn’t been since he was young, so he drives a carriage like a god, I tell you.”

Jian Wuji had never said such things, but Taesu believed them as solidly as iron.

Jihan shot Jian Wuji a sidelong glance.

No matter how many times he looked, the man was not from an escort agency. No, what sort of escort or escort leader in this world could make the Wan’e Sanren take his own life?

*For a thief, he’s quite obtuse.*

Not knowing that he was becoming a tactless person in Jihan’s eyes, Taesu acted like he knew once again.

“And you wouldn’t know how well he knows the roads, I tell you.”

As he boasted about Jian Wuji, Taesu felt a hollow feeling. Because Jian Wuji’s subordinate was not himself, but Jihan.

“And his cooking, how good it is. If you try the food he makes for you, you’ll be shocked, I tell you.”

Jihan was curious about Jian Wuji’s bottom line. The leaders he had seen until now wouldn’t even think of driving a carriage themselves, let alone knowing the roads well. That was all work for subordinates to do.

Yet he was good at cooking too? And personally made it to give to his subordinate?

Jihan felt a strange sense of anticipation. Where would he get the chance to meet such a leader while living as a martial artist?

*No, I shouldn’t get my hopes up too high.*

For great expectations lead to great disappointments. He was still in a state where he didn’t even know what kind of person this leader was.

Out of the blue, Jian Wuji asked Jihan.

“As I recall, you were the one who applied the Soul-Chasing Incense to the treasure map at the beginning, correct?”

“That is correct.”

“Do you know how the Sawang Gate acquired the treasure map?”

“No. I don’t know. At the time, someone from the higher-ups came to me with the treasure map. He said it was an item I absolutely must not lose, so I should use the most effective Soul-Chasing Incense.”

“Do you know who he is?”

Jihan shook his head.

“No, he was wearing a bamboo hat pulled low, so I couldn’t see his face properly.”

“A man who deliberately hid himself.”

Jihan didn’t attach any particular significance to that person. The Sawang Gate was a place filled with all sorts of secretive figures.

“But I think I would recognize him if I saw him again.”

Jian Wuji turned his head to look at Jihan and said.

“You remember his scent.”

“Yes, a very unique scent emanated from him.”

This time, Jihan asked Jian Wuji.

“But why do you ask about that?”

“Because it’s strange. For an organization like the Sawang Gate to have acquired the treasure map, only to lose it again—that makes no sense, does it?”

Jihan asked in surprise.

“Could you mean that the Sawang Gate deliberately leaked the treasure map into the Central Plains?”

Taesu, who had been listening from the coachman’s seat, also waited for Jian Wuji’s answer with a tense expression.

If that were the case? Then that would mean he had been moving according to their intentions.

“I think that’s a possibility.”

Jian Wuji added with a deepening gaze.

“The struggle to seize the treasure map drew our eyes as well.”

Listening to Jian Wuji’s words, Jihan suddenly had a thought.

*Who exactly is this ‘us’ you speak of?*

Now he had become part of that ‘us’ too.

* * *

They stopped the carriage in a field.

While the horse grazed and rested, everyone took their own rest.

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon and the One Flower Sword Venerable went for a stroll side by side, and Taesu lay down in the field to rest his eyes for a moment.

Jian Wuji stood on a hill and looked up at the sky.

Because there was something to say quietly, Jihan approached Jian Wuji’s side.

“When was the last time you looked up at the sky?”

At Jian Wuji’s question, Jihan looked up at the sky together with him and answered.

“I don’t remember.”

“Make an effort to look often from now on. Those I lead are obligated to look at the sky once a day.”

“Yes, I will keep that in mind.”

“If there’s anything you want to ask, ask.”

It was a question to ask while separated from Taesu. Somehow, it seemed he hadn’t revealed his identity to that man.

“I wish to know my lord’s name.”

Songno had said so. Those three characters were his everything.

Contrary to the overly cautious question, Jian Wuji answered as if it were no big deal.

“I’m Jian Wuji. It’s a nice name, isn’t it?”

“It’s very nice.”

Jihan, who had answered thoughtlessly, flinched.

“Jian Wuji?”

It was definitely a name he had heard somewhere before.

His body reacted before his memory.

He suddenly felt some dark, unknowable energy creep over him. A chill ran down his spine, and the hair all over his body stood on end at once.

Jian Wuji, Jian Wuji. Where had he heard that before?

“!”

In an instant, lightning struck in Jihan’s mind. It was such a famous name that he couldn’t recall it right away.

He had never dreamed that he would hear that name from the mouth of the man who had become his leader, standing alone together in such a field.

*No, of course not. What nonsense am I thinking!*

The more he denied it, thinking it must be someone with the same name, the faster his heart began to race.

With a *surely not* heart, Jihan looked at Jian Wuji.

Jian Wuji smiled and said.

“Yes, that’s right.”

At that answer, as if he knew exactly what Jihan was thinking right now, and at that relaxed expression looking at him, Jihan could tell.

That the Jian Wuji was this Jian Wuji.

“Do you regret becoming my subordinate?”

This question drove the nail in.

*He really is the Young Cult Leader of the Heavenly Demon Cult!*

Jihan’s body trembled like a leaf.

“No. I do not regret it. It’s just… I was merely surprised.”

Jihan was in a half-dazed state.

He had imagined all sorts of things about who his new leader might be. A scion of a righteous orthodox sect? A famous expert of the unorthodox path? A rejuvenated expert who had emerged from reclusion? He had truly thought of every possibility.

But among all those possibilities, there was no Young Cult Leader of the Heavenly Demon Cult.

He had never imagined that the Young Cult Leader of the Heavenly Demon Cult would smile so brightly at a person. Such a Young Cult Leader did not exist in his mind.

Songno suddenly came to mind. How ridiculous had he been when he asked to be told the name of the Heavenly Demon Cult’s Young Cult Leader?

On the one hand, *that damned old man!* slipped out.

What was he going to do about the Young Cult Leader of the Heavenly Demon Cult? Charge at him?

But while he hadn’t known, it was one thing, but now that he knew the truth, was it okay to stand side by side like this? Was it okay to meet eyes? In the Heavenly Demon Cult, shouldn’t everyone prostrate themselves before the Young Cult Leader?

“Then who are those two over there?”

Shaking off his thoughts, Jihan summoned his courage and asked.

“They are the Blood Heaven Blade Demon and the One Flower Sword Venerable of the Eight Demon Lords.”

“Ah!”

They were truly the Eight Demon Lords he had only heard of in tales.

Now he understood. Why the Wan’e Sanren had taken his own life. Having shown such lust toward the One Flower Sword Venerable, he was a dead man anyway. Knowing he could not be forgiven, he had died by his own hand. Otherwise, he would have been torn to shreds and killed.

It was astonishing that the Young Cult Leader and two Demon Lords were wandering the Central Plains without other subordinates. He had thought they traveled with hundreds or thousands.

He couldn’t believe it even seeing it with his own eyes; who would believe it?

“I never knew I would come to serve such a great person.”

“Yes, from now on, revere and uphold me like the heavens!”

Jihan knew Jian Wuji spoke as if joking, but how could Jihan take it as a joke?

“I shall devote this body entirely and serve you with loyalty!”

At this moment, this was what Jian Wuji wanted to tell him.

“If you devote your whole body like that, there will come a day when it is too hard. When that happens, don’t look at the ground, don’t look at your subordinates, don’t look into your own heart—look over there. Don’t look at me, I said look over there.”

Jihan followed Jian Wuji and looked up at the sky.

On the vast sky, patchwork clouds drifted slowly. A bird spread its wings and crossed that sky.

“Sigh while looking over there, rage while looking over there. If you do, you’ll feel yourself growing smaller, and if you do, your worries will grow smaller too.”

The Young Cult Leader of the Heavenly Demon Cult, who said to look up at the sky when things are hard.

This man was his leader.

And Jihan finally got his answer.

*We are the Heavenly Demon Cult.*

That fearsome name had now become his own name.

At that moment, Taesu walked over to them.

“What are you talking about so seriously?”

“My lord was teaching me the way a man becomes small.”

Taesu blinked, wondering what that meant, but Jihan didn’t tell him.

Taesu felt an unnecessary sense of being left out.

Feeling that Jihan’s attitude toward Jian Wuji had become even more respectful, that sense of being left out only grew.

*Is that what loyalty is?*

Until now, just the thought of being under someone had made his chest feel tight. Yet for this Jihan alone, he felt jealousy that wasn’t quite jealousy. He had stolen Jian Wuji’s heart, so why did he feel like the one who had lost it?

“When this matter is finished and we return, you will travel all over the Central Plains under the name of the Heavenly Demon Escort Agency.”

At Taesu’s envy, Jihan flinched. To utter the word “Heavenly Demon” so carelessly!

“You’ll get yourself killed doing that!”

Without knowing such worries, Taesu subtly boasted as well.

“He said he’d introduce me to the leader of the Heavenly Demon Escort Agency later.”

*Then you really will die!*

* * *

“That’s the one! The place with the tasty Five-Spice Braised Pork!”

The area in front of the inn was packed with people, and those trying to enter were standing in a long line.

At the entrance, a waiter shouted.

“If you wait just a moment, a table will open up!”

Jian Wuji, who had gotten off the carriage, first walked toward the people standing in line.

Jihan, who followed behind, was inwardly nervous.

What would Jian Wuji do? Would he intimidate and chase away the people in line? Or would he ignore them all and walk right in?

Neither case seemed to suit Jian Wuji.

The next moment, Jihan’s eyes went wide.

Jian Wuji had gone to stand at the end of the line.

*The Young Cult Leader of the Heavenly Demon Cult is standing in line to eat?*

He couldn’t believe it even seeing it with his own eyes.

Fine, let’s say that Young Cult Leader is a special person.

An even more astonishing sight unfolded. The Blood Heaven Blade Demon and the One Flower Sword Venerable followed Jian Wuji and stood in line. The two were not displeased at all. Rather, they were delighted and intrigued.

“It’s your first time eating while standing in line, isn’t it?”

At Jian Wuji’s question, the One Flower Sword Venerable nodded.

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon put on a deliberately displeased expression.

“Why a line just to eat a meal!”

“Please go rest in the carriage over there. I’ll call you when it’s your turn.”

“Forget it. Too annoying.”

Though he spoke that way, the very act of standing in line with the One Flower Sword Venerable like this made the Blood Heaven Blade Demon happy. He was fully enjoying the unfamiliar pleasure of a first-time experience on this outing.

At that moment, the One Flower Sword Venerable raised her hand to shield her face from the sunlight shining on it.

Then the Blood Heaven Blade Demon casually lifted the large blade he was wearing as if resting it on his shoulder. As the large blade blocked the sun, the sunlight shining on the One Flower Sword Venerable’s face was shaded.

When she subtly moved back, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon also moved along, pretending not to notice.

The One Flower Sword Venerable smiled faintly as she looked at the Blood Heaven Blade Demon’s back. Then, looking at the cloth-wrapped knot, she spoke.

“This knot is holding out quite well.”

Without turning around, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon answered her.

“A bet is riding on it. How could it come undone?”

The two were still burning with competitive spirit.

Chatting about this and that while people-watching, the line shortened faster than expected.

“See, there are finally more people behind us than in front of us. This is what makes standing in line worth it.”

Listening to Jian Wuji’s words, Jihan thought to himself.

*Do these people know? That they are standing in front of the Heavenly Demon Cult’s Young Cult Leader, and behind him. That their meal today is with the Heavenly Demon Cult’s Young Cult Leader.*

The line gradually shortened until they were next to enter.

Just then, a lavish carriage stopped in front of the inn.

Seeing the martial artists on horseback guarding around the carriage, it was undoubtedly a carriage from a wealthy family.

A man and a woman got off the carriage.

The man got off first and helped the woman down.

The woman was wearing expensive, splendid clothes, and had shining jewels around her neck and wrists. Moreover, the accessories on her body were also very valuable.

The man also looked like a son of a wealthy family, but the fact that he didn’t take his eyes off her for a single moment showed he was completely smitten with the woman.

“The Five-Spice Braised Pork here is truly worth recommending.”

At the man’s words, the woman frowned slightly and looked at the inn.

“How disappointing. To bring me to such a shabby place with our wedding just ahead.”

From her disdainful gaze and the irritation mingled in her tone, it was clear what sort of person she was. If revealing one’s character with a single sentence could be called a talent, then it was a talent indeed.

“Even so, the food truly is excellent.”

The woman’s gaze turned this time toward the people standing in line. Her expression twisted of its own accord.

“I am not going to stand in line just to eat.”

“Of course not.”

The man gave his guards a look. At once, they stepped forward and blocked Geom Mugeuk’s path in an overbearing manner.

As if it were only natural, the man and woman cut in line and went inside, and the martial artists followed after them.

The waiter said to Geom Mugeuk with an apologetic expression,

“I’m sorry, sir.”

Geom Mugeuk smiled at the apologetic waiter and said,

“It’s all right. A skilled waiter knows to close his eyes at times like this rather than step forward.”

The waiter endured it, Geom Mugeuk endured it, and the Demon Venerable endured it, but in his position as a subordinate, Jihan could not.

“I’ll go in and give them a proper lesson.”

Judging by the level of the martial artists accompanying them, he could handle it well enough on his own. How dare they! He had every intention of giving them a thorough beating.

Then Geom Mugeuk said something strange.

“Why? Are you trying to forgive them?”

“Pardon?”

Jihan thought Geom Mugeuk had misheard him. He had said he would go and give them a sharp scolding.

But Geom Mugeuk had not misheard.

“Leave them be. If you step in and punish them for no reason, it might ruin the relationship between those two, wouldn’t it?”

Only then did Jihan understand. Leaving them alone was not an act of forgiveness.

It meant they should meet someone just like themselves and suffer together for the rest of their lives.

At those words, Hyeolcheon Doma shook his head.

“Terrifying brat! This one is worse than me. Isn’t he?”

At Hyeolcheon Doma’s gaze seeking agreement, Ilhwa Geomjon merely smiled.

Before long, it was their turn.

When they opened the inn door and stepped inside, the heat of the crowd and the smell of food rushed over them. The interior was truly spacious, and it was packed with countless people.

Amid the noisy bustle, people were drinking and eating, while waiters hurried busily between the tables carrying dishes on trays.

Looking around at the interior, which resembled a battlefield, Geom Mugeuk spoke with solemn resolve.

“If we leave, we won’t be able to come back and eat here. So even if someone steps on our foot, we let it go. If someone spills food on us, we let it go. If someone picks a fight, we let it all go. We’ll endure everything except insults to our parents! What matters is that our elders get to eat the five-spice braised pork!”

Chapter 744: Not Even Mediating the Orthodox-Demonic War Would Be That Diligent
-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
The waiter guided Geom Mugeuk’s party to their seats.

Following behind him, Geom Mugeuk asked,

“This is your first time in an inn this crowded, isn’t it?”

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon and the One Flower Sword Venerable nodded. To be honest, they had only come in because they were with Geom Mugeuk. When would they ever have had reason to stand in line just to enter such a crowded inn?

The One Flower Sword Venerable said something that put the Blood Heaven Blade Demon in a good mood.

“Brother, you are not the sort of person who would stand in line even if it led into a treasure vault.”

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon was pleased by the word brother, and pleased again that she had seen him so accurately.

Of course, Geom Mugeuk was not about to let that pass.

“If there’s a line like that, call me! I’ll stand there all night! I’ll come three days early and lay out a mat.”

The One Flower Sword Venerable laughed. The Blood Heaven Blade Demon glanced at her. It had truly been a long time since he had seen her laugh this often.

“Coming through!”

A waiter passed by them, holding a tray aloft with both hands.

His movements were so nimble it was as if he were displaying lightness skill. From every direction, people shouted for more wine, for more dishes. Even in that chaos, the waiters responded and moved with agility.

“Those people are the true heroes of this battlefield.”

Jihan, who was following behind, was observing Geom Mugeuk.

A man who saw everything and meddled in everything—that was his master. He was someone who even paid attention to a passing waiter.

Then they saw the man and woman who had cut ahead of them earlier.

Four guard warriors stood around the seats where they were sitting. It hardly seemed necessary to go that far, but the man clearly wanted to show off, and the woman enjoyed it.

Geom Mugeuk’s party was led to a table a little distance away from them.

“Now, leave the ordering to me.”

Geom Mugeuk ordered a large serving of five-spice braised pork.

“And two bottles of your best wine.”

“As you can see, there are many guests, so the food may take some time.”

“Tell them to make it delicious! We have honored guests here!”

“Yes, understood!”

Watching the waiter run off in good spirits after receiving the silver Geom Mugeuk had pressed into his hand, Jihan thought to himself.

How could they know just how honored the guests were? If they did, the owner would put it up beside the signboard. A house visited by the Young Cult Leader of the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult and Demon Venerables. The waiting line would probably grow several times longer.

“We’re finally getting to eat it!”

“No matter how good five-spice braised pork is, it can only be so good.”

“Once you try it, you won’t be able to say that. You may even ask to come again later.”

It was Geom Mugeuk’s way of expressing his wish that the two Demon Venerables would come again later.

In the meantime, the wine came first, along with some simple side dishes to go with it.

Geom Mugeuk poured wine for the Blood Heaven Blade Demon and the One Flower Sword Venerable in turn.

“Thank you, truly.”

It was a sincere expression of gratitude. On this journey, the two of them had been following wherever he led. There must have been times when they were reluctant, and parts of it that made them uncomfortable. Yet they were showing him what true adults looked like.

The One Flower Sword Venerable, receiving the wine, also spoke sincerely.

“I am the one who should be grateful.”

For the sake of the five-spice braised pork she liked, Geom Mugeuk had brought her all the way here.

She had lived through many emotions in her life. She had known joy, and she had known sorrow. There had been moments of being moved, moments of emptiness, anger, and regret.

But among all those emotions, there was one she had never truly felt.

Fun.

Had there ever been a time in her life when she had thought, so purely, that something was fun?

“This journey is truly enjoyable.”

“I’m glad you are enjoying it.”

This time, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon poured wine for Geom Mugeuk.

“Take it.”

Drip.

As always, the clear sound of wine falling into the cup carried the Blood Heaven Blade Demon’s feelings within it. For creating a moment like this.

Thank you.

After receiving the wine, Geom Mugeuk poured for Jihan and Taesu this time.

“A cup for my subordinate. And another for the gentleman thief who stole my heart.”

Taesu, who had grinned at Geom Mugeuk’s phrasing, suddenly realized that he had forgotten his own situation.

He had worried that the moment he revealed the location of the Sword Tomb and helped them pass through its mechanisms, they would get rid of him. But lately, he had not even been thinking about that.

‘Have I been possessed by something that wants me dead?’

There was nothing he could do. His opponent was someone who raised his cup high and shouted such things.

“Now, to our five-spice braised pork!”

Jihan and Taesu followed loudly.

“To the five-spice braised pork!”

Because they spoke too loudly, someone shouted from the other side.

“Who’s making all this noise?”

At a glance, it was a table full of warriors who looked thoroughly disreputable.

No sooner had the words fallen than Geom Mugeuk spoke loudly.

“My apologies! We’ll drink quietly!”

At Geom Mugeuk’s swift apology, they picked no further quarrel.

Geom Mugeuk lowered his voice and said,

“We endure everything until we eat the five-spice braised pork! Absolutely no bloodshed! Now, cheers!”

After that whispered toast, everyone emptied their first cup.

“Ah, the wine is excellent! Hurry up and come out, five-spice braised pork!”

But instead of the five-spice braised pork, something else burst forth.

An irritated voice rang out from the entrance of the inn.

“Move, you bastards!”

Geom Mugeuk’s gaze turned toward the entrance.
