A man with fierce eyes stood there, looked around the interior, then began walking toward them.

Geom Mugeuk quickly turned his head back and spoke in a low voice.

“Don’t look that way! Absolutely do not make eye contact!”

Even if he told them to look, would they have bothered with something so laughable? The Blood Heaven Blade Demon and the One Flower Sword Venerable filled each other’s cups, paying no attention to the man walking toward them.

Fortunately, the place the man was heading was not where Geom Mugeuk sat.

He walked over to the man and woman who had cut in ahead of them earlier.

When the woman saw the man approaching, she was startled. Soon, her expression turned vicious. Her eyes were every bit as poisonous as the man’s fierce gaze.

“Why are you here?”

“You threw me away just to marry this cripple?”

At the man’s words, the expression of the man sitting with the woman also hardened.

The command he gave his guards was as terrifying as his wounded pride.

“What are you doing? Cut off his arms and legs and throw him out.”

The guard warriors all stepped forward at once to block him.

“How like a cowardly bastard. Planning to hide behind your men and bark?”

“It’s because you’re not worth dealing with, you dogshit bastard.”

They seemed to know each other.

The guests around them rose from their seats and went outside. Food was important, but one’s life was more important.

At the sight, Geom Mugeuk sighed. They had caused trouble from the entrance, and now they were trying to make a scene inside as well.

“Why did it have to be today of all days?”

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon did not miss the chance to tease Geom Mugeuk.

“When you are on the move, how could even eating a single meal be easy?”

He meant that Geom Mugeuk always dragged storms of blood in his wake.

In any case, they had to be stopped. The moment blades were drawn, everything smashed apart, and corpses appeared, business for the day would be over.

While everyone watched their confrontation tensely,

“Just a moment!”

When Geom Mugeuk stepped forward, the man who had barged in responded coldly.

“What are you?”

“As you can see, I’m a guest at the next table.”

“Then shut up and eat. Why are you butting in?”

The man’s eyes had rolled back with rage, and he saw nothing. Not the Blood Heaven Blade Demon’s presence, as intense as a great saber, nor the One Flower Sword Venerable’s air that made her so difficult to approach. None of it entered his eyes right now.

“There is something I’d like to tell you quietly for a moment.”

When Geom Mugeuk approached, the man stepped back and tried to draw his sword.

But before anyone knew it, Geom Mugeuk was standing beside him, whispering in his ear. To everyone watching, it looked as though the motion in between had suddenly been omitted.

Geom Mugeuk and the man looked at the Blood Heaven Blade Demon at the same time. Anyone could tell. Geom Mugeuk was now speaking about the Blood Heaven Blade Demon.

The man’s expression changed from moment to moment.

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon shook his head, wondering what kind of absurd nonsense he was spouting this time.

When the whispering ended, the man who had seemed ready to overturn the entire place quietly left. He did not even look once at the woman.

When Geom Mugeuk returned to his seat, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon asked,

“You talked about me, didn’t you?”

“I did.”

“What did you say?”

“That the person over there is a great demon who once turned the martial world into a sea of blood, and that he has broken his seclusion and emerged into the world for the first time in a very long while. I also said he tears people’s arms and legs off while they’re still alive.”

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon asked with an incredulous expression,

“He believed that?”

“He saw your impression, sir, and just believed it.”

Should he laugh at this, or get angry? The Blood Heaven Blade Demon sipped his wine and said,

“You drove him off with something else, and now you’re using me as an excuse!”

“I said this too. That because you have lived as a bachelor for so long, you tear off the family jewels first from anyone having a lovers’ quarrel! The moment he heard that, he left.”

Listening to their conversation, the One Flower Sword Venerable laughed.

Geom Mugeuk could feel the Blood Heaven Blade Demon’s change—no, his effort.

When he usually visited the Blood Heaven Blade Demon and spent time with him, the man would never have played along with jokes like this. Yet today, the fact that he was answering every little thing must have been because of the One Flower Sword Venerable. Because he wanted to see her laugh like that even one more time.

‘Yes, please laugh that much.’

Just then, the man who had cut in earlier approached Geom Mugeuk.

“What did you say to that man?”

If someone drove a troublemaker away for him, he ought to thank them, but instead he asked in a quarrelsome tone.

Behind the man, the woman could be seen. She had probably said she was curious, and he had undoubtedly come over saying he would find out.

Jihan wondered whether the Young Cult Leader would deal with him pleasantly even in this situation. If it had been him, curses would have flown first. What sort of rudeness was this, after being saved from danger?

This time, Geom Mugeuk did not tell the story about tearing off arms and legs, but a different one.

“My uncle and aunt over there have been married for thirty years.”

At the unexpected lie, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon and the One Flower Sword Venerable widened their eyes in surprise.

“That very seat is where my uncle proposed to her.”

At the word proposed, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon’s eyes trembled slightly.

“I told him they had come here to reminisce about those days and eat the five-spice braised pork they ate here back then. Then he said it reminded him of his own parents, and that he would let it go just for today.”

“That fellow said such a thing? That can’t be.”

“Doesn’t everyone have stories others know nothing about?”

Then Geom Mugeuk changed the subject.

“I heard at the entrance. The two of you are getting married, yes? Congratulations. The two of you truly look good together.”

More than the congratulations on their marriage, the words that they looked good together softened the man’s expression.

“One day, after the years have passed, the two of you will surely return here as well, won’t you? Ah, I envy you.”

When someone spoke like that, what more fault could he find? Besides, Geom Mugeuk had driven off that fellow earlier.

The man returned to his seat, and Geom Mugeuk sat back down.

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon asked as if arguing,

“Do I look like the kind of person who would propose in a noisy place like this?”

The One Flower Sword Venerable answered in his stead.

“You also looked like a demon who tears off arms and legs.”

Geom Mugeuk laughed loudly, as if he found it amusing.

But the pleasant mood lasted only a moment. Another scuffle broke out in the inn with a crash and a clatter.

Those disreputable warriors who had shouted earlier had gotten into a fight with the guests at the next table.

Without turning around, Geom Mugeuk covered his face and sighed.

“Ah! I’ve eaten Jongyu Seontae and Seondo Yeonggwa, yet eating five-spice braised pork is this difficult?”

Geom Mugeuk shot up from his seat and ran toward the place where the fight had broken out.

Since Geom Mugeuk had gone to stop it, there was no way the fight would properly begin.

The men trying to draw their swords frowned. No matter how hard they tried to pull them out, the blades would not come free, as though they were wedged tightly in their scabbards. One man who had already drawn his sword sheathed it against his own will.

“What the hell is this!”

“What trick did you pull?”

Amid the uproar, Geom Mugeuk said one thing to this person, another to that person.

He went back and forth diligently, saying something to each of them.

Watching the scene, the One Flower Sword Venerable said,

“He looks as though he is going back and forth between the Martial Alliance and the Unorthodox Alliance, mediating a ceasefire in the Great War of Orthodox and Unorthodox.”

“He would not work that hard even to mediate the Great War of Orthodox and Unorthodox. What is he doing, fussing over people who would turn to dust and vanish if I blew on them?”

“It is his terrifying obsession with making us eat five-spice braised pork.”

The two of them laughed together. With only a slight exaggeration, they had laughed more on this journey than they had in their entire lives.

Fortunately, the Great War of Orthodox and Unorthodox stopped just before breaking out. Whatever Geom Mugeuk said to them, they reconciled, roared with laughter, and shared drinks.

Geom Mugeuk returned after helping the waiter clean up the food and broken wine bottles spilled beneath the table.

“I asked the waiter whether fights like this usually happen.”

“And what did he say?”

“He said this is his seventh year as a waiter, but he has never seen a day with this many incidents.”

“I told you it’s because of you!”

“Is it truly because of me? Or because of a terrifying demon?”

Geom Mugeuk and the Blood Heaven Blade Demon each insisted it was the other’s fault.

In the meantime, other guests sat down at the next table. As soon as they took their seats, they brought up a certain topic.

“Have you heard the rumor? They say the person with the treasure map to the Sword Tomb is passing through Jiangxi on his way to Zhejiang.”

“I heard Hunan has been thrown into chaos because of that treasure map.”

“People from all over the Central Plains are gathering. Now Jiangxi is about to be thrown into chaos too.”

Hearing their conversation, Jihan was startled. He had definitely applied the Soul-Pursuing Incense properly to Manak Sanin’s corpse. That corpse should be moving toward Shaanxi.

Yet the rumor was not following Shaanxi, but them.

“What is going on?”

At Jihan’s question, Geom Mugeuk answered,

“It seems someone wants the entire martial world to move because of this treasure map.”

Jihan recalled the man in the bamboo hat who had come to find him. The man who had made him apply the Soul-Pursuing Incense to the treasure map. He thought it might be him.

Taesu was also shocked. He had merely snatched the treasure map, but ever since then, the matter had been growing larger and larger.

Meanwhile, the waiter brought the food.

“It’s finally here! Even if a war breaks out right now, we have to eat this!”

At last, the five-spice braised pork was set on the table.

The freshly boiled meat had been sliced neatly and was sending up wisps of steam, with finely chopped scallions scattered over it. The five aromas of star anise, cinnamon, fennel, clove, and Sichuan pepper mingled with the rich scent of the meat, stimulating the appetite.

“Now, you two should eat first.”

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon yielded the first chopstick to the One Flower Sword Venerable.

“You eat first.”

Just as the One Flower Sword Venerable lifted her chopsticks,

The surroundings began to grow quiet.

A chilling aura spreading from the inn’s entrance had begun to fill the inside of the inn.

From that aura alone, one could tell he was an extraordinary master.

Soon after came a cold sentence, heavy with internal energy. Words more unpleasant to hear than a declaration that he would kill everyone flowed out.

“Everyone, put down your chopsticks!”

Looking at the steam rising from the five-spice braised pork, Geom Mugeuk sighed.

“Ah! You’re supposed to eat it before it gets cold.”

Chapter 745: We Go Forward
--------------------------
The man who revealed a threatening aura with his massive build was Cheon Daeung, leader of the Fire Dragon Unit.

“From now on, anyone who moves without my permission dies. Don’t even breathe.”

His voice, infused with internal energy, rang out thunderously.

Behind Cheon Daeung stood seven elite members of the Fire Dragon Unit.

Known as the Seven Flames, they had served as Cheon Daeung’s hands and feet from early on, men of rough and brutal temperament.

By the time Cheon Daeung became the Fire Dragon Unit Leader of the Evil King Sect, the number of people the Seven Flames had killed exceeded the entire number of the Fire Dragon Unit.

Having received information, Cheon Daeung had hurriedly pursued them, bringing only these men with him rather than the whole Fire Dragon Unit.

Cheon Daeung swept his blazing gaze around the inside of the inn.

Then his eyes met Jihan’s. The instant Jihan saw him, he sprang to his feet without realizing it.

“So you really were here.”

From Cheon Daeung’s words, it was clear. Someone had informed him that Jihan was here. Originally, Cheon Daeung should have gone to Shaanxi in pursuit of the corpse of the Manak Mountain Hermit.

“A mere hunting dog that goes around sniffing dares to pull something like this?”

Jihan’s eyes trembled. The contempt and torment he had suffered as a matter of habit came back to him, and for a moment his body froze. At the same time, all sorts of unpleasant emotions shook him.

“How dare the likes of you!”

Cheon Daeung’s fury exploded at the fact that he had nearly been fooled by none other than Jihan. The abusive words he had been holding back on the way here poured out.

“Did your father teach you to betray the organization that raised you like this? Right, your father must have crawled around on the ground sniffing the stink of shit too, eh? A monster bastard like you dares deceive me?”

Whose fault was all this in the first place? When Cheon Daeung, turning the tables on him, insulted his father, killing intent surged from Jihan’s eyes. Though his father had sent him away to his master when he was young, it had been so he would not suffer in a poor household and could live well.

It was then.

“Sit down.”

Surprisingly, the one who spoke calmly was not Geom Mugeuk, but the Ilhwa Sword Venerable.

Jihan stared in astonishment at the woman seated across from him. It was the first time she had spoken to him since they met.

“When we eat, we eat together.”

“Yes.”

Startled and bewildered, Jihan sat down. With that single sentence, the anger and killing intent that had almost engulfed him subsided in an instant.

The inn was wrapped in silence, and everyone was staring at her. She displayed an elegant dignity completely contrary to Cheon Daeung, who had been creating such a menacing atmosphere.

The Ilhwa Sword Venerable was the first to eat the five-spice braised meat.

Everyone was holding their breath so tightly that it seemed one could even hear the sound of her chewing.

The Ilhwa Sword Venerable briefly closed her eyes as if savoring the taste.

Soon, her expression brightened radiantly.

“It’s delicious.”

Cheon Daeung, watching the scene, was dumbfounded. He had clearly told them to put down their chopsticks, yet she was eating as if to show him?

Curses should have burst from his mouth immediately, but the beauty of the Ilhwa Sword Venerable, with her strange charm, made him pause for a moment to see what she would do.

Geom Mugeuk was delighted.

“Ah, what a relief. After all that effort to protect this five-spice braised meat, I was worried about what I’d do if it didn’t taste good.”

Truly, he had run here and there, stopping all sorts of fights, to preserve this very moment.

The Ilhwa Sword Venerable offered some to the Blood Heaven Saber Demon.

“Brother, you should try it too.”

The Blood Heaven Saber Demon also picked up his chopsticks and ate the five-spice braised meat.

“Not bad.”

From him, that much of a reaction was high praise.

“If you only like noodle dishes when you get older, it’ll ruin your health. Eat things like this often too, in a balanced way.”

The Ilhwa Sword Venerable’s gaze turned back to Jihan.

“Why are you not eating?”

Jihan was in no mood to eat, but he picked up his chopsticks.

“I will eat.”

“Good food is meant to lift one’s spirits.”

When he realized that she was trying to look after his mood, Jihan was inwardly astonished and moved.

Jihan ate the five-spice braised meat.

“It’s delicious.”

It was a lie. His mind was in such turmoil that he could not really taste it.

Just then, Jihan’s gaze suddenly met Geom Mugeuk’s. Seeing him, he realized he had momentarily forgotten whose subordinate he had become, and whom he was now with.

Freezing up at the sight of Cheon Daeung was discourteous to Geom Mugeuk.

“I apologize.”

When Jihan lowered his head, Geom Mugeuk said with a smile,

“You judged him correctly.”

“Yes?”

“A fool is certainly a fool.”

Earlier, when Geom Mugeuk had said he would kill Cheon Daeung for him, Jihan had stopped him, saying there being a fool in the enemy camp was better. And now that fool had come to find them on his own two feet.

Realizing that he had been called a fool, Cheon Daeung poured out killing intent.

“If you’re done stuffing your faces, prepare to die.”

Whoooooosh!

He did not care whether the people inside the inn were injured or not.

But the killing intent could not spread far and was blocked by something. Poong, poong, the killing intent bounced back softly.

Cheon Daeung flinched in surprise. It was as if an invisible membrane had wrapped itself around him. Since he had learned martial arts, this was the first time anything like this had happened.

Geom Mugeuk rose from his seat, then walked toward him with a bright smile.

“You must be my subordinate’s former superior.”

“Former superior?”

“Jihan has become my subordinate now.”

“Who decided that?”

It seemed like he would say, “I did,” but Geom Mugeuk looked at Jihan and said,

“By that man’s own will.”

He had not taken Jihan as a subordinate. Jihan had come asking to be taken in.

When the killing intent he had released was blocked, Cheon Daeung became somewhat cautious. First, he brought out the power backing him.

“Are you confident you can handle the Evil King Sect?”

At the words “Evil King Sect,” the customers watching turned deathly pale. They had already guessed from his words and actions that Cheon Daeung was not a good man, but the Evil King Sect? Their evil deeds were so severe that everyone shuddered at the very name.

“I can handle it, but the other guests will get indigestion.”

Tap.

Before anyone realized it, Geom Mugeuk had his arm around Cheon Daeung’s shoulders.

“Now, now. Instead of doing this here, let’s step outside for a moment.”

The two of them had clearly been standing a little distance apart, facing each other, yet suddenly they were side by side, so to everyone’s eyes, it looked as though the process in between had been omitted.

Naturally, the person most shocked was Cheon Daeung himself, and next were the Seven Flames standing beside him.

“Oh, your muscles are incredible! I happen to like muscular people.”

Geom Mugeuk poked Cheon Daeung’s forearm with his finger. Yet, for some reason, Cheon Daeung did not shake him off.

Because of that, the Seven Flames watching the scene instinctively realized Cheon Daeung had been subdued by him. He was someone who loathed others touching his body.

The two walked outside. The flustered Seven Flames followed behind them.

Watching that, Jihan sprang to his feet.

“I’ll be back too!”

His leader had gone out to fight because of him; he could not simply sit here like this.

When he ran out, Taesu also sprang to his feet and ran after him.

“They’re all young.”

At the Ilhwa Sword Venerable’s words, the Blood Heaven Saber Demon silently drank his wine. The Ilhwa Sword Venerable filled his cup.

“Do you miss it?”

Of course I miss it, she seemed likely to say.

But the Ilhwa Sword Venerable shook her head.

“I did before I met the Young Cult Leader. I regretted and longed for the past. Back then, I was very angry at my life.”

“And now?”

The Ilhwa Sword Venerable smiled without a word. There was no need to answer. She was showing all the changes in herself, from her outward appearance to her inner transformation.

This time, the Ilhwa Sword Venerable asked,

“Do you regret it, Brother?”

After a brief silence, the Blood Heaven Saber Demon finally drew out an emotion he had not revealed even once in the past thirty years.

“I regret it.”

The one who broke the heavy silence was the Ilhwa Sword Venerable.

“Let’s come here again. Let’s stand in line together too.”

The Blood Heaven Saber Demon looked at her in surprise.

“Next time, let’s come just the two of us.”

Gazing quietly at her, the Blood Heaven Saber Demon silently nodded.

* * *

Cheon Daeung could not resist.

He had no idea when his internal energy had been suppressed. On top of that, the blood points in his upper body had also been sealed, so he could only walk and could not move otherwise.

Was it because the god of death had covered his eyes?

Instead of thinking, “So he was a master!” he was having a different regret.

“I was careless!”

That was because he had been certain the masters were the old man and the woman. Geom Mugeuk was too young to be called a master. His behavior was too light, and he talked too much.

Cheon Daeung glanced behind him. Fortunately, his subordinates were sticking close behind. Yes, if he waited for an opportunity, he could get rid of this bastard.

Even in the meantime, Geom Mugeuk kept talking.

“Everyone lined up just to come eat something delicious. Who are you and I to cause a ruckus there?”

What the hell is this lunatic talking about? Already furious that he had been played by a hunting dog, Cheon Daeung grew even angrier at the fact that he had been caught by someone like this as well.

“But why did you curse my subordinate’s parents like that earlier? It was very unpleasant to hear. I’m usually quite good at enduring things, but I can’t stand insults to parents.”

“I wasn’t insulting you, was I?”

“If you had been, your head would be rolling around right now, kicked by the feet of busy waiters going to and fro. Before that, your body would have been cut in half. Ah, I’m not saying I would have done it. I’m the sort of person who absolutely hates smashing and killing things in taverns or inns. It’s just that the two people who were with me earlier happen to like my father very much. The moment the words ‘your father’ came out, your neck would have been sliced clean off, and your body too.”

As they spoke, the two arrived at an empty clearing behind the inn.

When they stopped, the Seven Flames surrounded them with their swords aimed. Since Cheon Daeung might be in danger, they could not attack rashly.

Trusting in the momentum of his subordinates, Cheon Daeung asked,

“The treasure map. You have it, don’t you?”

“That’s right. We have it. Then let me ask one question too. Who told you we were at this inn?”

Cheon Daeung did not answer.

Crack.

“Aaaaaaagh!”

A scream burst from Cheon Daeung’s mouth.

When Geom Mugeuk lightly twisted, one of his shoulders was crushed and broken.

“Oh dear, I apologize. I must have gripped too hard. I got nervous waiting for your answer. This time, I’ll hold this arm while I wait.”

When Geom Mugeuk tried to grab his other arm, Cheon Daeung shouted,

“I don’t know either! I only know it was someone the sect master sent personally.”

“If you don’t know, you should at least describe his appearance.”

“He was wearing a bamboo hat, so I couldn’t see!”

Geom Mugeuk could guess. It was the same man who had brought the treasure map and told him to apply the Soul-Chasing Incense.

At any rate, he had learned that the man had some connection to the Evil King Sect Master.

“I broke your arm too, so I feel bad. I’ll tell you one piece of information. In truth, the treasure map you’re looking for was deliberately leaked by the person who sent you.”

“What nonsense is that?”

“You called my subordinate a hunting dog, but you are the one who’s a hunting dog that knows nothing. Compared to you, my subordinate possesses a special talent that no one else in the world has.”

Geom Mugeuk looked toward a tree behind them.

“Jihan.”

Then Jihan, who had been hiding behind the tree, revealed himself.

“If you fought these men here, how many are you confident you could take?”

“Three.”

The Seven Flames’ expressions hardened viciously. How dare a mere hunting dog claim he could handle three of them?

“With your talent, if you train just a little, you’ll be able to face all seven.”

Geom Mugeuk had grasped Jihan’s ability and the strength of these Seven Flames with precision.

“I’ll show you the path later.”

At those unexpected words, Jihan’s heart began to pound. The Young Cult Leader was saying he would bestow guidance for his sake.

“There’s no need to be too moved. Everyone’s fallen into training hell and resents me for it.”

“If it’s that kind of hell, I’ll go as much as you like.”

Cheon Daeung aimed for the very moment Geom Mugeuk was conversing with Jihan and letting his guard down.

“Now!”

At the same time, the Seven Flames thrust the swords they had aimed at Geom Mugeuk. The sound of flesh being torn rang out in an instant. Not a single sword missed.

Thud! Thuuud! Thud! Thud! Thud-thud-thud!

The next moment, the Seven Flames’ eyes bulged at the scene before them.

The person stabbed by the swords in that spot was none other than Cheon Daeung. At some point, the two men’s positions had been switched. It was as if they had been aiming at Cheon Daeung from the start.

Since every strike had aimed only at vital points, Cheon Daeung vomited a gush of blood and died instantly.

Craaaack! Crack! Crack!

The necks of the Seven Flames, who were surrounding Cheon Daeung with their swords still thrust into him, began to sag limply.

Geom Mugeuk had moved in a swift circle and snapped their necks from behind.

His movements were truly like a flash of lightning, so before the Chilhwa could even pull out the swords they had thrust into Cheon Daeung’s body, all their necks were broken. It was over in a blink, and sound could not keep up with action.

Crack! Crack! Crack! Craaack!

With Cheon Daeung dead in the center, pierced by swords, the Chilhwa standing in a circle around him had all died with their necks snapped where they stood. If someone were to discover them, it would be a sight they could only call grotesque.

A shudder ran through Jihan’s entire body. He had known Geom Mugeuk was strong, but he had never imagined he was this strong. The gap between them was like heaven and earth.

The Young Cult Leader looked at him and spoke, the mischief gone from his gaze, replaced by a deep seriousness.

“Don’t look back anymore. We’re going forward.”

Jihan dropped to the ground and gave a deep bow.

“I will never be trapped in the past. Thank you.”

Just then, someone shouted from behind.

“Me too!”

Taesu revealed himself and was about to say something, but Geom Mugeuk raised a hand to stop him.

Then, as if he knew exactly what Taesu was about to say, he spoke calmly.

“Make your decision after you meet your father.”

Taesu realized he had been far too hasty and let out a long breath, as if taking a deep breath to steady himself.

“Yes, I understand.”

And so the three of them returned to the inn.

When they returned safely, the guests and waiters rejoiced with bright smiles. Some people even applauded.

Jihan and Taesu ate the five-spice braised meat at ease and drank wine. A special dish was brought out from the kitchen as well.

“It’s incredibly delicious.”

Only now did Jihan truly taste the real flavor of the five-spice braised meat.

Geom Mugeuk ran to the front of the kitchen and shouted through the opening where the food came out.

“It’s incredibly delicious! Please keep protecting this flavor without letting it change! There are people who will come here again someday!”

Watching him, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon and Ilhwa Sword Venerable looked at each other. They had said they should come again when it was just the two of them.

He was practically a ghost.

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon shook his head and held out his cup, and the Ilhwa Sword Venerable clinked his against it.

What filled their cups was not wine, but a promise.

* * *

The carriage ran and ran, and at last arrived in Zhejiang Province.

On the way here, they had seen countless martial artists gathering toward Zhejiang Province. Some were masters, and some were third-rate. Some had come in groups, and some had come alone. They were flocking in like moths to a flame.

When the carriage arrived at the foot of the mountain, only then did Taesu tell them the exact location of the Sword Tomb.

“The Sword Tomb is here on Tianmu Mountain.”

It was Tianmu Mountain, known as the Eye of Heaven.

“There are ponds on the eastern peak and the western peak of the mountain. They say that when you look down from the sky, they appear like eyes, which is why it is called Tianmu Mountain.”

Taesu revealed the exact entrance to the Sword Tomb.

“Beneath that left eye is the entrance leading to the Sword Tomb.”

Chapter 746: Shall We Go Treasure Hunting?
------------------------------------------
A new star shone, completing yet another constellation.

“Haa.”

A sigh slipped from the lips of Gaye, Palace Lord of the Divine Maiden Palace, as she looked up at the ceiling.

Around the largest star at the center, several constellations had lit up.

“The speed at which he is breaking through it is growing faster and faster.”

Behind her, as she reported with trembling eyes, stood Hwa Mu-gyeong, Palace Lord of the Dark Palace.

“At this rate, he will break through the seal of the Seventy-Two Star Golden Soul Formation and emerge even sooner than expected.”

Hwa Mu-gyeong merely looked up at the ceiling in silence, without the slightest agitation. The layers upon layers of wrinkles on his face concealed his emotions completely.

On top of that, a faint aura of darkness enshrouded him, making it impossible to even guess what he was thinking.

And so Gaye had to ask him directly.

“Are you not afraid, Palace Lord?”

“I am afraid.”

His voice, resonating like the toll of an ancient bell, spoke the truth.

Gaye knew. The only person in the world this Dark Palace Lord feared was the one within that seal.

That was why this Sword Tomb plan absolutely had to succeed.

“This is our last chance. Will he be able to handle the Young Cult Leader alone? Moreover, are the Blood Heaven Blade Demon and the One Flower Sword Venerable not with him as well?”

At Gaye’s concern, Hwa Mu-gyeong said something unexpected.

“He is not alone.”

At those words, Gaye’s eyes gleamed.

“Whom did you send?”

“Someone trustworthy enough.”

There was one more important fact.

“He has completed the song.”

“!”

Gaye asked in a trembling voice.

“How? He should still have a long way to go before reaching Great Accomplishment.”

Then astonishing words flowed from Hwa Mu-gyeong.

“I gave him the Heavenly Will Divine Pill.”

Gaye knew. The Heavenly Will Divine Pill was the greatest elixir of the Heavenly Will Palace, kept by the Dark Palace Lord to use at the most crucial moment.

He had judged that moment to be now.

“We will recover all of the Six Paths Primordial Qi, and the Young Cult Leader will die this time.”

* * *

“The entrance to the Sword Tomb is through an underwater cave hidden beneath the pond.”

Taesu’s voice trembled as he spoke of the entrance.

Inwardly, he was tense. Now that he had revealed his final card, his life was naturally in danger.

Afraid, he did not dare say anything to test Geom Mugeuk.

Of course, Geom Mugeuk was not about to miss the opportunity to tease him while he was so tense.

“You’re no longer needed, then.”

When Geom Mugeuk looked at him with cold eyes, Taesu flinched in surprise, then forced himself to show some nerve.

“Do you really think I told you the true location?”

“If it isn’t the lake on the left, we can try the one on the right.”

“What if it isn’t this mountain?”

“It isn’t this mountain?”

When Geom Mugeuk asked so bluntly, Taesu was momentarily at a loss for words. He had seen Geom Mugeuk all this way. Would saying it was not so at this point really work?

Instead of answering, Taesu let out a sigh.

“I’ll bury you in a sunny spot by the pond.”

“No, please bury me somewhere else. Every time I lie there and look at the pond, won’t I regret it? I’ll think, I should have run away back then.”

The playfulness vanished from Geom Mugeuk’s face.

“I’ll give you the chance now so that you won’t regret it.”

When Geom Mugeuk’s expression turned serious, Taesu tensed inwardly as well.

“From now on, think carefully before you decide. You know well that even if I don’t kill you, there is a high chance you will die in there. You may leave now. If you want to go, I’ll let you.”

At Geom Mugeuk’s offer, Taesu fell into thought for a moment. If he left like this, he could certainly preserve his life.

To begin with, he had meddled with a treasure map he should never have touched and still survived, so this was not a bad outcome at all.

If he abandoned his greed, he could live. Leaving when he was told he would be spared would be the wise choice.

“I… want to stay with you until the end.”

This time, he had a reason to remain until the very end.

The choice was not given only to him. Geom Mugeuk asked Jihan.

“What about you? There is no need to take unnecessary risks. You can wait for me in the village at the foot of the mountain.”

As if he had already thought about this part, Jihan answered at once.

“How could I alone remain somewhere safe when my lord is in a dangerous place? I, too, wish to attend you until the end. Instead, please promise me just one thing.”

“What promise?”

“Please treat me as though I am not there. Even if I fall into danger, there is no need to save me. You do not have to glance my way even once.”

He said it because he was worried that, with his inferior martial arts, he might become a burden to Geom Mugeuk.

His martial ability was by no means lacking wherever he went, but Geom Mugeuk’s skill was so extraordinary that such worries naturally arose.

“I wouldn’t have paid attention in the first place. But because you said that, now I’ll end up paying attention.”

“Ah! That was not what I meant.”

After enjoying the sight of Jihan flustered and at a loss, Geom Mugeuk said to him,

“Do not expect my help. If I have the leeway, I’ll help you, but when the situation is urgent, you must survive on your own.”

“That is only right. I will absolutely not become a burden.”

Seeing the resolve to face death in his eyes, Geom Mugeuk spoke words of reassurance with a gentle gaze.

“When you are with me, you don’t need to worry about that. If it is an enemy I cannot protect you from, then it is an enemy I could not defeat even if I fought alone anyway.”

Jihan was sincerely grateful that Geom Mugeuk was taking him along.

He did not know what sort of work he would come to do in the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult in the future, but he thought this experience of being with the Young Cult Leader of the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult would be both the first and last of its kind. And not only the Young Cult Leader. He would be with two Demon Venerables as well—truly, the greatest moment of his life.

He did not want to give up this once-in-a-lifetime event simply because he was afraid.

Geom Mugeuk gave orders to Taesu and Jihan.

“You two go to that village over there right now, rent a room at an inn, and leave the carriage there. Wait, unload this baggage first.”

Geom Mugeuk took his leather pack from the carriage.

At the sight, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon asked,

“Don’t tell me you’re planning to bring that baggage with you.”

“I have to. There might be a time when the two of you need to sleep in there.”

“We’re fine.”

“I’m not fine. When you’re away from home, you must be even more particular about where you sleep.”

After they unloaded the leather pack, Jihan and Taesu took the carriage and went to the village.

Now only Geom Mugeuk and the two Demon Venerables remained there.

Geom Mugeuk took a small piece of leather from the pack and spread it over a rock.

“Please sit here.”

The two sat side by side. Whatever they said, their backsides were comfortable on the soft leather.

Just then, the sound of weapons clashing came from up the mountain.

Even on the way here, they had seen martial artists gathering in Zhejiang. Yet within this vast Zhejiang Province, they were heading precisely to this Mount Tianmu. It meant someone was guiding them here.

“This is a trap, isn’t it?”

At the Blood Heaven Blade Demon’s question, Geom Mugeuk nodded.

“Yes, it seems so.”

“Does setting such an obvious trap even work?”

At that, Geom Mugeuk looked up the mountain.

“It is working, isn’t it? Everyone has gathered here like this.”

The One Flower Sword Venerable, who had been quietly listening to their conversation, asked Geom Mugeuk,

“Are those bastards behind the scenes involved in this as well?”

“It isn’t certain yet, but I believe so.”

Geom Mugeuk had only guessed, but the Blood Heaven Blade Demon drew a conclusion.

“If you think so, then it must surely be their doing.”

That was how much the Blood Heaven Blade Demon trusted Geom Mugeuk’s judgment.

“You, did you perhaps know from the beginning when you came out here?”

“I anticipated that it might be so. That is why I brought Elder along as well. If my expectations are correct, this may become a difficult fight.”

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon and the One Flower Sword Venerable exchanged glances.

“We should earn our keep for the elixirs, shouldn’t we?”

At the Blood Heaven Blade Demon’s words, the One Flower Sword Venerable smiled and answered,

“We should earn it handsomely. Both I and you, elder brother, have the results showing on our faces.”

A smile naturally formed at the corners of the Blood Heaven Blade Demon’s mouth. The tremendous increase in inner power? It was not important. These days, nothing pleased him more than being told he looked younger.

Meanwhile, Jihan and Taesu returned.

“On the way back, I saw martial artists arriving here one after another.”

It seemed the rumor had been spread, and spread quite properly at that.

“Now then, shall we go search for treasure too?”

With Geom Mugeuk in the lead, they began climbing Mount Tianmu.

Amid the sounds of fighting coming from all around, Taesu spoke quietly.

“The treasure map said this was Mount Tianmu, but the fact that the entrance had to be reached through the pond on the western peak could only be learned by deciphering the coded message. As for me, my father trained me from a young age to decipher such codes, so I was able to solve it, but others will not find it easy.”

How long had they climbed the mountain like that?

Halfway up the mountain, there was a large clearing, and a great fight was taking place there. It was not a halfhearted skirmish; people were being stabbed to death by swords one after another.

“Hand over the treasure map!”

Astonishingly, even here, the reason they were fighting was the treasure map.

“I told you I don’t have it!”

“That bastard has the treasure map!”

“That’s a lie!”

It was a veritable hellscape. They killed people, searched their clothing for the treasure map, and then someone else framed another person.

Judging from the situation, it was clear that most of those here did not even know where the entrance to the Sword Tomb was and had merely been swept up by the rumor.

The true experts, however, did not join the fight and instead stood well back, observing the situation.

Geom Mugeuk’s group also stayed far away and waited for the fighting to settle down somewhat.

The killing and being killed continued.

Then someone spoke, infusing their voice with inner power.

“Everyone, stop fighting! I will guide all of you to the Sword Tomb!”

At those words, those who had been fighting all stopped at once and stepped back.

Guide them to the Sword Tomb? Who would have thought someone would say such a thing in this situation?

Everyone’s astonished gazes turned toward the person who had spoken.

Not only Geom Mugeuk, but also the Blood Heaven Blade Demon, the One Flower Sword Venerable, Jihan, and Taesu all looked over in surprise.

The person who had stepped forward to guide them was wearing an iron mask over his face. Perhaps because he wore an iron mask, his voice sounded unusual. Because of that, it was not easy to guess his age.

“How do you intend to guide us there? Do you perhaps have the treasure map?”

As if to say indeed he did, the iron-masked man took something from his robes.

“That is correct. The treasure map to the Sword Tomb.”

A murderous greed surged in the eyes of those surrounding him.

The next moment, the iron-masked man spread the treasure map out before the people.

“Now, do not fight. All of you, look.”

The martial artists approached and looked at the treasure map. In their hearts, they wanted to snatch it and run, but the unique atmosphere and composure they felt from the iron-masked man up close did not allow it.

The location written on the treasure map was indeed Mount Tianmu, but the exact place was not marked, and instead there were incomprehensible characters and numbers written on it.

“As you can see, the precise entrance is written in code. Therefore, even if you obtain this, you cannot go there right away.”

Then someone shouted,

“How do we know that treasure map of yours is real?”

“The treasure map is real. I deciphered this code and discovered the entrance to the Sword Tomb.”

At those words, the martial artists murmured among themselves.

“And yet you say you will take us to the Sword Tomb?”

“That is right.”

Naturally, the martial artists did not believe him.

“Ridiculous. You expect us to believe that? This is a situation where even killing everyone here would not be enough, and you say you’ll take us all there?”

Among those present, there were many with hearts vicious enough to kill even the companions who had come with them. How could they possibly believe such words?

The iron-masked man revealed the reason.

“Because I cannot break through that place alone.”

At those words, everyone wore startled expressions.

“That place can only be entered if many people join forces. It was somewhere that absolutely cannot be passed through alone.”

Someone among the gathered heroes asked again,

“Who are you?”

“Rumors will spread later that someone obtained the treasure. Is there anyone here who wants to reveal who he is? Then you reveal your identity first. I will reveal mine as well.”

At that, no one asked who he was any further.

Just then, someone else stepped forward.

“This is a trap.”

The man who came forward this time was a middle-aged martial artist with a long sword scar running from his forehead down to his chin. It was a face one could not forget after seeing it once.

“Why is it a trap?”

He took something from his robes and spread it out before the gathered heroes.

“Because there is a treasure map here too.”

Those nearby checked its contents.

“It’s the same treasure map!”

Indeed, it was the exact same treasure map as the one the iron-masked man had shown. Not a single character was different; it was completely identical.

The scarred man said to everyone,

“What reason would there be to make several identical treasure maps? Is this not a conspiracy by someone to kill us all here?”

The martial artists murmured and wavered. Now that two treasure maps had appeared, it was a situation worthy of suspicion.

Then the iron-masked man asked in a loud voice,

“So? Will you not go in?”

The iron-masked man understood their desires more accurately than anyone.

“Whether there are two treasure maps, three, or even ten circulating, will you leave without confirming the place? If so, leave right now. Anyone who thinks it is a trap, leave immediately.”

Given the circumstances, there was a high chance that it was indeed a trap.

But no one left.

What if it was real? It was possible several treasure maps had been made, wasn’t it? What if he left and those who remained seized the treasures of the Sword Tomb? That was something they could not endure.

Someone said loudly,

“Someone must have made two treasure maps to make us think it is a trap and stop us from going to the Sword Tomb!”

Then the other martial artists shouted,

“I will not leave. Take me with you!”

“I will go too!”

“We will not leave.”

“We will follow you!”

Did they even know who the person beneath that iron mask was?

But everyone had lost their senses to the treasures of the Sword Tomb.

The iron-masked man said to the scarred martial artist,

“You say you think it is a trap, so why do you not leave?”

The scarred martial artist said nothing. But in the end, he did not leave this place.

Watching this, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon immediately suspected the iron-masked man first.

“That fellow is suspicious.”

At his quiet words, Geom Mugeuk looked at the scarred martial artist who had warned that it was a trap.

“By that logic, he is suspicious too.”

“Why?”

“The one who insists on taking everyone with him is suspicious, yes, but why did that man bother to step forward to stop them?”

Hearing it, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon found it plausible and nodded.

Looking around at the countless people nearby, Geom Mugeuk said quietly,

“Among them, there is surely an enemy of ours.”

It was at that very moment. Jihan inhaled through his nose with a startled expression.

“I smell something.”

Chapter 747: Your Death Leads Us to a New Path
----------------------------------------------
“It’s the smell from back then.”

The smell Jihan spoke of was the scent of the bamboo-hatted man who had told them to apply the Soul-Chasing Incense.

“He is here.”

But Jihan soon frowned.

“There are too many other smells mixed in here. I think I’ll only be able to tell if I get closer.”

In a place with this many people, it was already remarkable that he had detected that faint scent at all.

Just as Jihan was about to step forward to get a proper smell, Geom Mugeuk pulled him back and shook his head.

Jihan realized he had grown too excited. If he approached recklessly like that, they would reveal that they had noticed him.

The iron-masked man took the lead and climbed toward the peak.

“Now, everyone, follow me!”

The martial artists followed after him, though they were still half in doubt.

Geom Mugeuk and his group also followed behind.

The appearance of the iron-masked man had truly brought about a completely unexpected turn of events.

As if he really had deciphered the secret words, the iron-masked man climbed the western peak.

There was a large pond there—though calling it a pond was modest; it was large enough to be called a lake.

“Beneath this pond is an underwater cave that leads to the Sword Tomb.”

He knew the entrance to the Sword Tomb precisely.

“You must hold your breath for quite a while to pass through. Anyone who cannot swim, or who fears water, had best not enter.”

At that, one of the martial artists who feared water asked,

“How long must we hold our breath?”

“Until you can slowly count to one hundred.”

Most thought they could at least count to a hundred.

At that moment, Geom Mugeuk warned Taesu and Jihan, who were confident in their swimming.

“If you let your guard down after hearing only that, you’ll die. You’ll have to swim, and the bigger problem is that you won’t be able to see well down there. There may be obstacles you didn’t expect, and someone whose breath runs out might grab onto you and drag you down. Don’t think of that hundred as the same hundred you count outside the water.”

“Yes!”

Taesu and Jihan steadied themselves.

Geom Mugeuk was not worried about the Blood Heaven Blade Demon or the Ilhwa Sword Venerable. Experts on the level of Demon Venerables were people who could even fight a bout against waterfolk underwater.

“You must follow closely behind me.”

When the iron-masked man leapt into the water without hesitation, the martial artists jumped in one after another, fearing they might lose sight of him. They had judged it safest to stay right behind him.

Geom Mugeuk, on the other hand, was leisurely.

He took from his leather satchel a cloth used as material for a wind cloak and wrapped it around the satchel.

“You really do carry all sorts of things!”

Geom Mugeuk’s leather satchel truly seemed to lack nothing.

“Can the two of you swim somewhat?”

At Geom Mugeuk’s question, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon snorted. The Ilhwa Sword Venerable explained that confidence on his behalf.

“He’s a seal.”

Geom Mugeuk entrusted the leather satchel wrapped in wind-cloak cloth to the Blood Heaven Blade Demon.

“I’ll ask Lord Seal to carry this baggage.”

It seemed he would have to look after the two young fellows, so he was handing off the luggage. After all, they would have to pass through a narrow passage.

“What a fine thing to do to an old man.”

“You don’t look that old anymore.”

At that moment, the Ilhwa Sword Venerable held out her hand.

“Give it to me. I’ll carry it.”

Just as he was about to give it to her, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon took the leather satchel first.

Taesu jumped into the water first, followed by Jihan, then Geom Mugeuk and the two Demon Venerables.

Taesu guided the way from the very front.

True to his word, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon swam like a seal even while carrying that large baggage and his great saber, and the Ilhwa Sword Venerable also dove in with graceful movements.

The farther down they went, the darker their surroundings became. Fish appeared, then vanished from sight in an instant.

Then what suddenly loomed before them was a human corpse. Someone had already drowned.

Startled, Taesu lost his direction for a moment. The sight of the corpse had shaken him, and the bottom was darker than he had expected. Just as he floundered here and there for a brief moment—

Geom Mugeuk’s voice transmission reached him.

—To the right.

Sure enough, in the direction Geom Mugeuk indicated, there was a hole between the rocks. It had originally been hidden by waterweed, but those who had entered ahead had torn it all away, exposing the entrance.

As he entered, a sudden doubt crossed Taesu’s mind.

‘But does voice transmission work underwater?’

As far as he knew, he had heard that it did not. Had he perhaps heard things?

The cave was large enough for two or three adults to swim side by side, but it was dark, and they could not see ahead.

It was the sort of cave one would never enter unless one was certain there was a place to breathe at the end.

How long had they gone like that? Taesu panicked again. A drowned corpse was blocking the passage. He tried to push it aside, but the floating body moved however it pleased.

Because his mind was in a hurry, it would not clear away easily.

It was then.

Swaaaash.

When the Blood Heaven Blade Demon, following behind, stretched out his hand, bubbles surged up and the corpse was instantly pushed far forward.

Now the Blood Heaven Blade Demon swam at the front, clearing away the floating corpses as he advanced. There were more than just one or two bodies of those who had died along the way.

The passage was longer than expected. It felt as though they had already counted to a hundred.

‘At this rate…’

Taesu, whose martial arts were relatively weak, began to suffocate.

The terror of suffocation led to fear of death. To die without even reaching the entrance of the Sword Tomb! No, there was something even more shameful than that.

The guide drowning to death?

That would be shameful even after death.

‘I’ll do it! I’ll make it!’

But contrary to his will, his mind grew more and more hazy.

It was right then.

Geom Mugeuk’s voice transmission reached him.

—Close your eyes!

Was this an auditory hallucination again? Even if he had been told not to close them, Taesu’s eyes were already drifting shut at that very moment.

Swaaaaaash.

His body instantly cut through the current and shot forward. Jihan, who had been swimming behind him, was also pulled along by Geom Mugeuk’s hand and advanced with him.

They cut through the water in an instant and surged forward.

“Open your eyes.”

When he opened his eyes, the two of them were already sticking their heads out of the water. The corpses the Blood Heaven Blade Demon had pushed ahead floated there.

After throwing the two of them out of the water, Geom Mugeuk came out as well.

Taesu bowed his head and expressed his gratitude.

“Thank you very much for saving me.”

Jihan might have made it on his own, but Taesu had truly nearly died. He felt ashamed and embarrassed that he had once worried about what he would do if Geom Mugeuk arrived and killed him.

Soon after, the Ilhwa Sword Venerable came out of the water last.

There was enough space there for around ten people to stand, and it connected again to an underground cavern. Whether those who had come out earlier had all gone into the cavern, no one remained there.

The Ilhwa Sword Venerable’s wet clothes clung tightly to her body. The Blood Heaven Blade Demon turned around and subtly shielded her with his great saber.

Fwoosh.

The Ilhwa Sword Venerable raised yang heat energy within her body and dried her clothes while standing there.

“I think this is the first time I’ve swum through an underwater cave like this. What about you, Brother?”

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon also dried his clothes with yang heat energy as he replied.

“As if I would have.”

Geom Mugeuk did not dry his clothes. It was enough for two people to appear fresh and dry.

When they walked into the cavern, a wide space appeared, and those who had entered before them were there. Many had jumped into the water, but after the deaths, only about twenty martial artists remained here.

Their gazes turned toward the five of them. At least one person should have been reduced from the count, yet five had joined them, so naturally their gazes were not friendly.

At that moment, someone shouted,

“Hurry and open the door!”

Ahead, behind where the iron-masked man stood, there was a stone door.

This was the entrance to the Sword Tomb.

Thinking that beyond this door lay something that would change their fates, the martial artists’ faces as they looked toward it were full of greed and longing.

The iron-masked man slowly opened the door.

When the door opened, the interior was revealed.

What overwhelmed everyone’s gaze were hundreds, thousands of swords. Swords were embedded everywhere the eye could see. It was truly a scene befitting the name “tomb of swords.”

Looking closely, this place was not merely flat ground.

It was a recreation of some great hall. Pillars and chairs, stone statues lining the walls—swords were embedded in everything visible.

Countless swords, each seeming to have its own story.

The martial artists first examined the swords around them. Their eyes burned, wondering whether some treasured blade might be hidden among the old iron swords.

“Look only. Do not draw them under any circumstances!”

But whenever many people gathered, there was always someone bound to ignore such a warning.

The very moment someone drew a sword from an ornate scabbard—

Shhk! Thunk!

A sword blade shot up from the floor and pierced through his groin. It sprang up with a speed as fast as light, making it impossible to avoid.

No one lamented his death.

The iron-masked man looked toward the grand preceptor’s chair far ahead and said,

“We must reach the door behind that grand preceptor’s chair. And the moment we leave this spot, the mechanism activates.”

The distance from where he stood to the grand preceptor’s chair was ambiguous.

It was a distance that anyone confident in their lightness skill would want to attempt at least once.

When that ambiguous distance combined with the greed to pass through first, one person launched himself forward.

When he had crossed about halfway—

Shhhk! Thunk!

Contrary to the first dead man, his head was pierced through by a sword blade that fell from the ceiling, killing him.

At the same time, another person was already cutting through the air.

‘The chance is the very instant the mechanism activates!’

Before the previous man’s body had even fallen to the floor, he was passing through that space.

Shhk! Clang!

He struck away the hidden weapon that fell toward his head.

‘I did it!’

The moment he once again kicked off the air with force and was about to rush toward the door—

Shhhhk!

A sword blade flew in again with light-like speed and pierced through his heart. It was an attack that had come diagonally from the ceiling at an oblique angle; the angle was difficult to parry, but more than that, its speed was far too fast.

Clank!

The floor opened, and just as the corpse fell through—

Another expert rushed toward that place.

The moment the corpse is disposed of is the opportunity!

Contrary to his hopes, reality was this:

Disposing of the corpse was the trap.

The three corpses vanished underground in an instant.

A brief silence followed.

Their greed had been excessive, but that did not mean their skills had been lacking.

Then someone roared,

“Let’s just smash everything and go in!”

At that, the iron-masked man spoke firmly.

“That will not do. It was written on the treasure map. If we try to force our way in by destroying the mechanisms, this place will collapse and be submerged.”

More terrifying than death was the fact that they might not be able to obtain the treasures here.

“Now do you understand why I brought you all in?”

Geom Mugeuk looked at Taesu. Previously, he had been confident. He had said that he knew not only the location of the Sword Tomb, but that only he would be able to break through its mechanisms.

But Taesu whispered with a flustered expression.

“The description of the mechanisms written on the treasure map is completely different.”

Geom Mugeuk was not surprised in the slightest. There was no way they would have obediently revealed the mechanisms of this place in advance while scattering multiple treasure maps.

The iron-masked man asked everyone,

“Is there anyone who knows how we should pass through this place?”

When no one answered, the iron-masked man pointed to Geom Mugeuk.

“Young hero with the baggage over there.”

Everyone’s gaze turned toward Geom Mugeuk.

“Might you have a method?”

“Why are you asking me?”

“Because of that baggage. The fact that you swam all the way in here carrying such a large burden means you must have skill to match, does it not? If you possess such skill at a young age, you must have a clever mind as well. A problem like this is something a young, clever person should be able to solve.”

Geom Mugeuk could feel it. The man knew his identity. He was testing him.

‘Is it you?’

But for someone behind all this, he had appeared before him far too openly.

Geom Mugeuk slowly walked forward and looked around at those standing there. As people died, only the true experts and the cautious were being left behind. The one behind this might be among them.

“Have none of you found a single strange thing about this place?”

At that, the people looked around. Soon, eyes that had failed to find an answer turned back toward Geom Mugeuk. The same was true of the Blood Heaven Blade Demon and the Ilhwa Sword Venerable.

What Geom Mugeuk had found was something no one had thought of.

“To me, the strangest things in this place were the swords.”

Everyone wore puzzled expressions. The iron-masked man spoke for all of them.

“Swords are strange in a tomb of swords?”

Rather, everyone looked at Geom Mugeuk as though he were the strange one. After all, the swords stuck everywhere were the least strange thing in this place.

Geom Mugeuk asked them,

“Was this place a Sword Tomb from the very beginning?”

For a moment, everyone flinched.

“Is this not the place that was named the Sword Tomb because, decades ago, countless masters entered and died here? The name Sword Tomb was given after they died, so isn’t it strange for this gate to have been made up like a Sword Tomb?”

“!”

It meant that this gate had been created after the Sword Tomb came into being.

The possibility that this place was a trap had grown even greater, but no one said they would turn back.

Geom Mugeuk slowly looked around.

At length, his steps came to a halt before a stone statue into which several swords had been thrust.

It was the statue of a woman holding a pipa. The woman wore an expression that looked truly sorrowful.

Geom Mugeuk had come here once in the life before his regression. Back then, this statue had definitely not been here. If it had been, it was the sort of statue he would certainly have remembered.

“Was this woman a musician? Or was she a master of sound arts?”

Geom Mugeuk flicked the sword embedded in her abdomen with his finger.

Ting.

A clear metallic sound rang out.

This time, Geom Mugeuk flicked the sword embedded in her side.

Tiiing.

Next was the shoulder, then the thigh. Whether because the metal was different, or because the depths to which they were embedded differed, every sword in her body produced a different sound.

Then Taesu cautiously said to Geom Mugeuk,

“That sound just now was exactly the same as a pipa note.”

“The same as a pipa note?”

“Yes.”

“You know how to play instruments?”

“When I was young, I learned a little of this and that.”

His father had taught him to play, saying that to become a proper thief, one had to know about instruments as well.

Geom Mugeuk looked back at the stone statue.

“Could it be?”

After asking Taesu, Geom Mugeuk began with the sword that had produced the lowest note and drove them deep into the statue one by one. The sword that produced the highest note was the last to be embedded in her heart.

The moment all the swords had been driven into the statue—

Ruuumble.

The earth suddenly began to shake, and the floor started to move.

Clatter, clatter, clatter.

The swords embedded in the floor seemed to shuffle and mix here and there.

Then, as though inviting them to step across, sword hilts of the same color lined up in a row all the way to the door.

At the sight, Taesu shouted in delight.

“The mechanism has been broken!”

Everyone looked at Geom Mugeuk in shock. Some were impressed, while others were suspicious.

Once one knew the answer, it was a simple method, but without knowing it, it was also a method of breaking through that one could never discover even after spending a lifetime here.

The iron-masked man looked at Geom Mugeuk with eyes filled with many emotions.

“That was truly remarkable!”

“I was lucky. It just so happened that the person I brought with me had learned instruments.”

Of course, only the Blood Heaven Saber Demon and the Ilhwa Sword Venerable knew that it had been possible precisely because it was Geom Mugeuk. The two looked at each other, shook their heads, and then nodded as well.

Before leaving, Geom Mugeuk offered a final farewell to the statue.

“Your death has guided us onto a new path. Thank you.”

Everyone stepped on the sword hilts and passed through. Sure enough, there was no attack.

Having passed through the first gate, they opened the second door.

There were swords here as well.

But there was one difference from before. If the previous room had contained countless swords, this place held only a single blade.

In the center of the room sat a corpse reduced to white bones, and a single sword was resting against its body.

Chapter 748: Long Time No See, Friend
-------------------------------------
Everyone’s gaze turned to the sword leaning against the skeleton.

This single sword struck them far more intensely than all the countless swords they had seen before.

“Do not act rashly!”

Even if the iron-masked man had not gone out of his way to warn them, none of them could have charged in recklessly. They had already experienced just how terrifying the mechanisms here were.

Moreover, even if one seized the sword first, there was a high chance they would be attacked by everyone else at once.

Everyone fixed all their attention on the sword while watching one another’s reactions. Amid the hair-trigger tension, the iron-masked man pointed to Geom Mugeuk again.

“Young Hero with the Baggage.”

“It seems my name has been fixed as Young Hero with the Baggage.”

“I refused to reveal my own identity, so how could I ask another man’s? That is why I call you so.”

“Why did you call me?”

After carefully looking around the room once, the iron-masked man asked again.

“What sort of mechanism do you think is hidden here?”

“Why are you asking me that?”

“Because you were the one who dismantled the previous mechanism. You called it luck, but luck only comes when one who is prepared meets an opportunity.”

Everyone’s gaze turned to Geom Mugeuk. They all seemed to be harboring subtle expectations.

Even after learning that multiple treasure maps existed, even after it had been revealed that these mechanisms had been made after this place came to be called the Sword Tomb, they still did not know how to give up.

Their greed and ambition were not something he could do anything about.

What Geom Mugeuk was curious about was this. If this entire affair was a conspiracy by those behind the scenes?

Why had they gone out of their way to drag even these people into it?

Was it to lure him here naturally? Even without them, he would have entered this place. Then why had they scattered several treasure maps and drawn people in? To the point of throwing the entire Central Plains into an uproar.

Geom Mugeuk’s gaze went to the sword resting against the skeleton.

“To tell the truth, obtaining that sword is easy.”

At the word easy, everyone looked startled.

“The problem will be what comes after obtaining it.”

After saying those cryptic words, Geom Mugeuk slowly walked toward the skeleton in the center of the room.

Hyeolcheon Doma and Ilhwa Sword Venerable watched the situation with taut nerves. They were ready to leap in at any moment if the situation turned dangerous.

The mechanism they had experienced in the previous room had been a supreme-grade device into which an enormous amount of money had clearly been poured. There was no knowing what danger might be lurking in this room as well.

But Geom Mugeuk was certain it was safe.

“There likely won’t be any mechanism attack.”

It seemed as if blades might come flying from the ceiling and floor at any moment, but just as Geom Mugeuk predicted, no attack came until he reached the skeleton.

Geom Mugeuk did not immediately pick up the sword. Instead, he clasped his fist before the skeleton and paid his respects.

“I do not know who you are, senior. Whether you were a virtuous man or a wicked one, whether you too entered this place to obtain treasure like us today and died, or whether you came here for some other reason, I know nothing. But whatever sort of person you were, I believe it is wrong to use you, now reduced to white bones, in a conspiracy. They will surely pay for their crimes. So I hope you cast aside your resentment and rest in peace.”

The moment those words ended—

Clatter.

The skeleton collapsed.

The people let out brief exclamations. It looked as though it had understood Geom Mugeuk’s words.

Just as Geom Mugeuk bent down to pick up the sword—

Whoosh.

He felt a single breath of poisonous smoke blooming from the skeleton enter his body. It had neither color nor smell; if he had not been immune to all poisons, he would not even have known he had been poisoned.

‘It’s Purple Cloud True Qi Poison!’

It was a poison he had learned of while studying various poisons as he helped the Poison King.

Purple Cloud True Qi Poison was not a poison that killed the victim instantly. One would not even know one had been poisoned until it activated only when one used fierce internal energy. Once the poison took effect, the entire body would turn purple, movements would grow sluggish, and the blood and qi would become tangled.

It was truly a supreme poison that required long years and vast sums of money to create, but against Geom Mugeuk, who was immune to all poisons, it had no effect whatsoever.

If they had meant to kill me, they would have used Formless Venom, wouldn’t they?

It was then. A strand of voice transmission flew to Geom Mugeuk.

—You have just been poisoned by an extremely potent poison.

Geom Mugeuk slowly picked up the sword lying by the skeleton and turned around.

Everyone was looking at him.

The voice in the transmission was not the iron-masked man’s, nor was it the voice of the martial artist with the sword scar.

Geom Mugeuk sent a voice transmission to one of the martial artists there.

—How did you know I was poisoned?

He was a middle-aged martial artist of ordinary appearance and build standing among the crowd.

Until now, he had never once revealed his presence. He had simply seemed like one of the people who had joined in because they were crazed over treasure.

His eyes trembled slightly.

—How did you know I was the one who sent the transmission?

—Because you alone were looking at me, not the sword.

—!

The man was truly shocked. He had not expected him to find him among all these people for such a reason.

In truth, that was not why Geom Mugeuk had noticed him. Though his back had been turned, he had precisely sensed where the voice transmission had come from. Geom Mugeuk’s martial skill lay beyond ordinary common sense.

—Who are you?

Judging from the fact that he had informed him of the poisoning, he did not seem to be an enemy.

The man did not reveal his identity.

—I have the antidote. If the opportunity comes, I will give it to you.

Geom Mugeuk could tell. Not only did the man know that he was the young cult leader of the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult, he was also deeply connected to those who had made the mechanisms here. At the very least, he was someone who had known about the poison arranged in that skeleton.

Just then, one of the anxious martial artists shouted.

“Hurry and draw that sword!”

“Draw it!”

Geom Mugeuk slowly drew the sword. The blade that revealed itself emitted a cold chill. At a glance, it was unmistakably a treasured sword.

There were characters engraved on the blade in vigorous strokes.

Danhon (斷魂).

It was the personal sword of Danhon Wanderer, the peerless master who was said to have entered the Sword Tomb in the past. Seeing the characters engraved on the sword, the man who had exchanged voice transmissions with Geom Mugeuk shouted.

“It’s the Danhon Sword!”

He was also someone who knew that shouting and showing greed would make him less conspicuous instead.

Once it was revealed that the sword was the Danhon Sword, many people were shaken. A burning desire filled the eyes of the martial artists.

Yes, so what if this place was a trap? If only they could obtain such a treasured sword and leave!

Geom Mugeuk poured cold water on that desire.

“In this room, there is a mechanism even more terrifying than the previous one.”

At the sudden words, everyone was startled and looked around. They examined the walls and the floor, but no matter how much they searched, it did not seem as though any mechanism was hidden there.

“Where is this mechanism?”

To the iron-masked man’s question, Geom Mugeuk gave an answer no one had expected.

“This time, the mechanism is all of you.”

As they stared at him with expressions asking what on earth he meant, Geom Mugeuk revealed his meaning.

“The greed within all of you who want this sword is the mechanism of this room.”

He meant that the bloody struggle to seize the sword was itself the mechanism device in this room.

Since these were people who had waged that slaughter to seize the treasure maps, his words were not an incorrect conjecture.

“Who will have this sword?”

They all must have wanted to answer, Me, but no one replied.

Geom Mugeuk asked the iron-masked man.

“Since you guided us to the entrance of this Sword Tomb, should you have it? Or should I have it, since I broke through the previous trial? Or should we hold duels among ourselves and let the strongest person take it?”

Everyone fell silent. If the first person to hold the sword had not been Geom Mugeuk, a battle for it might already have broken out.

The iron-masked man asked Geom Mugeuk.

“So? What does Young Hero with the Baggage think?”

Geom Mugeuk stared straight into the two eyes within the holes of the iron mask and said,

“Let us leave it here and go. As if to show those who devised this vile scheme, let us leave this sword as it is and pass by. Let us show them once that we do not move as they intended. That is the way to break through this room.”

Perhaps this place was far more difficult than the room they had passed through before. Because they had to break through the greed of those filled with avarice.

“If you have a better method, speak.”

As everyone fell briefly into thought, Jihan’s voice transmission flew in.

—I have discovered who the smell is coming from. It was coming from that man wearing the iron mask.

They had suspected two people, the iron-masked man and the martial artist with the sword scar, and this was the moment it was confirmed that the iron-masked man was the one behind the scenes. Yes, it had also been the iron-masked man who led him to be poisoned.

—Well done.

At Geom Mugeuk’s praise, Jihan felt pleased. It was the first moment since becoming his subordinate that he had properly played a role.

Geom Mugeuk again sent a voice transmission to the man who had spoken to him earlier.

—If you do not wish to tell me who you are, let us at least know each other’s names.

After hesitating for a moment, the man revealed his name.

—I am Dan Sojin.

—I am Geom Mugeuk.

—I know.

As expected, he knew who he was.

—Then tell me just one thing. Who is that man with the sword scar on his face?

He thought that if it was this Dan Sojin, he would know not only the iron-masked man’s identity but also who the scarred martial artist was. And indeed, he knew the identity of the man with the sword scar.

—He is someone from the Sado Alliance.

Geom Mugeuk was not surprised. Considering where this place was in the first place, it was not at all strange that an expert from the Sado Alliance had come.

This place was the tomb of Ju Gasin, the Sado One Sword, who had been the alliance leader of the Sado Alliance three hundred years ago and was famed as the greatest master of the Unorthodox Faction.

The iron-masked man belonged to the force behind the scenes, and the scarred martial artist was a martial artist of the Sado Alliance.

Now what remained was which side this Dan Sojin belonged to.

Then the iron-masked martial artist made a decision.

“I will follow Young Hero with the Baggage’s wishes. I do not think we can stand here fighting among ourselves over a single sword.”

At that, the other martial artists also eventually followed his decision. It was not an atmosphere where one could take it simply by being greedy alone.

“Now, let us go.”

As they walked toward the door at the back, in the end, someone threw himself toward the Danhon Sword.

In an instant, he snatched up the Danhon Sword and launched himself toward the first room they had entered from.

He could not endure it. If only he could carry this sword and leave through that door! If only he could!

The Danhon Sword, which severed the soul, had severed restraint from his soul.

“Not a chance!”

The other martial artists surged toward him. They were already furious that they could not have it themselves; they were not the sort to let someone else have it.

In an instant, three people attacked him together. Unable to escape the room in time, he drew the Danhon Sword and swung it.

Clang!

The Danhon Sword broke his opponent’s sword in a single stroke and cleaved straight through his body.

Slash! Slash!

The swords of the other two martial artists cut his thigh and side.

The man clenched his teeth and swung the Danhon Sword again, cutting down the martial artist who had slashed his thigh. Among ordinary swords, the Danhon Sword displayed truly formidable power.

But his skill could not match the sword. Cutting down three men with the power of the sword was his limit.

With a stab, a sword emerged from his back, and his movements stopped. There was no regret in his eyes as he looked at the Danhon Sword.

“…I got to swing it once before dying. That’s enough.”

After saying those words, he died and collapsed.

“Damn bastard!”

The martial artist who killed him picked up the Danhon Sword, seeming as though he would return it to its scabbard.

But he too launched himself toward the entrance they had come through. After seeing every sword that clashed with it break apart, he too had been unable to resist.

However, he did not even get a chance to swing the sword.

Shhhk.

A dagger that flew like light pierced through his back.

It was a dagger thrown by the martial artist with the sword scar on his face.

The fleeing martial artist staggered, then sank to the ground and died.

The way he died embracing the sword was, by coincidence, no different from how the skeleton had first appeared here.

If someone were to come here after time had passed and see this person, they would witness the exact same sight as before.

Now no one attempted to seize the sword any longer.

The iron-masked man opened the door leading to the next room.

Everyone was shocked by the sight that unfolded before their eyes.

Inside, martial artists were fighting one another. They were not real people, but dolls made in actual size.

They were crafted so intricately that they looked as though real people were fighting.

When they first saw it, they naturally thought it was a recreation of a battle that had taken place in the Sword Tomb.

But it was not.

Several people were attacking one person together, and the person being attacked at the center was, astonishingly, Geom Mugeuk.

The dolls surrounding and attacking him were the Twelve Zodiac Kings he had faced until now.

From the Wood King, who had died at the Murim Alliance, to the Illusion King and Hwanyeo, who had died at the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult; the Fighting King, who had died at the Sado Alliance; the Blood King, who had died at the Northern Sea Ice Palace; the Killing King, who had died at the Golden Dragon Clan; the Fist King, who had died at the Yellow Dragon Martial Hall; and the Dark King, who had died at Cheonhwa Pavilion.

They were attacking Geom Mugeuk from every direction. Branches stretched out from the Wood King’s body, and evil spirits were leaping out of the door that the Illusion King and Hwanyeo had opened in midair.

The Fighting King was charging like a mad bull, and the Blood King’s blood was scattering everywhere. As the Fist King’s fist flew in terrifyingly, darkness had settled around the palanquin in which the Dark King rode, and the Killing King was leaping over that darkness to attack Geom Mugeuk.

Every small detail had been made with astonishing delicacy and vividness. It truly seemed as if a battle was unfolding before their eyes.

Because they were all dead, these dolls felt as though they had been made to commemorate the deceased Twelve Zodiac Kings.

The reason Geom Mugeuk was shocked was not because there was a doll of himself. Nor was it because the dolls surrounding him were the Twelve Zodiac Kings.

A living person was standing in front of those dolls.

Because he blended in naturally with the dolls, he too had looked like a doll at first.

He stood barefoot, wearing shabby martial robes, his hair tied tightly back, and an old iron sword at his waist.

The person in this murim who suited the Sword Tomb best.

Sword King Ak Gunhak, who had been looking at the dolls, turned toward Geom Mugeuk.

Greeting him with a bright smile, at his waist hung the shoes Geom Mugeuk had given him as a gift.

“It’s been a long time, my friend.”

Chapter 749: Every Last One of Them Is an Eyesore
-------------------------------------------------
