Had there been a time in recent days when he had been this surprised?

Geom Mugeuk had truly never dreamed he would see Ak Gunhak in this room.

His voice rang thunderously through the place as he called out to him.

“Brother Ak!”

Geom Mugeuk called him Brother Ak with ease. They had agreed that, when they met again, they would be friends. So now he was not the Sword King, but Brother Ak.

Geom Mugeuk’s eyes trembled as he looked at him. He did not hide his joy at seeing him. He forced himself to hold back the urge to run over at once and throw his arms around him.

Do you remember what I said?

See each other when we miss each other, help each other when help is needed. At times, even throw shoes at one another—be that kind of friend to me.

He was someone Geom Mugeuk had truly missed. But he was not someone he should be seeing here.

“When we met again, I thought we’d run into each other while roasting meat out in a field. I thought we’d meet again drunk in a tavern, bumping shoulders. No, I even thought it would be fine if we never met again.”

Geom Mugeuk let out an exaggerated sigh and said,

“But why are you in this gloomy underground place? No matter how much you like the underground, this isn’t it, is it?”

He was referring to the time they had broken through the underground gate of the Yellow Dragon Martial Hall. The moment they had climbed up together and looked at the stars, the moment they had stuffed themselves full of elixirs until their bellies nearly burst, came back to him as if it had happened only yesterday.

“I’m sorry.”

How could Geom Mugeuk not know? There had to be some reason he had been unable to leave.

“Were you unable to repay all your debts?”

After a brief pause, the Sword King answered.

“I repaid them all.”

“Then why couldn’t you leave?”

The Sword King did not tell him.

What on earth had held him back? What could have shackled the feet of someone this strong, someone who dreamed so fiercely of freedom?

“If a man said he was leaving, he should have left. You should have smacked anything that blocked your way over the head with those shoes and gone.”

Geom Mugeuk’s gaze fell on the Sword King’s shoes. They were new, as if he had never worn them even once.

“I bought them for you to wear, but you didn’t even wear them. I held a gathering of friends, and you didn’t even come. You knew I held that gathering, didn’t you?”

To his surprise, the Sword King nodded. It meant he had been paying attention to Geom Mugeuk.

“I was afraid you’d make me dance, so I couldn’t go.”

“You can’t dance, you can’t leave—what is this!”

Yet contrary to his words, Geom Mugeuk’s face was filled with happiness. How could he possibly hide this joy?

“Still, I’m so glad to see you again.”

Everything he had said before had been for the sake of saying this.

The two of them looked at each other and smiled brightly. Whatever position they each found themselves in now, being glad to see each other was being glad to see each other.

By contrast, the expression of the Blood Heaven Saber Demon, who had been watching them, hardened. He had guessed who the other man was.

—It’s him.

Since Geom Mugeuk told him all sorts of stories whenever he left the cult and returned, he knew this man was the master swordsman who had survived the battle at the Yellow Dragon Martial Hall.

He could feel it just by looking. He could feel how strong this barefoot master was. Among all the enemies he had met so far, this man radiated the most powerful presence.

—Yes, that’s right. It’s him.

The One Flower Sword Venerable had met the Sword King together with the Fist Demon during that battle.

Because Ak Gunhak had emerged from the underground first, a fight had nearly broken out. In that hair-trigger situation, Geom Mugeuk had followed him out, and fortunately, no fight had occurred.

The Blood Heaven Saber Demon looked displeased. This felt different from Geom Mugeuk being friends with Jin Hagun or Bisa-in.

—He looks dangerous.

—He’s strong.

In truth, he had not said the man looked dangerous because he seemed strong. It was because he sensed that Geom Mugeuk was opening his heart too defenselessly to an enemy.

—Those bare feet are dangerous.

The One Flower Sword Venerable looked at the Blood Heaven Saber Demon. He was seeing something she could not.

Geom Mugeuk and the Sword King’s conversation continued.

“In the meantime, our Brother Ak even gained a woman who likes him.”

“Me?”

“We talked about Brother Ak so much. Because of Brother Ak, one woman’s life changed.”

Cha Iran should have been here. He found that truly regrettable. Would she be embarrassed? Flustered? Or would she, unexpectedly, handle the atmosphere with ease?

“Is she pretty?”

“You’re supposed to ask who she is!”

The two of them laughed together.

Of course, the Sword King knew the woman he was talking about was Cha Iran. She was the woman who had left the organization after fighting him.

“After this is over, let’s go meet her together.”

The Sword King did not answer that they should. Knowing that this silent answer was not because he disliked her, Geom Mugeuk insisted to the end.

“You changed a woman’s entire life, and now you’re acting this shamelessly? No, that won’t do! I’ll make sure to take you with me.”

Still, the Sword King said nothing.

I don’t know how you see the end of this fight, but I’m taking you back to our cult with me. If you’re going to leave, leave from there.

The Sword King, reunited with Geom Mugeuk like that, turned his gaze toward the two Demon Supremes.

The Sword King clasped his fist first and paid his respects to the two of them.

The Blood Heaven Saber Demon glared at him without hiding his displeasure, while the One Flower Sword Venerable lightly clasped her fist and returned his greeting.

Geom Mugeuk asked the Sword King the most important question.

“Why did you come here?”

An astonishing answer flowed from the Sword King’s mouth.

“To protect the person trying to kill you.”

Geom Mugeuk was not particularly surprised by those words. He had already expected as much the moment he first saw him.

“Then we’re in trouble. If Brother Ak is protecting him, no one will be able to kill him.”

At that moment, the Blood Heaven Saber Demon’s cold words came from behind.

“I’ll kill him.”

Geom Mugeuk knew he meant it.

“While I hold this man here tight, please kill him quickly.”

The Blood Heaven Saber Demon spat out,

“For heaven’s sake, make friends with normal people!”

Geom Mugeuk turned back to the Blood Heaven Saber Demon and answered,

“I buttoned the first button wrong, you see.”

Knowing that he meant him, the Blood Heaven Saber Demon shook his head.

Taesu, who had been watching everything until now, felt his heart pounding wildly.

It was not because Geom Mugeuk had met some unknown person in a place like this.

It was because, watching the doll being attacked from all sides, his heart was pounding for no reason. He had seen Geom Mugeuk in action several times before, but he had never felt this kind of pounding then. Was it because Geom Mugeuk had dealt with them too quickly?

But seeing him fight alone among terrifying-looking masters, for some reason he felt like a person of a heaven beyond the heavens, someone he could never approach.

Originally, once this matter was over, he had intended to meet his father and tell him that he would quit being a thief.

But now, a thought like this came to him.

‘Can I tell this man I want to become his subordinate?’

He wondered if he had been gazing up at a sky he could never reach, grasping alone at drifting clouds.

Taesu looked at Jihan.

‘Don’t you feel the same?’

Jihan, on the other hand, was watching the situation with calm eyes.

Because he knew Geom Mugeuk’s identity, he was not surprised by this situation at all.

A doll being attacked from all sides? Of course he should be the protagonist among those dolls. The person he served was the Young Cult Leader of the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult, after all.

However, because he had no idea how this situation would unfold, he was inwardly tense.

Was it really all right for his first battle serving the Young Cult Leader to become something this grand?

If the two of them felt this way, how shaken must the other martial artists have been?

The martial artists were greatly flustered by the unexpected situation. They had thought that if they opened this room, there would be mechanisms, and treasures would be waiting for them.

They had expected a scene where there was not one skeleton, but dozens, with all kinds of treasures piled beside them.

But reality did not forgive dreamers.

‘We were deceived!’

They thought Geom Mugeuk had deceived them and brought them here. The doll was one thing, and the fact that he was greeting someone who had been here so warmly was another.

But soon they realized that the person who had led them here was not Geom Mugeuk.

The iron-masked man respectfully greeted the Sword King and revealed his identity.

“It has been a long time.”

“Isn’t it hot walking around with that mask on?”

“I am fine.”

Judging from the casual exchange, the iron-masked man’s position did not seem very low.

“The person you’re looking for?”

“He should be among them.”

The iron-masked martial artist walked toward the martial artists.

He took an incense stick from his robe and lit it.

“This is Soul-Seeing Incense. It will react to the person we are looking for.”

The smoke flowing from the incense stick slowly spread around them.

As the martial artists watched the smoke approach them, they all felt fear. A situation they truly could not have imagined was unfolding.

Geom Mugeuk could tell. The person the iron-masked man was searching for was none other than Dan Sojin, the one who had sent him a voice transmission.

Srrr.

The smoke that had been slowly circling around began to coil about one person as if it were alive.

The person the smoke chose was Dan Sojin, just as Geom Mugeuk had expected.

Geom Mugeuk prepared for the worst.

If the iron-masked man tried to kill him, he intended to save him for now. Of course, that did not guarantee Dan Sojin was not an enemy.

After all, all he knew about him was his name. Even so, instinctively, he felt he had to keep him alive.

Fortunately, the iron-masked man had no intention of killing him.

“Come out.”

Dan Sojin trembled in terror. From the beginning until now, he had acted quite well.

“Why are you doing this?”

But it was already too late for acting.

“Stop and come out. You have to come out from there if you want to live.”

At those words, the remaining people were startled. Did that not mean he intended to kill everyone else?

The iron-masked man pointed out one more person.

“You come out as well.”

It was the man with the sword scar on his face. According to Dan Sojin, he was a martial artist from the Evil Alliance.

The iron-masked martial artist also knew exactly who he was.

“This man was sent by the Evil Alliance Leader. I was ordered to keep him alive as well.”

The scarred man who had been pointed out also walked forward and stood beside Dan Sojin. Now was neither the situation nor the atmosphere to resist.

After selecting those two, the iron-masked man walked toward the remaining martial artists. He intended to kill all the rest.

At that moment, the Sword King spoke calmly.

“It’s the day I met a friend after a long time. Don’t make it reek of blood.”

Understanding that he meant not to kill them, the iron-masked man was flustered.

“You must not do this.”

At that, the Sword King silently looked at the iron-masked man. He did not glare, nor did he reveal killing intent, but the iron-masked man immediately lowered his head deeply.

The Sword King spoke to the martial artists standing awkwardly.

“Leave without looking back.”

The martial artists rushed out along the path they had come.

“If I build up merit like this, won’t they dip me in a slightly less hot pit of fire in hell later?”

He spoke as though he had spared them for his own sake, but Geom Mugeuk could tell. He had let them go for him. Because he knew Geom Mugeuk disliked such indiscriminate slaughter.

It was right then.

Shwaaaaaak!

From the place where they had exited, the sound of astral qi whirling erupted.

Screams rang out, and the smell of blood suddenly wafted from that direction.

Soon after, four men entered the room.

Their attire was unusual. Two wore black martial robes with black cloaks, and black sabers hung at their waists. The other two wore red martial robes with red cloaks, and red sabers hung at their waists.

Between the two pairs, a man walked in.

He was a man with an absolutely overwhelming presence.

Tall and broad, with a solid build, he wore twin sabers at his waist.

The left saber and right saber he carried were each different.

The left saber was a jet-black Black Saber, and the right saber was a Blood Saber red as blood. On the black saber, a moon was drawn in pure white lines, while on the Blood Saber, a sun was drawn.

His features were sharply carved as if cut by a blade, and his pitch-black eyes shone like saber aura.

That gaze was so sharp that merely looking at it felt as though a blade had been placed against one’s throat.

The moment Geom Mugeuk saw him, he knew who he was.

The second of the Twelve Branch Kings, the Saber King Leng Wuxiang.

With those twin sabers, he had risen to the position of greatest master of saber arts during the era of the Twelve Branch Kings. His martial arts were so profound that it was rumored he possessed saber arts strong enough to rival the Sword King’s.

Everyone feared him. His terrifying saber arts were one reason, but even more than that, it was because of his cruel temperament, which at times approached madness.

The four martial artists who had entered ahead of him were absolute masters known as the Four Dark Blood Sabers, the Saber King’s hands and feet.

Now, following the Sword King, the Saber King had also appeared in this place. It was truly the moment when the first and second kings among the Twelve Branch Kings were gathered in one place.

The Sword King looked at the Saber King and said,

“The fires of your hell just grew hotter.”

The gazes of the two men intertwined in the air. In the Saber King’s eyes as he looked at the Sword King, there was not the slightest trace of fear. Nor was it a gaze that looked down on the Sword King.

At last, a low voice flowed from the Saber King’s mouth. His voice was sharp and dry, like the wind in the depths of winter.

“No matter how hard I tried to understand you, I could not.”

As if to say this person was the reason, the Saber King turned his head and looked at Geom Mugeuk.

Swaaaaaaa.

A strand of killing intent flew toward Geom Mugeuk. There was emotion contained in that killing intent. It was unpleasant, filled with hostility.

Just before that killing intent reached Geom Mugeuk—

Boom!

The Heaven-Destroying Great Saber struck into the ground before Geom Mugeuk.

The energy emitted by the Heaven-Destroying Great Saber, still wrapped in cloth, repelled even that single strand of killing intent without allowing it through.

Before anyone knew it, the Blood Heaven Saber Demon stood in front of Geom Mugeuk, his aura colder than anything. He was not the gentle man who usually read books and drank tea.

“This place is full of bastards I don’t like, every last one.”

The Blood Heaven Saber Demon untied the knot binding the Heaven-Destroying Great Saber.

Sararararak.

As the wrapped cloth came undone, the form of the Heaven-Destroying Great Saber was revealed.

“Let’s say I lost this wager.”

Chapter 750: So, You’ve Got a Temper Too?
-----------------------------------------
The Ilhwa Sword Venerable extended her snow-white hand forward.

Srrrk.

The cloth that had fallen to the floor flew into her hand. After neatly folding the cloth that had wrapped the Heaven-Annihilating Great Saber, she tucked it into her robe.

“You lost the wager, so please grant what I want.”

She did not refuse what the Blood Heaven Saber Demon yielded. She believed that not refusing was the proper courtesy to show him.

“Very well.”

The Blood Heaven Saber Demon pulled up the Heaven-Annihilating Great Saber embedded before him and unleashed his demonic qi.

Until now, throughout the journey, he had kept his demonic qi hidden.

Craaaaack!

The demonic qi tore through the air ahead and spread forward. At first, it looked as if the ice on the ground was slowly cracking and splitting apart, but the collapse gradually accelerated.

Then, as though something long asleep had awakened—

Swaaaaaaak!

The demonic qi ripped through space with terrifying force and shot toward where the Saber King stood. It was demonic qi announcing the opening of battle.

At the threatening wave of demonic qi, the Dark Blood Apostles covered themselves with the cloaks they wore and raised their protective qi.

Puaaaak!

Struck by the oncoming demonic qi, they slid backward in a line. That was how powerful the force that had flown at them was.

Swaaaaak.

The Saber King received the demonic qi while standing in place. His hair whipped about, and the hem of his clothes fluttered.

Even before this powerful demonic qi, his gaze, still filled with hostility, was directed past the Blood Heaven Saber Demon toward Geom Mugeuk.

“A coward, are you?”

It was a taunt, mocking him for hiding behind the Blood Heaven Saber Demon.

At a glance, it seemed as though he had provoked Geom Mugeuk, but in the end, he had provoked the Blood Heaven Saber Demon.

After all, he was ignoring the Blood Heaven Saber Demon, who had released the demonic qi, and was dealing with Geom Mugeuk instead.

It was a provocation either of them could have responded to, but unfortunately, his opponent was Geom Mugeuk.

From behind the Blood Heaven Saber Demon, Geom Mugeuk asked,

“Do you have one?”

For a moment, it was unclear what Geom Mugeuk was asking.

“I mean, do you have someone who can make you a coward like this? You don’t, do you?”

In that instant, the Saber King’s face twitched. He had tried to provoke his opponent, only to be provoked in return.

Looking at the Blood Heaven Saber Demon’s back, Geom Mugeuk said,

“I trust only our Left Envoy.”

To the Blood Heaven Saber Demon, those words sounded like this.

Do not get angry.

It was not, Do not fall for the provocation. Geom Mugeuk trusted that he would not fall for a provocation of this level.

That was how provocation worked. You knew perfectly well it was a provocation, and so you did not fall for it, but it still made you angry.

Geom Mugeuk had not even allowed that. Since this was a Young Cult Leader who cared for him to such an extent, he ought to show at least this much composure.

“You’re certain the Left Envoy ranks higher, right?”

At his question, which he asked every time, Geom Mugeuk smiled and answered vigorously.

“Of course! After the Cult Leader comes the Left Envoy!”

The Saber King, who had been listening to their conversation, looked at the innocent Sword King standing beside him. The emotion in the Saber King’s gaze was clear. I cannot possibly understand you, being entangled with that Young Cult Leader.

The Sword King stood with his arms crossed and showed no reaction. After all, neither the Saber King on this side nor the Demon Venerable on that side could understand his relationship with Geom Mugeuk.

The Blood Heaven Saber Demon walked forward.

Geom Mugeuk did not intervene in this fight. Whatever their goal was, whatever had been prepared afterward, once the Blood Heaven Saber Demon stepped forward himself, this was a fight that could not be stopped.

When one had to fight, one had to fight. For experts like him or the Blood Heaven Saber Demon, the times they grew the most were when they engaged in real combat against powerful enemies.

The opponent was formidable, but the Blood Heaven Saber Demon was no longer the man he had once been.

After Geom Mugeuk’s regression, the Blood Heaven Saber Demon’s skills had steadily improved through constant training, and during this excursion from the cult, he had consumed the rarest of spiritual medicines: the Stalactite Immortal Embryo and the Immortal Peach Spirit Fruit.

And what Geom Mugeuk trusted most was the sight of the Blood Heaven Saber Demon sitting by the window and reading a book. It was a peaceful daily life that had nothing to do with martial arts, but he believed that what had been gained from that daily life would protect the Blood Heaven Saber Demon.

Of course, that did not mean this was a fight in which one could relax.

The opponent was the Saber King, who had once coveted the Sword King’s level of skill.

Geom Mugeuk provoked the Saber King one last time.

“Don’t worry. There’s an empty spot for you among those dolls over there.”

The Saber King’s angular face grew even sharper. It was impossible to imagine a pleasant expression on his face.

The Sword King silently watched Geom Mugeuk.

Geom Mugeuk had said he liked the Sword King’s free way of being, but whenever he saw him like this, the Sword King found himself thinking:

Though he complained that he was bound by all manner of worries and human relationships, the person far freer than himself was Geom Mugeuk.

Whenever he looked at Geom Mugeuk, he felt that true freedom was not something felt when one stood apart alone, but something gained when one was properly connected to those around him. Just as one needed wings on both sides to fly.

The Saber King slowly drew his saber.

“I wanted to cross blades with you at least once someday.”

But of his two sabers, the Saber King drew only the red one. From his eyes flowed confidence and superiority, as though he could kill his opponent with a single saber.

The Blood Heaven Saber Demon did not let that arrogance pass either.

“Don’t you need two chopsticks?”

He had been peaceful lately, but how could his temperament ever have been ordinary?

Swaaaaaak!

The two men charged toward each other.

Kaang!

The Heaven-Annihilating Great Saber and the Red Saber clashed in midair.

Though it was merely saber against saber, an immense force erupted from the collision.

“Old Demon Venerable, is that saber not heavy for you now?”

“If it’s too heavy and you’re struggling, you can use two sabers.”

Swaaaaaak!

The Heaven-Annihilating Great Saber pushed him back and cleaved through the air.

Kaaang!

The Red Saber blocked the great saber’s tremendous power. That small saber withstood the massive blade.

Kakakakang!

Saber and saber sparked in the air.

It was clearly different from sword against sword. They flew at one another with a force that seemed capable of shattering anything they struck.

One question naturally arose in the minds of those watching.

How are the sabers not breaking under that force?

Each time they exchanged moves, that precarious thought surfaced.

Swaaaak! Swaaak!

The Red Saber flew at the Blood Heaven Saber Demon again and again.

Its attacks were fast, and the trajectories the Red Saber found were always connected to death.

Attacks that would surely split the opponent’s torso apart.

Yet at the final instant, severing the trajectory connected to death—

Kaang!

There was the Heaven-Annihilating Great Saber.

One would think there was no way he could keep up with that speed using such a heavy great saber.

But the Heaven-Annihilating Great Saber was blocking every saber strike that came flying in.

Kaang! Kang! Kaaang!

The Blood Heaven Saber Demon was using his saber like a shield. Taking full advantage of the great saber’s strengths, he blocked the opponent’s attacks with the smallest possible movements.

The Saber King’s swings grew even faster.

Dozens of saber lines created by their blades embroidered the air.

With every collision, dazzling flashes burst forth, and sparks scattered like fireworks.

They were not using signature martial arts that raised astral qi; they were waging a contest of force and speed in its purest form.

I can kill you with this alone.

A clash of pride between men who wielded sabers.

The sabers grazed past each other’s faces. They narrowly avoided attacks that would have blown their heads away entirely. Only by dodging this way could each block the next attack.

An exchange where even a single inch of unnecessary movement would lead to death.

Ordinary experts fight their opponents. But experts who have reached the extreme fight their fear.

Watching the battle, the Ilhwa Sword Venerable turned her head toward the quiet heat she sensed beside her.

Geom Mugeuk was staring fixedly at the fight. With all his nerves focused, he was already fighting alongside them.

She could tell. If a truly dangerous moment came, Geom Mugeuk would intervene.

Even if the Blood Heaven Saber Demon scolded him afterward, scolded him again and again, he would definitely intervene.

She could feel that burning resolve: he would absolutely not let the Blood Heaven Saber Demon die.

And Geom Mugeuk would be hoping for it. Hoping that the Blood Heaven Saber Demon would grow even further through this fight.

Her gaze returned to the Blood Heaven Saber Demon. The Blood Heaven Saber Demon was smiling as he fought.

‘He’s rejoicing in this battle.’

The exhilaration granted by a fight against a truly powerful enemy reached her. She felt a desire to rush in and fight as well.

‘Brother, we no longer need to long for our youth. Because right now, we are living through days even hotter than those.’

At that moment, as the fierce battle continued—

An unexpected attack flew from the Blood Heaven Saber Demon. The Saber King could not understand it.

‘He’s thrusting with a great saber?’

A reckless move always demanded a price.

A chance to deflect the saber as though letting it flow past, ride along the side of that blade, and cut him down in one stroke! Just as the Saber King was about to move according to his instincts—

Whiriririririri!

The Heaven-Annihilating Great Saber spinning as it flew toward him. The speed was unbelievable, that such a massive great saber could be rotated so instantly, and so quickly.

It tore through the surrounding air and came flying toward his chest.

‘I can’t dodge!’

In that fleeting instant, countless thoughts flashed through the Saber King’s mind.

If he simply dodged, he would certainly be wounded by that rotation, and if he tried to knock it away at an angle, his own saber would be sent flying. It was too late to retreat. If he dodged clumsily, he would suffer even greater damage!

Teong!

A great sound like an explosion erupted.

The Heaven-Annihilating Great Saber had stopped in midair. Its rotation had stopped as well.

Even though his attack had been blocked, the Blood Heaven Saber Demon was smiling.

It was not only the Red Saber that had blocked the Heaven-Annihilating Great Saber. In the Saber King’s other hand was the Black Saber. Left with no choice, he had drawn the Black Saber and stopped the rotation by crossing his twin sabers to block.

The Blood Heaven Saber Demon grinned and said,

“Now it’s two, isn’t it?”

Flames seemed to rise in the Saber King’s two eyes.

His aura became even more intense.

Since no one knew what kind of move would come next, Geom Mugeuk spoke to everyone.

“Now, now, everyone, let’s move back farther.”

Geom Mugeuk had Jihan, Taesu, Dan Sojin, and the Sado Alliance martial artist withdraw. As he did, he had Dan Sojin and the Sado Alliance martial artist come closer to his side.

The two, who had been standing a little distance away, obediently came toward Geom Mugeuk.

While everyone’s attention was focused on the Blood Heaven Saber Demon and the Saber King, Dan Sojin discreetly handed something to Geom Mugeuk.

When he received it, it was a single medicinal pill.

—It is the antidote to the poison you suffered earlier.

—How can I trust that?

—The you I know is someone who can see the truth. I trust you will judge wisely.

Geom Mugeuk looked at him silently. In any case, it would not matter even if this pill he had given him was poison.

—I will trust you.

Geom Mugeuk swallowed the pill before his eyes. After taking it, he found it was not poison. It seemed it really was the antidote.

—I trust you, but do you trust me?

To Geom Mugeuk’s question of whether he was truly on their side,

—If I had not trusted you, I would never have come here in the first place.

With that voice transmission, he said nothing more.

Why had those bastards sought this man out? Why was this man helping him?

And there was one more question.

Why had they told him to keep that Sado Alliance martial artist alive?

The iron-masked martial artist had said so earlier. He had said he had been ordered to keep him alive.

From the feel of those words, the person who had given that order was neither the Sword King nor that Saber King.

‘There is still an enemy who has not come to this place.’

Geom Mugeuk was certain of it. The fact that they had called the Sword King and the Saber King meant they, too, had staked everything. There were still enemies remaining.

Geom Mugeuk did not relax his vigilance inwardly.

For now, he intended to protect Dan Sojin and the Sado Alliance martial artist sent by the leader of the Sado Alliance as much as possible.

The Saber King said coldly,

“Old Demon Venerable, you are worthy of receiving my twin sabers.”

“Yes, showing off is something you do in your prime. Once you get old, it becomes too much of a bother.”

The Blood Heaven Saber Demon never lost a war of words. Geom Mugeuk thought it was all because he had read so many books.

The Blood Heaven Saber Demon and the Saber King charged toward each other.

Kaaang!

The twin sabers blocked the Heaven-Annihilating Great Saber.

The Saber King swiftly brought his twin sabers down. The two blades flew in, crossing at terrifying speed.

Two sabers defeat one saber.

If he had not held that belief, he would never have chosen twin sabers in the first place.

And yet, though he believed he could surely suppress him with twin sabers—

The Heaven-Annihilating Great Saber was not pushed back by the two blades in the slightest.

At the very moment that taut exchange reached its peak—

When the two powers, each exerted to the utmost, collided, what erupted was not an explosion, but silence. A fleeting instant passed in which sound devoured sound.

Kwaaaang!

An ear-splitting boom followed.

Slide—

The one who was pushed back several more steps was the Saber King. The Blood Heaven Saber Demon had overwhelmed him with inner power.

“You were this strong?”

Surprise was contained in the Saber King’s dry voice.

The Blood Heaven Saber Demon had definitely grown stronger.

He felt the vitality overflowing within his body. It was not simply a matter of having more or less inner power.

The energy flowing through his body was young. When the power born from vivid youth combined with the seasoned Blood Heaven Saber Demon, it produced truly tremendous results. His mind worked better, and his true qi moved more smoothly. This was not a matter of inner power.

‘Yes, this was what it felt like back then.’

This feeling that he could fight all day and never tire.

Hooooooo.

The moon drawn on the Red Saber in the Saber King’s hand and the sun drawn on the Black Saber began to shine.

He began to release an even more powerful aura.

At that tremendous energy, the surrounding space began to distort.

Swaaaaaak.

Taesu and Jihan trembled all over. It was truly the first time they had ever experienced such a terrifying aura, one that seemed as though it would tear their entire bodies apart.

Geom Mugeuk released his inner force to protect the four people standing behind him. Without it, they would have suffered internal injuries from the aura alone.

But within the storm-like surge of his aura, the Blood Heaven Saber Demon was not shaken in the slightest.

Through this aura, one could tell what sort of person the Saber King was. Cold, heartless, merciless, cruel. It was an aura in which not the slightest trace of warmth could be found.

“Yes, you’ve got quite the temper too, don’t you?”

Demonic qi erupted from the Blood Heaven Saber Demon’s body. It was powerful demonic qi that could not be compared to what he had released at the beginning.

Swaaaaaaaa.

It was the Blood Heaven Saber Demon’s rough and violent demonic qi, something neither Geom Mugeuk nor the Ilhwa Sword Venerable had ever seen before. It was also demonic qi he had hidden from everyone for a long time.

As the Blood Heaven Saber Demon’s demonic qi tangled with the Saber King’s aura, the place felt as though they were standing in the very heart of hell.

Resting the massive Heaven-Annihilating Great Saber upon his shoulder, the Blood Heaven Saber Demon said,

“I am the one who went through all that filthy temper and made it this far.”
